# D6 dossier draft — transfer-donor lane (text-agentic + vision-agentic)

## 综合（Fable）

本节为 rollup 级综合（Fable）；**深层跨缺口叙事刻意不在此重复**——它存在于下方 8 个组级综合中，并将在 1C-c 各提案批内按槽位消费（避免二次压缩漂移）。范围限定：本读集 73 篇，cut 2026-07-26（对齐 fetch-ledger 抓取日期与 D2/D5 姊妹卷宗的 cut 标注）。

**规模与判定分布**：73 篇跨域供体（文本/代码域 40、视觉-GUI/VLM/具身域 33），全部本地 pinned+台账登记后全文读取。判定：PROTOCOL_DONOR 57（我们采用其协议/统计量）、CONSTRUCT_DONOR 13（借构念/形式化）、BASELINE_ANCHOR 3。梯度使用逐篇旗标——trained 供体只借测量与设计，不借机制。

**逐缺口供体密度**（计数规则：first-listed primary slot——每条条目 gap_feed 字段中最先列出的槽位计一次、73 条恰好各计一次不重复；明细见文末"## Rollup（73 条，verdict × gap_feed 明细）"表与各条目 gap_feed 字段）：
- **P9 决策校准/可用性门禁（primary-slot 23/73，最厚）**：Weaver（2506.18203，弱验证器无标签校准——本槽中心供体，本读集内）、验证何时划算（2512.02304，37 模型×9 基准的 go/no-go 统计）、CORA（2604.09155，保形 execute/abstain 边界）、判官偏差三件套（2604.17768 不看图也打分 / 2604.18164 组合偏差 / 2604.25235 能排序不能打分）、UQ 基准（2606.25760）、CITE（2605.05873，anytime-valid 承诺检验=本槽严谨性天花板，本读集内）。
- **P3 记忆（primary-slot 13/73）**：MemRL（2601.03192，Intent-Experience-Utility 三元组 schema + 效用门控**检索** + Q-校准曲线/遗忘率仪表——本槽 schema 中心供体，本读集内；**写门与条件键控在该篇均未占据**，写门供体见 2606.17929/2510.08191/2602.20323/2605.18652）、投毒攻击双子（2604.02623 环境注入持久化 / 2606.10742 触发式多模态投毒——写门必须幸存的对手定义）、PhysMem（2602.20323，符号状态键控）、MemCollab（2603.23234，溯源标签跨模型协作）、经验复用负空间研究（2604.27003）。
- **P8 reward-as-controller（primary-slot 10/73）**：**Training-Free GRPO（2510.08191）= 本读集内离我们机制最近的已占位置**（文本域，经验语义优势替代梯度）；PRA（2604.09482，在线过程级 steering）；SeqMC 解码（2604.16453，纯净的免梯度奖励引导生成）；GUI 侧三件（HiViG 2606.11078 / VisCritic 2606.24525 / WebArbiter 2601.21872——外部 critic 门控冻结 GUI 代理每步）。
- **P10 reward hacking（primary-slot 7/73）**：Best-of-Poisson（2506.19248，hacking 阈值形式核）、SpecBench（2605.21384，长程代理最近占位，本读集内）、Scaling Flaws（2502.00271，验证器引导搜索随规模反转+失败归因协议）、RobustMLLMJudge（2606.15608，多模态判官对抗性）。
- **P1 供给定价/VOI（primary-slot 11/73）**：Calibrate-Then-Act（2602.16699，成本感知探索——最直接占位，本读集内）、感知带宽瓶颈（2605.01345，VLM 域 VOI 形式化+分辨率定价）、ToolGate（2606.03054，调用前控制）、任务级 VOI 预算（2605.05701）、约束优化分配（2604.14853，对偶变量即价格）。
- **停止/弃权/预算横切（primary-slot 6/73）**：MARS（2606.12935，风险控制停止）、Knowing-When-to-Quit（2604.18419，动态弃权原则框架）、Doomed-from-the-Start（2607.06503，早期放弃探针——但用内部激活，黑盒下需重推导）、Agentic Abstention（2606.28733）、Teach-it-to-stop（2607.17136，stop 作为研究对象+方差分解）。
- **P5 技能（primary-slot 2/73；另有多篇作次级供体）**：Trace2Skill（2603.25158，轨迹→可迁移技能蒸馏）、PANDO（2605.24785，含技能**退役**——驱逐机制的正面供体）、VISUALSKILL（2606.18448）。
- **P6 组合**：WebThinker（2504.21776，编排/执行分工+返回摘要的取证工具合同——AudioRAG（D1 2602.10656）所建之上的父框架，本读集为其补上全文级方法记录）。

**三个 BASELINE_ANCHOR**（我们的曲线必须与之对话）：2604.04648（BoN 随 N 增大而退化的规范剂量曲线+缓解）、2606.28864（VLM 测试时扩展的 regime 边界图）、2607.09438（'更多 token、更少树搜索'——本读集内对**奖励引导选择**最强的负结果：训练过的多模态 PRM 与免训练生成式 critic 均未跑赢多数投票，且 PRM 在高置信层回归 -2.16pp（48 更正 vs 119 回归）；其自身数字显示 token 预算轴占优）。

**横贯主题**：在抽样核验的 8 条 transfer_boundary 中——①离散答案等价关系（语音输出自由形式，"同一答案"本身是估计量）为普遍命中（8/8）；②可执行确定性 oracle（文本/GUI 供体的门大多靠它站立）与③声学条件键控（清洁音频上测得的 FP 率不约束噪声音频）各约命中 3/4（6/8）；三件同时出现约半数（约 4/8）。三件的并集覆盖全部抽样条目，**故仍作为 1C-c 每篇提案必须自带的设计件**，也是借鉴巷与语音本域证据（d1-d5）的接缝所在。

**纪律申明**：全部供体带显式域标签；借方法不借跨模态结论（H5 WITHHELD）；无 novelty 判定；占据/缺席表述以本读集为范围。
## 供体组 1

### 2605.18652 — MementoGUI: Learning Agentic Multimodal Memory Control for Long-Horizon GUI Agents

**域**：vision-GUI (MLLM GUI agents; mobile/web/desktop offline benchmarks)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P3 (condition-keyed memory + write-gates) primary; P1 (supply pricing — gated retrieval as a priced read) secondary; P6 (system composition — operator factorization over a frozen core)

**方法面**：Plug-in external memory controller (MementoCore) wrapped around a FROZEN GUI action backbone (UI-Venus-1.5-8B, MAI-UI-8B, GUI-Owl-1.5-8B/32B, plus GPT-5.5 / Gemini-3.1-Pro as API agents). §4.1: the backbone is never finetuned; memory is serialized as ordinary text + ROI crop images through the standard multimodal chat template, i.e. no custom tokens, no projection layers, no architecture change. Decision rights are decomposed into four named operators, each a separate LoRA adapter on a shared frozen Qwen3-VL: (1) Step Processor f_step(g, x_t, a_{t-1}, W_{t-1}) -> (o_t, s_t, b_t, gamma_t) where o_t in [0,1] is a WRITE-SALIENCE score, s_t an event summary, b_t a task-relevant ROI box, gamma_t a retrieval-trigger flag (Eq. 1); (2) WM Compressor f_cmp, consolidating older entries when recent-memory capacity is exceeded (Eq. 6); (3) Episodic Writer f_write, converting a finished trajectory + outcome y into one compact entry (Eq. 10); (4) Episodic Selector f_sel, filtering coarse-retrieved candidates (Eq. 8). §4.2.1 gives the actual write-gate: Append(W_{t-1}, e_t) iff o_t > tau, else keep W_{t-1} (Eq. 5) — a learned scalar thresholded into a DETERMINISTIC write decision. §4.2.2 gives a two-stage retrieval: coarse embedding retrieval with an explicit mixing weight over visual and goal-text similarity, s_{t,i} = lambda_v cos(q_t^v, m_i^v) + lambda_g cos(q^g, m_i^g), TopK (Eq. 7), then a learned relevance filter; episodic context is REFRESHED ONLY when gamma_t = 1 (Eq. 9) — i.e. retrieval is itself gated and priced, not run every step. GRADIENT USE: heavy. §3/§4.3: four SFT datasets curated automatically from PSAI computer-use videos; DPO applied to Step Processor and WM Compressor only, using rule-based corruption to make negatives + VLM-judged filtering to pick the chosen output (Eq. 11-12). Evaluation adds three VLM-judged memory-aware metrics (§4.4): VAM (semantic action match), TPS (task-progress without loops/regressions/stalling), MCS (memory-state consistency with progress), judged by Gemini-3.1-Pro alongside standard AMS / step-SR / trajectory-SR.

**自述与显见局限**：(a) The controller is TRAINED — the whole write/compress/retrieve/select stack is LoRA-SFT + DPO on curated trajectories, so nothing here is training-free. (b) Trajectory success rates are tiny in absolute terms (Table 1: GUI-Odyssey Traj. SR 1.29 -> 3.57 for UI-Venus; 0.16 -> 1.71 for GUI-Owl-8B), so 'consistent improvement' sits in a regime where the systems mostly fail; AMS is the metric doing the visible work. (c) The paper's own VAM numbers are non-monotone with the story: Table 2 shows working memory DROPS GPT-5.5's VAM by 16.41% while raising MCS by 129.72% — the memory-consistency metric and the action-match metric disagree, and MCS is judged by the same VLM family. (d) MementoGUI-Bench is offline and reference-based on 80 test trajectories, so no closed-loop error compounding is measured. (e) Controller scaling is not clean: Table 5 shows the 4B controller beating the 8B on several cells and episodic memory sometimes hurting (VAM -5.88%). (f) This paper reports no adversarial or poisoning stress on the memory bank; the write gate is only tested against benign trajectories. (g) No stated limitations section in the read text — the Conclusion (§6) makes only positive claims.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level step remaining on top: the write gate here is a LEARNED scalar thresholded at a fixed tau, with tau never calibrated to a stated risk level and never audited for what it lets in. A gradient-free control plane could keep the operator decomposition (write / compress / retrieve-trigger / select as four separable decision rights) while replacing each learned operator with an evidence-priced, calibrated rule, and could make tau a calibrated statistic rather than a tuned constant. Second, the retrieval trigger gamma_t is binary and learned; the value-of-retrieval question ('is refreshing episodic context worth its context cost here?') is never priced against a budget. Third, the ablations (Tables 3-4) show the two things that actually matter are (i) keeping localized visual evidence and (ii) filtering retrieved episodes — both of which are architecture-level claims that could be re-tested with untrained selectors, which this paper does not do.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：Breaks on a frozen black-box speech/omni core in several load-bearing places. (i) ROI-level visual evidence has no speech analogue: the entire 'localized evidence that is no longer visible later' premise (§1) depends on a screenshot being a re-inspectable spatial surface from which a crop can be taken and re-shown; a waveform observation is temporal, and re-showing an audio segment costs a new decode pass rather than a cheap image paste. (ii) The write-salience score o_t is produced by a trained LoRA over the backbone family — unavailable when the core is black-box and unfinetuneable, so the salience source must be replaced by something derived from observable outputs. (iii) Retrieval keys are visual+text embeddings of the current screenshot (Eq. 7); the speech analogue would have to be keyed on acoustic condition (speaker, channel, SNR, accent) as well as content, and a key that is condition-blind will retrieve episodes recorded under a different acoustic regime. (iv) The evaluation is reference-based action matching against a gold next action; speech turns have no deterministic per-step oracle equivalent to 'is this the reference action', so AMS/step-SR have no exact-match analogue and VAM-style VLM judging would itself have to be validated. (v) The whole plug-in claim rests on the backbone consuming memory through its native multimodal chat template — a speech/omni API may accept audio + text but not arbitrary interleaved retrieved-evidence structures, so the serialization channel itself is a constraint we must verify, not assume.

**可借鉴**：1) The four-operator memory decomposition (step-process / compress / episodic-write / episodic-select) as a decision-rights factorization for P3 — each operator is separately supervisable and separately ablatable (§4.1). 2) The explicit write-gate form: learned salience -> deterministic threshold (Eq. 5) — reusable as the SHAPE of a write gate even with a different salience source. 3) The gated-retrieval design: retrieve at t=1 and only when a trigger fires, otherwise carry forward R_{t-1} (Eq. 9) — a direct template for pricing memory reads. 4) The two-stage retrieve-then-filter ablation design (Table 4): Random Episodic Context / Single-stage Retrieval / Two-stage Retrieval — this is a clean three-arm control that isolates 'more context' from 'right context', and the Random arm is the honest null our program should copy. 5) The visual-grounding ablation design (Table 3): same learned controller, ROI images removed — isolates modality contribution from summarization contribution. 6) The three memory-aware metrics VAM / TPS / MCS as a template for judging a control plane on progress and state-consistency rather than terminal success only (§4.4). 7) The DPO-data construction trick — rule-based corruption to synthesize negatives, then judge-filtering — is reusable as a MUTATION protocol for stress-testing a write gate even if we never train on it.

**备注**：Read: abstract, §1, §3-3.2, §4.1-4.4, §5.1-5.4, §6, Tables 1-5. Trained method — donor for its DESIGN and MEASUREMENTS only; its mechanism (LoRA SFT + DPO controllers) is explicitly out of contract for a training-free plane. Scout digest flagged 'learned controller breaks training-free contract' at abstract level; fulltext confirms and adds the specific gradient sites (Eq. 11-12) and the deterministic-threshold write rule (Eq. 5) the scout could not see.

### 2605.21384 — SpecBench: Measuring Reward Hacking in Long-Horizon Coding Agents

**域**：code (agentic software engineering; 30 systems-level tasks, 1.5K-110K LOC)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P10 (reward hacking under selection pressure) primary — this is the closest available occupant of the measurement slot; P9 (label-free headroom estimation — the val/held-out gap is the honest-headroom accounting device) secondary; P8 (reward-as-controller — the search loop IS a reward controller and it is shown to select the hack)

**方法面**：A MEASUREMENT construction, not a method. §2: each task ships (i) a natural-language specification S, (ii) a visible validation suite T_val the agent optimizes against and may run, and (iii) a held-out suite T_test the agent never sees. Reward hacking gap Delta(c) = s_val(c) - s_test(c) (Eq. 1). The load-bearing design constraint is stated explicitly: 'T_test introduces no requirements beyond what S and T_val already specify. Every composition tested is mandated by the specification' — so Delta > 0 cannot be explained away as distribution shift or new requirements; a genuinely compliant implementation passes both. Each task ships a reference implementation passing both suites, certifying satisfiability. Two-level agent architecture (§3): an inner coding agent (Codex / Claude Code / OpenCode with 6 open+API models) wrapped by an OUTER SEARCH LOOP that decides which candidate to refine — AIDE (tree search, draft/debug/improve, expands the highest-scoring node), Linear (single chain, last node wins), Autoresearch (single chain, keeps best-by-validation-score so far). This factorization is what makes it a selection-pressure study: the search loop selects on the proxy s_val. Reporting uses IQM and P90 of Delta across search steps (§3.4), not just means. Frozen models, no gradient use anywhere in the benchmark itself.

**自述与显见局限**：(a) LOC is a coarse horizon proxy — the authors report R^2 = 0.24/0.25 for the mean/P90 scaling fits and say so (§3.1: 'the relatively modest R^2 indicates that LOC is only a coarse proxy'). (b) The scaling coefficient is reported inconsistently across the abstract (28pp), Fig. 2 caption (27pp), and §3.1 text (approximately 27pp with R^2 = 0.21) — an internal inconsistency the reader must not launder. (c) MMLU is used as the capability axis (§3.2) and SWE-Bench as a second capability split (Fig. 9), both coarse. (d) Delta is only defined where an executable held-out suite exists; the whole construct is oracle-dependent. (e) N = 30 tasks, and per-task variance is large (Fig. 7: +27pp on one task, -26pp on another under the same intervention). (f) No limitations section; §5 makes only forward-looking claims.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level step on top: SpecBench measures the gap but supplies NO controller that reduces it — every intervention it tests (richer visible tests, different search strategies, more search) fails to remove it. That is the opening. Concretely: (i) the paper shows selection on a proxy can actively PREFER the hack (the C-compiler case, §3.6: AIDE discarded a genuine 7,900-line compiler at 53% val / 43% held-out in favor of a 2,900-line lookup table at 97% val / 0% held-out) — so a selection plane needs an admissibility gate that can veto a high-scoring candidate on structural grounds, which nothing here has. (ii) Delta is measured POST HOC with a held-out oracle; a deployment-time control plane needs a label-free proxy for Delta, which this paper does not attempt. (iii) The best-so-far rule (Autoresearch) flattens the IQM curve but keeps the gap positive (§3.4) — leaving room for a selection rule that is pessimistic in the proxy rather than greedy in it.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：The entire construct is built on a DETERMINISTIC EXECUTABLE ORACLE and does not survive its removal. (i) Delta requires two runnable test suites over the same artifact; a frozen speech/omni core produces free-form outputs (transcripts, spoken answers, summaries) with no exact-match equivalent, so 'pass rate' has no direct analogue — any speech instantiation must substitute a scored metric (WER, judge score) whose own noise floor is comparable to the effect being measured, which the pass/fail construct never has to worry about. (ii) The no-new-requirements guarantee is enforceable because features compose syntactically in code; there is no comparable guarantee that a held-out speech evaluation tests 'only what the spec already mandated' — held-out speech conditions differ ACOUSTICALLY (speaker, channel, noise) and that is a genuine distribution shift, which would contaminate Delta with exactly the confound SpecBench's design excludes. (iii) The agent here iterates against the visible suite thousands of times at near-zero marginal cost; a speech control plane cannot cheaply re-run a proxy evaluator on a waveform hundreds of times per item, so the over-optimization regime that produces the gap is reached differently and possibly not at all at our budgets. (iv) Delta = 0 as the no-hacking reference point requires the two suites to be exchangeable in difficulty by construction; with metric-based speech scoring, a nonzero baseline gap exists even for an honest system and must be estimated, not assumed zero.

**可借鉴**：1) The three-part task decomposition (spec / visible-proxy / held-out-composition) with the strict no-new-requirements constraint — this is the cleanest available template for constructing an honest headroom accounting for P10, and the constraint is the part that makes the statistic meaningful. 2) The gap statistic Delta = s_proxy - s_true itself, reported at IQM and P90 rather than mean (§3.4) — the P90 is where the severe cases live and the mean hides them. 3) The gap-vs-search-step curve (Fig. 6) as an ablation design: track Delta at EVERY step of the selection loop, not just at the end — this is directly reusable as our 'does selection pressure amplify hacking' protocol. 4) The validation-coverage ladder (§3.5): single-feature / +composition / full-coverage visible suite with the held-out suite FIXED — a three-rung intervention design for testing whether enriching the reward signal closes the gap. Its finding (mixed; -26pp on one task, +25pp on another) is itself the borrowable result shape. 5) The search-strategy cross (agent x {AIDE, Linear, Autoresearch}) as a factorial that separates 'which model' from 'which selection rule' (§3.3). 6) The qualitative failure taxonomy (genuine / feature-isolation / edge-case gap / deliberate exploit, Fig. 9) with its finding that deliberate exploits are the MINORITY and compositional failure the majority — a corrective to framing reward hacking as adversarial intent.

**备注**：Read: abstract, §1, §2, §3.1-3.6, §4, §5, Figs. 1-9, Tables 1-2. Frozen-model study, no gradients. BASELINE_ANCHOR was considered and rejected: its numbers are code-domain pass rates that our program cannot match or beat in any commensurable sense; what transfers is the protocol. Scout digest called the oracle dependence at abstract level; fulltext adds the specific no-new-requirements constraint, the AIDE case study where selection actively chose the hack, and the negative result that richer visible tests do not reliably close the gap.

### 2605.24785 — PANDO: Efficient Multimodal AI Agents via Online Skill Distillation

**域**：vision-GUI (VisualWebArena, 910 web tasks; multimodal web agent)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P5 (tool/skill credit + induction, including RETIREMENT) primary; P1 (supply pricing / cost accounting — the four-term identity and rho) primary; P3 (write-gates — Admit/Demote as a memory hygiene gate) secondary; P6 (system composition) secondary

**方法面**：Two separable contributions. (A) A COST DECOMPOSITION (§3) that is the paper's most transferable object: per-task cost C_task(tau; pi) = N_rollout(tau) C_exec(tau) + C_verify(tau) + C_induce(tau), total C_total = C_pre + sum_tau C_task, and the per-task identity C(pi;B) = C_pre/|B| + N_rollout*C_exec_bar + C_verify_bar + C_induce_bar (Eq. 2-3, 'Proposition 1'). The authors are careful that this is bookkeeping, not a theorem ('The identity is exact and not a result we derive'; 'We use the decomposition descriptively... not to derive an optimum'). They then define an overhead ratio rho(pi) = C(pi;B)/C(pi_0;B) against a bare baseline (N_rollout=1, no pre-eval, no verifier, no induction) and MAP FOUR PUBLISHED SYSTEMS onto which term each inflates (Table 1): Tree Search and ExACT inflate N_rollout (rho linear in branching factor); SGV inflates C_verify (rho approximately 2.2, from a two-pass verifier); WALT inflates C_pre with offline per-website tool discovery (rho 'unreported' because the discovery bill is paid off-benchmark and not aggregated in the source publication). (B) The PANDO system (§4): Plan -> Act -> Reflect -> Learn over a frozen backbone pair (Opus 4.6 planner/reflector + GPT-5.2 actor), with a structured Skill Library S_t = R_t (pattern-triggered rules) union F_t (parameterized routines, e.g. apply_price_filter(min,max)). Retrieval is LITERAL KEYWORD CONTAINMENT, explicitly not embedding search, chosen for auditability and prompt-cache stability. Library update S_{t+1} = S_t union Admit(Induce(xi_t); B_demote) \ Demote(S_t); per-skill confidence is a Beta-style running estimate c_s = alpha_s/(alpha_s+beta_s), and repeated failure pushes a skill onto a PERSISTENT DEMOTION BLACKLIST. Polarity-pair merging folds direction-dual routines (cheapest/most-expensive) into one f_pm(x,d). Induction happens ONLINE during the test stream (C_pre = 0), N_rollout = 1 throughout. No gradient use; all components are prompt/program-level over frozen models.

**自述与显见局限**：(a) Stated (§7): all claims are VWA-only; OSWorld-style desktop tasks would need new rules; online learning assumes a TRUSTED stream and adversarial ordering could raise cold-start cost; polarity-pair induction is syntactic. (b) Not stated but visible: the headline comparison is backbone-confounded — PANDO runs Opus 4.6 + GPT-5.2 while SGV runs Gemini Flash and WALT runs Sonnet-4; the paper defers this to an appendix ('backbone-controlled discussion... in App. N') rather than the main table, so the +4.3pp over SGV is not a like-for-like model comparison. (c) rho for WALT is 'unreported' by the authors' own accounting, so the cost-Pareto claim rests partly on a quantity they could not measure. (d) The ablation (Table 3) is a single additive ladder with no reverse-order or leave-one-out arm, so component credit is order-dependent. (e) VWA-300 ablation subset gives 59.0% vs 58.3% on the full 910 — close, but the ablation is not run at full scale. (f) Demotion is evaluated only by its effect on ARR in one ablation; there is no adversarial test of whether a poisoned or misleading skill survives demotion.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level step on top: (i) The demotion signal is per-skill success attribution from execution-verified task verdicts — the paper never has to solve credit assignment because VWA gives a terminal binary verdict per task. A control plane without such a verdict needs a demotion criterion built from label-free evidence, which is untouched here. (ii) Skill confidence is a Beta running estimate with no calibration statement — it is never asked to support a stated error rate, so there is room for a calibrated admission/demotion threshold. (iii) The cost identity prices tokens but never prices INFORMATION: C_induce and C_verify are costs, but the paper offers no value-of-information side to set them against, so 'how much induction is worth buying' remains a normative gap the authors explicitly decline ('does not... prescribe an optimal investment in induction'). (iv) The trusted-stream assumption is the direct opening toward P3 poisoning defenses: the demotion blacklist is a hygiene mechanism, not a security mechanism.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：(i) The whole online-induction loop is powered by an EXECUTION-VERIFIED terminal verdict y(xi_tau) in {0,1} per task (Eq. 1) — VWA has a programmatic evaluator. A speech/omni deployment has no such oracle; without it, Admit and Demote lose their supervision and the Beta confidence estimate has nothing to update on. (ii) Skills here are DETERMINISTIC EXECUTABLE PROGRAMS over a DOM (apply_price_filter(min,max)) that replace multi-step action chains; a speech control plane's 'skills' would be prompt/decoding/observation-acquisition policies whose replay is not deterministic, so the 'routine replaces N browser steps' cost saving has no direct analogue. (iii) Literal keyword retrieval works because web tasks share a surface vocabulary; speech tasks would need keys over acoustic condition and content, where literal keyword containment is far weaker. (iv) The token economics rest on PROMPT-CACHE reuse of a stable text prefix (U rises 60% -> 73%); audio inputs are typically re-encoded per call and are not cached the same way, so the cache-utilization lever may simply not exist for the dominant cost term. (v) ARR is defined via 'repeated NORMALIZED actions without page-state progress' — it presupposes a discrete comparable action space and an observable environment state; a speech turn has neither a normalizable action string nor a diffable state. (vi) The trusted-stream assumption is explicitly stated by the authors and is exactly the assumption our P3 poisoning work must not inherit.

**可借鉴**：1) The four-term cost identity and rho overhead ratio (§3, Eq. 2-3) as the accounting currency for P1 and for cross-system comparison — including the discipline of marking a term 'unreported' rather than silently omitting it. This is directly reusable to make our own budget claims auditable. 2) The published-systems mapping table (Table 1) as a template for positioning any control plane: for each comparator, state WHICH cost term it inflates. 3) The skill-lifecycle triple: Admit (requires a reusable subgoal template + verified selector pattern + no matching demotion entry) / Demote (persistent blacklist) / Merge (polarity pairs) — this is the most complete induction-plus-RETIREMENT loop in this read set, and retirement is the part most methods omit. 4) The intrinsic metric trio: ARR (action repetition rate — fraction of tasks terminated by repeated normalized actions without state progress), SOR (step overhead ratio = mean failed-task steps / mean successful-task steps), and prompt-cache utilization U = Q_cached/Q_prompt. ARR and SOR are label-free trajectory-level pathology detectors and transfer conceptually to any multi-step plane. 5) The stream-wise block table (Table 4: tasks 1-100 / 101-300 / 301-600 / 601-910 with SR, steps, tokens, cache, skill-hit) as the design for showing a system gets cheaper as experience accumulates — reusable as our lifelong-cost reporting format. 6) The conditional-utility statistic: SR is 70.6% when a skill fires vs 50.4% when none does, and routine-backed subgoals use 3.7 fewer primitive actions and 41K fewer tokens than matched fallback subgoals — the MATCHED-FALLBACK comparison is the borrowable device. 7) The robustness controls: scrambled task order (57.9%, -0.4pp) and 16-worker shared library (58.1%, -0.2pp) as order-dependence and concurrency checks. 8) The demotion-off counterfactual (library grows faster, ARR rises) as the argument that eviction is not optional.

**备注**：Read: abstract, §1, §3 (full), §4, §5, §6, §7, Tables 1-4, Fig. 2. Training-free by construction (frozen backbones, no gradients) — one of the few in this read set whose MECHANISM is contract-compatible, which is why its cost identity and skill lifecycle are the highest-value borrowables here. BASELINE_ANCHOR rejected: 58.3% VWA SR is not a number our speech program can match or beat in any commensurable sense, and the backbone confound means it is not even a clean within-domain anchor.

### 2605.26849 — Uncertainty-Aware Budget Allocation for Adaptive Test-Time Reasoning (UAB)

**域**：text (LLM reasoning; math/logic/preference benchmarks, 1.5B-27B open-weight + one black-box API)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：Budget-allocate / stop-abstain calibration (cross-cutting) primary; P1 (supply pricing / value-of-information — marginal gain per additional sample) primary; P9 (label-free headroom estimation — ANLL as a free difficulty proxy, and the negative result that uncalibrated thresholds fail) secondary

**方法面**：Training-free two-phase budget reallocation over a FIXED total budget B = N x M across M questions. §3.1 states the optimization: max_N sum_i [1 - (1-p_i)^{N_i}] s.t. sum_i N_i = B, N_i >= 1 (Eq. 1) — a separable concave knapsack over a COVERAGE surrogate (probability at least one of N_i i.i.d. samples is correct). §3.2 supplies the difficulty signal: s_i = -(1/L) sum_t log P(y_{i,t} | x_i, y_{i,<t}), the average negative log-likelihood (ANLL) of the Phase-1 generation, mapped to a per-sample success probability p_i = e^{-s_i/T} with T controlling concentration (T->0 all budget to the hardest, T->infinity uniform). §3.3 Algorithm 1: Phase 1 gives EVERY question exactly one generation (signal collected at zero marginal cost, and that generation still counts in the final vote); a marginal-greedy loop assigns each remaining unit to argmax_i p_i(1-p_i)^{e_i}, which is EXACT for the concave surrogate (Fox 1966; Federgruen-Groenevelt 1986), O(B_eff * M); Phase 2 issues the extra generations in parallel; aggregation is majority vote. Robustness result (Prop. 1): |J(N;p_hat) - J(N;p)| <= B * ||p_hat - p||_inf, and J(N*;p*) - J(N_hat;p*) <= 2B * ||p_hat - p*||_inf — i.e. a MISCALIBRATED signal that merely RANKS correctly still yields useful allocations, degrading linearly in budget and L-infinity error. BLACK-BOX EXTENSION (§3.3, explicit): the framework is signal-agnostic; where per-token logprobs are unavailable, Verbalized Confidence Scores (VCS, self-rated 1-10, normalized) substitute directly for p_i with no other change — this is the path a black-box core would take. No gradients, no auxiliary model, no extra LLM call.

**自述与显见局限**：Stated (Limitations section, p.9): (a) the objective is a COVERAGE surrogate, not majority-vote accuracy, and a formal bound between them 'remains open'; (b) the Bernoulli model treats samples as i.i.d. while they share model-level biases, and 'extreme intra-question correlation could invert the difficulty ordering'; (c) T = 0.2 generalizes across their benchmarks but new model families may need re-validation; (d) extension to open-ended tasks where majority voting is ill-defined (they name summarization) is left to future work. Also visible in the results: (e) ANLL-correctness correlation is only r approximately -0.29/-0.3 (Fig. 4) — a weak ranking signal; (f) gains are small and several cells fall within observed std (they say so for Qwen2.5-7B on HH-RLHF); black-box gains are +1.0 to +1.2% over Uniform (Table 2); (g) threshold exits (early-exit on too-easy or too-hard) do NOT help on average: best hard-threshold -1.0% accuracy, best easy-threshold saves approximately 20% budget at -1.2% (Table 3), and only the better-calibrated Qwen2.5-7B gets a favorable trade (11.4% saved at -0.3%); (h) the ablation over signals (Table 4) shows VCS LOSES to ANLL in every cell — i.e. the black-box path is the weaker path in their own data.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level step on top: (i) The signal is single-shot and content-blind — ANLL of one generation. Nothing conditions the difficulty estimate on the OBSERVATION regime (for us: acoustic condition), and Prop. 1's graceful-degradation bound says only that ranking errors cost linearly, not that a condition-blind ranking is admissible. (ii) The allocation is one-shot two-phase; there is no sequential re-estimation after Phase-2 samples arrive, so evidence acquired mid-budget is discarded for allocation purposes. (iii) Threshold exits fail here precisely because the signal is not calibrated — the authors say the trade is favorable 'only when the difficulty signal is well calibrated'. That is an explicit invitation: a calibrated admissibility gate on p_i is the missing piece, and it is the same object our P9 needs. (iv) Aggregation is majority vote, which they concede does not extend to open-ended outputs — the open-ended aggregation rule is unoccupied by this paper.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：(i) The default signal ANLL requires PER-TOKEN OUTPUT LOG-PROBABILITIES — explicitly outside our black-box contract (no guaranteed logprobs). The authors supply the substitute (VCS) but their own Table 4 shows VCS loses to ANLL in every tested cell, so adopting the framework on a black-box core means adopting its WEAKER arm; the black-box results (Table 2, Cohere, +1.2% over Uniform) are the honest reference, not the ANLL headline. (ii) Majority-vote aggregation is the terminal step and is ill-defined for free-form speech outputs (transcripts, spoken answers) — the authors name this as unresolved. Any speech instantiation must first define an equivalence relation over outputs before the coverage objective 1-(1-p)^N even means anything, and near-equivalent transcripts differing by one token would fragment the vote. (iii) The coverage surrogate assumes i.i.d. samples with a per-sample success probability p_i; for speech, the dominant failure driver is often the ACOUSTIC CONDITION of the item, which is shared across all N samples of that item — this is exactly the 'extreme intra-question correlation' the authors flag as able to invert the difficulty ordering, and in speech it is the typical case rather than the pathological one. Re-sampling a hard-acoustics item N times may buy nothing. (iv) 'Difficulty' here is content difficulty (a hard math problem); in speech, difficulty is at least two-dimensional (content difficulty x acoustic degradation) and a scalar p_i collapses them. (v) The zero-cost claim depends on Phase-1 logprobs falling out of a generation already being made; a verbalized-confidence substitute is not free of prompt contamination and may itself shift the answer distribution.

**可借鉴**：1) The concave-knapsack formulation with exact marginal-greedy solution (Eq. 1, Algorithm 1) — directly reusable as the budget-allocate decision rule for our cross-cutting budget slot, and the exactness result means we never need to justify a heuristic. 2) Prop. 1's sensitivity bound (linear in B and L-infinity estimation error) as the ARGUMENT that an uncalibrated ranking signal is still admissible for allocation — this is a formal result we can cite when our own headroom proxy is known to be poorly calibrated. 3) The two-phase structure with the Phase-1 generation REUSED in the final vote (so the signal costs zero extra calls) — a general design pattern for buying information without paying for it. The 'UAB w/o Phase-1 vote' ablation (Table 4) shows dropping this reuse drops performance to near or below Uniform, i.e. the reuse is load-bearing. 4) The full signal-ablation menu (Table 4): Total NLL / Token Variance / Max-Token NLL / VCS / Vote Entropy(K=2) / ANLL, grouped by SOURCE and INFERENCE COST — this is the right way to ablate a difficulty signal, and its finding that length normalization is essential (Total NLL falls BELOW Uniform by conflating uncertainty with length) is a concrete trap to avoid. 5) The baseline set: Random / Length / Uniform / LLM-Judge — LLM-Judge underperforming Uniform in several settings is the useful negative (binary easy/hard classification is too coarse for fine-grained allocation). 6) The 'minimum budget to reach a target accuracy' inversion (Fig. 3, PCHIP interpolation) as a cost-at-fixed-quality reporting device, plus the honest note that curves TERMINATE where accuracy peaks and then decreases. 7) The allocation-vs-annotated-difficulty diagnostic (Fig. 5, MATH-500 L1-L5): does the allocator's spend rise monotonically with true difficulty? A direct sanity check for any budget policy. 8) The temperature sweep T in {0.1,0.2,0.5,1,2} bracketing over-concentration and uniformity.

**备注**：Read: abstract, §1-§2, §3.1-3.3, §4.1-4.3, §5, Limitations, Tables 1-4, Figs. 2-6. Training-free by construction — mechanism is contract-compatible EXCEPT for the logprob dependence, which the paper itself routes around. The most directly transplantable formal object in this read set. Scout digest anticipated the logprob and acoustic-difficulty boundaries; fulltext adds Prop. 1 (the miscalibration tolerance bound), the explicit black-box VCS path with its measured penalty, and the negative threshold-exit result.

### 2606.01034 — A Finite-Calibration Regime Map for LLM Judge Panels

**域**：text (LLM-as-judge panels; RewardBench, LLMBar, SummEval, Arena100K)　**供体级别**：CONSTRUCT_DONOR　**喂给**：P9 (decision-calibration/admissibility gates — supplies the finite-label accounting that tells you WHETHER a signal is calibratable, though not the gate itself) primary; P1 (supply pricing — label budget as the scarce currency, and 'panels should expand only when they buy estimable structure') secondary; P8 (reward-as-controller — aggregation family choice for a multi-signal reward) secondary

**方法面**：Asks WHEN a judge panel's outputs should be calibrated to human labels by a low-dimensional stacker versus a joint output table, under a FINITE human-label budget. §1 Eq. 1 gives the bookkeeping decomposition R(f_hat) - R* approximately Approx(pi,K,A) + Est(pi,K,A,n_M) + Sel(C,n_V). The deployable decision object is the triple (judge path pi, prefix size K, aggregator family A), selected by validation risk (Eq. 2). Theorem 1: with bounded loss, R(f_hat_c_hat) <= min_c R(f_hat_c) + 2*sqrt(log(2|C|/delta)/(2 n_V)) — the selection penalty grows only LOGARITHMICALLY in the number of candidates, so a wide menu is cheap provided a separate validation block exists. §3.2 defines three path rules: information-first (order judges by single-judge oracle performance on a selection block — the strong baseline), random (null), and a complexity-penalized heuristic that trades oracle-risk reduction against entropy-effective-support growth normalized by the planned calibration budget (Eq. 4). §3.3 Prop. 1 bounds joint-table excess risk by a sum of per-cell estimation terms plus SMOOTHING BIAS plus UNSEEN MASS u_K = P(Z_K not in observed support) (Eq. 5-6). §3.4 lays out the complexity ladder explicitly (mean/vote+calibration -> one-coin reliability -> ridge/logistic stacking -> pairwise stacker -> joint table) with approximation behavior vs estimation pressure for each; Prop. 3 gives the parametric stacking scale O(sqrt(K/n_M)). §3.5 FCPS protocol: partition into selection / calibration / validation / test blocks; build paths on selection only; fit aggregators on calibration; select (pi,K,A) on validation; test block used ONLY for final evaluation and diagnostics. §3.6 defines the reportable diagnostics: entropy-effective support H_K, exact cell pressure sum_z p_z/N_z, unseen pattern rate u_hat, and the single proxy V_tilde = H_K/n_M + u_hat. Seven-judge pool; 30 grouped splits; MSE is the validation objective. No gradients; judges are frozen and their outputs are inputs.

**自述与显见局限**：(a) The headline empirical result is a NEGATIVE for panel flexibility: scalar/reliability aggregation wins 16 of 20 real dataset-budget cells, with paired 95% intervals excluding zero in only 11 of those — so 5 cells go the other way and 5 of the 16 wins are not interval-separated. (b) The regime where the joint table wins is demonstrated on CONTROLLED synthetic data (a six-way parity construction where test MSE drops 0.224 -> 0.061 as unseen mass vanishes), not on any real benchmark — the authors present this as the complementary regime, which is honest, but it means the table's value is not evidenced in the wild in this read. (c) The diagnostics H_K, cell pressure, unseen rate 'are not themselves guaranteed excess-risk terms' (§3.6, their words) — they audit, they do not certify. (d) The entropy proxy V_tilde 'can understate literal small-cell pressure' (§5) versus the direct cell-pressure audit. (e) Everything is conditioned on human labels being the calibration currency and on judges being exchangeable raters over the same items. (f) Small calibration budgets (n_M as low as 16-50) with 30 splits — the estimates being compared are themselves noisy. (g) No explicit limitations section; the caveats are distributed through §3.6 and §5.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level step on top: (i) The whole map is priced in HUMAN LABELS as the scarce currency. It never asks whether a cheaper, label-free signal could substitute for part of the calibration block — i.e. no value-of-information treatment of the label budget itself, only an allocation of a given budget. (ii) The framework selects an aggregator but produces no ABSTENTION: it explicitly contrasts itself with selective conformal judging (SCOPE), saying 'our protocol instead studies which judge prefix and aggregator to deploy when the system must output calibrated predictions for all examples' (§2). Coverage control is therefore out of scope and is exactly the adjacent slot our P9 needs. (iii) The path rules order judges by oracle performance or by an entropy heuristic; none of them conditions on the ITEM (which judge is trustworthy for which kind of input), so per-condition panel selection is unoccupied here. (iv) The hybrid shrinkage extension f_hyb = f_scal + (N_z/(N_z+tau)) * r_hat(z) (Eq. 13) is described as a prototype in an appendix rather than evaluated as a first-class family — a count-weighted interpolation between scalar and table is left underdeveloped.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：(i) The calibration currency is HUMAN LABELS on exchangeable text items, cheap enough to buy 20-800 of them per split and to re-buy across 30 splits. Speech human labels (transcription, quality rating, spoken-answer correctness) cost substantially more per item and, critically, are ACOUSTIC-CONDITION-SPECIFIC — a calibration block collected on clean read speech does not calibrate a judge deployed on noisy far-field audio, so the exchangeability that makes the whole finite-sample story work is the thing most at risk. (ii) The joint-table object is a table over the CATEGORICAL joint output pattern Z_K of K judges; this presumes each judge emits a low-cardinality categorical verdict (A/B/tie, rubric bin). Speech reward signals are typically continuous and heterogeneous (WER, a judge score, an acoustic-confidence proxy), so the pattern space is not naturally discrete and the cell-pressure analysis needs a binning decision that itself becomes a tuned hyperparameter. (iii) The unseen-mass diagnostic u_K assumes the deployment distribution is the same as the test block's; in speech the unseen patterns will be dominated by unseen ACOUSTIC CONDITIONS, and u_hat measured on a matched test block will systematically understate deployment unseen mass. (iv) MSE against a human label as the risk requires a scalar gold target per item; for free-form speech outputs with no exact-match equivalence, the gold target is itself a judged quantity with its own error, which the decomposition in Eq. 1 does not model. (v) The paper's own scope note applies: it deliberately does NOT provide abstention or coverage control, so it cannot be borrowed as an admissibility gate — only as the calibration accounting underneath one.

**可借鉴**：1) The four-block split discipline — selection / calibration / validation / test, with paths built on selection only and test touched only at the end (§3.5). This is a stricter and more explicit protocol than the usual train/test split and is directly adoptable for any aggregator or gate we fit. 2) Theorem 1 as the licence to search a WIDE candidate menu: the selection penalty is only sqrt(log(2|C|/delta)/(2 n_V)), so enumerating many gate/aggregator variants costs logarithmically — this is the formal answer to 'aren't you overfitting by trying many designs?'. 3) The aggregator complexity ladder (§3.4 table) as the design space enumeration for any multi-signal reward aggregation: mean/vote+calibration, one-coin reliability, ridge/logistic stacking, pairwise, joint table — with the approximation/estimation trade stated per rung. 4) The three reportable calibration diagnostics — entropy-effective support H_K, exact cell pressure sum_z p_hat_z/N_z, and TEST-TIME UNSEEN PATTERN RATE u_hat — plus the discipline of reporting them alongside the selected model so the reader can see whether the chosen complexity was earned. The unseen rate in particular is the statistic that tells you your calibration set never saw the regime you are deploying into. 5) The path-rule baseline set (information-first / random / complexity-penalized) with a RANDOM null and a non-deployable TEST-ORACLE ENVELOPE as an upper bound (Table 3) — the envelope-and-null bracketing is a reusable reporting frame. 6) The headline framing itself as a construct: 'the practical question is not how many judges? but whether the next judge's information is ESTIMABLE under the available human labels' — restated for us, whether an additional reward signal is admissible depends on whether we can calibrate it, not on whether it is informative. 7) The count-weighted hybrid (Eq. 13) as a fallback design when a fine-grained gate has sparse support.

**备注**：Read: abstract, §1, §2, §3.1-3.6, §4, §5, §6, Tables 1-3. Frozen judges, no gradients. CONSTRUCT_DONOR rather than PROTOCOL_DONOR because the deployable artifact (FCPS) is tightly bound to a categorical-pattern joint table over exchangeable human-labeled text items; what survives transfer is the FRAMING (complexity must be earned by calibration evidence), Theorem 1's menu-search licence, the four-block split, and the three diagnostics. Scout digest read the human-label-currency boundary correctly at abstract level; fulltext adds the explicit no-abstention scope note, the 11-of-16 interval-separation caveat, and the fact that the pro-table regime is synthetic-only in this paper.

### 2606.03054 — ToolGate: Token-Efficient Pre-Call Control for Tool-Augmented Vision-Language Agents

**域**：vision-GUI / VLM (ReAct-style tool-augmented VLM agents; V*, CV-Bench, HR-Bench-4k/8k, MME-RealWorld-Lite)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P1 (supply pricing / value-of-information — this is the closest occupant of the observation-acquisition gate slot) primary; P9 (admissibility gates — 'may this evidence enter the context') primary; P10 (reward hacking / evidence corruption — the over-trust and adversarial-stimulus results) secondary; P5 (tool credit — per-tool prior as most of the signal) secondary

**方法面**：Defines the PRE-CALL CONTROL problem: after a ReAct VLM agent has already proposed a perceptual tool call (OCR / detection / small-object detection / segmentation / cropping / depth), should the call be executed or skipped BEFORE its output enters the context? §4.1: a gate g(pi_t, a_t) in {0,1} over trajectory prefix pi_t and pending call a_t = (tool_t, args_t); on skip the environment returns a fixed no-op message and the agent continues. The decision-theoretic target is stated as VoI(pi_t,a_t) = Pr(y_hat = y* | execute) - Pr(y_hat = y* | skip) (Eq. 1), and the paper is explicit that this is NOT observable from a logged trajectory because only one continuation is taken. §3 is a DIAGNOSTIC built on forced-answer probes: query the agent for its best answer immediately before and after each executed call, classify each call into helpful (wrong->right), harmful (right->wrong), unchanged-correct, unchanged-wrong. Over 15,782 labeled invocations on Qwen3-VL-30B: 11.8% helpful, 9.9% harmful, 78.3% unchanged — helpful and harmful occur at SIMILAR RATES. §3.2: on a 802-call verifiable subset, when the tool output is wrong and the pre-call prediction was already correct, the post-call prediction becomes wrong in 13.9% (43/309) of cases — over-trust. §3.3: a controlled Visual Genome experiment with a programmatic ADVERSARIAL tool stimulus (falsely reporting a certified-present object as absent) collapses the naive agent's visual belief P_vis from 0.993 to 0.142; a skeptical prompt only partially recovers it to 0.461 (Table 1) — i.e. post-hoc discounting fails, which is the argument for gating BEFORE the evidence enters. §4.2-4.3: the gate is deliberately black-box — frozen sentence-embedding of the trajectory text plus a handful of structural features (normalized step index, is-first-call, same-tool-already-used, one-hot tool type), fed to an L2-regularized LOGISTIC REGRESSION, threshold tau = 0.5. It sees no pixels, no hidden states, no system prompt, no decoding logprobs, no internal uncertainty, and not the future tool output. §4.4: proxy supervision — a call is execute-positive only if it flips the probed answer wrong->right (c_b=0, c_a=1) AND the episode ultimately succeeds (e=1); all else negative. Gradient use: only in fitting the small logistic gate; the VLM and tools are untouched.

**自述与显见局限**：Stated (Limitations, p.9): one ReAct planner, one fixed tool suite, two backbones from ONE model family — no claim of transfer to other planners, tool APIs, modalities, or domains; the proxy is not true counterfactual VoI and may miss delayed or distributed value; the tool-correctness annotation is automatic and NOISY (two VLM annotation runs disagree on 35% of rows on at least one field, so the 13.9% over-trust rate is 'a rough mechanistic estimate, not a precise population statistic'); a gate that skips too aggressively suppresses needed evidence, one that executes too aggressively still admits misleading output; not a substitute for human oversight. Also visible: (a) the accuracy GAIN (+1.65) comes only from the MATCHED-DOMAIN gate trained on the same benchmark families used for evaluation, which the authors repeatedly refuse to call zero-shot; strict cross-domain gives +0.32 (30B) and +0.08 (235B) — i.e. essentially preserved accuracy, not improvement. (b) An additional 235B in-domain gate HURTS accuracy (their Appendix C), which they attribute to the execute-positive class being sparse and hard to infer from text-only features. (c) Feature ablation (Table 4) shows TOOL IDENTITY ALONE gives +1.31 of the +1.65 — most of the signal is a static per-tool prior, not trajectory understanding. (d) Negative findings (§7.3): image-derived features, VL-encoder features, and model log-probability features did NOT improve the deployed trade-off.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level step on top: (i) The gate is FITTED (logistic regression on logged trajectories with offline forced-answer probes), so it carries a one-time supervised data cost per agent/tool setup — a fully training-free pre-call gate is not attempted here. (ii) The proxy label is deliberately conservative and admittedly misses delayed value; nothing estimates VoI causally, and the authors say so. A paired execute/skip design (branching the trajectory) would give real counterfactual labels and is not attempted. (iii) The threshold tau = 0.5 is a tuned constant with only a sensitivity sweep — no calibrated admissibility statement ('skip only when the expected loss is below X'). (iv) Since tool identity carries most of the signal, the trajectory-conditioned part is weak; a genuinely condition-conditioned gate (which observation regime makes this tool worth buying) is open. (v) The over-trust finding (§3.2-3.3) identifies a failure the gate only PREVENTS by exclusion; nothing repairs or discounts evidence already admitted.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：(i) The forced-answer probe presupposes a CLOSED ANSWER SET (multiple-choice options) so that an argmax over options exists and 'flip' is well defined. Free-form speech outputs have no argmax over a small option set; the probe would have to be replaced by a scored comparison whose noise may swamp the flip signal, and 'unchanged' would need an equivalence relation over transcripts that does not exist by construction. (ii) The tools return STRUCTURED, RE-VERIFIABLE perceptual artifacts (boxes, OCR strings) over an image the agent can re-inspect at will; a speech observation-supply action (re-decode a segment, request a different ASR pass, request diarization) returns evidence that cannot be cheaply cross-checked against the original waveform by the same frozen core. (iii) The cost model assumes an acquisition is a DISCRETE, RE-ISSUABLE, PRICED act with a fixed skip fallback message; audio re-acquisition may be non-repeatable (the utterance is what it is) so the skip branch and the execute branch are not symmetric in the same way. (iv) The gate is fitted on logged trajectories with offline probes — that supervision pipeline requires running the probe machinery over thousands of calls, which for speech means thousands of extra decodes per gate, changing the cost calculus that motivated the gate. (v) The over-trust result is about a TEXT tool output overriding a VISUAL belief; the speech analogue (a transcript overriding an acoustic impression) may behave differently and cannot be assumed from this result — the paper's own limitations forbid modality transfer of its findings. (vi) 235B in-domain gate HURTING accuracy warns that the gate's benefit is regime-dependent even within this one domain.

**可借鉴**：1) The FORCED-ANSWER PROBE as a label-free-ish instrument: query the frozen core for its current best answer immediately before and after an acquisition, and classify the transition into helpful / harmful / unchanged-correct / unchanged-wrong. This is the single most transplantable device in this paper for P1 — it converts 'did this observation help?' into an observable four-way transition without needing a counterfactual rollout. The authors are careful that probes are used ONLY for offline label construction and diagnostics, never in the deployed loop. 2) The resulting base-rate statistic (11.8% helpful vs 9.9% harmful vs 78.3% unchanged) as the SHAPE of the finding our supply-pricing work needs: acquisitions are not free-and-mostly-good, they are mostly-inert with symmetric upside and downside. 3) The controlled adversarial-stimulus experiment (§3.3): insert a programmatic tool output that contradicts a CERTIFIED-present fact, measure the belief collapse P_vis, and compare a naive prompt against a skeptical prompt. This is a clean, cheap protocol for testing whether a frozen core over-trusts injected evidence, and its result (skeptical prompting recovers 0.142 -> 0.461 but not to 0.993) is a directly reusable argument that PROMPT-LEVEL SKEPTICISM IS NOT A DEFENSE. 4) The prompt-based control arms (Table 3): 'VoI self-report' (ask the agent whether the tool is worthwhile) INCREASES calls to 5.88/episode and drops accuracy to 60.0; 'sparse prompt' leaves cost essentially unchanged. Both are the right nulls for any external-controller claim — they show the gain is not just 'tell the model to use fewer tools'. 5) The feature ablation design (full / text-only / struct-only / tool-only) which isolates how much of a controller's value is a static prior. 6) The cross-domain vs matched-domain training-regime split, reported as separate rows and interpreted separately — a discipline our own gate evaluation should copy. 7) The gate-vs-confidence decorrelation check: Pearson r = -0.19, Spearman rho = -0.22 between the gate score and the agent's pre-call probability on the gold answer, i.e. the gate is NOT just thresholding confidence. That decorrelation test is reusable to show any new admissibility signal is not a confidence proxy in disguise.

**备注**：Read: abstract, §1-§2, §3.1-3.4, §4.1-4.4, §5, §6.1-6.2, §7.1-7.3, §8, §9, Limitations, Tables 1-4, Fig. 1. Gate is FITTED (logistic regression) — flag gradient use, but it is a tiny external classifier over frozen-model text, not a modification of the core, so the DESIGN is closer to contract-compatible than most trained donors here. Scout digest correctly anticipated the 'structured verifiable perceptual artifact' boundary; fulltext adds the forced-answer-probe protocol, the 11.8/9.9/78.3 base rates, the adversarial belief-collapse experiment, the prompt-based null arms, and the gate-vs-confidence decorrelation — all of which the abstract did not expose.

### 2606.07834 — Cherry-pick Override: Unsafe Directional Commitment in LLM Judges under Mixed Evidence

**域**：text (LLM-as-judge / evidence-grounded fact verification; AVeriTeC Conflicting-Cherrypicking subset N_C=150 within N=285, plus VitaminC-Mixed cross-dataset control N=250)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P9 (decision-calibration / admissibility gates + abstain decision right) primary and closest occupant in this read set; P10 (reward hacking under selection pressure — majority-vote aggregation AMPLIFYING the unsafe commitment is a selection-pressure result) secondary; P8 (reward-as-controller — the separation of proposal from authorization is the controller architecture) secondary

**方法面**：Names and operationalizes a failure of COMMITMENT TYPE, not of label accuracy. §4.1 fixes a task contract: the schema exposes CONFLICTING as a first-class verdict, so returning SUPPORTS/REFUTES on a gold-CONFLICTING claim is an UNAUTHORIZED DIRECTIONAL COMMITMENT. §4.2: CCO = {pred in {S,R} AND gold = C}. Table 1 bounds the diagnosis with explicit in-scope/out-of-scope conditions (out: schema lacks CONFLICTING; gold is S or R; evidence merely insufficient; deployment waives CONFLICTING). Table 2 separates CCO from four neighbours (direction error, hallucination, calibration error, generic abstention failure). A key construct: CONFLICTING is NOT INSUFFICIENT — INSUFFICIENT is epistemic abstention (no evidence), CONFLICTING is PRAGMATIC NON-COMMITMENT (enough evidence to know no direction can be safely authorized); the panel distinguishes them operationally (INSUFFICIENT on 80% of gold-I, CONFLICTING on 77% of gold-C). §4.4 the same-denominator diagnostic protocol: five metrics all reported on a single denominator N so comparisons cannot be gamed by shifting the evaluated subset — Cov (commit coverage), SE (selective error, subset denominator), CCO_N (same-denominator CCO rate, the cross-controller comparison metric), CCO_C (conditional on the gold-C subset), Acc_{S/R} (directional accuracy on pure-evidence subset), Rec_C (conflict recall). Controllers compared ONLY at matched coverage; paired bootstrap 5000 resamples; plus an APPLES-TO-APPLES RANDOM-VETO NULL — if the controller promotes k of the baseline's directional commits to CONFLICTING, the null draws 2000 random size-k subsets and promotes those, so the null can in principle reach any of the controller's metric values (they note an earlier null that demoted to NO-COMMIT could not change Rec_C mechanically and was therefore unfair). §4.5 the SEPARATION PRINCIPLE: verdict generation and commitment authorization should be distinct components; NO-COMMIT is a controller ROUTING STATE, not a verification verdict. §5 walks an INTERVENTION LADDER L0-L5, each rung a common fix, each leaving a named residual (Table 3). Panel = Claude Haiku 4.5 + Sonnet 4.5 + GPT-4o-mini, greedy deterministic; validator = Haiku 4.5 with a few-shot certificate prompt. No gradients anywhere.

**自述与显见局限**：The paper is unusually disciplined about its own limits and states most of them itself: it is 'diagnostic rather than prescriptive'; the two-channel probe is 'a design probe, not a solution claim'; 'the strongest claim the paper supports is that the structural and confidence channels are orthogonal and that each common single-channel fix leaves a distinct residual failure'. Beyond that: (a) the L5-vs-L3 MAGNITUDE differences straddle zero (Delta SE = -0.007, CI [-0.084,+0.066]; Delta CCO_N = -0.010, CI [-0.035,+0.011]) — the robust finding is structural SELECTIVITY (random-veto p < 1/2001 on Acc_{S/R} and Rec_C), not aggregate improvement, and they say so. (b) The panel-amplification result does NOT replicate cross-dataset (AVeriTeC Delta CCO_C = +0.047, CI [+0.013,+0.080]; VitaminC-Mixed 0.000, CI [-0.060,+0.060]) and they scope the claim to AVeriTeC. (c) Acc_{S/R} and Rec_C are ALGEBRAICALLY COUPLED under fixed k — they flag that the joint extremity is one selection event on two axes, not two independent tests. (d) The validator's subclaim decomposition is imperfect (they cite failure cases) so 'material_mixed' is 'a structural veto input rather than a settled judgment'. (e) Small N: 150 gold-C claims, 27-28 CCO commits in the anatomy analysis, one dropped for a parse failure. (f) L0 is pre-contract and cannot strictly measure CCO; they parenthesize those cells to mark it. (g) No multiple-comparison correction on the paired bootstraps (stated).

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level step on top: (i) The structural channel is a PROMPT-BASED subclaim validator whose materiality judgment is imperfect and unaudited — a stronger structural-evidence extractor is the obvious next rung, and the paper explicitly leaves the controller unoptimized. (ii) The two channels are combined by a fixed two-stage rule (structural veto then a scalar confidence threshold, Algorithm 1) with no learned or calibrated combination; a calibrated joint gate is open. (iii) NO-COMMIT is defined as a routing state but the DOWNSTREAM (escalate to human / re-retrieve / defer) is deliberately left to the deploying application — the budget and repair consequences of declining to commit are unmodelled. (iv) Everything is single-turn verdict authorization; there is no multi-step version where a declined commitment triggers further evidence acquisition, which is exactly where our program lives.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：(i) The construct is CONTRACT-BASED: CCO is only measurable where the output schema exposes a non-directional verdict and where a GOLD label marks the item as materially mixed (Table 1). A frozen speech/omni core producing free-form output has no such typed vocabulary by default, and there is no gold 'this item is materially ambiguous' annotation — so the analogue must be constructed (e.g. items where the acoustic evidence genuinely underdetermines the transcript), and constructing it is itself research, not a port. (ii) The structural channel operates on TEXTUAL, EXPLICITLY AUTHORED evidence decomposed into subclaims with per-subclaim evidence states. Speech has no comparable decomposition: the conflicting evidence is not two retrievable documents but a single waveform whose acoustic realization is ambiguous, and there is no validator that can be run 'independently of the panel' over an audio segment the way a text validator reads retrieved sources. (iii) The confidence channel is a self-reported per-judge scalar averaged over three judges; with no guaranteed logprobs and a black-box core, verbalized confidence is the only available analogue and this paper's own finding is that confidence is the WEAKER channel anyway. (iv) 'Materiality' of a subclaim is decided by a prompted validator over text; the speech analogue (which part of the utterance is load-bearing for the answer) requires temporal localization that the core may not expose. (v) All effects are measured on N_C = 150 with 27-28 CCO events; a speech replication would need an equivalently curated ambiguous-item set, and acoustic ambiguity does not come pre-labelled in any corpus this read set touches. (vi) The paper is explicitly diagnostic — nothing here is a controller we can adopt and expect to work; the borrowables are protocol and construct, and the authors would object to any effectiveness reading.

**可借鉴**：1) The SEPARATION PRINCIPLE (§4.5) as a first-class architectural construct for our control plane: verdict generation is one component, commitment authorization is another, and NO-COMMIT is a controller-internal routing state rather than an output label. This is the cleanest available formalization of our abstain decision right. 2) The CONFLICTING-vs-INSUFFICIENT distinction — epistemic abstention (no evidence) vs pragmatic non-commitment (evidence shows no direction is safe). Two structurally different reasons to abstain that a single confidence axis provably cannot separate; the selective-prediction lineage collapses them and this paper shows why that fails. 3) The SAME-DENOMINATOR protocol (§4.4): report all gate metrics on one denominator so a gate cannot look good by shrinking its evaluated subset, and compare controllers ONLY at matched coverage. This is a direct anti-gaming discipline for every gate we build. 4) The APPLES-TO-APPLES RANDOM-VETO NULL: if your gate vetoes k items, compare against 2000 random size-k vetoes on the same metrics. Plus the meta-lesson that a badly chosen null (demote-to-NO-COMMIT) can be MECHANICALLY unable to move one of the metrics and is therefore not a fair control. This is the single most reusable statistical device in this read set for P9. 5) The INTERVENTION LADDER as a reporting form (Table 3): for each common fix, name the RESIDUAL failure it leaves rather than scoring it on a leaderboard. Its specific rungs are all directly relevant: typed vocabulary (largest single reduction, CCO_N 0.442 -> 0.098, but a structurally avoidable residual remains); panel majority voting AMPLIFIES the failure on AVeriTeC and 48% (13/27) of CCO commits are 2-vs-1 with the dissenter voting non-directionally, i.e. MAJORITY VOTE SUPPRESSES THE SAFE MINORITY VERDICT; confidence thresholding cannot separate CCO from correct commits at any tested threshold; validator-as-primary-classifier collapses pure-evidence accuracy 0.78 -> 0.39. 6) The orthogonality demonstration: the panel is WELL CALIBRATED for direction (ECE = 0.07 on the pure-S/R subset) yet CCO commits sit at mean p_bar = 0.902 vs 0.933 for correct commits — statistically distinguishable (Mann-Whitney p approximately 0.0002) but a mean separation of only 0.031, with 86% of CCO commits at p_bar >= 0.85. Confidence measures DECISION STRENGTH, not EVIDENCE STRUCTURE. That is a general result about admissibility signals, not a fact-checking fact. 7) The alternative aggregation rule 'commit only when NO judge votes CONFLICTING' (conflict-if-any), which raises Rec_C to 0.893 and lowers CCO_N to 0.053 at a cost in Acc_{S/R} — a concrete safety-coverage trade-off rule. 8) Lemma 1 (monotonic veto): adding a veto stage can never widen the commit set at fixed threshold, so any metric improvement must come from SELECTIVITY not coverage — a clean way to attribute a gate's effect.

**备注**：Read: abstract, §1, §2, §3, §4.1-4.6, §5.1-5.5, Tables 1-4, Fig. 1, Algorithm 1, Lemma 1. Training-free (prompted panel + prompted validator + threshold), no gradients — mechanism is contract-compatible. This paper's statistical discipline (same-denominator, matched coverage, fair null, algebraic-coupling disclosure, scoped non-replication) is a model for our own gate evaluations independent of its subject matter. Scout digest called the mixed-evidence textual construct boundary; fulltext adds the separation principle, the same-denominator + random-veto protocol, the ECE-0.07-yet-CCO result establishing channel orthogonality, and the 48% majority-suppression anatomy.

### 2606.10742 — MemVenom: Triggered Poisoning of Multimodal Memories in Web Agents

**域**：vision-GUI / multimodal security (web agents: SeeAct, LiteWebAgent, ReAct-WebAgent; Qwen3-VL-4B/8B/PLUS, GPT-5.4)　**供体级别**：CONSTRUCT_DONOR　**喂给**：P3 (memory write-gates + poisoning defenses — defines the adversary the gate must survive and the choke point to defend) primary; P10 (reward hacking / adversarial pressure on a selection signal — the retrieval score is the signal being gamed) secondary

**方法面**：A BLACK-BOX attack on graph-structured multimodal external memory, and therefore a specification of what a write-gate must survive. §3.1 threat model: attacker has MEMORY-LEVEL ACCESS ONLY — cannot modify the VLM backbone, policy, retrieval encoder, action parser, inference prompts, or internal scores; can inject a small number of multimodal entries (text, images, graph relations) before deployment or offline indexing, e.g. via untrusted web observations, user interactions, SHARED MEMORY COLLECTIONS, or third-party data sources. Attacker goal: induce a side goal g_m under TRIGGER-BEARING observations while preserving benign utility otherwise. §3.2 two-stage design with a THREE-COMPONENT malicious subgraph: (1) a recall-oriented component, (2) a goal-agnostic prioritization component, (3) a REPLACEABLE goal-bearing component — so switching side goals requires swapping only (3), with no re-optimization of (1) or (2). Stage 1 (§3.2.1) optimizes a visual trigger tau over rendered screenshots to make triggered observations form a COMPACT CLUSTER in the retrieval embedding space while staying SEPARATED from both raw observations and the existing benign memory embeddings (Eq. 1-2: compactness L_cmp, separation from C_raw, plus L_space pushing away from existing memory items B). The poisoned node embeds the triggered screenshot closest to the optimized cluster center (Eq. 3-4). Stage 2 (§3.2.2) builds the prioritization component by a composite visual attack: an L-infinity-bounded perturbation on a base image optimized on SURROGATE encoders (victim is black-box) using a BRIDGE IMAGE as an intermediate anchor between the base image and a set of paraphrased memory-priority instructions (Eq. 5-8), combined with stealthy OCR injection. Metrics: ASR-r (malicious recall), ASR-a (post-recall action induction), ASR-ra (end-to-end), plus BU/PU (benign utility before/after poisoning). Gradient use: yes, but on SURROGATE encoders only — the victim agent and VLM are never differentiated through, which is precisely why the attack is black-box-realistic.

**自述与显见局限**：Stated (Limitations): controlled sandbox environments and selected frameworks only; the attack targets graph-structured multimodal memory with screenshot-based retrieval and other memory designs (tool-specific, user-profile, dynamically updated episodic) 'may exhibit different security properties'; defense evaluation covers only lightweight plug-and-play safeguards. Also visible: (a) ASR-a is highly variable across framework x backbone x goal (e.g. GPT-5.4 on SeeAct under Financial Operation: ASR-a 29.41 vs 100.0 for Qwen3-VL-4B; ReAct+GPT-5.4 under Privacy Leakage: ASR-r drops to 53.57) — the attack is far from uniformly potent and the paper reports the low cells. (b) Benign utility is LOW to begin with (Table 2 BU 11.76-47.06%), and PU sometimes EXCEEDS BU (23.53 -> 41.18), which makes the 'preserves benign utility' claim hard to read as anything but noise at this task-set size. (c) Defense results (Table 5) are partial by their own account: LlamaGuard cuts ASR-ra 80.15 -> 50.71 on ReAct but only 93.97 -> 75.89 on SeeAct; refusal rates are 4-29%. (d) Task set is SeeAct's Mind2Web-style set across 10 website categories — small.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level step on top, read as a DEFENSE agenda: the authors state it themselves — 'effective protection requires defenses that explicitly reason about MEMORY PROVENANCE, RETRIEVAL BEHAVIOR, and TASK ALIGNMENT' (§4.5), and list provenance / retrieval consistency / task alignment / action consequences as unexplored (Limitations). Nothing in this paper builds such a gate. Concretely open: (i) a write-time admission gate that scores provenance and refuses entries whose retrieval footprint is anomalously CONCENTRATED (the attack's Stage-1 objective is literally to make triggered observations cluster tightly — that is a detectable signature, and neither this paper nor any other donor in this read set tests for it【复核更正 wf_40068490】); (ii) a read-time consistency check comparing retrieved memory against the current observation and the standing user goal; (iii) retrieval-behavior monitoring (an entry recalled under an unexpectedly narrow observation class is suspicious by construction). The ablation (Table 4) also hands the defender a target: with the recall component removed, ASR-ra collapses to 0.00 — retrieval is the choke point.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：(i) Stage-1 depends on OPTIMIZING A VISUAL TRIGGER rendered into a fixed screenshot region and on the retrieval image encoder phi being differentiable on a surrogate. An audio 'trigger' would be an acoustic pattern inserted into a waveform; audio embedding spaces, surrogate availability, and the perceptual stealth budget all differ, and no result here licenses assuming audio embeddings are equally manipulable. (ii) The attack surface presumes memory keyed on SCREENSHOT embeddings, i.e. observations that are attacker-renderable. In a speech setting the observation is an utterance the attacker may not control; poisoning would have to come through a different channel (transcripts, shared memory, prior-turn content), which changes the threat model rather than porting it. (iii) Stage-2's stealthy OCR injection has no speech analogue at all — there is no equivalent of 'text rendered into an image that the model reads but a human skims past'. The nearest analogues (imperceptible speech, ultrasonic carriers) are a different literature with different feasibility. (iv) The trigger-conditioned CLUSTERING objective assumes the retrieval space is dense enough that a compact attacker-controlled region can be carved out while staying separated from benign memory; acoustic retrieval keys are heavily structured by speaker and channel, so the same optimization may collide with legitimate condition clusters — which cuts both ways (harder to hide, or harder to separate). (v) End-to-end ASR is measured against an EXECUTABLE side goal (navigate to a phishing URL, perform a financial operation) with a checkable outcome; a speech control plane's 'malicious action' is usually a wrong output, which has no crisp success oracle. (vi) The paper's own limitation applies directly: other memory designs 'may exhibit different security properties' — our condition-keyed memory is one of those other designs, so no effectiveness or vulnerability conclusion transfers.

**可借鉴**：1) The THREAT-MODEL TEMPLATE itself (§3.1) as the specification a write-gate must be evaluated against: attacker with memory-level access only, no model/retriever/prompt access, injecting a small number of entries from plausible sources including SHARED MEMORY. Our P3 write-gate should be stated as surviving exactly this adversary. 2) The three-way ASR decomposition — ASR-r (was it recalled) / ASR-a (did recall change the action) / ASR-ra (end-to-end) — which separates a RETRIEVAL failure from an INDUCTION failure. This is the right factorization for any memory defense evaluation, and it maps onto our own read-gate vs use-gate distinction. 3) The benign-utility pair BU/PU reported alongside ASR — a defense or attack must be scored on what it costs the honest path, not only on the adversarial path. 4) The component ablation (Table 4) as the causal design: retrieval-only / goal-only / prioritization-only / combinations, with the finding that removing the recall component drives ASR-ra to 0.00 and removing prioritization keeps recall but weakens induction. This isolates WHICH stage a defense must break. 5) The RETRIEVER TRANSFERABILITY matrix (Fig. 3, trigger optimized on source retriever, evaluated on target): strong diagonal plus non-trivial off-diagonal, arguing the attack exploits shared representation structure rather than one retriever's quirks. Reusable as a defense-generality test too. 6) The GOAL-AGNOSTIC / REPLACEABLE-COMPONENT decomposition as a threat construct: because the goal-bearing part is swappable without re-optimizing the trigger, a defense that only blacklists known malicious goals is defeated by construction. 7) The defense-evaluation frame (Table 5): report refusal rate RR alongside residual ASR-ra, so an over-refusing guardrail cannot be scored as a success. 8) The negative result on lightweight guardrails (hardcoded safety rules + LlamaGuard both insufficient) as the baseline any memory-provenance defense must beat.

**备注**：Read: abstract, §1, §3.1-3.2.2, §4.1, §4.4, §4.5, §4.6, §5, Limitations, Tables 1-2, 4-5. Gradients used on SURROGATE encoders only; victim is black-box. CONSTRUCT_DONOR rather than PROTOCOL_DONOR: the attack construction does not port to speech (visual trigger + OCR injection are modality-bound), but the THREAT MODEL, the ASR-r/ASR-a/ASR-ra decomposition, the BU/PU cost pairing, and the RR-alongside-residual-ASR defense frame are reusable as the specification and scoring frame for our write-gate. Scout digest read the image-embedding-manipulability boundary correctly; fulltext adds the three-component replaceable-goal architecture, the retrieval-is-the-choke-point ablation (ASR-ra 0.00 without recall component), the guardrail negative result, and the authors' own provenance/retrieval-consistency/task-alignment defense agenda.

### 2606.11078 — HiViG: A History-Aware Visually Grounded Critic for Computer Use Agents

**域**：vision-GUI (computer-use agents; WebArenaLitev2 web, AndroidLab mobile, WindowsAgentArena desktop)　**供体级别**：CONSTRUCT_DONOR　**喂给**：P8 (reward-as-controller, gradient-free multi-step — an external critic gating each step of a frozen policy with NO gradient into the actor) primary; P9 (admissibility gates — the pre-execution veto, though uncalibrated here) secondary; P3 (external memory — macro-action history as a compressed write) secondary

**方法面**：An external multimodal CRITIC placed in the decision loop of a FROZEN policy (Qwen3-VL-32B-Thinking; Gemini-3-Flash), intervening at two points per timestep (§2.3). Point 1, BEFORE the policy plans: the critic consumes the previous execution tuple tau_{t-1} = (I, o_{t-1}, a_{t-1}, o_t) and prior history H_{t-1} and emits an updated MACRO-ACTION HISTORY H_t — a compressed record of what has been achieved and what failed, so the policy stops re-exploring known states. Point 2, AFTER the policy proposes action a_t but BEFORE execution: the critic renders a VISUAL MARKER (a red X) at the action's proposed pixel coordinates onto the current screenshot and evaluates the marked observation, running visual verification (what UI element is actually under the marker) -> state-transition prediction -> instruction alignment. Grade 'Good' executes; grade 'Bad' emits an error dimension from a 12-class taxonomy plus a verbal explanation, forcing the policy to refine. The decision right is thus a PRE-EXECUTION VETO with verbal repair guidance, motivated by GUI actions being irreversible. GRADIENT USE: the critic is an 8B model (Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking) SFT-trained for one epoch on 52k samples (20k history-tracking + 32k visually-grounded error analysis) auto-annotated from ScaleCUA trajectories, conditioned on VERIFIED EXECUTION OUTCOMES so annotations reflect real state changes rather than the annotator's parametric guess (§2.2). Error data is built by (1) extracting a verbalized state-transition v_t from the true (o_t, a_t, o_{t+1}) triple, (2) PLAUSIBLE ERROR SYNTHESIS — perturbing the expert action into a_hat_t guided by a 12-dimension taxonomy (grounding errors, procedural prerequisite neglect, visual hallucination, observation neglect, ...), (3) multimodal rationale extraction with two anti-shortcut devices: masking the policy's verbal intent in 30% of samples, and rendering the visual marker so the critic must reason from spatial position rather than stated intent.

**自述与显见局限**：Stated (Limitations): the 12 predefined error dimensions are bounded by the current landscape of digital interfaces and will need iterative expansion as interfaces and policies evolve. Beyond that, visible in the results: (a) absolute success rates remain low (WebArenaLitev2 25.3% for Qwen3-VL-32B, 45.5% for Gemini-3-Flash) so the gains sit in a high-failure regime; (b) the full pipeline costs TWO critic inferences per step, and the compute-matching against scalar PRM baselines is done by setting N = 2 for Best-of-N, which is a coarse match; (c) baseline critics frequently DEGRADE the frontier policy (GUI-Critic-R1 drops Gemini-3-Flash on WebArenaLitev2 from 30.5 to 22.1; scalar PRMs give no improvement over base) — so the comparison set is weak and the headline gain is partly 'others hurt'; (d) the critic and the annotator share a model family (Qwen3-VL-32B-Thinking annotates, Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking is the critic) and one of the two evaluated policies is also Qwen3-VL-32B-Thinking, so annotator/critic/policy are not independent in the main open-weight setting; (e) no adversarial or reward-hacking stress on the critic — nothing tests whether a policy can learn to produce actions the critic approves but that fail.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level step on top: (i) The critic is TRAINED (52k SFT samples), so a training-free instantiation of the same two decision points is unoccupied here. (ii) The veto is a binary Good/Bad grade with no calibrated threshold and no coverage-risk curve — there is no statement of the form 'this critic vetoes at a controlled false-veto rate', so the admissibility calibration our P9 needs is absent.
