(iii) There is no budget model: the critic runs at every step regardless of whether the step is risky, and the ablation (Table 2) shows either component ALONE already beats all baselines — which is an invitation to allocate critic calls adaptively rather than uniformly. (iv) The macro-action history is written unconditionally every step; there is no write-gate on what enters H_t. (v) Nothing measures whether the critic's approvals are gameable under selection pressure.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：(i) The visually-grounded verification step is the paper's core mechanism and does not survive the modality change: it requires rendering a marker AT THE PROPOSED COORDINATES onto a re-inspectable screenshot and asking what is actually there. A speech action has no coordinates and the waveform has no surface onto which a proposal can be projected for re-verification; the nearest analogue (aligning a claimed transcript span to an audio region) requires forced alignment machinery that is itself an error source. (ii) The pre-execution veto is motivated by GUI actions being IRREVERSIBLE (sending an email, deleting a file); most speech-pipeline actions are cheap to redo, which weakens the motivation for pre-execution critique relative to post-hoc selection — the cost asymmetry that justifies the architecture may simply not hold. (iii) State-transition prediction presupposes a discrete, observable environment state that changes as a consequence of the action; a speech turn has no comparable 'state' to predict or diff, so the critic's central reasoning step has no target. (iv) The 12-error taxonomy is GUI-specific (grounding, dropdown selectors, window focus); a speech taxonomy would have to be built, not ported, and the authors already flag their taxonomy as interface-bound. (v) The critic is a 52k-sample SFT model over verified GUI trajectories; a speech program has no comparable corpus of verified per-step outcomes because there is no deterministic per-step oracle to verify against — the annotation pipeline's grounding guarantee ('conditioned on verified action execution outcomes') is exactly what speech lacks. (vi) The reported gains are cross-modality effectiveness claims within vision-GUI and carry no implication for speech.

**可借鉴**：1) The TWO-INTERVENTION-POINT architecture as a decision-rights layout over a frozen core: (a) update the compressed history BEFORE the policy plans, (b) veto/repair the proposal BEFORE it executes. Cleanly separates a memory-write right from a stop/repair right, and both sit outside the frozen policy. 2) The MACRO-ACTION HISTORY as an external memory representation: not raw replay, but a progress summary plus a last-action-result field, recursively compressed by merging H_{t-1} with the newest execution tuple. 3) The two ANTI-SHORTCUT training-data devices, which are really EVALUATION constructs we can reuse as tests even without training: mask the proposer's stated intent (does the critic still judge correctly from the observation alone?) and render a spatial marker (does the critic verify the actual target rather than the claimed one?). Table 3 quantifies both: without intent masking AndroidLab drops 51.5 -> 47.1; without the visual marker WebArenaLitev2 drops 25.3 -> 20.8. The generalizable construct is: A CRITIC THAT CAN READ THE PROPOSER'S STATED INTENT WILL TAKE THE SHORTCUT AND STOP CHECKING THE EVIDENCE. That is a direct warning for any judge we build over a frozen core's self-reported reasoning. 4) The scalar-vs-verbal feedback argument with its supporting evidence (§3.2, Table 1): scalar PRM baselines yield NO improvement over the base agent, because when all candidates are poor a scalar offers no path to improvement; verbal feedback explains why and enables repair. This is a substantive argument about what a reward signal must carry to drive a REPAIR decision right rather than only a SELECT one. 5) The 12-dimension error taxonomy as a template for typed failure attribution (grounding / procedural prerequisite neglect / observation neglect / visual hallucination / ...), and the PLAUSIBLE ERROR SYNTHESIS procedure — perturb a known-good action into a taxonomy-guided plausible error — which is reusable as a MUTATION-TESTING protocol for our own gates regardless of whether we train on the output. 6) The component ablation (Table 2, 2x2 over visually-grounded analysis x history tracking) showing either alone beats all baselines and both together are best — plus the authors' note that this gives computational flexibility (one inference instead of two). 7) The annotation discipline: condition the annotator on VERIFIED EXECUTION OUTCOMES (the actual o_{t+1}) so labels are grounded in environmental reality rather than annotator reasoning.

**备注**：Read: abstract, §1, §2.1-2.3, §3.1-3.3, §4, §5, Limitations, Tables 1-3, Figs. 1-3. TRAINED critic (8B SFT, 52k samples) — donor for its MEASUREMENTS and DESIGNS only, never its mechanism; flagged gradient use. CONSTRUCT_DONOR rather than PROTOCOL_DONOR because the load-bearing mechanism (visual marker re-verification) is modality-bound and the critic is trained; what transfers is the two-intervention-point architecture, the intent-masking anti-shortcut construct, the scalar-vs-verbal feedback argument, and the plausible-error-synthesis mutation protocol. Scout digest anticipated the re-inspectable-observation boundary; fulltext adds the intent-masking ablation (the shortcut result), the scalar-PRM null result, the 12-dimension taxonomy, and the verified-outcome annotation discipline.

**组级综合（agent 提交）**：SCOPE. Nine papers, all read in fulltext; all absence claims below are scoped to THIS READ SET. Domain split: 5 vision-GUI/VLM (2605.18652 MementoGUI, 2605.24785 PANDO, 2606.03054 ToolGate, 2606.10742 MemVenom, 2606.11078 HiViG), 3 text (2605.26849 UAB, 2606.01034 FCPS, 2606.07834 CCO), 1 code (2605.21384 SpecBench). No cross-modality effectiveness conclusion is drawn anywhere below; every transfer_boundary is a hypothesis about what breaks, not a finding about speech. No novelty verdicts. No web queries were run.

WHAT THE DONORS SETTLE.
(1) The external-controller-over-frozen-core ARCHITECTURE is settled as a design pattern, in five independent instantiations with five different decision rights: memory write/compress/retrieve (MementoGUI §4.1-4.2), skill admit/demote/merge (PANDO §4), observation-acquisition execute/skip (ToolGate §4.1), pre-execution action veto + verbal repair (HiViG §2.3), and commitment authorize/decline (CCO §4.5). All five keep the core frozen and unmodified; three of the five (PANDO, CCO, UAB) use no gradients at all. The pattern is not contested in this read set; what is contested is where the CONTROL SIGNAL comes from.
(2) Budget allocation over a fixed sampling budget is SOLVED as an optimization, not a heuristic. UAB §3.1-3.3 gives a separable concave knapsack with an exact marginal-greedy solution and, critically, Prop. 1: a merely-RANKING (uncalibrated) difficulty signal still yields useful allocations, degrading at most linearly in budget and L-infinity estimation error. That is the formal licence our program needs to allocate on a noisy label-free proxy. PANDO §3 supplies the complementary accounting: a four-term per-task cost identity (pre-eval / rollout x exec / verify / induce) with an overhead ratio rho, and the discipline of marking a term 'unreported' rather than omitting it.
(3) Selection pressure on a proxy is SETTLED AS A HAZARD, from two independent directions. SpecBench §3.4/§3.6 shows more search does not close the validation-vs-held-out gap and can widen its P90 tail, with a documented case where tree search DISCARDED a genuine implementation (53% val / 43% held-out) in favor of a lookup-table hack (97% val / 0% held-out) purely because the proxy scored it higher. CCO §5.3 shows the same shape in an aggregation setting: three-judge majority voting AMPLIFIES the unsafe directional commitment on AVeriTeC, and 48% (13/27) of those unsafe commits are 2-vs-1 cases where a single judge produced the safe verdict and was outvoted. Two different domains, one lesson: the aggregation/selection rule is itself an attack surface on the reward.
(4) Confidence is SETTLED AS INSUFFICIENT for admissibility. CCO §5.4 is the sharpest result in the read set: the panel is WELL CALIBRATED for direction (ECE = 0.07 on pure-evidence items) and yet confidence cannot separate unsafe commitments from correct ones at any tested threshold (mean p_bar 0.902 vs 0.933, a 0.031 separation; 86% of the failures sit above 0.85). Confidence measures decision strength; the reason to withhold lives in the EVIDENCE STRUCTURE. UAB corroborates from the other side: its threshold-exit variants (skip the too-easy and the too-hard) REDUCE accuracy on average and only pay off for the one better-calibrated model. Two papers, same conclusion: a scalar confidence axis is not an admissibility gate.
(5) Acquisition is SETTLED AS MOSTLY-INERT-WITH-SYMMETRIC-RISK. ToolGate §3.1, over 15,782 logged tool calls: 11.8% helpful, 9.9% harmful, 78.3% no immediate effect. And §3.3: an adversarial tool output collapses the agent's belief in a certified-present fact from 0.993 to 0.142, with skeptical PROMPTING recovering only to 0.461. The design consequence is stated by the authors and is the one we should carry: preventing bad evidence from entering is more reliable than asking the model to discount it afterwards.

WHAT TRANSFERS (ranked by how load-bearing it is for our P-slots).
P1 supply pricing / VOI — strongest lane in this subset. ToolGate's FORCED-ANSWER PROBE (query the frozen core's current best answer immediately before and after an acquisition; classify the transition helpful/harmful/unchanged-correct/unchanged-wrong) is the most transplantable instrument here: it turns 'did this observation help?' into an observable four-way transition without a counterfactual rollout, and the authors keep it strictly offline (diagnostics and label construction only, never in the deployed loop) — a discipline we should copy verbatim given the information-boundary failure mode in my own history. PANDO's four-term cost identity gives the denominator. UAB's marginal-gain rule p_i(1-p_i)^{e_i} gives the allocation. Together these three cover pricing, accounting, and allocation.
P9 calibration / admissibility / abstain — CCO is the closest occupant and donates three things we should adopt outright: the SEPARATION PRINCIPLE (verdict generation vs commitment authorization as distinct components, with NO-COMMIT as a controller routing state rather than an output label); the CONFLICTING-vs-INSUFFICIENT distinction (pragmatic non-commitment vs epistemic abstention — two structurally different reasons to abstain that one confidence axis provably cannot separate); and the SAME-DENOMINATOR + MATCHED-COVERAGE + RANDOM-VETO-NULL protocol, including its meta-lesson that a badly chosen null (one that cannot mechanically move a metric) is not a fair control. FCPS supplies the calibration accounting underneath (four-block split; Theorem 1's logarithmic menu-search penalty licensing a wide candidate search; unseen-pattern-rate as the statistic that tells you your calibration set never saw your deployment regime) but explicitly declines coverage control, so it is accounting, not a gate.
P10 reward hacking — SpecBench donates the measurement frame (proxy/true decomposition with the no-new-requirements constraint; Delta reported at IQM and P90; Delta tracked at every search step, not just terminally; the three-rung validation-coverage ladder). CCO donates the aggregation-amplification result. Both are protocol, not mechanism.
P5 skill induction — PANDO is the only paper in this subset with a complete induct-ADMIT-DEMOTE-MERGE lifecycle including persistent blacklisting and a demotion-off counterfactual. Retirement is the part most methods omit and it is the part we need.
P3 memory — MementoGUI donates the four-operator factorization and the explicit write gate (learned salience -> deterministic threshold, Eq. 5) plus the RANDOM-EPISODIC-CONTEXT null that separates 'more context' from 'right context'. MemVenom donates the adversary that gate must survive (memory-level access only; goal-swappable poison subgraph) and the ASR-r/ASR-a/ASR-ra decomposition that separates a retrieval failure from an induction failure, with BU/PU pairing so a defense is scored on what it costs the honest path. The two are complementary and neither cites the other's concern: MementoGUI never stress-tests its write gate; MemVenom builds no gate. PANDO states the gap in one sentence — 'online learning assumes a trusted stream'.
P8 reward-as-controller — HiViG's two-intervention-point layout (update compressed history before planning; veto/repair the proposal before execution) with NO gradient into the actor, plus its scalar-vs-verbal argument: scalar PRM baselines gave NO improvement over the base agent because a scalar offers no path forward when all candidates are poor. If our controller must drive REPAIR and not only SELECT, that argues the signal has to carry structure, not just magnitude.
Cross-cutting anti-shortcut construct: HiViG's INTENT MASKING (mask the proposer's stated reasoning in 30% of critic training samples; without it AndroidLab drops 51.5 -> 47.1) generalizes past its training use into a test we should run on any judge we build — a critic that can read the proposer's own account of what it did will stop checking the evidence.

WHAT BREAKS (the shared boundary, stated once).
Three dependencies recur across all nine and none of them survives intact on a frozen black-box speech/omni core.
(a) A DETERMINISTIC PER-STEP OR PER-TASK ORACLE. SpecBench's Delta needs two runnable test suites; PANDO's Admit/Demote needs an execution-verified terminal verdict y in {0,1}; HiViG's annotation pipeline needs verified execution outcomes; MemVenom's ASR needs a checkable side-goal completion; MementoGUI needs a gold reference action; UAB needs majority vote over exact-matchable answers; ToolGate needs a closed answer set for its argmax flip. Free-form speech output has no exact-match equivalence, so every one of these statistics requires a substitute whose own noise floor may be comparable to the effect being measured — and for UAB and ToolGate specifically, 'unchanged' would need an equivalence relation over transcripts that does not exist by construction.
(b) A RE-INSPECTABLE, ATTACKER-RENDERABLE, DIFFABLE OBSERVATION. MementoGUI's ROI crops, HiViG's coordinate marker, ToolGate's re-verifiable perceptual artifacts, MemVenom's rendered visual trigger and OCR injection all assume a screenshot: a spatial surface you can crop, mark, re-read cheaply, diff across steps, and (for the attacker) render into. A waveform is temporal, re-decoding costs a real call, there is no coordinate to mark, and there is no stable 'state' between turns to diff. This is the single most consequential break and it hits four of the five vision-GUI donors at their mechanism.
(c) EXCHANGEABILITY BETWEEN CALIBRATION AND DEPLOYMENT. FCPS's finite-label risk story, UAB's i.i.d. per-sample p_i, and ToolGate's cross-domain gate all assume the calibration population matches deployment. In speech the dominant shift is ACOUSTIC CONDITION, and it breaks these in a specific way worth stating: UAB's coverage objective 1-(1-p_i)^{N_i} assumes independent draws, but if an item is hard because of its acoustics, ALL N samples share that handicap — this is exactly the 'extreme intra-question correlation could invert the difficulty ordering' the UAB authors flag as pathological, and in speech it is the typical case. Re-sampling a hard-acoustics item may buy nothing. Likewise FCPS's unseen-mass diagnostic, measured on a matched test block, will systematically understate deployment unseen mass when the unseen patterns are unseen CONDITIONS.
Two further breaks worth flagging separately: NO GUARANTEED LOGPROBS kills UAB's default ANLL signal, and its own ablation (Table 4) shows the black-box substitute (verbalized confidence) LOSES to ANLL in every tested cell — so adopting UAB on a black-box core means adopting its weaker arm, and the honest reference number is the black-box row (+1.0 to +1.2% over Uniform), not the headline. And FOUR OF NINE ARE TRAINED (MementoGUI LoRA-SFT+DPO; HiViG 52k-sample SFT; ToolGate a fitted logistic gate; MemVenom surrogate-encoder optimization) — all four are donors for measurements and designs only, never mechanisms.

TENSIONS AND GAPS INSIDE THIS SUBSET.
- MementoGUI and MemVenom are the two halves of P3 and neither meets the other: an unaudited write gate on one side, an unmitigated adversary on the other. MemVenom's ablation hands us the choke point (remove the recall component and end-to-end attack success collapses to 0.00 — retrieval, not induction, is where a defense should sit) and its authors name the missing defense explicitly: provenance, retrieval consistency, task alignment, action consequences. Nothing in this read set builds it.
- PANDO's demotion and MementoGUI's write threshold both depend on a per-unit success signal that our setting does not supply; UAB's Prop. 1 is the only formal statement in this subset about how badly a noisy signal can be tolerated, and it is about ALLOCATION, not about ADMISSION. The corresponding tolerance bound for a write gate or a veto gate is absent here.
- Nobody in this subset calibrates a gate to a stated risk level. HiViG's veto is a binary grade; MementoGUI's tau is a tuned constant; ToolGate's tau = 0.5 with a sweep; CCO's probe is explicitly presented as a design probe whose magnitude improvements straddle zero (its robust finding is structural SELECTIVITY under a fair null, p < 1/2001, not aggregate gain). FCPS supplies calibration theory but declines coverage control. So the calibrated admissibility gate is unoccupied across all nine, and the pieces needed to build one are distributed across three of them (CCO's separation principle + FCPS's finite-label accounting + UAB's tolerance-to-miscalibration argument).
- Reading discipline note: several headline numbers in this subset are not what they first appear. PANDO's +4.3pp over SGV is backbone-confounded (Opus 4.6 + GPT-5.2 vs Gemini Flash) and deferred to an appendix; ToolGate's +1.65 accuracy comes only from a MATCHED-DOMAIN gate the authors repeatedly refuse to call zero-shot (strict cross-domain is +0.32 and +0.08, i.e. preserved accuracy); MementoGUI's trajectory success rates move between 1.29 and 3.57 percent; SpecBench reports its own scaling coefficient inconsistently (28pp / 27pp / R^2 0.24 vs 0.21) across abstract, figure caption, and text. None of these should be carried forward as facts without the qualifier.


## 供体组 2

### 2603.25158 — Trace2Skill: Distill Trajectory-Local Lessons into Transferable Agent Skills

**域**：text (agentic tool-use: spreadsheet/PDF/PPTX/DOCX file agents, math reasoning; one vision-QA slice, DocVQA)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P5 (tool/skill credit + induction from traces) primarily; P3 (memory write-gates, compiled-artifact vs retrieval, condition keying by its ABSENCE); P6 (system composition — offline consolidation stage as a distinct component).

**方法面**：Gradient-free three-stage pipeline over a frozen ReAct agent pi_theta (S2). Stage 1: roll out pi_theta with an initial skill S0 on an 'evolving set'; each trajectory carries query, reasoning/tool history, final output, and a BINARY CORRECTNESS OUTCOME, splitting the corpus into T- (failures) and T+ (successes) (S2.2). Stage 2: asymmetric analyst sub-agents propose per-trajectory skill patches in parallel — the error analyst A- is itself a ReAct loop that inspects artifacts, compares outputs against ground truth, and validates candidate fixes before proposing; failures that cannot be causally explained are EXCLUDED so P- is grounded in verified failure mechanisms; the success analyst A+ is single-pass (S2.3). Stage 3: hierarchical many-to-one merge over L=ceil(log_Bmerge |P|) levels (merge batch 32) that deduplicates, resolves conflicts, prefers prevalent patterns over idiosyncratic ones, and applies diff-style edits under deterministic guardrails (reject edits to missing files, withhold line-range conflicts, validate format) (S2.4). The same frozen model is trajectory generator, analyst, and merge operator — no external teacher, no parameter update. Output is a static skill directory loaded unchanged at inference: no test-time retrieval, no memory index. Decision right exercised = a one-shot offline WRITE/CONSOLIDATE gate, not an online select/stop.

**自述与显见局限**：Stated (Limitations S): all consolidated patches are applied by default; Bayesian-optimization subset selection helps (Table 6: 65.83->69.83 Vrf) but each candidate subset must be materialized as a skill and scored, and their validation set was only 32 questions. Observed by me: per-condition deltas flip sign (122B Deepening +Success is -10.35 on WikiTQ; 35B Create +Error is -7.70 Soft) so the headline gains are not uniform; the ReasoningBank comparison excludes the OOD splits because 'retrieval is not applicable' there (S4.1), which removes the setting where retrieval would be weakest AND where Trace2Skill's largest deltas live; three seeds, per-seed SDs deferred to Table 7. The whole pipeline presupposes labeled correctness on the evolving set and ground-truth access inside A-.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level headroom on top of this paper: (1) the produced skill is UNCONDITIONAL text — no applicability key, no gate saying under which conditions a patch may fire, and no per-patch attribution at inference; (2) patch value is shown to be combinatorial (greedy plateaus below full aggregation, Fig 5) but the only combinatorial selector offered is BO whose cost grows with validation set x patch universe — a cheap admissibility statistic for 'may this patch enter' is missing; (3) nothing measures whether a consolidated SoP is still valid after the operating condition shifts; (4) the analyst's exclusion rule ('discard failures that cannot be causally explained') is a write-gate in disguise but its false-exclusion rate is never measured.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：REQUIRED. (a) Deployment-time oracle: Stage 1 needs a binary correctness outcome per trajectory and A- explicitly 'compare[s] outputs against ground truth' — our frozen black-box speech setting has no such oracle at deployment. (b) Deterministic re-inspectable artifacts: the agentic analyst's advantage over single-call analysis (Fig 4) comes from inspecting xlsx artifacts and re-running fixes; a speech turn produces no artifact you can re-open and diff, and re-running against a waveform is not a deterministic fix-validation step. (c) Failure localization: it assumes failures are traceable to a decision or code step visible in a text trace; speech failures are largely perceptual (misheard span, speaker confusion, endpointing error) and never appear as a locatable step. (d) Static-artifact assumption: a skill file loaded unchanged presumes a stable operating condition; acoustic conditions shift within a session, so the same SoP can be right and wrong minutes apart. (e) No logprobs needed and the merge operator is a black-box call, so THAT half transfers cleanly — the labels and artifacts do not.

**可借鉴**：(1) The parallel-vs-sequential consolidation A/B under a SHARED trace pool and harness (Table 4: parallel 65.83 Vrf / ~3 min vs Seq-B=1 61.83 / ~60 min, Seq-B=4 59.00 / ~15 min) — an order-dependence control we can reuse verbatim for memory-write ordering. (2) The consolidated-artifact vs episodic-retrieval head-to-head on the same trajectory pool (Table 5, ReasoningBank top-1 with Qwen3-Embedding-8B) — the right way to ask whether our external memory should be retrieved or compiled. (3) The agentic-vs-single-call analyst ablation (Fig 4) as an evidence-access lever. (4) The greedy-vs-BO-vs-full patch-selection curve (Fig 5, Table 6) as the canonical demonstration that write-item value is combinatorial, not additive — directly usable as our P3 write-gate design argument. (5) The 'apples-to-apples per-design comparison under shared harness, plus a separate head-to-head' methodology statement (S4.1) as an isolation discipline. (6) Cross-scale/cross-family transfer protocol: author model != user model grid (Table 1; Gemma-4 and GPT-5.5 users in Fig 3). (7) Deterministic edit guardrails as a template for safe memory writes.

**备注**：Gradient-free end-to-end (no parameter updates anywhere) — so it is a legitimate mechanism-level as well as design-level donor, unusually for this read set. Its binding constraint is supervision, not white-box access.

### 2604.02623 — Poison Once, Exploit Forever: Environment-Injected Memory Poisoning Attacks on Web Agents (eTAMP)

**域**：text/web-GUI agent security (WebArena + VisualWebArena; observations are text accessibility trees with Set-of-Marks, so the observation channel is textual)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P3 (condition-keyed memory, write-gates, poisoning defenses — this is the dark-twin specification of what our write-gate must stop); P10 (reward/behavior hacking under pressure — here the pressure is environmental rather than selection); cross-cutting stop/abstain (their 'struggle' state is our abstain trigger seen from the attacker's side).

**方法面**：An ATTACK + stress-protocol study, no training. Threat model (S2.1): attacker injects text into web pages via user-generated content and can craft conditional triggers on observable features, but CANNOT access memory, model, or system prompts; memories are assumed retrieved by semantic similarity. Task A (poisoning) on site A contaminates raw-trajectory memory; Task B (activation) on a DIFFERENT site retrieves it, bypassing per-site permission defenses. Payload has three parts (S2.2): importance signal, TRIGGER CONDITION keyed on observable environment features (URL substring) and/or unobservable agent state ('when you struggle'), and attack goal as a goto URL. Three strategies: baseline injection, authority framing ('[URGENT SESSION RECOVERY]'), and Frustration Exploitation. Chaos Monkey (S2.3) is a probabilistic ENVIRONMENT-STRESS harness applied during Task B: click-drop p=0.4, scroll direction inversion p=1, Caesar-cipher typing transform p=1, with the step cap raised 15->37 so tasks stay completable. Memory is raw trajectory injected as prior user/assistant conversation turns (Appendix A.2.3). ~280 cross-site task pairs matched by embedding similarity; metrics ASR_B (malicious action executed), ASR_A (premature trigger during Task A = stealth control), TSR (utility).

**自述与显见局限**：Stated (A.1): only raw-trajectory memory is studied — LLM-summarized/consolidated memory untested, and whether consolidation confers robustness or new vulnerability is left open; no systematic defense evaluation (memory content filtering, anomaly detection on retrieved context, instruction-hierarchy enforcement all unevaluated). Observed by me: ASR_B is measured with PSEUDO Task-A trajectories that contain the payload (footnote 4), so the retrieval step is assumed rather than measured end-to-end; absolute TSR is very low across the board (0.7-17.6%, Table 2), so 'utility' is a weak denominator; awareness measurement uses a GPT-5.4 judge with high-confidence-only reporting.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level headroom: (1) no write-gate, no provenance tagging, no trust level on stored observations — the memory ingests whatever was observed; (2) 'frustration' is discovered as a vulnerability multiplier but never turned into a monitored state variable that could itself gate memory writes or tool authority; (3) no calibrated defense with a stated budget (contrast CORA in this same read set, which supplies exactly the missing calibrated abstain); (4) the agent's own anomaly awareness is measured (1.1-7.4% under chaos, ~0 clean) but never fed back as a control signal.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：REQUIRED. (a) Trigger keys are exact observable strings (URL substrings). A speech deployment has no stable string key for condition-conditional behavior; its analogue is an acoustic/condition descriptor (speaker, SNR, device) that is itself an ESTIMATE, so both attack and defense keying become probabilistic. (b) Chaos Monkey corrupts discrete GUI ACTIONS; the speech analogue must corrupt the OBSERVATION channel (added noise, dropout, clipping, segmentation error), which changes what 'stress' means and makes the degradation indistinguishable from ordinary hard audio — the paper's clean separation between 'environment malfunctioning' and 'task hard' does not survive the move. (c) Raw-trajectory memory of accessibility trees is exactly replayable text; a speech memory unit is a transcript or embedding that has already discarded the evidence needed to audit whether it was poisoned. (d) Cross-SITE persistence relies on discrete site identity as the permission boundary; speech has no site boundary, so the permission-bypass argument must be re-derived over tool/authority scopes. (e) Nothing here needs logprobs or weights — the black-box constraint itself is fully compatible.

**可借鉴**：(1) Chaos Monkey as a reusable DEGRADED-CONDITION STRESS PROTOCOL with explicit corruption probabilities and a compensating step-budget increase — directly adaptable as our acoustic-degradation harness for P10/P3. (2) The paired ASR_B / ASR_A metric design: an effectiveness rate PLUS a premature-trigger rate that must be ~0 — i.e. a false-activation control that we should mirror for any conditional memory or tool rule (their ASR_A is 0% for most models, max 0.71%). (3) The 'frustration window' finding as a measured interaction: ASR_B GPT-5-mini 3.6%->32.5% (8x) and GPT-5.2 6.4%->23.4% under stress, while Qwen3-32B/Qwen2.5-72B move +0.7 — a template for reporting susceptibility-under-degradation as a per-core quantity rather than a single number. (4) The decoupling of capability from safety: GPT-5.2 has the higher TSR and the higher vulnerability; Qwen robustness is shown NOT to be a recall failure (>=98.9% attack-URL recall, Table 9) — an isolation move worth copying. (5) Near-zero anomaly awareness under clean conditions and only 1.1-7.4% under chaos (Table 3) is a directly citable negative result against assuming a system notices its own condition degradation. (6) Embedding-similarity task pairing as the construction procedure for 'memory that would plausibly be retrieved'.

**备注**：No training, no white-box access anywhere — mechanism-compatible with our contract. Domain label matters: this is a security study, so its 'effectiveness' numbers are attack rates, not capability claims, and must never be read as cross-modality performance evidence.

### 2604.04373 — Decocted Experience Improves Test-Time Inference in LLM Agents

**域**：text (math reasoning AMC/AIME/HMMT/BeyondAIME, web browsing WebShop, software engineering SWE-bench)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P1 (supply pricing / value-of-information — the information-gain formalism plus its pre-generation proxy); P3 (memory representation, consolidation level, retrieval organization); P6 (context as a scaling axis complementary to compute — a composition argument for budget allocation between supply and sampling).

**方法面**：An analysis paper, not a system. Frozen LLM pi_theta; experience = per-problem sets of (trajectory, final reward) pairs; memory organization mu, retrieval R (top-K by embedding inner product with Qwen3-Embedding-4B), concatenation context constructor (S2). Four studied axes. (i) Input-size scaling: lesson DISTILLATION from experience beats raw experience and scales better with context size (S3.1). (ii) Experience-size scaling: k-means memory consolidation over embeddings e_i = Normalize((1-alpha) pi_e(x_i) + alpha pi_e(l_i)) keeping the entry nearest each centroid; retrieval relevance rises MONOTONICALLY with memory size but task performance is NON-MONOTONIC with a sweet spot at intermediate size that beats full memory (S3.2) — 'higher retrieval relevance does not necessarily yield better task performance'. (iii) Formal construct: information gain I(Y;C=c|X=x) = H(Y|x) - H(Y|x,c) can be negative for a misleading context; Proposition 4.1 bounds E[tau|x,c] <= H(Y|x,c)/h, so more informative context contracts the search space (S4). Validated with a Monte-Carlo conditional-entropy estimate from TOKEN-LEVEL LOG-PROBABILITIES (r=0.91 against output length; relevant context mean I-hat 172.2 vs 97.2 random, dominating 76% of samples). (iv) Pre-generation proxy: Q = Rel + lambda*Div where Rel is mean query-entry similarity and Div is NEGATIVE mean pairwise similarity among retrieved entries; Pearson r=0.25 with the paired improvement statistic delta avg_m(x) = avg_m_context(x) - avg_m_vanilla(x), peaking at lambda=0.6 (S4). Finally a hierarchical concept tree: LLM-extracted structured concept descriptions, recursive bisecting k-means, concept-level retrieval (S5). No gradients.

**自述与显见局限**：Stated: the sweet-spot operating point is 'difficult to determine in advance' and flat similarity retrieval 'may fail to capture deeper conceptual relationships' (S5 opening); information gain is unavailable before generation, hence the proxy. Observed by me: the proxy correlation is weak (r=0.25) and lambda=0.6 is selected by sweeping on the same evaluation set (Fig 6b), so the trade-off coefficient is not independently validated; main results use a single base model (Seed-OSS-36B-Instruct) with one extension (GPT-OSS-20B); the concept-tree section is evaluated against baselines but the tree depth/branching choices are not ablated in what I read; 'efficiency' is defined as output tokens / interaction steps, which is a proxy for cost, not for user-facing latency.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level headroom: (1) the information-gain construct is diagnostic only — there is no online estimator and no decision rule that spends or withholds compute based on it, so the paper stops one step short of the supply-pricing controller we need for P1; (2) consolidation level is chosen by sweep, not by any statistic computable at deployment; (3) the relevance-diversity proxy predicts improvement but is never used AS a retrieval objective in the main pipeline; (4) nothing distinguishes context that is uninformative from context that is actively misleading, even though the formalism admits negative information gain.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：REQUIRED. (a) The empirical validation of Prop 4.1 requires token-level log-probabilities to estimate H(Y|x,c) — our black-box contract explicitly does not guarantee logprobs, so the construct transfers but its ESTIMATOR does not; we would need a sampling-based dispersion proxy over decoded outputs, whose relation to the bound is unproven. (b) The efficiency payoff is measured in generated tokens / interaction steps; for a speech core the output is audio and 'shorter trajectory' has no clean cost analogue, so the headline mechanism (informative context contracts the search space) loses its measurement. (c) Rel/Div are computed in a TEXT embedding space; a speech embedding entangles content with speaker/channel, so a nearest neighbor may be an acoustic-condition match rather than a task match — the relevance-diversity trade-off would first need a condition-disentangled embedding, and until then 'diversity' may just mean 'different microphone'. (d) delta avg_m needs repeated attempts scored by a reward; with no exact-match equivalence for free-form speech output, avg_m itself becomes an estimate with its own variance. (e) The consolidation and concept-tree machinery is gradient-free and black-box-compatible.

**可借鉴**：(1) The paired context-on/context-off improvement statistic delta avg_m(x) — a per-query attribution measure we can adopt directly for 'did this memory item help', and the correct shape for our P3 write-gate evidence. (2) The consolidation sweep protocol showing retrieval relevance rising monotonically while performance is non-monotonic (Fig 4) — this is a ready-made falsification of 'more relevant retrieval is better retrieval' and a template for reporting memory-size ablations. (3) The relevance + lambda*diversity quality score with its lambda sweep as a CHEAP pre-generation proxy for context value (P1). (4) Proposition 4.1 as a formalism donor: value-of-context = reduction in output uncertainty, with an explicit sign convention where negative gain flags misleading context. (5) The random-context CONTROL arm (Fig 5b) — an essential null we should copy for any 'our memory helps' claim. (6) The concept-extraction-then-hierarchical-clustering memory structure as a condition-agnostic organization baseline.

**备注**：Fully gradient-free with respect to the agent; the only 'white-box' dependency is the logprob-based entropy estimator used for VALIDATION, which is separable from the design.

### 2604.04648 — From Curiosity to Caution: Mitigating Reward Hacking for Best-of-N with Pessimism (ICLR 2026)

**域**：text/LLM (GSM8K, MATH-500, BigBench-Hard; policy Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct, reward models OASST / Qwen3 RM / Skywork variants)　**供体级别**：BASELINE_ANCHOR　**喂给**：P10 (reward hacking under selection pressure — this is the canonical 'BoN degrades as N grows' anchor plus a cheap mitigation our program must match or beat); P9 (uncertainty-discounted reward as an admissibility gate); P8 (reward-as-controller in its simplest one-shot selection form).

**方法面**：Inference-time selection with a pessimistic reward: r_LCB(x,y) = r_hat(x,y) - lambda*alpha(x,y), select argmax_i r_LCB (S2.1-2.2). The uncertainty term alpha is an RND-style prediction error, but INVERTED — where curiosity rewards prediction error as novelty, 'caution' penalizes it as distributional uncertainty. Target network T(x,y) = h^R_L(x,y), the hidden state at layer L of the FROZEN REWARD MODEL (not the policy); predictor P_theta is a lightweight trainable net fit by MSE against T on responses sampled from pi on the benchmark train split — GRADIENT USE, but the data requirement is prompts only, no labels. At inference alpha = ||P_theta - T||^2, costing two extra forward passes on the same order as evaluating r_hat, fully parallelizable. lambda=0 recovers plain BoN. Theorem 1 (S2.3, informal): in a stylized linear-reward setting the caution-selected response beats BoN by ~sqrt(log N) and relative regret vanishes as N grows. Ground truth r* is binary correctness. Policy is frozen and never touched.

**自述与显见局限**：Stated: theorem assumptions are 'necessarily somewhat strong'; prior work is either overly conservative (chi^2-regularized BoN) or quadratic in N (Wasserstein). Observed by me: the mechanism requires WHITE-BOX access to the reward model's internal layer-L hidden states, so it is not a fully black-box selector; lambda is swept and the best value (0.8) is chosen in-domain (Table 2); the RM pool is small and dated (OASST 2023, Skywork ~0.6-1B); the authors select OASST as the BEST-performing comparable RM (§3, Fig. 1), so the effect is not an artifact of picking a weak scorer — though whether it survives a 2025+ frontier RM is untested (§7 does not check); on MATH-500 and BBH the 'Pessimism Only' arm (lambda=1.0, the paper's label for λ=1; note the score still contains r̂, so the label is the authors' shorthand, not a literal RM ablation) is the best (13.6/22.9 peak vs 12.3/18.5 for the mixture), which means on those datasets the learned reward contributes little and the headline is closer to 'typicality selection beats this reward model' than 'pessimism repairs it'; a more accurate predictor gives WORSE caution (architectural ablation, Table 3), so the training objective is misaligned with the use — an unresolved tension; single policy model, 3 bootstrap runs.【复核更正 wf_40068490】

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level headroom: (1) the uncertainty is purely distributional typicality with no task conditioning — a correct-but-atypical response is penalized identically to a hacked one, and the paper's own case study (S3.3) shows the signal is largely detecting FORMAT compliance; (2) no risk budget, no admissibility statement, no calibrated level — lambda is a knob, not a guarantee (contrast CORA in this read set); (3) nothing decides N or when to stop sampling; (4) no abstain option — the procedure always emits an argmax; (5) the reward-hacking diagnosis is retrospective (curve shape) rather than a deployment-time detector.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：REQUIRED. (a) alpha is read off the REWARD MODEL's hidden states. That is compatible with our black-box POLICY contract only if we own an open-weight reward model; if the evaluator is an API judge with no hidden states or logprobs, the mechanism is unavailable and we need a black-box typicality proxy (e.g. embedding-space density), which the paper does not test and whose theory does not carry. (b) The entire construction assumes a scalar r_hat over COMPLETE candidate responses plus a binary exact-match r* for evaluation; free-form speech output has no exact-match equivalence, so both the Peak/Final/Degradation protocol and the very definition of 'hacking' need a different correctness relation with its own noise floor. (c) 'Typical responses from pi' is defined over text sampled from the policy; for a speech core, atypicality is driven at least as much by ACOUSTIC condition as by content, so one typicality model would conflate 'hard audio' with 'hacked content' and could systematically penalize exactly the degraded-condition cases we most need to select well in. (d) Response-level abstraction (prompts and responses as single entities) is actually favorable for us — no token-level prefix control is required.

**可借鉴**：(1) THE reporting triple for selection pressure: Peak accuracy / Final accuracy at max N / Degradation = peak-final, with N swept 2^0..2^9 (Table 1: RM 79.3/71.5/7.7, Pessimism-only 81.3/80.3/1.0, RM+Pess 82.6/81.1/1.5 on GSM8K; BBH RM 17.3/1.7/15.6 vs 22.9/22.1/0.9). Any BoN-shaped selector we build should report this triple — it converts 'reward hacking' from anecdote to measured quantity. This is the single most reusable artifact in the paper. (2) The N-scaling curve across in-domain / OOD / out-of-domain prompt distributions with the uncertainty module trained only on the in-domain split (Fig 3) — a generalization protocol for any auxiliary control module. (3) The caution-vs-traditional-RND ablation at matched lambda (Table 2) isolating 'pretrained features matter, random features do not'. (4) The mixing-weight sweep as an admissibility-strength dial. (5) The qualitative case study design: pick a 99.8th-percentile-reward INCORRECT response and show the uncertainty term catches it (S3.3) — a clean hacking-instance exhibit format. (6) The pessimism/LCB formalism itself (reward minus uncertainty penalty) as a construct for P9 admissibility discounting.

**备注**：Trained component (predictor network) — donor for its MEASUREMENTS and DESIGNS, not its mechanism, per directive. Gradient use flagged: MSE training of P_theta; the policy itself is never updated and is treated as a black box at response granularity.

### 2604.06240 — The Art of Building Verifiers for Computer Use Agents (Universal Verifier + CUAVerifierBench)

**域**：vision-GUI (computer-use agents over web; multi-screenshot trajectories, Fara-7B and GPT-5-SoM agents)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P9 (decision-calibration and admissibility gates — FPR-as-currency, human-agreement-band sufficiency); P10 (a near-zero-FPR verifier is the hardening that makes selection pressure survivable); P6 (system composition: verifier as a named component with its own quality contract); P8 (verifier is the reward source for any downstream controller).

**方法面**：A prompt-engineered, gradient-free verifier V: (g, tau) -> (r_proc in [0,1], r_out in {0,1}, diagnostic d) (S4, Alg 1). Four design principles (S3). (1) Rubrics: criteria must be specific and non-overlapping; rubric GENERATION is separated from scoring and done from the task alone WITHOUT seeing the trajectory (otherwise the model tailors criteria to observed behavior); phantom criteria and cascading dependent criteria are named failure modes; conditional criteria are marked at generation time and resolved after the attempt so mutually exclusive criteria do not interfere; the whole rubric is scored TWICE, with and without screenshot evidence, to surface hallucination. (2) Process and outcome rewards are separate signals: r_proc = sum earned / sum max over APPLICABLE criteria, r_out is a binary 'would a reasonable user consider this done'; an agent blocked by a login wall gets full process credit and outcome failure. (3) Explicit controllable (intent mismatch, reasoning errors, hallucination, insufficient effort, execution errors) vs uncontrollable (CAPTCHA/login walls, entity non-existence, availability, no search results) factor lists. (4) Divide-and-conquer context management: score EVERY screenshot against EVERY criterion into a relevance matrix R in R^{(T+1) x N}, take top-k per criterion, then evidence analysis, conditional disambiguation, reality check, multimodal rescoring where screenshot evidence takes precedence over agent claims, a dedicated unsolicited-side-effect pass, outcome verification, and failure diagnosis over a hand-crafted 7-category / 24-subcode taxonomy. Variance reduction: steps 7-9 run as parallel instances, median rubric score and majority-vote outcome.

**自述与显见局限**：Observed by me (the paper has no limitations section in what I read): in the at-scale study (Table 3) the UV is itself the reference label, so 'native verifiers have FPR > 0.20' is circular unless UV's own error is accounted; the human-labeled sets are small (n=140 internal, n=106 Browserbase); the process label is binarized at an arbitrary >=0.8 threshold for agreement computation; the outcome convention ('focus on primary intent', users forgive nitpicks but not unsolicited side-effects) is an explicit POLICY CHOICE encoded as ground truth; the pipeline costs a multimodal pass per screenshot x criterion; the auto-research finding (70% of expert quality in 5% of time) is a single run of one agent configuration.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level headroom: (1) a verifier with outcome kappa ~0.58-0.64 is then consumed downstream as if it were an oracle — nothing propagates verifier error into the decision that uses it (this read set contains the missing half, CORA's calibrated gate, but the two are never composed); (2) FPR is driven near zero by construction and by the auto-research constraint, but it is an OPTIMIZATION side-constraint, not a guaranteed level at a stated confidence; (3) no label-free headroom estimate — the paper establishes verifier quality bounds achievable training/eval signal but never quantifies the bound; (4) FNR remains 0.31-0.32 and is not treated as a cost with its own budget.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：REQUIRED. (a) The load-bearing mechanism is a RE-INSPECTABLE per-step visual evidence store and a relevance matrix over it. A speech trajectory's per-step evidence is audio, and 'screenshot evidence takes precedence over agent claims' fails when the evidence itself is only accessible through another fallible model — re-listening yields another estimate, not an arbiter. (b) The controllable/uncontrollable split maps naturally onto acoustic conditions (noise, accent, overlapping speech = uncontrollable) but there is no observable analogue of a login wall or CAPTCHA that declares itself; attributing failure to condition rather than to the agent becomes an inference, not an observation. (c) Rubric criteria in GUI tasks are entity-level and crisply checkable (correct product in cart, reservation on the right date); rubric criteria for a spoken answer are semantic and would inherit the same free-form-equivalence problem the rubric was meant to solve. (d) The outcome label rests on a terminal state a user can inspect; speech tasks frequently have no terminal state, only a transcript of what was said. (e) Everything here is gradient-free prompting over a frozen judge, so the black-box constraint per se is fully satisfied.

**可借鉴**：(1) FPR-PRIMARY verifier evaluation: report FPR, FNR, accuracy, F1, Cohen's kappa separately and treat near-zero FPR as the design target (UV outcome FPR 0.01 internal / 0.08 external vs WebVoyager >=0.45, WebJudge >=0.22). (2) The sufficiency criterion 'verifier agrees with humans as often as humans agree with each other': UV outcome kappa 0.58 and process 0.43 fall inside the inter-annotator ranges 0.53-0.57 and 0.36-0.45 — this is exactly the admissibility bar our evaluation component should adopt for P9 rather than an absolute kappa threshold. (3) The two-stage UV-blind / UV-informed annotation protocol with flip-rate reporting (16.6% of outcome judgments flipped, nearly all success->failure), which measures how much a verifier's reasoning moves a human — a superb design for validating an external signal without assuming it. (4) The backbone-upgrade control: upgrading baselines to the same GPT-5.2 cuts FPR (0.45->0.10) but raises FNR (0.24->0.44) with only modest kappa gain — proving the advantage is ARCHITECTURAL not model-driven. We must run this control before claiming any control-plane gain. (5) Process/outcome reward separation with an explicit controllable/uncontrollable taxonomy — directly transposable to agent-fault vs acoustic-condition-fault. (6) The auto-research optimization rule 'maximize Cohen's kappa without increasing FPR; any FPR-increasing change is automatically rolled back' plus a compliance agent auditing against test memorization — a reusable guardrail for automated prompt/config search. (7) Rubric-generation-before-trajectory as an anti-contamination rule; conditional criteria as declared-in-advance applicability conditions. (8) The 7-category/24-subcode failure taxonomy structure. (9) Their independent audit of AgentRewardBench: 8/30 human-labeled successes judged false positives (FPR ~0.27) — evidence that human gold labels in agent benchmarks carry a nontrivial FP rate.

**备注**：Gradient-free, prompt-engineered, frozen judge — mechanism-compatible with our contract; the obstacle is evidence modality, not access. Domain label vision-GUI: METHOD donor only, no cross-modality effectiveness inference.

### 2604.09155 — CORA: Conformal Risk-Controlled Agents for Safeguarded Mobile GUI Automation (+ Phone-Harm benchmark)

**域**：vision-GUI (mobile VLM GUI agents; base policies GPT-5, Gemini-3, UI-TARS-1.5, AutoGLM-VLM)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P9 (decision-calibration and admissibility gates — the calibrated execute/abstain boundary is the single most direct occupant in this read set); cross-cutting stop/abstain/budget calibration; P6 (system composition: guardian + calibrator + diagnostician + intent contract as named components with distinct decision rights).

**方法面**：Post-policy, pre-action SELECTIVE EXECUTION wrapped around a fixed base policy pi — parameters of pi are never modified (S3.1-3.2). Three components plus a cross-cutting contract. (1) Guardian R_psi: pretrained VLM backbone + compact TRAINABLE scalar risk head on the last-valid-token representation, giving action-conditional s_t = R_psi(g, o_t, h_t, a_hat_t) in [0,1]; weighted BCE with omega>1 prioritizing recall of rare harmful events (S3.3) — GRADIENT USE. (2) Conformal Risk Control (S3.4): executed-harm loss L(Z;tau) = l(Z)*1{s<=tau}, monotone non-decreasing in tau; tau_hat = sup{tau : (sum_i L(Z_i;tau) + 1)/(n+1) <= alpha}; under exchangeability the expected rate of autonomously executed harmful actions on test is <= alpha — a user-settable 'deployable knob' independent of the model's raw calibration quality. (3) Generative Diagnostician D_phi (LoRA-adapted VLM, CLM loss on diagnostic tokens only) that takes rejected actions and emits (free-text rationale, harm type in {MISUSE, INJECTION, MISBEHAVIOR}, intervention in {ABORT, CONFIRM, REFLECT}) (S3.5). Goal-Lock (S3.6): the initial user goal is frozen as the normative objective and ALL on-screen text is treated strictly as untrusted evidence, never instruction. Shift handling (S3.7): weighted CRC with w(c) proportional to p_target(c)/p_cal(c) over a deployment descriptor c = (app, OS, device, ui-layout, locale), clipped to [w_min, w_max]; blockwise trajectory splitting for temporal dependence.

**自述与显见局限**：Stated: standard CRC assumes exchangeability while 'real-world GUI deployments face distribution shifts' (S3.7) — the weighted extension is offered as a pathway, not a validated result. Observed by me: both Guardian and Diagnostician require in-house per-step harm supervision, so the guarantee is conditional on that labeling AND on exchangeability; the 2.42% <= 5% operating point is reported on the n=1075 set that Appendix E's preamble calls the calibration set and its figure captions call the holdout set — the paper does not disambiguate, so whether the budget is verified out-of-calibration is UNRESOLVED from the text; on MobileRisk CORA's FPR is 53.92% — far above every baseline — which the Pareto framing does not foreground; OIR 19.23% on the harmful subset is a real interruption cost; AndroidWorld capability retention is 8/20 vs GPT-5's 11/20 on a 20-task suite (small n); the step-level harm judge used for scalable evaluation is itself an LLM (Qwen-3).【复核更正 wf_40068490】

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level headroom: (1) the risk budget bounds HARM but nothing bounds the interruption/abstention cost symmetrically — there is no dual budget and no value-of-intervention pricing; (2) the descriptor-weighted CRC is proposed but its shift coverage is asserted rather than certified, and the weights are estimated from unlabeled deployment windows without an error term; (3) the Guardian's risk score is a point estimate with no uncertainty of its own feeding the calibration; (4) abstention routes to ABORT/CONFIRM/REFLECT but there is no learned or calibrated choice among them beyond the Diagnostician's generation; (5) no label-free headroom estimate — you cannot tell from deployment data how much of the residual harm is reachable.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：REQUIRED. (a) CRC needs a calibration set carrying PER-STEP BINARY HARM LABELS and a deployment stream exchangeable with it. A frozen speech core has no per-step action with a step-identifiable harmful affordance ('tap Confirm'); the harmful unit is an utterance or a tool call, and its harm label is often only determinable after the fact — so both the loss definition L(Z;tau) and the labeling protocol must be re-derived. (b) Goal-Lock depends on the instruction channel being syntactically separable from the observation channel (user goal in the prompt, untrusted text on the screen). In speech BOTH arrive on the same acoustic channel, so 'treat on-screen text as evidence not instruction' has no markable boundary — this is the sharpest structural break in the paper for our setting. (c) The deployment descriptor c is observable metadata (app id, OS version, device family, locale). Our analogue is acoustic (SNR, speaker, mic, room, codec) and is NOT observable metadata — it must be estimated, so the importance weights w(c) inherit estimation error and the weighted-CRC validity argument no longer holds as stated. (d) The Guardian is action-conditional on a discrete parameterized action space (TAP/TYPE/SWIPE with coordinates); free-form speech output is not a discrete action to score before execution, so 'pre-action' gating must become 'pre-emission' gating over a continuous output. (e) Trained components (Guardian head, LoRA Diagnostician) are outside our training-free constraint — the CRC layer itself is training-free and IS transferable given labels.

**可借鉴**：(1) CRC as the calibration protocol for an abstain threshold: monotone loss, tau_hat = sup{tau : (sum L + 1)/(n+1) <= alpha}, leakage-aware disjoint train/cal/test at task and episode level, blockwise splitting for temporal dependence. This is the cleanest available recipe for turning our stop/abstain threshold from a tuned constant into a calibrated statistic (P9, cross-cutting). (2) The MATCHED BENIGN CONTROL SET design: Harm-150 plus Normal-150 authored under an identical protocol from the same task distribution, then a merged 300-task 'deployment-realistic mixture' evaluation. The decisive statistic is that CORA's merged OIR 9.83% DECOMPOSES into 19.23% on harmful and 0.42% on benign traffic — i.e. false-alarm cost reported separately by traffic type. We should copy both the paired-set construction and the decomposition. (3) Step-level annotation using ONLY step-local evidence with access to future states explicitly disallowed to prevent future-information leakage into supervision (S4.2) — an information-boundary discipline that directly matches our own known failure mode. (4) The metric quartet HR / mHR / GAR / Intervention-F1 / OIR reported as a Pareto rather than aggregated, plus the explicit note that near-zero harm is trivially achievable by refusing (GPT-5: HR 0.40% with GAR collapsing to 37.50%) — a refusal-degenerate-solution guard we must adopt. (5) The alignment-tax probe on benign tasks (AndroidWorld core20) as a capability-retention check for any gate we add. (6) Ablation design: sweep fixed thresholds tau in {0.5..0.9} and show NO fixed point recovers the CRC operating point — the right way to prove a calibrated threshold beats a tuned one. (7) Goal-Lock as a CONSTRUCT for intent anchoring against injection, and the ablation showing that disabling it forces CRC to pick an aggressively conservative tau_hat (i.e. injection resistance buys back coverage). (8) The MISUSE / INJECTION / MISBEHAVIOR harm taxonomy and the ABORT/CONFIRM/REFLECT intervention menu as a decision-rights vocabulary.

**备注**：Trained Guardian and Diagnostician (gradient use flagged) — donor for MEASUREMENTS and DESIGNS only. The CRC layer is itself training-free and is the transferable core, but it is supervision-hungry rather than access-hungry. Domain label vision-GUI: METHOD donor only, no cross-modality effectiveness inference.

### 2604.09482 — Process Reward Agents for Steering Knowledge-Intensive Reasoning (PRA)

**域**：text (medical reasoning: MedQA, Medbullets, MedMCQA, MMLU-Med, GPQA, Lancet, NEJM; frozen policies Qwen3-4B / Llama-3.1-8B / Qwen2.5-0.5B-3B / Llama-3.2-1B-3B)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P8 (reward-as-controller, gradient-free multi-step: the cleanest online process-level steering of a frozen policy in this read set); P1 (supply pricing / value-of-information via the margin-shift criterion and the search-frequency Pareto); P6 (decoupling of domain-specific reward module from replaceable frozen backbone as a composition principle).

**方法面**：An external control module over a FROZEN policy pi. The PRA mu_phi has two token-level readouts from one shared-parameter model (S3.2): an action controller mu^act(q, tau_t) in {search, reward} and a scorer mu^rwd(q, tau_t, D_t) obtained by two-way softmax over the logits of tokens '0'/'1' at a designated output slot. Control is exercised through beam search (S3.3): beam width B=4, branching b=16 (64 total = matched Self-Consistency budget); each partial trace is ranked by CUMULATIVE reward R(tau_t) = sum_i r_hat_i; top-B retained, rest pruned; the highest-cumulative-reward completed trace is the answer. Crucially the policy NEVER sees retrieved documents and receives NO gradient from the PRA, so outputs stay inside the policy's own distribution and backbones are drop-in replaceable. Training labels come from a Qwen3-235B teacher (GRADIENT USE on the PRA only): reasoning labels are the teacher's binary step-correctness token; SEARCH labels come from a margin-shift value-of-information criterion — m = log p(1) - log p(0) without documents, m_d with documents, Delta m = m - m_d, label 'search' iff |Delta m| > epsilon_global set to the median (Eqs. 6-8), i.e. retrieval is deemed necessary only when evidence would move the evaluator's posterior. Main results use always-search as a practical upper bound; the margin-shift variant enables a swept deployment threshold theta_dep for selective retrieval. Infrastructure: a global queue partitioning traces by pending stage (policy generation / retrieval / reward readout) for stage-level batched execution.

**自述与显见局限**：Stated (Impact Statement): may not eliminate hallucination or remove all incorrect intermediate steps; explicitly 'a method contribution and not a ready-to-deploy system'. Observed by me: the PRA is fine-tuned, so the mechanism is not training-free; correctness assumes a verifiable answer function C(y_hat, y) — all benchmarks are multiple-choice/exact-match; process 'ground truth' is itself a large LLM's judgment, so the reward is distilled from a model, not from an oracle; single domain (medicine) with a curated four-corpus knowledge base and a fixed MedCPT retrieve-200-rerank-to-64 configuration; the always-search main configuration hides the cost axis that Fig 3 then reintroduces.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level headroom: (1) the step reward is a point scalar with no uncertainty, and the beam ranks by an UNWEIGHTED cumulative sum — no discounting, no admissibility gate, no pessimism (contrast 2604.04648 in this same read set); (2) there is no stopping rule — search runs until all beam traces complete, so budget is spent uniformly; (3) no abstain: the top-cumulative trace is always emitted regardless of how low its reward is; (4) the margin-shift VOI criterion is computed OFFLINE by a teacher and then distilled into a classifier, so no deployment-time VOI estimate exists; (5) reward is applied per step but never attributed back to which evidence made the difference.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：REQUIRED. (a) The controller requires PREFIX-RESUMABLE generation: partial traces must be extendable, scorable, and prunable mid-generation. A black-box speech/omni API typically returns a completed response and does not expose resumable prefixes, so online step-level pruning — the exact source of the paper's headline gain (81.9 online vs 77.6 process/post-hoc, Table 4) — is unavailable without prefix control. This is the single most important boundary in this entry. (b) It requires a clean TEXTUAL step decomposition where 'the last reasoning step' is a well-formed scoring unit; a spoken response has no comparable step boundary, and imposing one changes the object being scored. (c) The margin-shift criterion needs the evaluator's log p(0) / log p(1) — fine if we own the reward module, unavailable if the judge is an API without logprobs. (d) 'External evidence' here is a document corpus with a dense retriever and reranker; the speech analogue of evidence is another OBSERVATION of the same waveform (re-listen at different gain, different segmentation, different ASR hypothesis), which has no retrieval index, no relevance reranker, and — unlike a document — is not independent of the original error. (e) Answer verifiability C(y_hat, y) as exact match has no free-form speech equivalent. (f) Favorable: policy frozen, no gradients into the policy, no policy internals used — the ARCHITECTURE (external reward module, replaceable backbone) is exactly our shape.

**可借鉴**：(1) THE 2x2 that isolates where control-plane gain comes from (Table 4): fix the SAME trained scorer and vary only reward LEVEL (outcome vs process) and reward TIMING (post hoc vs online) — outcome/post-hoc 75.7, process/post-hoc with min/max/avg reduction 74.3/77.5/77.6, process/online 81.9, against CoT+SC 74.8. This is the most reusable experimental design in my read set for arguing that a controller's benefit is about WHEN a signal is applied, not merely about having a better signal. (2) The compute-matched comparison discipline: beam B=4 x b=16 = 64 candidates matched against Self-Consistency at 64 samples. (3) The untrained-backbone-as-reward-agent control (Table 3): using the untrained policy as its own scorer within the same search structure matches plain SC, proving 'inference-time restructuring alone is insufficient' — the null we must run before claiming a scaffold gain. (4) Cross-backbone transfer of ONE frozen-policy controller to unseen policies 0.5B-8B (Table 2: Qwen2.5-0.5B 28.4->54.1, Llama-3.2-1B 36.2->61.2, Llama-3.1-8B 67.0->82.3), with the notable pattern that gains are LARGEST for the weakest backbones. (5) The margin-shift statistic Delta m as an operational value-of-information definition for P1: acquire evidence only when conditioning on it moves the evaluator's posterior beyond a threshold, with the threshold set at the median and then swept (theta_dep 0->1) to trace a cost/accuracy Pareto (Fig 3). (6) The diagnostic that SC saturates past ~8 samples while step-guided search keeps improving (Fig 2) — a scaling-shape contrast for P8. (7) Margin shift as a difficulty/correctness diagnostic: larger for eventually-correct traces and on harder questions, and near zero at the final answer-selection step (Figs 4-5). (8) Stage-partitioned global-queue batching as a systems pattern for asynchronous multi-component control planes.

**备注**：Trained reward agent (gradient use flagged, on the PRA only — never on the policy). Donor for measurements and designs; the mechanism additionally requires prefix-level generation control that our black-box contract does not guarantee.

### 2604.13552 — Training-Free Test-Time Contrastive Learning for Large Language Models (TF-TTCL)

**域**：text (GSM8k, MATH-500, AIME24, Minerva for closed-ended; DomainBench Geography/Agriculture/Medicine/Finance for open-ended; Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct plus API models Qwen-Plus and DeepSeek-V3.2)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P3 (memory write-gates and rule induction without labels — the write-abstention rule, positive/negative separation, pruning-as-regularization); P8 (reward-as-controller in its weakest admissible form: an internal ranking signal with no external verifier); P9 (label-free operation is the constraint our headroom estimator must live under).

**方法面**：Fully gradient-free online adaptation of a frozen model with NO external knowledge base and NO ground-truth access (S3 setup: y*_t is explicitly inaccessible). An 'Explore-Reflect-Steer' loop with three modules. (1) Semantic Query Augmentation (S4.1): three roles sharing the same frozen LLM with different system prompts and decoding configs — TEACHER produces a greedy high-confidence anchor y^T; TUTOR rewrites the query into N stylistically distinct but semantically equivalent variants (test-time input augmentation); STUDENT samples one response per variant under the same retrieved rules. (2) Contrastive Experience Distillation (S4.2): consistency-based partition — for closed-ended tasks, majority voting over answers, with the sample DISCARDED entirely (no rule written) if all agents disagree, all-same -> all positive, ties broken by lowest perplexity; for open-ended tasks, rank candidates by embedding similarity to the teacher anchor and take the top 50% as positives. Then uncertainty-aware selection via sequence-level perplexity: the lowest-PPL positive is y+, and — deliberately — the lowest-PPL NEGATIVE is the hard negative y-, targeting confident errors. A summarizer distills the gap into a paired (r+, r-) rule. (3) Contextual Rule Retrieval (S4.3): two DISJOINT repositories R_pos and R_neg keyed by rule embedding; top-K=30 from each by cosine similarity to the query embedding; injected under explicit headers so the negative set acts as an interdiction constraint. Systems: TUTOR/STUDENT run in parallel and rule summarization is decoupled as an async background step, capping user-perceived latency at 2.01x a single call; similarity-based FIFO pruning bounds the repository at 1,000 rules.

**自述与显见局限**：Stated (Limitations): diminishing returns as the model approaches its capability ceiling; one-shot injection of all retrieved rules with no progressive disclosure. Ethical note flags increased exposure to adversarial prompt injection. Observed by me: min-PPL selection (Eq. 5) requires sequence log-probabilities, which sits in tension with the paper's own 'black-box API' positioning in Table 1 and Table 4; open-ended positives are defined by similarity to the model's OWN greedy answer, so self-confirmation bias is reduced but not eliminated; AIME24 has 30 items so 13.33% is 4 problems and 3.33% is 1 — those cells carry no resolution; single-seed results as far as I can see; the random-rule control matches the method on GSM8k (87.41 vs 87.49), so on math the retrieval mechanism is doing almost nothing.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level headroom: (1) the only write-gate is 'discard when all agents disagree' — a coarse abstention with no measured false-discard rate and no graded admissibility; (2) rules are unconditional text with no applicability key, so nothing prevents a rule learned under one condition from firing under another; (3) no poisoning defense on a repository that ingests self-generated content and is retrieved by similarity (their own ethics note names the exposure); (4) no measurement of whether a retrieved rule actually fired or changed the output — no per-rule attribution; (5) the perplexity confidence proxy is used without any calibration check against correctness.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：REQUIRED. (a) Perplexity as the confidence proxy is the most fragile import: our contract does not guarantee logprobs, and for a speech/omni core the sequence whose PPL you would compute may be audio tokens whose normalization is not comparable to text tokens — both y+ and y- selection collapse without it. (b) Majority-vote partitioning presupposes ANSWER IDENTITY. For free-form spoken outputs, 'same answer' is itself an estimate with its own error rate, so the positive set is noisy in a way this paper's closed-ended regime never tests; the open-ended branch (similarity to the teacher anchor) is closer to our situation and is exactly where the method's margin over baselines rests on ROUGE-Lsum, a weak surrogate. (c) Query augmentation by stylistic rewriting has an obvious speech analogue (re-prompt, re-segment, alternative front-end hypotheses) but a 'semantically equivalent' re-rendering in speech also changes ACOUSTIC DIFFICULTY, so response divergence under augmentation confounds model uncertainty with input difficulty — the paper's core signal loses its interpretation. (d) The rule artifact is text distilled from readable reasoning gaps; a speech failure's gap is often perceptual and not verbalizable from the output alone. (e) Favorable: no gradients, no weights, no hidden states, no external verifier or knowledge base — the paradigm (frozen core, external evolving rule memory, retrieval-conditioned steering) is precisely our shape, and the paper explicitly frames itself against methods needing 'deterministic external feedback (e.g., code execution results)'.

**可借鉴**：(1) The LABEL-FREE, FEEDBACK-FREE positive/negative construction protocol — this is the closest analogue in my read set to our no-oracle deployment constraint, and its comparison table (Table 1: external knowledge / source data / gradient-free / online) is a useful axis chart for positioning our own contract. (2) The hard-negative-by-minimum-perplexity idea as a CONSTRUCT: target the errors the system is most confident about, not the most obviously wrong ones. (3) The positive/negative rule ASYMMETRY ablation (Table 6): removing negative rules costs more than removing positive rules (87.49->86.88 vs ->87.19), with the interpretation that positive rules restate what the model already knows while negative rules supply unique interdiction signal. This directly informs what our memory should store. (4) The RANDOM-RULE control arm (Table 6): random rules match on GSM8k (87.41) but collapse on Finance (0.2665 vs 0.2863) — i.e. retrieval precision matters only where the output space is unstructured. A mandatory null for any 'our retrieval helps' claim, and a warning that math-style benchmarks cannot detect retrieval quality. (5) The memory-pruning result: bounding the repository to 1,000 rules via similarity-based FIFO slightly IMPROVES accuracy (87.49->87.72) while capping overhead — compression as regularization (P3). (6) The 'discard the sample and skip rule summarization when all agents disagree' rule as an explicit write-abstention gate. (7) The latency accounting (Table 7: sequential 10.25s, parallel 4.11s, single call 2.05s) with asynchronous memory update proven to complete before the next query — an honest overhead-reporting template for a control plane. (8) The black-box API arm (Table 4) as a deployment-regime check.

**备注**：Gradient-free end-to-end, so it is mechanism-eligible — but the min-PPL component silently reintroduces a logprob dependency that our black-box contract does not grant. If we borrow the loop we must replace the confidence proxy, and the paper offers no evidence about how the method behaves under a non-logprob substitute.

### 2604.14004 — Memory Transfer Learning: How Memories are Transferred Across Domains in Coding Agents

**域**：text/code (LiveCodeBenchv6, Aider-Polyglot, SWE-Bench Verified, TerminalBench2, ReplicationBench, MLGym-Bench; gpt-5-mini, DeepSeek V3.2, Qwen3-Coder-480B)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P3 (cross-instance memory with condition keying — abstraction level as the eligibility variable; write-gates; negative transfer as the failure to defend against); P5 (skill/experience induction from traces at varying abstraction); P1 (memory-pool supply scaling).

**方法面**：An empirical STUDY with a deliberately simple gradient-free agent (mini-swe-agent), designed to isolate memory variables (S3.1). Offline memory generation from trajectories, with an LLM judge assigning success/failure and different generation prompts per case. Four memory representations at increasing abstraction: Trajectory M_T = (t, [(a_i, o_i)]) — raw commands and execution results; Workflow M_W = (goal, action subset); Summary M_S = (task summary, experience paragraph with analysis of why it succeeded/failed); Insight M_I = (title, description, content), ReasoningBank-style and explicitly prompted for generalizable insights without naming specific files. Retrieval: text embeddings (text-embedding-3-small), cosine top-N=3; because Workflow/Summary/Insight carry no task field, the QUERY is a model-written 4-5 sentence coding plan rather than the task text. The cross-domain pool is constructed by exclusion: P_tau(B_i) = {M | source task not in B_i}, so every evaluated memory comes from a different benchmark. Pass@3 reported; 100-task subsamples.

**自述与显见局限**：Observed by me (no explicit limitations section in what I read): effect sizes are small and not uniformly positive — average +3.7% / +2.6% / +1.8% across the three models, with per-benchmark cells flat or negative (Aider-Polyglot 0.0% for gpt-5-mini and -1.0% for DeepSeek; MLGym and ReplicationBench 0.0% for Qwen3-Coder); Pass@3 over 100-task subsamples with no confidence intervals reported in the tables I read; success labels come from an LLM judge; the domain-count axis in Fig 6 runs to 9 while only 6 benchmarks are named, so 'domain' is under-specified in the main text; the abstraction probe uses an LLM to reconstruct the source task, so the abstraction measure is itself model-mediated.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level headroom: (1) abstraction level is identified as THE keying variable but the inverse-inference abstraction score is used only analytically — there is no write gate that refuses a memory below an abstraction threshold, and no abstraction score computed at write time; (2) negative transfer is carefully taxonomized but not prevented — no detector, no admissibility test before a memory is allowed to condition the next task; (3) retrieval is shown to be the bottleneck (LLM reranking and adaptive rewriting both UNDERPERFORM plain embedding similarity) but no alternative is proposed beyond pointing at domain routing and step-wise retrieval; (4) 'domain' is treated as a discrete observable label with no notion of a continuously drifting or estimated condition; (5) no cost accounting for the memory pipeline against the 1.8-3.7% gain.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：REQUIRED. (a) 'Domain' here has EXPLICIT, OBSERVABLE identity — a benchmark, a language, a repository — which makes both the exclusion protocol P_tau(B_i) and the cross-domain claim well-defined. A speech deployment's domains are acoustic conditions that drift continuously WITHIN a session and carry no label, so there is no clean analogue of the held-out-domain construction and abstraction-vs-specificity cannot be keyed to an observable tag. (b) The directional finding may INVERT for us: the paper's mechanism is that abstraction strips brittle implementation anchors, but if a memory item encodes an acoustic condition (a speaker profile, a noise signature, a device characteristic), high abstraction destroys exactly the condition information a condition-keyed speech control plane needs. Their 'abstraction dictates transferability' is therefore a hypothesis to be re-tested with an opposite plausible outcome, not a result to import. (c) The negative-transfer failure modes are diagnosed by READING the commands the agent executed (e.g. an R-language file-writing routine misapplied in C++); speech has no command log to attribute a failure to a specific memory item, so the same taxonomy cannot be populated the same way. (d) Retrieval queries are model-written plans embedded in a text space; a speech-condition query would need an embedding that separates content from channel, which is not available off the shelf. (e) Favorable: entirely gradient-free, black-box models via API, no logprobs, no hidden states.

**可借鉴**：(1) The abstraction LADDER as an explicit design axis with four concrete instantiations (Trajectory / Workflow / Summary / Insight) — a ready-made memory-representation design space for P3. (2) The ISOLATION experiment for abstraction (S4.3.3): hold the format fixed (Insight only), score each memory by INVERSE INFERENCE — prompt an LLM to reconstruct the source task from the memory and measure similarity to the true task — then split top-30% task-specific vs bottom-30% task-agnostic and compare (Table 4, +1.1% for task-agnostic). This inverse-inference probe is directly reusable as an operational abstraction metric AND, inverted, as a leakage/over-specificity detector for our write-gate. (3) The embedding-geometry diagnostics: t-SNE plus Davies-Bouldin Index and Local Inverse Simpson's Index over memory embeddings, showing benchmark clusters dissolving as abstraction rises (Figs 4-5) — a quantitative way to ask whether our memory is condition-keyed or condition-agnostic. (4) The NEGATIVE-TRANSFER taxonomy derived from cases where zero-shot succeeded but memory-augmented failed: domain-mismatched anchoring, false validation confidence (verification memories creating self-confirming loops), misapplied best-practice transfer. Note the second mode is a direct warning for our evaluation component. (5) The memory-pool scaling study along two axes (pool size at 1/4, 2/4, 3/4, full; number of source domains) as a supply-scaling protocol. (6) Cross-model transfer matrix (Table 6) with the consistent finding that transfer works in both directions but self-generated memory is always best — a portability/bias measurement design. (7) The retrieval-method comparison (Table 7) where LLM reranking (0.598) and adaptive rewriting (0.608) both LOSE to plain embedding similarity (0.630) — a negative result worth citing before we add retrieval sophistication. (8) The memory-efficiency comparison: 431 memories outperform AgentKB's ~5,899 (Table 2).

**备注**：Fully gradient-free. Its central directional claim (higher abstraction transfers better) is the one finding in my read set that I expect to be CONTESTED rather than confirmed under acoustic condition keying — worth designing an explicit test rather than assuming.
