**组级综合（agent 提交）**：SCOPE. Nine papers, read in full text from the provided files; no web queries were issued. Domain split: five text (2603.25158 agentic tool-use, 2604.04373, 2604.04648, 2604.09482, 2604.13552), one text/code (2604.14004), one text/web-agent security (2604.02623), two vision-GUI (2604.06240, 2604.09155). All statements below are scoped to THIS READ SET. No cross-modality effectiveness conclusion is drawn anywhere; every number is a within-domain measurement of the cited paper.

GRADIENT LEDGER. Six of nine are gradient-free end-to-end and are therefore mechanism-eligible as well as design-eligible: Trace2Skill, Decocted, UV, TF-TTCL, MTL, eTAMP. Three fit parameters and are donors for MEASUREMENTS and DESIGNS only: 2604.04648 (predictor network trained by MSE against reward-model hidden states), 2604.09482 (process reward agent fine-tuned on teacher labels; policy never touched), 2604.09155 (Guardian risk head + LoRA Diagnostician; base policy never touched). Note the useful pattern in all three: the gradient is spent on the EXTERNAL module, never on the core — architecturally our shape, which is why their designs transfer even when their mechanisms do not.

WHAT THE DONORS SETTLE.
P9 (admissibility + calibration). Two independently derived and complementary currencies arrive here. CORA supplies the calibrated GATE: a monotone executed-harm loss, tau_hat = sup{tau : (sum L + 1)/(n+1) <= alpha}, leakage-aware disjoint train/cal/test at task and episode level, blockwise splitting for temporal dependence, and an ablation proving no fixed threshold in {0.5..0.9} recovers the CRC operating point. UV supplies the calibrated SIGNAL: FPR-primary reporting, and — more valuable than any single number — the sufficiency criterion that a verifier is good enough when its kappa with humans falls inside the INTER-ANNOTATOR band (UV 0.58/0.43 inside 0.53-0.57 / 0.36-0.45), plus the backbone-upgrade control proving the gain is architectural rather than model-driven. The two are never composed in this read set: CORA assumes the harm label exists for calibration, and UV produces a kappa~0.6 label that is then consumed downstream as if it were an oracle (and is literally the reference label in its own at-scale Table 3). Composing a UV-quality signal with a CORA-style budget, and propagating the verifier's own error into the threshold, is open ground within this set.
P8 (reward-as-controller). 2604.09482 gives the sharpest available isolation: hold the SAME trained scorer fixed and vary only reward LEVEL and TIMING — outcome/post-hoc 75.7, process/post-hoc (min/max/avg) 74.3/77.5/77.6, process/online 81.9 against SC 74.8 at matched 64-sample budget. Read with its untrained-backbone control (restructuring alone merely matches SC), this settles that the leverage is in WHEN the signal is applied, not merely in having a better signal. That is the design our P8 argument should be built on — with the caveat that online step pruning requires prefix-resumable generation.
P10 (reward hacking under selection). 2604.04648 converts hacking from anecdote into a reportable triple — Peak / Final at N=512 / Degradation, with N swept over 2^0..2^9 (GSM8K: RM 79.3/71.5/7.7 vs Pessimism-only 81.3/80.3/1.0; BBH: 17.3/1.7/15.6 vs 22.9/22.1/0.9). Any BoN-shaped selector our program builds should report that triple; this is the clearest BASELINE_ANCHOR in the set. Its mitigation (typicality penalty via inverted RND on reward-model features) needs only the REWARD model's internals, not the policy's — a materially smaller ask than most white-box methods.
P3 (memory keying, write-gates, poisoning). Three donors converge from different directions and produce one genuinely sharp tension. MTL: abstraction dictates transferability, low-abstraction traces induce negative transfer, and abstraction can be MEASURED by inverse inference (reconstruct the source task from the memory; low reconstruction similarity = high abstraction). Trace2Skill: consolidating many traces into one compact artifact beats per-episode retrieval on the same trace pool, and patch value is COMBINATORIAL not additive (greedy selection plateaus below full aggregation). eTAMP: the same raw-trajectory memory that makes retrieval work is the persistent attack surface, triggers key on observable environment features, and agent STRESS multiplies susceptibility up to 8x (GPT-5-mini 3.6%->32.5%). Read together they imply a write-gate axis where raising abstraction simultaneously buys transfer and destroys verbatim payloads — but MTL's own probe warns abstraction erases source-condition information, which for a CONDITION-KEYED speech memory is precisely what we must retain. That abstraction-buys-safety-and-transfer versus abstraction-kills-condition-keys tension is the single most load-bearing constraint this read set hands our P3 design, and it means MTL's directional finding is a hypothesis to re-test under acoustic keying with a plausible inversion, not a result to import.
P1 (supply pricing / VOI). Two formalizations of the same idea, both defining evidence value as CHANGE IN THE EVALUATOR'S UNCERTAINTY rather than as content relevance: PRA's margin shift Delta m = m - m_d thresholded at the median with a swept deployment threshold tracing a cost/accuracy Pareto, and Decocted's Proposition 4.1 (E[tau|x,c] <= (H(Y|x) - I(Y;C=c|x))/h) with the admission that information gain is unavailable before generation, hence the pre-generation proxy Q = Rel + lambda*Div (best lambda=0.6, though only r=0.25 with realized improvement). Decocted additionally contributes the falsification that retrieval relevance rises monotonically with memory size while performance does not — 'more relevant is better' is dead within this read set.

WHAT BREAKS MOVING TO A FROZEN BLACK-BOX SPEECH/OMNI CORE. Four dependencies recur and our contract denies all four in their given form. (1) TOKEN LOG-PROBABILITIES: Decocted's entropy estimator, TF-TTCL's min-PPL positive/negative selection, PRA's margin shift. TF-TTCL is the instructive case — it advertises black-box applicability in its own comparison table while Eq. 5 requires sequence logprobs; if we borrow the loop we must substitute the confidence proxy and the paper offers zero evidence about behavior under substitution. (2) A DEPLOYMENT-TIME CORRECTNESS ORACLE: Trace2Skill's binary trajectory outcomes and its ground-truth-comparing error analyst; caution's binary r*; PRA's exact-match C(y_hat, y); CORA's per-step harm labels for calibration. (3) RE-INSPECTABLE DETERMINISTIC EVIDENCE: Trace2Skill's xlsx artifacts, UV's screenshots ('evidence takes precedence over agent claims' fails when the evidence is only readable through another fallible model), eTAMP's exactly-replayable accessibility trees. (4) AN OBSERVABLE DISCRETE CONDITION KEY: MTL's benchmark identity, eTAMP's URL-substring trigger, CORA's deployment descriptor (app/OS/device/locale) driving weighted CRC. Our condition descriptor is acoustic and ESTIMATED, so importance weights and memory keys inherit estimation error that none of these papers models. Two further structural breaks are worth naming individually: CORA's Goal-Lock depends on the instruction channel being syntactically separable from the observation channel, which collapses when both arrive as audio; and PRA's whole controller depends on prefix-resumable generation that a completed-response API does not offer.

TRANSFERABLE RESIDUE. What survives is not mechanisms but (a) EXPERIMENTAL DESIGNS — the level x timing 2x2 (PRA), Peak/Final/Degradation across swept N (caution), paired memory-on/off improvement statistic (Decocted), matched benign control set with false-alarm decomposed by traffic type (CORA: OIR 9.83% merged = 19.23% harmful + 0.42% benign), UV-blind vs UV-informed annotation with flip-rate, the backbone-upgrade control, the random-context and random-rule nulls (Decocted, TF-TTCL), the untrained-scorer null (PRA), parallel-vs-sequential consolidation under a shared trace pool (Trace2Skill), inverse-inference abstraction probe (MTL); (b) STATISTICS — FPR-primary, kappa-within-inter-annotator-band, executed harm <= alpha, greedy-vs-full aggregation curves, margin shift, Rel + lambda*Div; (c) TAXONOMIES — controllable/uncontrollable (UV), MISUSE/INJECTION/MISBEHAVIOR with ABORT/CONFIRM/REFLECT (CORA), negative-transfer modes (MTL), 7-category/24-subcode failure codes (UV); and (d) STRESS HARNESSES — eTAMP's Chaos Monkey with explicit corruption probabilities plus a compensating step budget, and its ASR_A near-zero premature-trigger control.

HEADROOM ON TOP OF THIS SET (direction-level, scoped to these nine). None of the nine prices its own control-plane overhead against its gain (TF-TTCL's latency table is the closest and it stops at latency). None composes a calibrated verifier with a calibrated gate. None estimates LABEL-FREE HEADROOM — how much accuracy remains reachable under a given signal; Decocted's information gain and PRA's margin shift both measure evidence value, which is a different quantity. None keys a memory or a gate on a descriptor that is ITSELF ESTIMATED rather than observed, which is exactly our deployment situation. And two safety-relevant negatives should be carried forward as constraints rather than as results to beat: eTAMP measures near-zero self-awareness of environmental degradation under clean conditions and only 1.1-7.4% under induced chaos, so 'the system will notice when its conditions degrade' is an assumption this read set actively disconfirms in its own domain; and MTL finds LLM reranking and adaptive memory rewriting both LOSE to plain embedding similarity, so retrieval sophistication is not a free improvement.


## 供体组 3

### 2604.27283 — Learning When to Remember: Risk-Sensitive Contextual Bandits for Abstention-Aware Memory Retrieval in LLM-Based Coding Agents (RSCB-MC)

**域**：text (LLM coding agents / issue memory)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P9 (decision-calibration/admissibility gates - this is literally 'may this retrieved signal steer?'), P3 (condition-keyed memory + read-gate; write-gate only by explicit absence), P8 (a scalar risk-adjusted score picking the next action on a frozen core, gradient-free), stop/abstain calibration (cross-cutting)

**方法面**：Read-time memory GATE, not a retriever. Pipeline (§2.1-2.9): raw debugging context x_t -> deterministic normalization phi -> query profile q_t; retrieval adapter R(q_t,M) proposes candidates C_t; candidates+profile are projected into a FIXED 16-feature state z_t (Table 1: top1/top2 score, score margin, candidate entropy, candidate count, failure-family confidence, entity/command/path/stack signature match, session rejection count, historical acceptance rate, historical FP rate, est. latency, est. token cost, remaining token budget). Action space A (Eq. 11, Table 2) has 7 actions of which 3 do NOT inject: no_memory, abstain, ask_feedback; injecting ones are top1_resolution, top3_summary, high_precision_retrieval, high_recall_retrieval. Decomposed reward (Eq. 12-13): r = 2.0*verified + 1.0*accepted + 0.5*correct_abstain - 4.0*false_positive - 1.0*rejected - 0.001*latency - 0.01*tokens, with the DESIGN INVARIANT gamma>alpha>beta (Eq. 14): a false-positive injection is strictly more harmful than a successful reuse is beneficial. Action score (Eq. 16-17) = empirical action quality + adoption evidence + UCB-style exploration bonus + contextual reward model g_theta MINUS action-level FP evidence, context-conditional FP penalty, contextual risk model h_theta, cost term; argmax then a separate deterministic SafetyOverride stage can replace an unsafe injection with abstain/no_memory (Alg. 1 lines 9-12). §2.7 Decision boundary 2 re-reads the score as the one-step Lagrangian of max R s.t. p_fp<=eps, cost<=b, so mu and lambda_c are risk-budget/cost-budget multipliers. Memory is a three-level pattern-variant-episode schema (§2.2, Eq. 6) so the controller can inspect whether only the symptom matches or also operational signatures. The counter/adoption/FP terms are statistic updates; the fitting procedure for the contextual models g_theta and h_theta (Eq. 17) is NOT SPECIFIED in the paper, so gradient use is unknown rather than absent — treat as UNSTATED for the gradient ledger. Frozen agent - the controller never touches the LLM.【复核更正 wf_40068490】

**自述与显见局限**：Explicitly scoped in §3.1 and §4.8: smoke-scale artifacts only (Table 3: 24 canonical queries, 96 paraphrases, 32 hard negatives, 40 replay events/seed, 16 memory records) against a stated full target 25-40x larger; no hosted LLM, no embedding service, no network - all 'success' labels come from deterministic replay/proxy metadata, not live outcomes (§2.6 explicitly says the FP judgment is supplied by replay metadata); the hot-path validation (§3.9) measures in-process decision latency with the same proxy labels; contextual-bandit myopia - no delayed multi-turn repair effect, no non-stationary memory aging; over-conservative calibration (§3.5: on the 56-case abstention benchmark it answers 0.0% of cases, correctly abstains on 57.1% and WRONGLY abstains on 42.9%); §3.8 admits reward-magnitude sweeps change reported utility but may not change action selection; §4.5 scopes it out of the memory WRITE path entirely - it inherits stale/duplicated/contradictory entries and 'will eventually trust' a wrong fix that got re-validated.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level steps remaining on top: (1) calibrate the safe-reuse/refusal boundary instead of running at the conservative corner - the paper names this as the next step but does not do it; (2) close the read-gate/write-gate loop (it gates reads only, so poisoned writes propagate); (3) replace replay-metadata FP labels with a deployment-available FP proxy - the whole safety claim currently rests on an offline oracle; (4) move from myopic contextual bandit to a formulation that charges delayed damage; (5) make the risk budget eps and cost budget b explicit operating knobs (its own Lagrangian reading invites this but it is not exercised).

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：REQUIRED: the 16-feature state is load-bearing and half of it does not exist for a frozen black-box speech/omni core. Features 8-10 (command/path/stack signature match) are crisp symbolic string-structure matches over code artifacts; the speech analogue - 'is this stored episode about the same acoustic/task condition' - has no deterministic matching semantics and would itself be an estimate keyed on noisy acoustic-condition inference (SNR, speaker, channel, accent), so a mis-key silently corrupts the gate rather than failing loudly. Features 1-4 (top1/top2 score, margin, entropy) assume a scalar retrieval score over a discrete candidate list, which is available, but feature 13 (historical FP rate) and the whole reward (Eq. 12) require a per-decision verdict of 'this injection was causally wrong', which in this paper comes from replay metadata; a black-box speech core gives no such oracle - free-form spoken/transcribed outputs have no exact-match equivalence, so 'verified reuse' and 'false positive' both become estimates and the gamma>alpha>beta invariant would be enforced on estimated labels. No logprobs / no hidden states means the 'confidence' half of the state must come from resampling, not from the core. Finally its sub-millisecond p95 (331 us) budget is irrelevant at speech latency scales, so the cost terms eta*L and lambda*T would need re-derivation.

**可借鉴**：(a) The asymmetric reward design invariant gamma>alpha>beta (Eq. 13-14) as the default contract for ANY admissibility gate in our program; (b) abstain/no-action as first-class ACTIONS in the action set rather than missing predictions (Table 2) - directly instantiable as our stop/abstain decision right; (c) false-positive-injection rate reported as its OWN first-class metric separate from end-task accuracy (§4.6-4.7) - the paper's argument that a bad injection is invisible at the output layer transfers verbatim to memory/tool context in speech; (d) the ablation design that carries the argument: 'no abstention' raises FP 0.0%->17.5% and cumulative reward -31.74->-87.80 (Table 7, Fig. 5) - a template for proving a gate is functional and not cosmetic; (e) the context-budget proxy table (Table 8) that scores modes by risk-adjusted utility, showing no_memory dominates top-1/top-3 injection once FP influence and token cost are charged - a ready-made 'more context is not better' measurement design; (f) separating scoring from a deterministic SafetyOverride stage (Alg. 1 lines 9-12) so the audit can ask why retrieved AND separately why injected; (g) the Lagrangian re-reading (§2.7 Decision boundary 2) letting risk/cost multipliers be budgets rather than free hyperparameters; (h) the pattern-variant-episode schema (§2.2) as a condition-keying design for P3.

**备注**：Single-author, journal-format, self-declared 'empirical engineering' scope. Numbers are NOT baseline anchors for us (smoke-scale, proxy labels, different domain) - the donation is the reward geometry, the action-set shape, the metric choice, and the ablation design. Table 10 (gated object / first-class failure / cost of a wrong decision) is a useful comparison-axis template for our own related-work positioning.

### 2605.01345 — The Perceptual Bandwidth Bottleneck in Vision-Language Models: Active Visual Reasoning via Sequential Experimental Design (S-BOED / FOVEA)

**域**：vision-VLM (high-resolution / gigapixel VQA, tool-integrated agent)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P1 (supply pricing / value-of-information - the rare paper that both formalizes VOI and states its own intractability, then declares a surrogate), P9 (probe validity as an admissibility check on the steering signal), P8 (a scalar surrogate selecting the next observation on a frozen core, gradient-free), budget-allocate (compute-accuracy frontier)

**方法面**：Formalizes evidence acquisition as sequential Bayesian optimal experimental design: latent state theta={location l, semantic identity y}, design d = a crop, EIG(d)=I(z;theta|d), sequential value via a Bellman equation (§3.1 Eq. 4) that is admitted intractable. §3.2 derives a tractable surrogate under three named assumptions - 2.4 Factorised Belief (l independent of y at planning time), 3.2 Calibrated Visibility (H(S|z,d)~0, the model can tell 'blurry/empty' from 'resolved'), 3.3 Ideal Observer / Entropy Collapse (H(y|z,S=1,d)~0) - yielding Proposition 3.5: U_t(d) ~ H_t(y) * J_t(d) where J_t(d) = [integral of belief mass inside the crop] x [resolution probability phi(d)] (Eq. 7-8). Since H_t(y) is design-independent, maximizing task-relevant EIG reduces to maximizing the COVERAGE x RESOLUTION product. §3.1 Remark 3.1 'Information Cliff': constrained active vision is super-additive, not submodular (wide view locates but cannot read; random zoom reads but misses), so greedy is provably not enough and look-ahead is motivated. §3.3 gives the Bayes update including NEGATIVE evidence: a high-resolution crop returning background noise pushes belief mass OUT of the visited region, driving exploration. FOVEA (§4, Alg. 1) is the TRAINING-FREE instantiation: it intercepts the VLM's own crop command, samples a candidate pool around that seed, and scores each candidate INDEPENDENTLY with a binary resolvability probe J_hat(d)=P(r=1|I_d,Q) ~ P(VLM says 'Yes') (Eq. 10), then picks argmax (greedy), MCMC-refines, or scores by simulated next-state value (Lookahead). Frozen backbone, no gradients; history H_t is the search state.

**自述与显见局限**：Stated in §6 Limitations: (1) the Ideal Observer approximation fails in practice - even oracle-level crops still cause backbone recognition/reasoning failures (Fig. 3: FOVEA-Lookahead 54.7% vs ReAct 45.1% on the remote-sensing subset, with a residual gap to the oracle-crop baseline attributed to backbone errors, i.e. acquisition and recognition are distinct bottlenecks); (2) PROPOSAL-LIMITED cold start - if the relevant region never enters the candidate pool, neither local refinement nor look-ahead can recover it (Appendix H.2); (3) probing and search add inference-time overhead, so FOVEA is a compute-accuracy frontier, not a fixed-cost policy (Fig. 4: accuracy rises monotonically with budget within each policy family from ~0.5M to 5M tokens). Also, the probe is explicitly declared NOT an estimator of information gain, only an empirical surrogate (§4.1, Appendix F). The paper itself lists 'Adaptive Invocation' as future work: it currently refines EVERY crop call and has no meta-policy deciding when active foveation is worth invoking.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level: (1) the missing meta-gate - deciding WHETHER to spend on acquisition at all (the paper names it as future work under 'value of information'); (2) uncertainty calibration - the whole derivation leans on Assumption 3.2 that the model can self-report resolvability, which is only spot-checked; (3) cold-start proposal generation independent of the backbone's own (noisy) prior; (4) closing the acquisition-vs-recognition attribution loop so budget goes to the bottleneck that is actually binding on the current item.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：REQUIRED: the tractable objective is IRREDUCIBLY SPATIAL. J_t(d)=coverage x resolution prices WHERE to look in a 2D field where a crop is a cheap, re-issuable, deterministic re-read of a STATIC artifact at higher fidelity. A frozen black-box speech/omni core observes a waveform: re-listening returns the same signal, so there is no 'higher resolution' to buy - the 1D analogue would be temporal segmentation / re-representation (window choice, enhancement front-end, ASR-view vs audio-view), which changes the REPRESENTATION not the acquired evidence, so the coverage-resolution product has no direct speech instantiation and the paper supplies none. Assumption 3.3 (entropy collapses once resolved) is even weaker in audio: a clean segment does not collapse uncertainty about speaker intent/emotion/overlap. The resolvability probe is a single Yes/No forward pass validated (Appendix F) against HUMAN-ANNOTATED oracle regions and a hit-centre indicator - our deployment has neither an annotated oracle span nor a hit indicator, and free-form speech outputs give no exact-match downstream correctness to correlate against, so the probe-validity protocol transfers only if we manufacture a scoped oracle subset. No logprobs is fine here (the probe is a text 'Yes'), but Eq. 10's P(r=1) is approximated by the verbalized answer, which is itself a black-box read-out with no calibration guarantee.

**可借鉴**：(a) Appendix F.1 PROBE-VALIDITY PROTOCOL - the single most reusable artifact: score oracle vs distractor vs random candidates, report mean+-sd (0.633+-0.427 / 0.187+-0.310 / 0.187+-0.351), Cohen's d = 1.22 for oracle-vs-distractor, and Spearman rho of the probe score against both a grounding indicator (0.538) and downstream correctness (0.392), with the explicit falsification argument 'if this were generic answerability confidence, oracle and distractor would score alike'. This is exactly the discipline our observation-supply/reward probes need; (b) Appendix F.2 SELECTOR ABLATION on a FIXED candidate pool (3x3 grid) comparing Probe / VLM-direct-joint-choice / Random (QA 30% / 20% / 10%; hit-centre 52/34/12; IoU 0.260/0.188/0.061) - isolates the SCORING mechanism from candidate generation, a design we can copy directly for select-vs-supply attribution; (c) Appendix G.1 EVIDENCE-ONLY ABLATION: hold the observation sequence fixed and delete the intermediate reasoning trace (66.0% -> 48.0%), attributing the gap to integration rather than acquisition - a clean way to separate 'what was observed' from 'what was carried'; (d) Remark 3.1 the information-cliff / super-additivity argument as the formal reason a greedy one-step supply policy is insufficient; (e) §3.3 negative-evidence belief update (an uninformative high-fidelity look must push mass away) - a principled way to price re-observation without an oracle; (f) Fig. 4 compute-accuracy frontier reported WITHIN each policy family by varying that family's own budget knob.

**备注**：ICML-format, training-free, frozen Qwen3-VL-30B/8B backbones. Numbers (77.7% mean vs ReAct 75.1% vs Direct 73.3% on the 30B block) are in-domain vision anchors and MUST NOT be read as cross-modality effectiveness (H5 withheld). The load-bearing donation is the assumption ladder + the two appendix diagnostics, not the crop mechanics.

### 2605.03231 — cotomi Act: Learning to Automate Work by Watching You

**域**：vision-GUI / web (browser computer-using agent; AXTree-text observations + screenshots), system demo paper　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P1 (lazy/on-demand observation supply as a priced act), P3 (external memory with human-in-the-loop write curation; artifact typing), P5 (skill/script induction from traces with format-abstraction ladder), P6 (system composition: scaffold x knowledge as separable, separately measured layers), P8 (best-of-N action selection on a frozen core, gradient-free but vote-based not reward-based)

**方法面**：Two coupled subsystems over a frozen reasoning LLM. (1) Agent scaffold (§2.1): ReAct loop with four inference-time mechanisms - ADAPTIVE LAZY OBSERVATION (send only viewport-visible elements, let the agent REQUEST the full accessibility tree on demand; comparable accuracy at 471s -> 184s per task, ~2.6x faster); VERBAL-DIFF history compression (natural-language change descriptions e.g. "New element: [42] button 'Submit'" used both to enrich the current observation, +12.7pp to 53.3% from a 40.6% full-AXTree baseline, and to REPLACE stale history, +1.1pp accuracy at 3.8x lower per-step tokens, 23.9K -> 6.3K); COARSE-GRAINED actions (scrollInto(element) instead of scrollBy(x,y)); TASK DECOMPOSITION into parallel sub-agents when the model itself emits a multi-goal plan. Test-time scaling (Appendix A.1) = BEST-OF-N ACTION SELECTION: sample N<=5 candidate actions per step and take the MAJORITY-VOTED action BEFORE execution - explicitly 'pre-execution consensus' with no post-action verifier. (2) Behavior-to-knowledge pipeline (§2.2): passive capture of site-level actions/tab transitions/periodic screenshots with diff-based dedup, then an LLM-based agentic ETL in three stages - segment raw events into episodes by inactivity timeout, summarize each into a structured record + activity class, aggregate into typed artifacts (task board, wiki, activity timeline) in a SHARED workspace that the human can edit; the agent reads them only on demand via a search_workspace tool it decides to invoke. Bidirectional curation: the agent proposes, the user approves (Fig. 3). No gradients anywhere at inference; the ETL uses an LLM as annotator/abstractor.

**自述与显见局限**：Stated: the behavioral-knowledge study (§3.2, Appendix B.2) uses SUCCESSFUL AGENT TRAJECTORIES on WorkArena-L1 as a PROXY for observed user behavior, and the paper concedes real user traces would contain failures, interruptions and exploratory detours so 'the pipeline's robustness to such noise remains to be validated in a full deployment study'. WebArena's automatic scorer was found to produce false positives/negatives, so three annotators manually verified every judgment (§3.1) - i.e. the headline number depends on a human-corrected scorer. Best-of-N here is majority vote over sampled actions, not reward-guided selection, and N is capped at ~5 for latency. The knowledge-format result is non-monotone: raw trajectories DEGRADE performance at low coverage (-2pp at 17%) before becoming strongest at high coverage; scripts/insights are stable. Poisoning defense is weak-by-design: frequency weighting plus human curation (Appendix B.2), no automated write-gate. Privacy/PII handling is policy, not mechanism. It is a 7-page demo paper - no per-mechanism statistical testing on the headline WebArena numbers.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level: (1) an actual WRITE-GATE - the pipeline admits artifacts by frequency + human approval, so credit assignment from outcome to artifact is absent; (2) selection is majority vote, not reward-guided - swapping in an external reward and measuring the delta against this exact scaffold is a clean open step; (3) the lazy-observation trigger is model-discretion ('request the full tree on demand') with no pricing of that request - VOI-style pricing is unclaimed here; (4) demonstrating the pipeline on NOISY traces (failures/detours) is explicitly left open; (5) no measurement of artifact staleness/retirement.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：REQUIRED: every one of the four scaffold mechanisms depends on the observation being a re-queryable, diffable, symbolically-addressable STRUCTURE. 'Lazy observation' is only meaningful because acquiring more is a discrete, priced, re-issuable act (ask for the rest of the AXTree); a speech/omni core's observation is a waveform already fully delivered - there is no withheld remainder to request, so the acquisition action must be redefined as re-representation (re-decode, enhance, re-segment) with an entirely different cost/benefit shape. Verbal-diff compression presumes a DOM/AXTree state whose delta between steps is computable and small (their own measurement elsewhere in this read set: >50% of within-page steps have zero change); consecutive audio turns share no such addressable node identity, and a 'verbal diff' of two acoustic states is a generated description, i.e. a new error source rather than a deterministic delta. Best-of-N action selection requires an EXACT-MATCH equivalence over candidate actions so that a majority can be counted - free-form speech-conditioned outputs have no such equivalence, and the paper's whole variance-reduction argument evaporates without it. WorkArena/WebArena scoring is a programmatic (here human-corrected) task oracle; deployment on speech has no deterministic oracle, so the coverage ladder cannot be replicated without constructing one. Finally the 80.4%-vs-78.2%-human framing is a WebArena-specific claim and carries no cross-modality implication.

**可借鉴**：(a) DOMAIN-COVERAGE LADDER protocol (§3.2, Appendix B.3-B.4): vary the fraction of task categories whose derived knowledge is available from 0% to 100% in whole-category increments (no sub-sampling), average each point over SIX random category orderings to control ordering effects, and report the change in success rate relative to the zero-coverage baseline - a directly reusable design for measuring whether external memory actually causes gains in our program; (b) the three KNOWLEDGE-FORMAT arms (trajectory / script / insight = progressive abstraction levels) crossed with coverage, which is what exposes the non-monotone result that raw traces hurt when sparse - a template for our memory-representation ablation; (c) the retrieval discipline: artifacts are NOT in the initial prompt, they are pulled on demand by the agent's own tool call, 'matching the deployed system's retrieval behavior' (Appendix A.2/B.3); (d) the end-to-end causal episode (§3.2, Table 2): before catalog-ordering scripts ~75%, after 48 scripts ~99.2% averaged over six orderings, per-ordering breakdown given - a model for how to evidence 'this artifact caused this gain'; (e) the observation/history design trajectory plotted in accuracy-vs-token-cost space (Fig. 2) with the finding that current-observation and history axes PARTIALLY SUBSTITUTE (combined +13.8pp < sum of individual +22.1pp) - a genuinely reusable warning about additivity when composing supply mechanisms; (f) boundary-object framing (§5): artifacts as first-class human-readable applications rather than agent-internal memory, giving transparency by construction.

**备注**：CAIS'26 demo paper (7 pages). Its WebArena numbers (base 76.5% -> +TTS 80.4% on the 179-task human subset; 74.4 -> 75.7 on all 812) are a vision-GUI BASELINE ANCHOR for GUI work only, not for us; I record it as PROTOCOL_DONOR because what our program can actually reuse is the coverage-ladder + format-ablation + substitution-effect measurement design.

### 2605.04236 — Adaptive Consensus in LLM Ensembles via Sequential Evidence Accumulation: Automatic Budget Identification and Calibrated Commit Signals (DASE)

**域**：text (LLM ensembles, GPQA / AIME)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P9 (decision calibration + label-free-ish headroom via commit-type routing; coverage-matched comparison of competing admissibility signals), stop/abstain/budget calibration (adaptive stopping identifies the productive budget automatically), P10 (inverted-U: injection-based deliberation DEGRADES past ~30 inferences while independent self-consistency rises monotonically to 70)

**方法面**：A stopping+routing heuristic for iterative ensemble deliberation over FROZEN endpoints (Table 1: 3xQwen3-next-80b + 2xLlama-3.3-70b at the '70B' tier; 3xgpt-oss-120b + 2xQwen3-80b at '120B'). Protocol (§3): round 1 independent at tau=0.2; from round 2 each worker receives ONLY the extracted consensus answers (~15 tokens) at tau=0.4 and is asked to verify and correct, with a prompt (Appendix H.3) that explicitly instructs searching the minority view for calculation errors/sign flips. DASE-Spatial is an arena/drift-diffusion stopping rule: an agent at position x in [-W,+W] starting at 0; a heuristic evidence score g_t (Appendix H.1 Eq. 1, a regularized-incomplete-beta ratio over counts r of n workers with alpha_t = eps_t/n, w=0.2) drives two terminal values V_R = g_t - c_x(W-x_t) and V_L = (1-g_t) - c_x(x_t+W) with c_x=0.01; move one step toward the higher-value wall, wait if both <=0, COMMIT ONLY ON WALL CONTACT. Right-wall = consensus commit (return current plurality); left-wall = global-frequency fallback; ANY problem not reaching the right wall is flagged for escalation/human review. W controls deliberation depth. Borrowed structurally from a POMDP 2AFC evidence-accumulation model (Appendix H: dual terminal boundaries, collapsing threshold, hesitation region), with the paper conceding worker independence is violated from round 2 so the belief transition is intractable and all claims are empirical. No gradients, no training, no verifier/reward model.

**自述与显见局限**：§7 Limitations is unusually candid: the Opus-confidence comparison covers AIME 2010-2023 only and the 37% disagreement rate needs replication on GPQA; per-benchmark W preferences were NOT pre-registered (marked [expl.] in Table 2); the 120B bandwidth ablation is SINGLE-SEED and a 3.7pp round-1 accuracy difference between the sparse and dense runs partially confounds the overall comparison; L=15 runway never ablated; the 70B/4k -> 120B/8k shift confounds token budget with model capability; PRM-guided search comparisons outstanding; ~6x latency overhead makes it unsuitable for interactive use at the 120B tier. §6 'What DASE does not establish': on GPQA it only TIES Debate-Dense at its optimal budget (70.0 vs 69.4, adj p=0.34) and recently released single-call systems substantially exceed it in absolute accuracy - the contribution is declared structural. The inverted-U claim is explicitly labeled retrospective and hypothesis-generating. An implementation bug (g_t=0.5 default) was found and corrected by replaying cached logs (footnote 3).

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level: (1) the paper itself identifies the obvious next system - a TWO-TIER router that dispatches when EITHER the consensus partition or self-reported confidence signals high and escalates only when BOTH signal low, since the two are statistically equivalent in gap but disagree on 37% of items; nobody in this read set builds it; (2) pre-registered W selection / a rule for choosing deliberation depth from observable item features instead of per-benchmark tuning; (3) an anytime-valid error guarantee on the commit (which is exactly what 2605.05873 supplies and DASE lacks - DASE's partition is heuristic with no Type-I control); (4) reconciling the inverted-U into a stopping criterion rather than an observation.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：REQUIRED: the evidence score g_t is computed from COUNTS r of workers agreeing on an extracted answer - it requires (i) an ensemble of exchangeable workers and (ii) an exact-match answer-extraction/equality relation to count agreement. For a single frozen black-box speech/omni core, 'workers' must be resamples of one model (breaking the heterogeneity effect the paper reports as superadditive with injection, adj p=0.003), and 'agreement' over free-form speech-conditioned outputs is an ESTIMATED equivalence, so the plurality and hence g_t are themselves noisy - the drift dynamics would be driven by an estimator's error, not by evidence. The routing-gap statistic that carries the paper's value is measured as right-wall vs left-wall ACCURACY, which requires a ground-truth oracle (GPQA multiple choice / AIME integer answers); deployment on speech has no such oracle, so the gap can only be measured on a scoped labelled subset and never in the field. The left-wall interpretation ('36-42% ~ random chance for 4-choice MC, so the ensemble has no signal') depends entirely on a known chance level - free-form speech outputs have no chance baseline. The injection protocol re-feeds ~15 tokens of consensus text into each worker's prompt; for an omni core this is a text-channel intervention on an audio-conditioned generation, an untested composition. No logprobs are needed (a plus), but the comparison arm (verbalized confidence 0-99) presumes reliable self-report.

**可借鉴**：(a) The ROUTING-PARTITION GAP as a label-free-at-deployment / label-checked-offline headroom statistic: report coverage and accuracy per commit type and the gap between them (GPQA 70B W=4: right 81.1% n=380 at 70% coverage vs left 41.5% n=142, 39.5pp gap; W=8 widens to 45.8pp at lower 61% coverage; AIME 120B W=2: 98.3% vs 72.8%, 25.5pp) with 95% bootstrap CIs - this is the cleanest available template for evaluating a commit/abstain signal as a ROUTER rather than as an accuracy delta; (b) the COVERAGE-MATCHED EQUIVALENCE TEST against a rival signal: threshold Opus verbalized confidence at conf>=97 to match DASE's coverage, compare gaps (25.5 vs 25.7pp), bootstrap 95% CI on the gap DIFFERENCE [-12.0,+10.3] p=0.873, then separately report per-problem disagreement (37%, McNemar p=1.000) to argue complementarity rather than substitution - directly reusable for comparing any two of our admissibility signals; (c) the S1 baseline column (round-1-only consensus already separates 73.2 vs 35.9) which quantifies how much of the partition is free vs bought by deliberation (+7.9pp on the right wall); (d) the BANDWIDTH-vs-STOPPING DECOMPOSITION: construct Debate-Sparse to be byte-for-byte identical to DASE's round-2+ prompt so the ONLY difference is the stopping rule, making the residual attributable to stopping alone (AIME: bandwidth +0.3pp ns, stopping +6.0pp; GPQA: bandwidth +4.4pp, stopping +5.0pp) - an exemplary causal-isolation design; (e) the finding that DENSE injection improves overall accuracy (+1.3pp) while HALVING right-wall coverage and narrowing the gap 30.7->18.9pp (Table 4) - i.e. an intervention can improve the metric while destroying the routing signal, a direct warning for P10; (f) parameter-sensitivity discipline (Appendix I: 60 combinations of w x c_x, accuracy varies <=1.7pp, W ranking preserved in all); (g) 'DASE as an audit instrument' (§6): a machine-readable deliberation record per query vs an opaque confidence scalar.

**备注**：Independent single-author preprint; the author flags an earlier implementation bug and re-reports corrected values. Its accuracies are NOT anchors for our program (different modality, oracle-bearing benchmarks), but the inverted-U observation (all injection-based methods peak near 30 inferences and decay; SC is the exception) is the strongest structural claim in this read set about selection pressure, and it is labeled hypothesis-generating by the author.

### 2605.04531 — Reward-Guided Semantic Evolution for Test-time Adaptive Object Detection (RGSE)

**域**：vision (open-vocabulary detection, Grounding DINO test-time adaptation)　**供体级别**：CONSTRUCT_DONOR　**喂给**：P8 (a literal reward-as-controller instance: a scalar reward selects and weights the next control state with NO parameter update - but see transfer boundary, it is white-box), P9 (beat-the-parent survival filter as an admissibility gate), P3 (bounded, similarity-replaced, condition-agnostic memory + running-average global bank)

**方法面**：Training-free, BACKPROPAGATION-FREE test-time adaptation that treats TEXT-EMBEDDING adaptation as evolutionary search over a frozen Grounding DINO. Four forward-pass stages per test image (§III, Alg. 1): (1) PERTURBATION - compute an activation score s_k = max over proposals of p_init[k] (Eq. 3), keep only 'active' classes with s_k > tau_base=0.7, and for each generate N=1000 offspring t~_k^n = t_k + eps, eps~N(0,sigma^2 I), sigma=0.1 (Eq. 4); inactive classes are left untouched. (2) REWARD EVALUATION - r_k^n (Eq. 5) is a weighted sum of cosine similarities between the perturbed text embedding and (i) the CURRENT image's visual embeddings of proposals pseudo-labeled k and (ii) a per-class memory bank of historically high-confidence visual embeddings, weighted by alpha=0.2. (3) REFINEMENT - compute the PARENT's own reward r_k^base and DISCARD every offspring with r <= r_base (survival filtering), then fuse the survivors plus the parent by softmax-of-reward weighting (Eq. 6); a global embedding bank keeps a running average across images (Eq. 7) with fallback to the original embedding when a class has never been updated. (4) MEMORY UPDATE - a fixed-capacity per-class queue (M_max=2) with similarity-based replacement: a new proposal replaces the least-aligned existing entry only if it is more aligned with the current global text embedding (Eq. 9). Fully forward-pass, batch size 1, streaming/online.

**自述与显见局限**：The paper states none in a limitations section (IEEE-journal format ends at Conclusion). Read out of the evidence: the reward is SELF-REFERENTIAL - it is cosine similarity to the model's own confident proposals and its own historical high-confidence proposals, so it can only re-weight information the model already had (no new information enters); pseudo-labels drive both the reward and the memory update, so drift is possible and is not measured; Table V shows memory gains saturate hard (M_max 0->2 gives 49.24->50.97 but 2->3 only +0.11 and 3->4 only +0.07) while GPU memory grows linearly per class, and the authors concede this becomes prohibitive with hundreds/thousands of open-vocabulary categories; Table IV shows N=1000 -> N=1500 yields only +0.15 mAP50 for more time/memory; the activation threshold gates adaptation entirely to classes the frozen model is ALREADY confident about, so a class the model never proposes is never adapted; all evidence is corruption-benchmark (COCO-C/PASCAL-C/FoggyCityscapes) with fixed hyperparameters, no real-world stream, no failure analysis of adaptation collapse.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level: (1) the reward is a pure read-out of the model's own confidence - injecting an EXTERNAL signal (any evidence the core did not already produce) is the obvious open step and would change the character of the method entirely; (2) no gate on WHETHER to adapt this image at all (it adapts on every frame with an active class); (3) no detection or rollback of adaptation drift over a long stream; (4) memory keying is per-class only, not per-CONDITION - the same class under two different corruptions shares one bank; (5) the survival filter is a binary better-than-parent test, not a risk-calibrated admissibility gate.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：REQUIRED: this is training-free but it is NOT black-box. It reads and WRITES the model's internal text embeddings t_k and reads proposal visual embeddings v_m - both are prohibited by our black-box contract (no weights, no hidden states). Under our contract there is no t_k to perturb and no v_m to compute cosine against, so the mechanism does not transfer at all; only the CONTROL SHAPE (perturb -> score -> filter -> reward-weighted fuse -> bounded memory) survives, and it would have to act on a prompt/context object rather than an embedding, where 'Gaussian perturbation' has no meaning and cosine reward has no analogue. Second boundary: the reward requires a dense, cheap, differentiable-free similarity between a candidate control object and an observation embedding; for a speech core the only comparable quantity would be an external encoder's similarity, i.e. a NEW component with its own condition sensitivity, not a read-out of the core. Third: activation gating by max-proposal-confidence presumes calibrated per-class scores; a black-box speech core gives no guaranteed logprobs and free-form outputs have no per-class score vector. Fourth: the evaluation regime is keyed to synthetic image corruptions with a per-image ground-truth oracle (mAP50); the acoustic analogue (SNR/reverb/accent) has no per-utterance detection oracle and free-form outputs give no exact-match scoring, so neither the reward nor the evaluation carries over.

**可借鉴**：(a) SURVIVAL FILTERING as an admissibility gate: score the incumbent ('parent') under the SAME reward as the candidates and admit only candidates that strictly beat the incumbent (§III Refinement) - the component ablation (Table VI) shows filtering is the largest single-component gain (37.95 search-only -> 40.06); this is a clean, cheap, gradient-free admissibility primitive for P9/P8; (b) the SEARCH-ONLY BASELINE construction (§IV-C): a baseline that is explicitly NOT the zero-shot model but the same 1000-candidate perturb-and-fuse pipeline WITHOUT filtering, history, or the global bank - the right way to show the gain comes from the gates rather than from the search itself; (c) the full 2^3 component ablation grid over {history, filtering, global bank} showing complementary rather than redundant roles (Table VI: 37.95 / 39.22 / 40.06 / 39.91 / 42.89 / 42.44 / 43.05 / 43.86); (d) BOUNDED memory with similarity-based REPLACEMENT (Eq. 9) rather than unbounded accumulation, plus the honest capacity ablation showing saturation at M_max=2 and linear memory cost per class (Table V) - directly relevant to our external-memory sizing; (e) the efficiency-vs-quality table (Table IV: GDINO 147.6ms/3025MB at 42.91 -> RGSE N=1000 182.4ms/3823MB at 50.97, vs backprop-based HisTPT at 1240.8ms/12850MB) as a template for arguing a gradient-free controller is cheap; (f) hyperparameter-sensitivity sweep over tau_base, alpha, sigma reported as stability evidence (Fig. 3).

**备注**：IEEE journal submission. I downgrade to CONSTRUCT_DONOR because the mechanism violates our black-box contract (internal text/visual embeddings) even though it is gradient-free; its mAP numbers are in-domain vision anchors only. Its most valuable donation for us is arguably NEGATIVE and matches a known program failure mode: the reward is a self-referential read-out of the core's own confidence, so its gains cannot be read as evidence that an external reward would help - flagging this explicitly protects us from a false-gain lever.

### 2605.05701 — Inference-Time Budget Control for LLM Search Agents (task-level VOI controller + evidence-grounded finalizer)

**域**：text (multi-hop QA search agents; HotpotQA / 2WikiMultihopQA / MuSiQue / Bamboogle)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P1 (supply/acquisition pricing - the most direct occupant: value per unit budget with an explicit budget-dependent penalty), budget-allocate + stop/commit (the ANSWER action is the commit decision), P9 (safe-set + gain-risk admissibility gate on the repair action; harm-constrained objective), P8 (a scalar score choosing the next action on a frozen core, training-free, gradient-free)

**方法面**：Training-free two-stage controller layered on a tree-search backbone over a frozen LLM, under DUAL HARD budgets B=(B_tool, B_tok). §3 formalizes: max E[R(a_hat,y)] s.t. C_tool<=B_tool, C_tok<=B_tok, AND E[L_harm]<=rho_harm where L_harm(a_hat,a_base,y)=max{0, R(a_base,y)-R(a_hat,y)} - i.e. the damage from replacing the trajectory answer with a worse one is a first-class constraint. STAGE 1 (§4.1): at each step, for each feasible action k in {SEARCH, DECOMPOSE, ANSWER}, form utility u_t(k) = critic-derived progress signal + structural signals Psi_t (bridge structure, loop pressure, readiness, early-answer risk) - budget-dependent penalty Pi_t(k;b_t) (positive for SEARCH/DECOMPOSE under tight budgets, a tractable proxy for the oracle budget shadow-cost lambda*'g_t(k)); normalize to value-per-budget r_t(k)=[u_t(k)]_+/(d_t(k;b_t)+eps) - THE 'task-level VOI score', explicitly declared NOT Shannon information gain, NOT Bayesian posterior value, NOT a learned value model (Def. 4.1); then apply a deterministic guard operator G_t (premature-answer suppression, factoid-decomposition suppression, minimum-search enforcement on compositional cases) to get the executable score, argmax. Costs are charged with a PRE-EXECUTION estimated charge for selection and the REALIZED cost debited after execution. STAGE 2 (§4.2): after search, extract a base answer and construct a refined candidate from the same trace (no new tool call, no extra LLM call); build a consistency feature vector z (support type, slot type, contradiction indicators, unresolved bridge/comparative reasoning); accept the rewrite only if z is in a SAFE SET S_safe AND F(z)=G(z)-eta*H(z)>=tau, else keep the base answer. S_safe explicitly EXCLUDES unresolved bridge structure, unresolved comparative semantics, and missing direct support. Appendix C derives the gain-risk threshold from the harm-constrained objective and gives a ranking-consistency/one-step value-gap result under a margin condition, while disclaiming global optimality.

**自述与显见局限**：§6: budget-aware search is NOT a monotone scaling problem - larger budgets can introduce redundant search and noisier finalization, and on 2Wiki/Bamboogle MIDDLE budgets sometimes beat HIGH. The main boundary is high-budget regimes and backbone sensitivity: BATS stays competitive in several upper-budget cells, AFlow leads on 2Wiki lower-mid, and Qwen3.5-122B is a 'more mixed regime' relegated to an appendix as a backbone-sensitivity case; the paper explicitly refuses a dominance claim (§5.2: 'the main claim is therefore not that VOI wins every cell'). The finalizer is exactness-oriented, not reasoning-complete: it cannot recover from wrong retrieval paths or unresolved bridge relations, which is why it contributes ~0 on HotpotQA and least on MuSiQue. The budgets evaluated are tiny ((1,100) to (3,500) tool-calls/tokens). Stage-1 coefficients are fixed constants over hand-specified trajectory features - no principled derivation of the feature weights.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level: (1) the VOI score is admittedly an operational proxy with fixed coefficients - deriving or calibrating it against a measurable notion of information value is open; (2) the harm constraint rho_harm is formalized but never swept/audited as an operating knob; (3) guards are hand-written deterministic rules (premature-answer suppression etc.) - inducing them from traces is unclaimed; (4) the finalizer never abstains from ANSWERING, only from rewriting - a genuine abstain/escalate right is absent; (5) no calibration of the pre-execution cost estimate against realized cost.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：REQUIRED: the action menu {SEARCH, DECOMPOSE, ANSWER} presupposes a retrievable external text corpus with a discrete, re-issuable, priced retrieval act; for a frozen black-box speech/omni core the observation is a waveform already in hand, so 'SEARCH' has no direct analogue - the speech action set (re-listen with a different front-end, re-decode, request a different view, ask a clarifying question) has different cost structure and, critically, re-observation returns correlated rather than fresh evidence, which breaks the marginal-value-per-budget accounting. The structural signals Psi_t (bridge structure, comparative semantics, typed slots, alias completion) are multi-hop-QA-specific textual constructs with crisp detectors; speech tasks have no bridge-entity structure and the S_safe conditions cannot be evaluated. Both R(a,y) (EM / token-F1) and L_harm require a GOLD answer with exact-match equivalence - a frozen speech core producing free-form output has neither, so the harm constraint cannot be measured in deployment and the entire Stage-2 gain (13.4-41.9% of total on three benchmarks) is defined only against an oracle. The hard dual-budget audit counts tokens, which for an omni core mixes audio-token and text-token accounting with no established exchange rate. No logprobs are required (a plus) - the critic signal is prompt-level.

**可借鉴**：(a) The HARD DUAL-BUDGET AUDIT protocol (§5.1): score every method under the same tool-call AND output-token caps, and count ANY example exceeding EITHER constraint as FAILED - this is a far stricter and more honest budget contract than average-cost reporting and should be our default; (b) the four-level budget LADDER ((1,100)/(2,200)/(2,300)/(3,500)) crossed with 4 benchmarks x 3 backbones, reported as per-cell win counts (best-F1 in 7/16 cells, improving over four baselines in 14/16, 10/16, 16/16, 15/16) rather than a single aggregate - a template for claiming a positive aggregate WITHOUT a dominance claim; (c) the STAGE-1 COMPONENT ABLATION (Table 1: w/o penalty, w/o normalization, w/o structural signals, w/o guards) identifying the budget-dependent penalty as the largest single contributor - i.e. the remaining-budget term is load-bearing, not cosmetic; (d) the TWO-STAGE GAIN DECOMPOSITION (Fig. 4) reporting each stage's share of the total relative gain (Stage 2 contributes 0% on HotpotQA but 13.4% / 41.9% / 27.8% on 2Wiki / MuSiQue / Bamboogle) - the right way to report a composite system; (e) the L_harm formalization: an explicit constraint on the expected DAMAGE of a repair action, with the repair gated by a SAFE SET plus a gain-minus-eta*risk threshold and abstention as the default - directly instantiable as our repair decision right; (f) the wall-clock timing probe (Table 2) showing the added controller REDUCES mean latency 20.91s->15.23s (-27.2%) by shortening trajectories - the argument that a good controller can be latency-negative; (g) the pre-execution-estimate / post-execution-debit accounting discipline.

**备注**：36-page preprint with an appendix of formal support (ranking consistency under a margin condition, one-step value-gap bound) that explicitly disclaims global optimality. Numbers are text-domain and must not be read cross-modality. Pairs naturally with 2605.17609 (ADAP): this paper's VOI is an engineered heuristic with an audit; ADAP gives the theory of the same trade-off with a competitive-ratio guarantee - together they bracket P1.

### 2605.05873 — CITE: Anytime-Valid Statistical Inference in LLM Self-Consistency (Certification by Intersection-union Testing with E-processes)

**域**：text / statistics (LLM self-consistency stopping; stat.ML)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P9 (admissibility / when is evidence sufficient to commit - the rigor ceiling for this slot), stop/abstain/budget calibration (an anytime-valid stopping rule with a category-size-free rate and matching minimax lower bound), P8 (a purely external, gradient-free statistic that decides the stop action on a frozen core), P10 (indirectly: the mode-vs-correctness distinction is precisely the Goodhart gap - certifying the mode says nothing about correctness)

**方法面**：A sequential test certifying that a PRE-SPECIFIED target label r is the UNIQUE MODE of a frozen model's response distribution, valid under ARBITRARY data-driven stopping and WITHOUT knowing the category set. Setup (§2): i.i.d. draws X_1,X_2,... from an unknown categorical P over a countable, possibly INFINITE and unknown A; H_0: p_r <= sup_{a!=r} p_a vs H_1: strict dominance; anytime-valid Type-I control means sup_{P in H0} P(tau<infinity) <= eps. Three time-uniform processes (§3): (i) PAIRWISE e-process by testing-by-betting on Z_i = 1{X_i=r}-1{X_i=a}, E_t^{(r,a)}(lambda)=(1+lambda)^{N_t(r)}(1-lambda)^{N_t(a)}, mixed over a finite grid of lambda and evaluated against the EMPIRICAL RUNNER-UP (Def. 3.1); (ii) a time-uniform LOWER CONFIDENCE BOUND L_t on p_r from a mixture e-process on 1{X_i=r}-q (Def. 3.3, Prop. 3.4); (iii) an UNSEEN-CATEGORY upper bound U_t = min{u: u^{-1}(1-u)^t <= eps/3} covering all never-observed categories simultaneously (Def. 3.5, Prop. 3.6) - this is what defends against a hidden competitor that has not appeared yet. Stopping rule (Def. 3.7): stop at the first t where BOTH E_t >= 3/eps AND L_t > U_t; union bound over the three eps/3 components gives Theorem 4.1. Theorem 4.3: E[tau] = O((log(1/eps)+log(1/delta))/delta^2 + (log(1/eps)+log(1/p_r))/p_r), FREE of |A|, with delta = p_r - p_(2) the modal gap; Theorem 4.4 gives a matching minimax lower bound c*log(1/eps)*(p/delta^2 + 1/p). §5 extends to confidence-WEIGHTED voting (W-CITE) certifying argmax of mu_a = E[W_i 1{X_i=a}] with the same rate. Entirely external to the model - no gradients, no internals, only observed answer labels (and optionally a scalar weight per sample).

**自述与显见局限**：Explicit in the Limitations paragraph: CITE certifies that a label is the unique MODE of the response distribution, which is LOGICALLY DISTINCT from answer CORRECTNESS - the two compose and neither subsumes the other, so a downstream system must supply a separate correctness signal. The target r must be fixed A PRIORI, independent of the data (§2.2); certifying the empirically observed top category is left OPEN and would require a Bonferroni-type correction over the random sequence of empirical leaders, with the authors stating it is unknown whether the category-size-free rate survives. Assumption 2.1 requires i.i.d. draws. In the very small p_r regime the upper bound is looser than the lower bound in the pairwise term (gap left open). Power is a real cost: on near-tie (Setting 3) and very diffuse (Setting 4) simulations ALL methods remain low-power within the evaluated budget; the fixed-sample KR baseline often has HIGHER finite-sample power (CITE's certification rate is lower by up to 0.087) - CITE's advantage is validity under adaptive stopping, not raw power.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level: (1) data-adaptive target selection (certify the current empirical leader) is the single named open problem and is exactly what a deployed controller needs; (2) relaxing i.i.d. - real controllers resample under changing context/prompt, breaking exchangeability; (3) composing the mode certificate with a correctness signal (the paper says they compose but supplies no composition); (4) tightening the small-p_r regime; (5) power in near-tie regimes, where the certificate simply never fires and the system must fall back to some other rule that the paper does not specify.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：REQUIRED: the entire machinery is defined over a CATEGORICAL answer identity - N_t(a) counts exact repetitions of a label, and the unseen-category bound U_t counts categories never observed. A frozen black-box speech/omni core emits free-form outputs (spoken or transcribed) for which 'X_i = a' is not observable: equivalence between two responses is itself an ESTIMATE (paraphrase, disfluency, alternate transcription), so every count feeding E_t, L_t and U_t is corrupted by clustering error, and the Type-I guarantee - the paper's whole contribution - is only as valid as that equivalence relation. Worse, the unseen-category term is the component most sensitive to this: an over-merging clusterer hides competitors, an under-merging one inflates the apparent category count and blocks certification forever. The i.i.d. assumption is additionally strained under acoustic-condition keying: resamples of one utterance are exchangeable only if the sampling context is held fixed, and any adaptive control (changing the prompt, front-end, or context between draws) voids it. W-CITE's weights W_i in [0,1] are described as coming from logits or a verifier - our black-box contract gives no guaranteed logprobs, so the weight would have to be an external judge score with its own bias, which the guarantee does not model. Finally the target must be fixed a priori, but a deployed speech controller wants to certify whatever candidate is currently leading - precisely the case the paper leaves open.

**可借鉴**：(a) The DEFINITION of the guarantee: anytime-valid Type-I control of FALSE CERTIFICATION at a prescribed level eps, uniformly over all data-driven stopping times (Def. 2.3, Thm 4.1) - this is the strongest formal statement of 'when is accumulated evidence sufficient to commit' available to our program, and it is exactly the level of rigor P9 should aspire to; (b) the THREE-COMPONENT decomposition with an eps/3 union bound (target-beats-runner-up / target-is-large / unseen-mass-is-small) as a reusable architecture for any commit certificate - notably the explicit UNSEEN-MASS term, i.e. budgeting for hypotheses the sampler has not yet produced, which nothing else in this read set does; (c) the e-process / testing-by-betting construction itself (Eq. 3-5) - closed-form in the counts only, so it costs nothing to run alongside any sampler; (d) the EVALUATION PROTOCOL: set the target to the empirical RUNNER-UP to measure the false-certification rate under the null (max observed 0.052 across all methods/problems/budgets, at most 0.012 on every other model-dataset pair, vs nominal 0.05) - a directly reusable null-calibration design for our own gates; (e) reporting accuracy against MEAN STOPPING TIME for sequential methods vs prefix N for fixed-sample baselines on the same axis (Fig. 2), including the striking diagnostic that Bonferroni saturates at 0.111 certification for N>=256 while CITE reaches >=0.98 at mean tau ~ 12; (f) the component-level simulation check that empirical stopping-time scaling matches the two theoretical terms (tau_lu * p_r stays in 14-20 as p_r varies; tau_lu ~ 70 constant as delta varies) - a template for validating that a stopping rule behaves as its theory predicts; (g) the honest separation of validity from power (KR has more power but is invalid under adaptive stopping).

**备注**：stat.ML, theory-first, with LLM experiments on Qwen3-30B and gpt-oss-20b over AIME 2026 / FrontierScience-Olympiad / ER-REASON. The mode-vs-correctness distinction in the Limitations is the single most important sentence for us: a certificate over a proxy quantity is not a certificate over the thing we care about - which is the honest framing of every reward-guided commit our program will build.

### 2605.07134 — Region4Web: Rethinking Observation Space Granularity for Web Agents (+ PageDigest)

**域**：vision-GUI / web agents (AXTree-based observation; text-serialized), with TRAINED components　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P1 (supply pricing: granularity IS the price/informativeness knob, and re-observation frequency is a priced decision justified by measured environment stability), P3 (persistent per-region purpose summaries carried across steps = condition-keyed working memory with a deterministic update rule), P6 (system composition: an observation-space-only layer that leaves the actor policy unmodified and is therefore composable with SteP/AgentOccam)

**方法面**：Argues observation granularity is a design choice separate from action granularity, and moves observation from element-level to FUNCTIONAL-REGION level. §2 Preliminary analysis on Mind2Web (2,350 tasks, 137 sites, ~2,473 DOM nodes/page, 15,394 consecutive action pairs of which 78.0% are same-page) gives two measurements that motivate the design: (§2.1) consecutive action pairs have a median LCA-depth ratio of 0.48 vs a random baseline of 0.22, with 81.7% above the random median - i.e. consecutive actions are structurally co-located, so a region is a natural observation unit; (§2.2) 52.9% of within-page steps produce ZERO DOM change and 74.4% under 5%, so reconstructing the observation every step is wasteful. Region4Web (§3) reduces region partitioning to per-edge merge/cut classification over the AXTree in a SINGLE bottom-up traversal where a node's representation is computed only after its children's boundary decisions resolve (Eq. 1-2), then a fine-tuned small LM emits per-region (purpose, state summary) - purpose = what the region is FOR (stable across steps), state summary = its current actionable context. Both components are TRAINED (gradient use: an EdgeClassifier + RegionEncoder, and a fine-tuned SLM, supervised by gpt-5-mini annotations over 21,974 pages from 253 Tranco sites). PageDigest (§4) is the inference pipeline: on entering a page, the ACTOR's own backbone LLM selects task-relevant regions from the abstractions; selected regions are exposed as full AXTree subtrees (preserving element-level action granularity) while non-selected regions are represented by purpose alone; within a page, transitions are tracked DETERMINISTICALLY by diffing the current AXTree against its state at page entry (removed/modified nodes edit in place, ADDED nodes are listed as a separate group so they cannot silently shift an existing region's purpose); a view_all fallback action reveals everything for the rest of the page.

**自述与显见局限**：Appendix A: quality depends on how completely each page exposes accessibility semantics - canvas-rendered or non-semantic markup gives weak structural cues; evaluation is confined to WebArena because its AXTree fidelity is consistent, with live-web validation (Online-Mind2Web) left as future work. Results are mixed at the domain level even where the average improves (Table 1: GPT-5.1 overall 45.2->47.5 but CMS 57.1->54.5 and Reddit 65.1->60.5 both DROP; SteP overall 39.5->38.7, a net regression; AgentOccam Reddit 73.7->68.4 and GitLab 66.7->50.0 drop sharply while Map 11.5->23.1 rises). The pipeline adds auxiliary inference (region selection ~19.5% and view_all ~6.6% of task-total tokens). The trained annotator is a proprietary model, so the artifact inherits its judgments.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level: (1) the trained components are exactly what our contract forbids - a training-free region/segment inducer would be the transferable version; (2) region SELECTION is delegated to the actor backbone and is the largest in-pipeline error source (10.0% of failures) - a pricing/VOI-based selector is unclaimed here; (3) no per-region value estimate: regions are selected by relevance judgment, not by expected information gain against cost; (4) the paper suggests but does not do cross-modality generalization ('organizing observation by shared functional purpose generalizes across modalities' - asserted, not shown); (5) transition tracking is purely deterministic diffing and cannot handle environments where state is not addressable.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：REQUIRED: everything rests on a STRUCTURED, PERSISTENTLY-ADDRESSABLE observation. The whole method needs a tree with stable node identifiers (Appendix D: persistent backendDOMNodeId/bid that survives same-page mutations) so that boundaries can be classified once and diffs computed deterministically; a frozen black-box speech/omni core observes a waveform with NO node identity, so both the region partition and the zero-cost transition tracking have no analogue - the closest speech construct (temporal/speaker/topic segmentation) is itself an estimate that changes between passes, so 'diff against the state at entry' becomes a comparison of two noisy segmentations rather than a deterministic delta. The two motivating measurements do not transfer either: 'consecutive actions are structurally co-located' presumes a spatial/DOM hierarchy, and '52.9% of steps have zero change' presumes an environment that is static unless acted upon - an audio stream changes continuously and is never zero-change, which inverts the paper's central efficiency argument. Region PURPOSE is assumed stable across steps within a page; the acoustic analogue (condition, speaker, channel) drifts continuously and would need condition-keyed re-estimation. Finally the pipeline requires re-serializing a full observation on demand (view_all), i.e. a withheld remainder that can be revealed - a speech core has already delivered the whole signal. Trained components (EdgeClassifier, fine-tuned SLM) violate our training-free contract outright; they are donors for their MEASUREMENTS and DESIGNS only.

**可借鉴**：(a) The DESIGN-QUESTION-FIRST measurement pattern (§2): before proposing a mechanism, measure the environment property that would justify it - here the LCA-depth-ratio distribution against a random baseline (median 0.48 vs 0.22) and the step-to-step change-ratio distribution (52.9% zero change) - and derive the mechanism (what unit to observe; whether to re-observe at all) from those two numbers. This is the cleanest instance in this read set of grounding an observation-supply policy in measured environment structure rather than intuition; (b) joint reporting of SUCCESS RATE and OBSERVATION LENGTH with an explicit delta column (Table 1: -30% to -54% tokens across six actor configurations) so the compression/quality trade is visible per configuration; (c) the ABLATION isolating granularity from compaction (Table 2 on WebArena-Lite: backbone 48.5% at 5,410 tokens; +Region4Web alone 50.3% at comparable length; an ELEMENT-LEVEL variant of the same pipeline 46.1% at 4,013 - i.e. the same compaction at the wrong granularity HURTS; full PageDigest 53.9% at 3,814) - the element-level control arm is the load-bearing comparison; (d) STEP-SCALE vs TASK-SCALE accounting (Fig. 5) including the auxiliary overhead the method itself introduces, with the decomposition actor-observation 73.9% / region-selection 19.5% / view_all 6.6% and invocation counts (region selection 4.8x per task, view_all in only 38.1% of tasks, mean 0.64 calls) - an honest total-cost protocol we should copy; (e) the FAILURE-MODE ATTRIBUTION protocol (§5.3, Fig. 6): sample 50 failed trajectories stratified by domain, trace each to its triggering step, and label EVERY pipeline stage under multi-cause attribution, yielding decomposition 2.0% / abstraction 2.0% / region selection 10.0% / transition management 0% / actor action-selection 90.0% / environment 16.0% - i.e. 82% backbone vs 8% pipeline regression. This is the strongest per-component blame-assignment design in this read set; (f) the view_all FALLBACK as a designed escape hatch when the compressed observation is insufficient, with its usage rate reported as evidence the compression is rarely binding; (g) the additive-group rule for new content (added nodes kept separate so they cannot shift an existing region's purpose) as a memory/context write discipline.

**备注**：GRADIENT USE FLAGGED: two trained components (edge classifier + fine-tuned SLM, annotated by gpt-5-mini over 21,974 pages). Donor for measurements and designs only, never for its mechanism. Domain-level regressions in Table 1 are worth remembering as a caution about aggregate-only reporting - the paper reports them openly.

### 2605.17609 — Adaptive Generate-Rank-Verify: Inference-Time Search with Costly Verification (ADAP)

**域**：text / learning theory (math reasoning HMMT, competitive programming LiveCodeBench)　**供体级别**：CONSTRUCT_DONOR　**喂给**：P1 (supply/acquisition pricing with verification priced explicitly; the break-even threshold), budget-allocate + stop (shellwise dyadic escalation as a budget schedule), P10 (the centered-star-number lower bound: structural assumptions relating proxy score to verified success are NECESSARY, not optional), P9 (monotonicity of h* as the admissibility precondition on any steering signal)

**方法面**：Formalizes the cheap-reward + expensive-verifier pipeline as GENERATIVE ACTIVE SEARCH: a cost-sensitive FIRST-POSITIVE search. Per prompt x the generator+reward model induce two unknown objects (Def. 2.1): D_x, the distribution of reward scores, and h*_x(r)=Pr(verifier accepts | score=r), the score-conditioned success function. A sound policy (Def. 2.2) alternates GENERATE (draw a response, observe its reward score, pay c_rew) and VERIFY (query the verifier on one pooled candidate, pay c_ver), stopping only on a positive label; total cost J=c_rew*N_rew + c_ver*N_ver; crucially the policy 'does not inspect the contents of unverified responses except through their reward scores'. §3 Theorem 3.2 characterizes the DISTRIBUTION-AWARE OPTIMUM: with (D,h*) known the optimal policy is a single THRESHOLD rule - verify iff h*(r) > tau*, where tau* solves c_ver*E[max{h*(R)-tau*,0}] = tau*c_rew, and J* = c_ver/tau*. §4 ADAP (Alg. 1) is the online version: it does not know D or h*, and searches DYADIC (generation, verification) scales by COST SHELL - shell s collects pairs (a,b) whose cost c_rew*2^b + c_ver*2^{b-a} lies within a factor 2 of 2^s*c_min; at each shell it draws m_s new candidates into a persistent pool, re-ranks the WHOLE pool by reward, and verifies the top k_s, removing every queried candidate. Theorem 4.2: under Assumption 4.1 (h* NON-DECREASING in the reward score - rank monotonicity, NOT calibration) ADAP is sound and E[J] <= 400*J*, uniformly over all feasible (D,h*). §6 shows this inductive bias is NECESSARY: via the CENTERED STAR NUMBER s_0(H) (Def. 6.1), Theorem 6.2 proves every sound policy can be forced to pay (1/4)*min{s_0, c_ver/c_rew}*J*, with a matching upper bound O(min{s_0, c_ver/c_rew}) (Thm 6.3). No gradients; the frozen generator and reward model are used only as a sampler and a ranker.

**自述与显见局限**：§7 lists them: the guarantee REQUIRES monotonicity; the evaluation covers ONLY FEASIBLE prompts (problems with at least one correct sample among 512 - 22 of 60 HMMT problems and 83 LiveCodeBench problems survive this filter, so the hard tail is excluded by construction); costs are fixed scalars rather than length-dependent; decisions use reward scores ALONE while in practice a verifier could exploit response content directly (e.g. detecting syntactic invalidity without invoking the costly checker). The constant in Theorem 4.2 is 400 - a constant-factor guarantee that is loose in practice. The empirical gap to the retrospective lower bound is large: ADAP costs 1422.3 vs SampleAware 398.9 on math (x0.28) and 745.3 vs 179.9 on coding (x0.24). DAP10 (an ORACLE difficulty-stratified baseline using per-problem pass rates) actually BEATS ADAP on math (x0.82) and essentially ties on coding (x1.06), so ADAP's advantage over difficulty-adaptive policies holds only when the number of difficulty classes is small.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level: (1) using response CONTENT, not just its scalar score, to price verification (the paper names this); (2) variable / length-dependent costs; (3) multiple reward models and parallel generate-verify; (4) richer inductive biases between the raw-monotonicity assumption and the unstructured worst case - the star-number bound says the space between these is where all the leverage lives; (5) handling INFEASIBLE prompts, i.e. an abstain/give-up rule, which is entirely absent (the policy is defined to stop only on a positive label, so on an infeasible prompt cost is +infinity).

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：REQUIRED: the framework rests on an EXPENSIVE BUT CORRECT verifier - exact answer-string matching against a published boxed answer, or execution against the full hidden test suite where a sample counts only if every test passes. A frozen black-box speech/omni core has no such oracle at deployment: any 'verifier' we could afford is itself a judge with error, so the Bernoulli(h*(R)) label model is violated (labels become noisy AND correlated with the same failure modes as the reward), the stopping condition 'stop on the first positive' is unsound, and both the threshold theorem and the competitive ratio lose their meaning. Second, free-form speech-conditioned outputs have no exact-match equivalence, so 'a positive' is not even well defined without a scoring convention that is itself an estimator. Third, Assumption 4.1 (rank monotonicity of verified success in the reward score) is exactly what an acoustic-condition-keyed proxy reward can violate: a reward that is systematically higher under clean audio and lower under noise induces a non-monotone or condition-dependent h*, and the paper's own lower bound then says NO sound policy can be competitive - so this is not a mild assumption for us, it is the crux. Fourth, the cost model c_rew (generate+score) vs c_ver assumes the two are separable and repeatable; for an omni core, re-generation on the same waveform yields correlated candidates, so the i.i.d. sampling from pi(.|x) that underwrites the success-mass argument is strained. No logprobs are needed (reward is an external model), which is compatible with our contract.

**可借鉴**：(a) The THRESHOLD CHARACTERIZATION (Thm 3.2): the optimal verify/discard rule is 'verify iff the conditional success probability exceeds a single break-even threshold tau* balancing immediate success against the continuation value of drawing again', with J*=c_ver/tau* - the cleanest formal statement of the supply/verification trade our P1 needs; (b) the NECESSITY result: without structure on the reward-verifier relationship the worst-case adaptivity gap is Omega(min{s_0, c_ver/c_rew}) (Thm 6.2) - this is the strongest available formal framing for P10, because it says reward-guided selection cannot be competitive unless the proxy score is structurally tied to verified success, turning 'reward hacking' from an empirical nuisance into a stated precondition; (c) the REWARD-SIGNAL VALIDATION protocol (Appendix A.2), which we can run without any theory: rank samples by reward and plot empirical correctness at each rank position (must be monotonically decreasing); plot top-k coverage (probability the top-k-by-reward set contains a positive) against a hypergeometric random-k baseline; and report the PER-PROMPT AUC_p = Pr(R > R' | V=1, V'=0) distribution across problems to check the signal is consistent and not just good on average. The paper is explicit that only RANK monotonicity is needed, not calibration - a very useful weakening for a black-box setting; (d) the two-sided baseline construction: SampleAware, a RETROSPECTIVE per-trial lower bound that picks the cheapest (N_rew,N_ver) that would have worked on that exact trial (strictly stronger than any realizable policy), and DAP_k, an ORACLE difficulty-stratified policy that partitions problems by empirical pass rate into k classes by dynamic programming - bracketing the achievable range from below and from the 'learned difficulty predictor' side; (e) the COST-MATCHED comparison UniformC_ADAP: the best fixed policy whose cost does not exceed the adaptive policy's mean cost, which succeeds on only 84% (math) and 88% (coding) of trials - the right way to state 'adaptivity buys reliability at equal budget'; (f) the per-problem optimal-(N_rew,N_ver) scatter with iso-cost contours (Fig. 1) as the visual argument that no fixed budget can serve all items; (g) cost-ratio robustness sweep over c_ver/c_rew in {1,10,20,30}.

**备注**：Theory paper with a small empirical section; c_rew=1, c_ver=10, 10 permutations per (problem,policy) pair with matched permutations for paired comparison. I mark CONSTRUCT_DONOR rather than PROTOCOL_DONOR because the algorithm cannot run without a correct verifier - what transfers is the formalism (active search, break-even threshold, star-number necessity) plus the Appendix A.2 reward-validation diagnostics, which ARE directly runnable on any scoped subset where we can afford a reference. Its numbers are text-domain anchors only.

**组级综合（agent 提交）**：SCOPE: nine papers, all read in fulltext; claims below are scoped to THIS read set. No web queries were made. Domain mix: 4 text (2604.27283, 2605.04236, 2605.05701, 2605.05873), 2 vision-VLM/detection (2605.01345, 2605.04531), 2 vision-GUI/web (2605.03231, 2605.07134), 1 theory-with-text-experiments (2605.17609).
