Owner directive honoured: everything below is a METHOD/measurement donation, never a cross-modality effectiveness claim.

WHAT THE DONORS SETTLE.

(1) The commit/stop slot (P9 + stop-abstain) is now covered at THREE distinct rigor levels, and this read set makes the ladder explicit. CITE (2605.05873) is the ceiling: an anytime-valid certificate with false-certification controlled at a prescribed eps uniformly over all data-driven stopping times, a category-size-free stopping rate, and a matching minimax lower bound - plus, uniquely in this set, an UNSEEN-MASS term that budgets for hypotheses the sampler has not yet produced. ADAP (2605.17609) is the middle rung: no error certificate, but a competitive-ratio guarantee (400x) against a distribution-aware optimum whose form is a single break-even threshold tau* solving c_ver*E[max{h*(R)-tau*,0}]=tau*c_rew. DASE (2605.04236) is the empirical rung: no guarantee at all, but the best MEASUREMENT of a commit signal's value - the coverage/accuracy routing partition. Our program should treat these as three separable deliverables (certificate / competitive ratio / routing gap), not as competitors.

(2) The supply slot (P1) is genuinely crowded, and the four occupants price four DIFFERENT things: FOVEA prices WHERE to look (coverage x resolution over a 2D field); VOI-search (2605.05701) prices WHICH ACTION to spend the next budget unit on from a discrete menu under dual hard caps; cotomi Act prices WHETHER to acquire more of an available-but-withheld observation (lazy observation, +2.6x speed at comparable accuracy); Region4Web prices GRANULARITY and RE-OBSERVATION FREQUENCY, and is the only one that derives its policy from measured environment structure (median LCA-depth 0.48 vs random 0.22; 52.9% of within-page steps have zero DOM change). ADAP supplies the formal statement all four gesture at. Taken together this read set does NOT leave P1 open at the level of 'is observation supply a priced decision' - it is thoroughly occupied. What it leaves open is the speech instantiation (see below).

(3) The admissibility slot has two clean donors with the same shape: RSCB-MC's read-gate (asymmetric reward gamma>alpha>beta making a false-positive injection strictly worse than a missed reuse, abstention as a first-class ACTION, false-positive-injection rate reported as its own metric) and VOI-search's repair-gate (harm constraint E[L_harm]<=rho_harm, a SAFE SET excluding structurally-unresolved cases, and a gain-minus-eta*risk threshold with keep-the-incumbent as default). RGSE contributes a third, cheaper primitive: score the incumbent under the SAME reward as the candidates and admit only strict improvements. All three are gradient-free and instantiable on a frozen core.

(4) P10 gets its two strongest framings from this set, one empirical and one formal. Empirical: DASE's inverted-U - every injection-based deliberation method peaks near ~30 inferences and DECAYS thereafter, while independent self-consistency rises monotonically through 70, plus the sharper Table-4 result that dense injection IMPROVES overall accuracy (+1.3pp) while HALVING right-wall coverage and collapsing the routing gap 30.7->18.9pp, i.e. an intervention can improve the headline metric while destroying the signal you actually deploy. Formal: ADAP's centered-star-number lower bound, which says that without a structural tie between proxy score and verified success, NO sound policy is competitive - reward hacking is a stated precondition, not a nuisance. CITE adds the third framing: certifying the MODE is logically distinct from certifying CORRECTNESS, which is the Goodhart gap stated as a theorem-adjacent scoping remark.

WHAT TRANSFERS (highest-value, protocol-level, ranked).
(a) FOVEA Appendix F.1/F.2 probe-validity + selector ablation - oracle vs distractor vs random scoring with Cohen's d (1.22) and rank correlations against BOTH grounding and downstream correctness, plus a fixed-candidate-pool selector comparison that isolates scoring from candidate generation. This is the discipline every probe/reward we build should pass.
(b) DASE's coverage-matched gap-equivalence test - compare two admissibility signals at MATCHED coverage, bootstrap the CI on the gap DIFFERENCE, then report per-item disagreement separately (25.5 vs 25.7pp, p=0.873, 37% disagree) to argue complementarity rather than substitution.
(c) VOI-search's hard dual-budget audit - any example exceeding EITHER cap counts as FAILED, evaluated across a 4-level budget ladder x 4 tasks x 3 backbones with per-cell win counts and an explicit refusal of a dominance claim.
(d) ADAP Appendix A.2 reward-signal validation - rank-vs-correctness monotonicity, top-k coverage against a hypergeometric baseline, and the per-prompt AUC_p distribution; explicitly requires only RANK monotonicity, not calibration, which is exactly the weakening a black-box setting needs.
(e) Region4Web §5.3 failure-mode attribution - 50 stratified failures, traced to the triggering step, with EVERY pipeline stage labelled under multi-cause attribution (82% backbone vs 8% pipeline). The best blame-assignment design here.
(f) RSCB-MC's ablation logic - removing the abstention action raises false positives 0.0%->17.5% and collapses cumulative reward, proving a gate is functional rather than cosmetic; plus its context-budget table showing no-memory dominates injection once FP influence and token cost are charged.
(g) cotomi Act's coverage ladder (0->100% in whole-category steps, six random orderings, three abstraction formats) with its non-monotone finding that raw trajectories HURT at low coverage.
(h) DASE's bandwidth-vs-stopping isolation (a baseline byte-for-byte identical in prompt, differing ONLY in the stopping rule) and CITE's null-calibration trick (set the target to the empirical RUNNER-UP to measure false certification against nominal eps).

WHAT BREAKS AT THE SPEECH/OMNI BOUNDARY (the common failure, stated once).
Two structural dependencies run through eight of the nine papers. FIRST, a DISCRETE ANSWER IDENTITY: CITE counts N_t(a) and bounds unseen categories; DASE counts plurality across workers; cotomi Act majority-votes candidate actions; ADAP stops on the first verifier positive; RSCB-MC and VOI-search both score against EM/F1 or replay labels. For a frozen black-box speech/omni core, response equality is itself an ESTIMATE - and CITE's unseen-mass term is the most sensitive component of all: an over-merging clusterer hides competitors while an under-merging one blocks certification permanently. The composition gap this read set does NOT close is a commit certificate defined over an ESTIMATED equivalence relation with the estimator's error carried into the guarantee. SECOND, a DETERMINISTIC ORACLE: ADAP needs an expensive-but-CORRECT verifier (hidden tests, boxed answers), DASE and CITE need gold labels to measure their gaps, VOI-search needs gold to evaluate L_harm, RSCB-MC's 0.0% false-positive claim comes from replay metadata, FOVEA's probe validity comes from human-annotated oracle regions. None of these exists in speech deployment; every one of these protocols is therefore runnable only on a scoped labelled subset, never in the field.

Two further boundaries are specific and worth keeping separate. RE-OBSERVATION: the vision/GUI supply donors all assume a static artifact with a WITHHELD REMAINDER that can be cheaply and deterministically re-acquired at higher fidelity (crop, view_all, request-full-AXTree). A waveform is already fully delivered; re-listening returns the same signal, so 'value of information' collapses into 'value of re-REPRESENTATION' (front-end, segmentation, decode view), which changes the cost/benefit shape and invalidates the coverage-x-resolution product outright. Region4Web's motivating measurement actually INVERTS in audio: 52.9% zero-change steps is what justifies caching the observation, but a stream is never zero-change. CONDITION KEYING: RSCB-MC's structural compatibility features (command/path/stack signature match) are crisp symbolic matches; the speech analogue - same acoustic/task condition - has no deterministic matching semantics, so a mis-key corrupts the gate silently rather than failing loudly. This is the sharpest unclaimed slot in P3 across this read set: every memory donor here keys on class (RGSE), symptom-pattern (RSCB-MC), page URL (Region4Web) or task category (cotomi Act); none keys on a continuously-drifting, estimated deployment condition.

CONTRACT VIOLATIONS FLAGGED. RGSE (2605.04531) is training-free and backprop-free but NOT black-box: it perturbs internal text embeddings and reads proposal visual embeddings, so only its control SHAPE (perturb -> score -> beat-the-parent filter -> reward-weighted fuse -> bounded memory) transfers, not its mechanism - and its reward is a self-referential read-out of the core's own confidence, so its gains carry no implication that an EXTERNAL reward would help (a false-gain lever we should name explicitly). Region4Web (2605.07134) trains an edge classifier and fine-tunes an SLM on proprietary-model annotations - donor for measurements and designs only. cotomi Act's best-of-N is majority vote over sampled actions, not reward-guided selection, so it is a variance-reduction result, not evidence about reward-as-controller.

NET DIRECTION-LEVEL RESIDUAL FOR OUR PROGRAM (scoped to this read set): the individual slots P1/P8/P9 are well occupied by strong donors, but three compositions are unoccupied here - (i) a commit/stop certificate whose guarantee survives an ESTIMATED output-equivalence relation; (ii) a supply policy for an already-delivered observation where the priced act is re-representation rather than acquisition; (iii) condition-keyed memory admission where the key is a drifting estimate rather than a symbol. Notably, DASE names the two-tier either/both router as the obvious next system and does not build it, CITE names data-adaptive target certification as OPEN, FOVEA names the adaptive-invocation meta-gate as future work, and ADAP names content-aware (rather than score-only) verification pricing as future work - four independent papers pointing at the same missing layer: a gate that decides whether to invoke the machinery at all.


## 供体组 4

### 2604.14853 — Adaptive Test-Time Compute Allocation for Reasoning LLMs via Constrained Policy Optimization (AdaCompute / SOLVE-THEN-LEARN)

**域**：text / LLM (math reasoning: MATH, GSM8K)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P1 supply pricing / value-of-information (the dual variable IS the price); budget-allocate decision right; stop/abstain/budget calibration (cross-cutting); secondarily P6 (route-then-execute composition).

**方法面**：Formalizes 'which inputs deserve more compute' as max_pi E[Acc(x,pi(x))] s.t. E[C(pi(x))] <= B over a discrete budget set B={1,2,4,8,16} where each b is an inference operating mode (self-consistency with b samples) (§2). Lagrangian relaxation decouples the globally coupled constraint into per-instance sub-problems with closed-form oracle b*(x;lambda)=argmax_b[Acc(x,b) - lambda*C(b)] (Def.1, §3.1); lambda is explicitly a shadow price of compute. Theorem 1 (§3.2): the induced average cost C_bar(lambda) is non-increasing in lambda under a deterministic tie-break, so binary search on lambda exactly targets any B in O(NK log 1/eps). Proposition 1 (§3.3): zero duality gap for the empirical finite program, so the oracle labels solve the original constrained problem. Learn stage (§4.1-4.2): a GBM (XGBoost) classifier over 16 cheap pre-inference features (length/word/sentence counts, question marks, numbers, fractions, multi-step keywords, plus a normalized entropy estimate from ONE single-pass LLM call) is trained by cross-entropy on the oracle labels; deploy = route then execute once at the predicted budget, <1% routing overhead (§4.3). Theorem 2 (§4.4): Reg_lambda(pi_hat) <= E[M_lambda(x)*1{pi_hat != pi*}] <= M_bar * P(pi_hat != pi*), i.e. task regret <= imitation error x worst-case per-instance gap; Theorem 3: near-feasibility E[C(pi_hat)] <= B + eps*(C(b_K)-C(b_1)) and near-optimality V(B') - E[Acc] <= M_bar*eps. The LLM itself is frozen and only sampled (black-box w.r.t. the core); gradients exist only in the small external classifier. Results (§5.2, Table 1): 3 LLMs (DeepSeek-V3, GPT-4o-mini, Qwen2.5-7B) x MATH/GSM8K; at B=3.0 on MATH, +5.8/+5.2/+6.4 pp over Fixed-b, tracking the Lagrangian oracle within 0.4-1.4 pp with 91-99% imitation accuracy. Ablation (Table 2): linear/MLP classifiers collapse to b=1 for nearly every item (realized cost ~1.0) while tree models match the oracle cost distribution (~2.17) — realized cost is used as a degeneracy diagnostic. Allocation pattern is an inverted U over difficulty: b=1 for both easy and intractable, high budget concentrated on the responsive middle (§5.6).

**自述与显见局限**：Stated (§6): budgets are discrete; the Acc(x,b) utility table must be pre-computed offline at N x K model evaluations; no online oracle adaptation; single-objective (no joint latency/throughput constraint); the residual oracle gap is attributed to difficulty features being cheap but weak. Not framed as limitations but load-bearing: Acc(x,b) is estimated by partitioning 48 sampled responses into non-overlapping windows and majority-voting, which presumes an exact-match answer and a gold label per instance; only 200 questions per dataset; the only non-trivial heuristic baseline is prompt-length ranking; the classifier is a fitted component, so the pipeline as a whole is not training-free even though the LLM is untouched.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level steps remaining on top of this paper: (a) constructing the utility table without per-instance gold labels — every guarantee here is stated against oracle labels whose construction is label-supervised, and label noise is not in the Theorem-2 bound; (b) budgets as a heterogeneous acquisition vector (re-listen, tool call, extra candidate) rather than a scalar sample count, which changes C(.) from a scalar to a priced menu; (c) online re-targeting of lambda under drift, since C_bar(lambda) is fit once offline; (d) features drawn from the controller's own cheap observations rather than prompt surface statistics.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：The oracle label y* requires Acc(x,b) for every (instance, budget) pair, obtained here by exact-match verification of majority votes against gold answers. A frozen black-box speech/omni core producing free-form speech-conditioned output has no deterministic oracle and no exact-match equivalence class, so the table can only be filled with a noisy proxy reward; that error enters the labels, and Theorem 2 bounds imitation error against the labels, not label error against truth — the guarantee does not cover our dominant error source. 'Self-consistency accuracy at budget b' presupposes answer equivalence classes that free-form speech output lacks. The cheap features are lexical/structural properties of a text prompt; the speech analogue would have to be computed on waveform observations (duration, SNR, speaker turns, channel) whose difficulty-predictiveness is not evidence in this paper. Finally the deployed policy is a trained classifier: under our training-free contract it can only be scoped as an external non-core artifact, and the paper's own ablation shows the routing quality is exactly what the fitted non-linearity buys (linear models degenerate).

**可借鉴**：Def.1 oracle allocation with lambda as an explicit unit price of compute (§3.1); Theorem 1 cost-monotonicity + binary-search budget targeting (§3.2) — a directly reusable way to hit an exact average budget without a learned controller; Proposition 1 strong duality (§3.3); Theorems 2-3 regret decomposition and primal recovery (§4.4) as the template for 'how good must my router be for the system-level number to hold'; the four-baseline suite as our required comparator set (Lagrangian Oracle upper bound / Fixed-b / Random matched on E[b] / difficulty heuristic); reporting REALIZED cost alongside accuracy in every cell (Table 1) so a method cannot win by quietly overspending; the classifier-architecture ablation using average realized cost as a degeneracy detector (Table 2); the inverted-U allocation heatmap (Fig.5, §5.6) as a sanity check that a budget controller spends on the responsive middle band rather than the easy or hopeless ends.

**备注**：Frozen-core-compatible in the sense that the LLM is only sampled; gradient use is confined to the external GBM/MLP classifier. Effect sizes are text-domain reference points, not cross-modality claims.

### 2604.16453 — Sampling for Quality: Training-Free Reward-Guided LLM Decoding via Sequential Monte Carlo

**域**：text / LLM (code: HumanEval; math: MATH500; science MCQ: GPQA diamond)　**供体级别**：CONSTRUCT_DONOR　**喂给**：P8 reward-as-controller (gradient-free multi-step steering of a frozen generator — the purest instance in this read set); selection + budget (particle count N, rollout count J, MH steps S are explicit budget knobs); P1 (lookahead rollouts are priced observations).

**方法面**：Defines a reward-augmented target over COMPLETE sequences, Pi(x_1:T|q) proportional to prod_t m_t(x_t|q,x_<t) * prod_t psi_t(x_1:t,q) — a Feynman-Kac model with the LM inducing the Markov process and reward potentials acting as multiplicative potentials (Eq.1, §1). Weights are left unchanged; all gain comes from inference-time sampling. Two intermediate-target families (§3.3): prefix-only gamma^prf (Lemma 1) and lookahead gamma^look equal to the EXACT marginals of the full path measure via a twisting function L_t (Lemma 2, Eqs.9-12); L_t is estimated online by J Monte Carlo rollouts (Eqs.13-17) so no value function is trained — this is what keeps the method training-free. Algorithm 1 (§3.4) is block-wise resample-move SMC: N=16 particles, ESS-threshold resampling, incremental block weights computed from reward potentials only under a tempered-base proposal (Target I prefix-only — the paper notes these weights do NOT depend on base-model probabilities), followed by selective Metropolis-Hastings rejuvenation applied only to DUPLICATED particles whose reward falls below tau_R, where the MH acceptance ratio (Alg.1 line 22) uses Target II lookahead and does involve p(x_t|q,x_<t)^alpha and the tempered proposal density. Rewards are task verifiers: process reward models (Act-X, ThinkPRM-1.5B) for MATH500/GPQA; unit-test execution with 5 s timeout plus an auxiliary syntax reward (weight 0.3) for HumanEval. Block size B=512 for math/science (PRMs need long continuous chains before scoring is meaningful), B=64 for code (finer resampling enables early course correction). Results (§4): HumanEval pass@1 0.878 / 0.854 / 0.781 on Qwen2.5-7B / Qwen2.5-Math-7B / DeepSeek-Math-7B, up to +54.9% absolute over base; MATH500 0.790 and 0.604, reported as surpassing GRPO. Key ablation: versus prefix-only SMC with the SAME reward model and same (N,B,alpha), lookahead+MH adds +9.1/+10.4/+15.3 absolute on HumanEval and up to +8.8 on MATH500. Token-budget sweep (Fig.1): Best-of-N (ranked by log p) and prefix-only SMC both plateau at 0.73 pass@1 with no gain past N=16, while the full method scales monotonically to 0.94 at ~144K tokens per problem.

**自述与显见局限**：Stated (§5): lookahead estimation is the efficiency bottleneck; they propose adaptive rollout-budget allocation by particle uncertainty with early stopping as future work. Not stated but load-bearing: the method needs token-level transition probabilities for the MH acceptance ratio and the ability to resample/replace prefixes mid-generation; the HumanEval reward is executable unit tests (an oracle-strength verifier, not a proxy); the headline scaling point costs ~144K tokens per problem; results are pass@1 on three 7B models with no seed variance reported, and the budget sweep uses an 82-task HumanEval subset. No analysis of what happens as psi degrades in quality — reward potentials are treated as trustworthy throughout.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level steps remaining: pricing lookahead rollouts by particle uncertainty rather than a fixed J (their own suggestion); characterizing behaviour under an IMPERFECT psi — the paper itself motivates the design by noting rewards can be 'strong or misaligned with likelihood' but never measures the misaligned regime, which is precisely the reward-hacking question; and explicitly characterizing the logprob-free residue (SMC generation weighted only by psi) as a standalone controller rather than leaving it as the ablation baseline.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：Three concrete breaks against a frozen black-box speech/omni core. (1) The lookahead + MH stage requires p(x_t|q,x_<t) and the tempered proposal density inside the acceptance ratio (Alg.1 line 22); no guaranteed logprobs means this stage is unavailable — and the paper's own ablation attributes +9.1 to +15.3 points exactly to that stage, so the black-box-legal residue (prefix-only SMC on reward potentials) is the variant that PLATEAUS at 0.73 in Fig.1. (2) Particle resampling requires re-entering the core at an arbitrary prefix and continuing; a request-level black-box API cannot restart mid-generation, so block granularity would coarsen to whole utterances/turns and the fine-grained early-correction benefit (their B=64 code setting) disappears. (3) psi here is execution against unit tests or a PRM over a checkable target; speech has neither, so prefix potentials become proxy rewards over partial transcripts, and the paper provides no evidence about SMC behaviour under proxy potentials. Cross-modality effect sizes do not transfer.

**可借鉴**：The prefix-only vs lookahead ablation design (§4, 'Effect of the lookahead mechanism') as the way to isolate whether a controller's gain comes from myopic scoring or from future-value estimation — reusable even when the mechanism is not; the accuracy-vs-total-tokens sweep with baselines matched on TOKENS rather than on N (Fig.1) as the compute-honesty protocol our selection experiments should adopt, plus its finding that independent-sample baselines saturate early while a state-reusing sampler keeps scaling; 'selectively rejuvenate only duplicated low-reward particles' as a budget-targeting rule (spend the extra compute where the population has degenerated); the ESS threshold as a cheap, interface-legal population-health signal; the Feynman-Kac/potential formalism (Eq.1) as vocabulary for stating what an external reward does to a frozen generator's distribution; and the Lemma-1 note that under a tempered-base proposal the SMC incremental weights depend only on reward potentials and not on base-model probabilities — the single component that survives a no-logprob interface.

**备注**：Training-free with respect to model weights, but NOT black-box-legal: the winning configuration consumes token-level probabilities and prefix-level re-entry. Donor status is for formalism and ablation design, not mechanism.

### 2604.17768 — When Vision-Language Models Judge Without Seeing: Exposing Informativeness Bias (BIRCH)

**域**：vision-language / VLM-as-a-Judge (image + text; MLLM-as-a-Judge and VL-RewardBench)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P9 decision-calibration / admissibility gates (may this evaluator's score steer selection?); P10 reward hacking under selection pressure (a judge that pays for informativeness is a reward a selector will exploit toward verbosity); evaluation component of P6.

**方法面**：Pairwise judge f(Q,I,y_c,y_r) scored by agreement with human preference (§3.1). Three measurement constructs. (i) Image Reliance Score IRS = Acc(with image) - Acc(without image), i.e. how much the withheld channel is worth to the judge (§3.2). (ii) An informativeness labeler: GPT-4o, with the IMAGE WITHHELD, judges which candidate is more informative under rubrics reduced to helpfulness/relevance/depth/detail with accuracy explicitly removed, yielding y_i (§3.3). (iii) Informativeness Bias IB = Acc_IDS - Acc_CDS, the accuracy gap between the informativeness-driven subset (human-preferred answer is also the more informative one) and the correctness-driven subset (the preferred answer is the LESS informative one, so informativeness is actively misleading); IB=0 for unbiased humans by construction (§3.4). Length Bias LB is defined symmetrically as a control (§3.5). Findings: IRS <= 3% on MLLM-as-a-Judge and <= 4% on VL-RewardBench across six judges, negative for two (Table 1) — the image barely moves the verdict; IB exceeds 30% (Gemini-2.5-Flash) and 50% (GPT-4o, Llama-3.2-V-90B, LLaVA-1.6-34B) (Fig.3); judge headline accuracy is a MIXTURE ARTIFACT — at the benchmark's native 32% CDS ratio judges score >65%, at a balanced 50% ratio they fall to ~55%, barely above chance (Fig.4); after equalizing candidate lengths IB drops but remains 26-45.5% (Table 2), so IB is not reducible to length bias; and judges asked the question directly often answer correctly and name the flaw in the rejected answer, yet still prefer it as a judge (§4.2) — the failure is not capability. IRS is higher on CDS than IDS (Fig.5) and IB is larger when the image is removed (Fig.6), tying the two constructs together. Method BIRCH (§5.2, Fig.7): same judge, three steps in one extra pass — correct each candidate against the image and strip unsupported claims; merge the corrected candidates into a single truthful-and-informative anchor; judge candidates against the anchor. Training-free. Gains 2.73-9.84 points across 8 judges x 2 benchmarks (Table 3); GPT-4o 66.45 -> 75.78, Acc_CDS 26.3 -> 45.3 (+19), Acc_IDS 85.3 -> 90.1, IB 59.0 -> 44.8, LB 32.9 -> 20.3 (Table 4). Ablations: Standard Ref (self-generated reference WITHOUT seeing candidates) lowers IB but costs Acc_IDS (-4.6) and net accuracy (over-penalizes valid extra detail); Image Caption sometimes falls below Base. Cost: one extra pass, ~2x inference time (3.87 s -> 7.47 s, Table 6). Anchor quality audited by scoring anchors vs candidates with a third model (Table 5).

**自述与显见局限**：Stated (§8): IB persists after BIRCH; the anchor generator is the same biased model, so it can fail to remove incorrect claims and degrade anchor quality; even with a good anchor the judge sometimes still picks the informative-but-wrong answer (App.D.3); gains are small where baseline bias is already small (Phi-4-multimodal). Ethical note (§9): human preference labels are themselves biased, so alignment to them inherits that bias. Additional read-level observations: benchmark labels were manually corrected by the authors before evaluation (App.B), so the comparison rests on their re-annotation; the informativeness axis is defined by one LLM (GPT-4o) judging others; pairwise-only, no abstention option.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level steps remaining on top: the CDS-fraction sensitivity (Fig.4) is diagnosed but never closed — a deployment-time estimate of how often the surface-attractive candidate is wrong is what makes a headline judge number interpretable, and nothing here estimates it without labels; the anchor is generated by the same model being debiased, so an independent-evidence anchor or an abstain-instead-of-choose option is untouched; and the constructs are measured offline per benchmark rather than turned into a per-instance admissibility gate.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：IRS requires a channel that can be withheld and re-supplied to compute an accuracy delta. Withholding audio from a speech/omni judge is mechanically possible, but the vision result is only interpretable because the TEXT alone nearly determines the verdict (hence near-zero IRS); for most speech tasks the residual text channel carries little of the answer, so a speech IRS would be dominated by task solvability rather than judge reliance, and a large IRS would not certify grounded judging. The truthful anchor is built by correcting text against an image the judge can re-inspect deterministically and unboundedly; a speech judge 're-listening' yields a fresh, possibly different, internal transcription, so anchor construction becomes stochastic rather than a correction, and the anchor-quality gate (Table 5) would be measuring a moving target. 'Informativeness' as helpfulness/depth/detail is a text-answer rubric with no validated speech-domain counterpart in this paper, and the human pairwise preference labels that define correctness for IB do not exist for free-form speech-conditioned output. Effect magnitudes (IB 30-59%) are vision-domain facts and are not a cross-modality effectiveness claim.

**可借鉴**：IRS (§3.2) as a general 'is this observation load-bearing for this reward?' statistic — ablate the channel, measure the accuracy delta; the IDS/CDS partition and IB (§3.4) as a reward-admissibility certificate: an evaluator is admissible only if it survives the subset where the surface-attractive candidate is wrong, and reporting Acc_IDS and Acc_CDS separately is mandatory because their average hides the failure; the length-equalized replication (Table 2) as the confound control before attributing a bias to a semantic axis; the CDS-ratio downsampling sweep (Fig.4) as the 'your headline number is a mixture' protocol — re-report every evaluator number at a balanced mixture; the demonstration that the judge can answer correctly yet judge wrongly (§4.2) as the design that separates capability failure from decision failure; BIRCH's three-step self-anchor (correct -> merge -> compare) as a training-free repair pattern with a measured one-extra-pass price; the Standard-Ref negative result (reduces bias, reduces accuracy) as evidence that a debiasing gate can overcorrect and must be scored on BOTH subsets; and the third-model anchor-quality audit (Table 5) as a required gate on any generated intermediate artifact a controller relies on.

**备注**：Training-free method (no gradients); judges include both proprietary APIs and open models, so the protocol is executable against a black-box evaluator. Domain is strictly vision-language.

### 2604.18164 — MM-JudgeBias: A Benchmark for Evaluating Compositional Biases in MLLM-as-a-Judge

**域**：vision-language / MLLM-as-a-Judge (pointwise 1-10 scoring; 1,804 pairs from 29 source benchmarks)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P9 admissibility gates and decision-calibration (BD/BC as the certificate); P10 reward hacking under selection pressure (a judge insensitive to evidence removal is trivially exploitable by a selector); stop/abstain calibration (the N/A analysis); evaluation component of P6.

**方法面**：Judge f_theta:(Q,I,R) -> y on a 1-10 scale (§3.1). Every unbiased triplet is paired with a controlled perturbed triplet (Q',I',R') and reliability is split into two expectations depending on what the perturbation SHOULD do (§3.2): Bias-Deviation BD = E[(y - y_hat)^+ / (y-1) | y>1] for perturbations that destroy essential evidence (higher = correctly penalizes), and Bias-Conformity BC = E[1 - |y - y_hat| / max(y-1, S-y)] for semantics-preserving perturbations (higher = correctly stable). Explicitly, BD and BC are not competing metrics; applied selectively they separate a 'volatile' judge (deviates everywhere) from a 'discerning' one (deviates only when justified). Nine bias types in three dimensions (§3.3): Integrality (Text-/Image-/Response-Dominance = remove a modality entirely), Congruity (Instruction-/Image-Misalignment = substitute a mismatched component), Robustness (Detail-Description, Unnecessary-Image, Visual-Transform, Texture-Insertion). Data construction (§3.4): human-in-the-loop synthesized queries deliberately chosen to REQUIRE all modalities, responses generated by three models in balanced proportion to spread the score distribution, perturbations applied by fixed transform pipelines (visual) or model generation (texture). 26 judges (closed, open, and critic-tuned), 3 runs, with BOTH inter-run and inter-sample variance reported specifically so that a degenerate constant scorer (which trivially achieves BC=1.0) is detectable (Table 2 caption). Findings: Integrality is the hardest dimension (mean BD 0.287 Text-Dom, 0.317 Img-Dom, 0.547 Resp-Dom) while Congruity/Instruction-Misalignment is near-saturated (0.976); Robustness BC is uniformly high (0.835-0.905); scale does not monotonically improve reliability (LLaVA-Critic-72B scores below the 7B overall because robustness gains are outweighed by drops on the hard bias types); reasoning mode generally helps but not universally; critic fine-tuning does not resolve the vulnerabilities. §4.3 confirms position, verbosity (worse than position), and self-enhancement biases persist. §4.4 runs three validity controls: an explicit N/A abstention option (abstention ratio 6.5-26.1%, overall scores shift only marginally and the category trends survive), score-guided prompting (marginal change, so results are not a score-scale-interpretation artifact), and modality-enforcing prompting (+Reason. moves Qwen3-VL 0.660 -> 0.726 but effects are model-dependent).

**自述与显见局限**：Stated (Limitations): scope is deliberately restricted to multimodality-specific biases, excluding cultural/social/geographic axes; the protocol is vision-language and POINTWISE only; the authors state BD/BC are 'inherently modality-agnostic' and name video/audio/interleaved as future expansion — i.e. this paper supplies no audio evidence at all and says so. Read-level additions: 'semantics-preserving' is asserted by the construction of the perturbation pipeline rather than independently verified per instance; texture perturbations are model-generated; a single scoring prompt template is used; and BD excludes y=1 samples by construction.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level steps remaining: the abstention analysis is used as a validity check on the benchmark, not as a decision rule — converting BD/BC into a per-instance ONLINE admissibility gate (this score may/may not steer a decision) rather than a per-judge benchmark aggregate is untouched here; and the whole design presumes perturbations are cheap and controllable along one axis at a time, so a version for modalities where single-axis minimal edits are not available is open.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：BD/BC require paired inputs in which exactly ONE semantic axis moves and the intended direction of score change is known a priori. Image perturbations satisfy this cheaply; single-axis audio perturbations are not established here — a plausible 'Visual-Transform' analogue (resample, reverb, mild noise, codec) simultaneously perturbs speaker identity, channel, and intelligibility, so a low BC could reflect a genuine quality change rather than judge instability, collapsing the metric's semantics. Modality removal has a clean analogue (drop the audio) but the normalization terms (y-1) and (S-y) presuppose a bounded Likert output the judge always emits; a black-box speech/omni judge without logprobs may refuse, abstain, or answer in free form, which their own N/A analysis shows changes the measured Integrality BD substantially (e.g. GPT-5 mini 0.223 -> 0.153, Qwen3-VL 0.377 -> 0.176). The authors themselves scope audio as untested future work, so all numbers here are vision-domain reference points, not cross-modality conclusions.

**可借鉴**：The dual-metric design — BD for should-drop perturbations and BC for should-not-move perturbations, applied SELECTIVELY by perturbation class (§3.2) — as the shape of any admissibility certificate for an external reward; the three-dimension taxonomy skeleton (Integrality = is each evidence channel necessary; Congruity = are contradictions detected; Robustness = is the score stable under irrelevant change) as the axis set to instantiate for speech evidence; the inter-sample variance column as a mandatory anti-degenerate guard (a constant scorer achieves BC=1.0 and must be caught); the abstention-aware re-run (Table 3) as a required validity control on any forced-choice or forced-score protocol, together with the abstention ratio as a reported number; the prompt-intervention ablation (+Guide / +Const. / +Reason., Table 4) as the cheap-baseline any controller-side fix must beat; the finding that judge reliability is not monotone in scale or general capability, which forbids inferring gate quality from backbone strength; and the deliberate construction of queries that REQUIRE all modalities, without which modality-removal metrics are uninterpretable.

**备注**：Purely diagnostic; no gradient use in the protocol itself (critic models evaluated are fine-tuned, but they are subjects not mechanisms). Executable against black-box judges.

### 2604.18419 — Knowing When to Quit: A Principled Framework for Dynamic Abstention in LLM Reasoning

**域**：text / LLM (GSM8K, OlympiadBench; plus RealToxicityPrompts as a second reward type)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：stop/abstain/budget calibration (cross-cutting, primary); P9 decision-calibration and admissibility; P8 (a scalar value estimate directly firing a stop action with no gradients into the core).

**方法面**：Token-level MDP with sparse terminal reward and a KL penalty to a reference policy (Eqs.1-3). Abstention is added as an explicit action bot with a fixed scalar fallback reward r_bot in (0,1]; because pi_ref assigns zero probability to bot, the KL term is computed over the abstention-stripped policy s(pi_dagger) (Eq.4), making the formulation a strict generalization of the no-abstention case. The objective decomposes as J = alpha*r_bot + (1-alpha)*S where alpha is the abstention rate and S the expected reward among non-abstained responses (Eq.6); at beta=0 with binary correctness S IS selective accuracy P(correct | not abstain), and sweeping r_bot traces the accuracy-abstention frontier — this connects the sequential problem to selective classification. Maximizing over the abstention probability yields a deterministic local rule: abstain iff E[R + V] < r_bot (Eq.7), and the constructed policy a(pi) abstains iff V_beta(x,y_1:t-1;pi) < r_bot (Eq.8). Guarantees (§4): Lemma 4.1 value dominance (V(a(pi)) >= max(r_bot, V(pi)) everywhere, strict iff some reachable state has V < r_bot); Prop 4.2 dominance over no abstention; Def 4.3/Prop 4.4/Cors 4.5-4.6 dominance over FIXED-POSITION abstention, with the residual term identified as an 'expected recovery surplus' = the option value of waiting, which vanishes when low-value states cannot recover. Value approximation (§5): because reward is terminal-only, V at any prefix collapses to P(correct | prefix), the SAME target for every prefix, so a two-layer MLP probe on final-layer hidden states trained with BCE across all prefixes of completed trajectories recovers it; overhead is one MLP pass per token. Experiments (§6): Qwen2.5-Math-7B-Instruct and Phi-3-mini across GSM8K/OlympiadBench against Constant-Step-Probe, self-assessment ('Can you correctly answer this question?' yes/no logits), and LoRA-abstention, each at t=0 (input processing) and t=k (fixed mid-generation, k=20/100 chosen arbitrarily — the arbitrariness is itself an argument for dynamic). Dynamic dominates pointwise at every abstention rate; OlympiadBench Phi-3 at alpha=0.9 reaches 0.64 selective accuracy vs 0.34/0.33 for the best input-processing and fixed-position baselines. §6.2 validates the reward objective using isotonic regression to recover the EFFECTIVE r_bot from the raw threshold T_alpha. §6.3 the probe transfers zero-shot across datasets. §6.4 three robustness checks: achieved abstention rate tracks target within 1.2 pp MAE; because T_alpha is an alpha-quantile, selective accuracy is EXACTLY invariant to monotone reparametrizations of V_hat (ranking suffices, calibration is not needed in this regime); performance degrades gracefully under additive Gaussian noise on V_hat.

**自述与显见局限**：Stated (§7): the method fails when hidden states do not encode eventual correctness or optimization cannot extract that signal; r_bot is a fixed constant while a nearly-solved question deserves a state-dependent r_bot(x,y_1:t); when r_bot is specified as a real fallback utility rather than used to target an abstention rate, calibration (not just ranking) matters and their miscalibration analysis shows room for improvement. Read-level: two models, two math datasets plus one toxicity set; k for fixed-position baselines is admittedly arbitrary; all rewards are binary and verifiable; the noise-robustness study perturbs V_hat but never the LABELS used to train it.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level steps remaining: state-dependent fallback utility r_bot(x, prefix) (their own); replacing the hidden-state probe with an interface-legal value signal, which is the entire gap between this paper and a black-box control plane; and the continuous/graded-reward case, which they state the theory covers (Props 4.2-4.9 hold for any bounded reward, MSE training via Prop B.3) but never measure — precisely the regime a proxy speech reward lives in.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：The value estimator is an MLP probe on FINAL-LAYER HIDDEN STATES, trained by gradient descent — categorically outside a black-box contract (no weights, no hidden states, no attention, no guaranteed logprobs). The mechanism therefore does not transfer at all; only the decision-theoretic layer does. Second, the abstention action is evaluated at EVERY token position, presupposing an autoregressive text trace whose prefix is the state and which can be halted mid-generation; a frozen speech/omni core behind a request-level API exposes at best utterance/turn checkpoints, so 'dynamic' degrades toward the paper's own fixed-position baselines — and their central empirical claim is exactly that dynamic beats fixed-position at every alpha, i.e. the advantage is what the granularity buys. Third, V = P(correct | prefix) needs a deterministic correctness oracle to build BCE targets; free-form speech output has only proxy rewards, and label noise is untested here. What DOES transfer cleanly: the r_bot / selective-accuracy decomposition, the abstain-iff-below-fallback rule as a formalism, the alpha-quantile calibration procedure, and the monotone-invariance result — which licenses a ranking-only (uncalibrated) reward signal whenever the controller targets an abstention RATE rather than a utility threshold.

**可借鉴**：The abstention-reward formulation and J = alpha*r_bot + (1-alpha)*S decomposition (Eq.6) as the way to price stopping against selective quality; abstain-iff-V<r_bot (Eq.8) with Prop 4.2's strict-improvement condition as the admissibility statement for a stop gate (a stop gate can only help if some reachable state falls below the fallback); the fixed-position-vs-dynamic comparison INCLUDING the 'expected recovery surplus' term (Prop 4.4, Cors 4.5-4.6) as the ablation that isolates whether continuous monitoring earns its cost versus a single checkpoint; alpha-quantile threshold calibration on a small held-out set with achieved-vs-target abstention-rate MAE reported as a first-class number (§6.4); the monotone-invariance argument (§6.4, App.G) licensing ranking-only reward signals; isotonic regression to report an effective threshold in utility units (§6.2); the standard plot — selective accuracy vs abstention rate with token savings annotated per point — and the complementary precision metric P(incorrect | abstained); the cross-dataset zero-shot probe-transfer test (§6.3) as the generalization control any fitted gate component must pass; and the graceful-degradation-under-noise protocol as the sensitivity analysis for an imperfect value signal.

**备注**：GRADIENT USE FLAGGED: value probe is a gradient-trained MLP on hidden states; LoRA-abstention baseline fine-tunes the base LLM. Donor for measurements, decision theory and protocol only — never for mechanism.

### 2604.18860 — Temporal UI State Inconsistency in Desktop GUI Agents: Formalizing and Defending Against TOCTOU Attacks on Computer-Use Agents (PUSV / DesktopTOCTOU-Bench)

**域**：vision-GUI / computer-use agents (desktop OSWorld, Ubuntu 22.04 + GNOME), security　**供体级别**：CONSTRUCT_DONOR　**喂给**：P9 admissibility gates (a signal is inadmissible once stale, regardless of content); P1 supply pricing / value-of-information (re-verification is a priced extra observation with a measured price); stop/abstain (abort-instead-of-dispatch is a stop decision); P6 composition (a middleware wrapper at the dispatch point, external to the frozen model).

**方法面**：Measures the observation-to-action gap on 10 heterogeneous real OSWorld tasks with Claude Opus 4.6, instrumenting T_obs at screenshot capture and T_act at pyautogui dispatch: mean 6.51 s, sd 3.59, range 3.18-13.23 (§3, Table 1), consistent with the 4.18-15.43 s window reported for Android Action Rebinding. Formalizes Visual Atomicity Violation (Def.1, §4.2): the agent intended element e* occupying click coordinate c at T_obs, but a distinct attacker-controlled element e_A occupies c at T_act. Threat model (§4.1): unprivileged same-session code execution only, no kernel/root, no access to the agent's code, prompt, or LLM channel. Three attack primitives: (A) Notification Overlay Hijack — borderless Tkinter window spawned 1 s after T_obs in three styles (corner banner 380x90, zenity center modal, fullscreen); (B) Window Focus Manipulation — a window pre-registered in a withdrawn state at T_obs and later deiconified, achieving 100% action redirection with ZERO visual evidence at observation time; (C) Web DOM Injection — form action attribute swapped, zero visual footprint. Defense PUSV (§5) re-verifies UI state immediately before dispatch using three DETERMINISTIC layers, no second model call: L1 masked pixel SSIM on a 160x160 grayscale patch centered at the click target, abort if < tau_1 = 0.92; L2a global pixel-diff excluding that patch, abort if > tau_2a = 0.2% of pixels changed beyond delta_noise = 20; L2b wmctrl X-window-registry diff with a keyword set. Thresholds calibrated on 50 benign + 30 adversarial trials: benign SSIM 0.97-1.00 and benign global diff < 0.05%/s, versus attack SSIM 0.632 (fullscreen) and 0.540 (window raise) and attack diffs 1.1% / 8.9% / 69% — margins of 5x to 345x. Results (§5.7): 100% Action Interception Rate over 180 adversarial trials for primitives A and B with ZERO false positives over 30 benign trials, and < 0.1 s overhead (~50 ms screenshot, <5 ms SSIM, <10 ms wmctrl) versus the ~5 s an LLM-based re-check would cost — an explicit design argument for deterministic checks (§5.8). Against primitive C, AIR is ~0% across all three layers and all three agent models. Per-layer independent ablation shows no single layer covers all primitives (L1 misses off-target overlays; L2a misses primitive B because the change is confined to the excluded patch; L2b misses compositor-rendered banners and pre-registered windows). Model-agnostic across Claude Opus 4.6 / GPT-4o / Qwen3.6-plus (Table 10).

**自述与显见局限**：Stated (§5.8, §7, §8): a structural blind spot to application-layer semantic manipulation with no visual footprint (primitive C, ~0% AIR), reported openly rather than omitted; an adaptive adversary making the substituted element pixel-identical in color/size/position would evade both L1 and L2a; thresholds are calibrated for Ubuntu 22.04 + GNOME and need ~30 benign trials to re-calibrate for another environment; one miss out of 45 was an artifact of a 30 s self-dismissing overlay timer rather than a structural failure. Read-level: 50 benchmark scenarios, a single-author VM environment, and a false-positive rate established on only 30 benign trials.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level steps remaining: the proposed L2c application-layer/DOM fingerprint layer (their own); calibrating the abort threshold against a COST of false aborts rather than to zero false positives on a small benign set — at 0.1 s the check is effectively free, so the interesting regime (where re-observation is expensive and the gate must be rationed) is never entered; and no treatment of what the agent should DO after an abort (re-observe, replan, escalate, stop) — the abort is the end of the pipeline here.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：The whole defense rests on a cheap, deterministic, RE-SAMPLABLE observation of a state that is static when unattacked — that is why benign SSIM sits at 0.97-1.00 and any deviation is signal. Speech has no static re-observable state: the acoustic scene has already moved on, re-listening to a past interval is impossible for a live stream, and even under benign conditions the signal changes continuously, so the benign/attack separation that makes tau_1=0.92 work has no obvious analogue and would have to be established empirically per acoustic condition. A speech 're-verify before acting' gate could only re-verify a DERIVED artifact (a transcript, a diarization, a VAD segmentation), which reintroduces the stochastic model whose non-determinism the defense was specifically designed to avoid, and whose disagreement would confound staleness with model variance. There is no wmctrl-style structural registry for audio. What does transfer is the framing: the age of the evidence a decision rests on is measurable, and a signal can be declared inadmissible on age alone, independent of its content.

**可借鉴**：Measure and REPORT the observation-to-decision gap distribution (mean, sd, min, max) as a first-class quantity before claiming a staleness gate is needed (§3, Table 1); Def.1's formalization pattern — name the state, the intended target, and the substitution that constitutes a violation — as a template for defining what makes a speech observation inadmissible; the deterministic-cheap-check-instead-of-a-second-model-call argument with the explicit latency budget comparison (0.1 s vs ~5 s, §5.8) as the cost discipline for any gate we add to the control plane; threshold calibration from PAIRED benign/adversarial trials with the achieved margin stated (tau_1=0.92 vs highest observed attack signal 0.632); the per-layer INDEPENDENT ablation table proving no single signal suffices and that layers cover disjoint failure classes; reporting Action Interception Rate and benign false-positive rate in the same table; the cross-model replication showing both the capability and the blind spot are model-agnostic (Table 10); and above all the reporting discipline of publishing the ~0% result on primitive C as a named structural blind spot — the pattern to mirror for gates our program cannot make work.

**备注**：Fully training-free and model-agnostic; no gradient use anywhere. Vision-GUI domain; effect sizes and thresholds are desktop-environment-specific.

### 2604.22273 — Self-Correction as Feedback Control: Error Dynamics, Stability Thresholds, and Prompt Interventions in LLMs

**域**：text / LLM (GSM8K 500, MATH 400, StrategyQA 200; 7 models across four capability tiers)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P9 admissibility gates (repair is admissible only above the stability threshold) and decision-calibration; stop/abstain/budget calibration (cross-cutting); P8 reward-as-controller (feedback-control framing of gradient-free multi-step); P10 (a loop that degrades an already-strong base is the same failure shape as reward hacking under selection pressure).

**方法面**：Recasts iterative self-correction as closed-loop feedback control where the same model is both controller and plant, and models each problem's correctness as a two-state Markov chain on {Correct, Incorrect} parameterized by Error Introduction Rate EIR(k)=P(c^(k+1)=0 | c^(k)=1) and Error Correction Rate ECR(k)=P(c^(k+1)=1 | c^(k)=0) (Def.1, §III-A/B). Theorem 1: net benefit is zero iff ECR/EIR = Acc/(1-Acc), giving the deployable rule 'iterate only when ECR/EIR > Acc/(1-Acc)'; Theorem 2: steady state pi* = ECR*/(EIR*+ECR*), which for high-accuracy models sits FAR BELOW baseline, making unconditional self-correction systematic compute waste; Theorem 3: geometric convergence at rate |1-EIR*-ECR*|. ASC (Alg.1) combines an instance-level stop (self-reported confidence gamma >= tau, tau=8 on a 1-10 scale) with a batch-level equilibrium stop (halt when running EIR_hat >= ECR_hat). Empirics (§IV): 4 refinement iterations x 500 GSM8K problems x 7 models. Only o3-mini (+3.4 pp), Claude Opus 4.6 (+0.6), o4-mini (+/-0) are non-degrading; GPT-4o-mini -6.2, GPT-5 -1.8, Sonnet 4 -1.2, GPT-4.1 -0.2 (Table II). A sharp near-zero EIR boundary (<= ~0.5%) separates the two groups (Table III); GPT-5 vs Opus 4.6 is the decisive contrast — near-identical baseline (96.2 vs 97.6) but 10x EIR difference (1.9% vs 0.2%) flips the sign. Causal ablation (§IV-E): a verify-first prompt ('first verify by re-solving independently; only change if you find a concrete, specific error') drives GPT-4o-mini EIR 2% -> 0% across all iterations and converts -6.2 pp into +0.2 pp (paired McNemar p < 1e-4, Delta Acc +6.4 pp, paired-bootstrap 95% CI [+4.2, +8.8]), while producing NO detectable change on GPT-4.1 and Sonnet 4.5 whose EIR was already sub-threshold (p = 1.0) — the targeted-null half is what makes the evidence causal rather than correlational. Mechanism (§IV-I): pool-size asymmetry — 456/500 correct vs 44/500 correctable, so 1.3% EIR on the large pool outweighs moderate ECR on the small one. §IV-G: verification RHETORIC is present in 84.8-100% of traces including the worst degrader, so surface verification language is not the discriminator; what matters is how often the model changes an already-correct answer, and such changes are harmful in nearly 100% of cases. Compute-matched at 3 API calls (Table IV): self-consistency 93.4% vs 3-iteration refinement 86.6% vs single-shot baseline 91.2%. ASC halts at iteration 0 for all 500 items (correctly), but the confidence-elicitation prompt itself costs 3.8 pp of accuracy.

**自述与显见局限**：Stated (§V-A): detailed analysis is GSM8K-only at 4 iterations; the stationarity the theorems assume is violated in their own data (EIR drifts 1.3% -> 3.8%), motivating a time-varying control formulation; only INTRINSIC self-correction is evaluated (no external oracle, no tools, no retrieval) and they note external feedback could shift the EIR/ECR balance; EIR/ECR are defined on binary exact-match correctness and they explicitly state open-ended generation would require redefining them with graded or preference-based notions, suggesting process-supervised step-graded states; compute trade-offs are at one fixed temperature. Read-level: o3-mini's StrategyQA number is an extractor artifact the authors flag and exclude.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level steps remaining: a budget-indexed EIR(B)/ECR(B) frontier (their own suggestion) so the gate is a function of spend rather than a single scalar; a time-varying/adaptive-threshold control formulation to handle the non-stationarity they observe; and — most relevant to our program — the EXTERNAL-feedback regime, which is the entire premise of an external reward-guided control plane and is exactly the case they do not measure: how EIR/ECR move when the repair trigger is a proxy reward rather than the model's own critique.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：EIR and ECR are estimated from per-round exact-match correctness against gold answers on a calibration set. A frozen black-box speech/omni core producing free-form output has no per-round label, so the rates can only be estimated through a proxy judge whose own error rate is confounded with EIR — and the paper's headline finding is that a 1-2 percentage-point EIR difference flips the sign of the loop, i.e. the quantity we would have to estimate is plausibly SMALLER than proxy-judge noise. The authors' own limitation section states the {C,I} state must be redefined with graded correctness for open-ended generation, which is precisely our regime, and offers no evidence that the threshold survives that redefinition. The verify-first intervention is a text instruction to re-solve independently; a speech/omni core 're-listening' does not re-derive an independent answer in the same sense, and independence between the original and the re-solve is what the intervention depends on. The inequality itself (iterate iff ECR/EIR > Acc/(1-Acc)) is modality-agnostic as algebra; the measurability of its terms under a black-box speech interface is what breaks.

**可借鉴**：EIR and ECR as the two rates any repair/self-correction loop must report SEPARATELY, since a net accuracy delta hides which one is moving (Def.1); Theorem 1's iterate-iff-ECR/EIR > Acc/(1-Acc) as a pre-flight repair-admissibility gate computed on a calibration set rather than assumed; Theorem 2's steady state pi* as the honest ceiling of an unbounded loop; the pool-size asymmetry argument (§IV-I) as the structural reason a HIGH-baseline system is intrinsically fragile to any repair operator — directly relevant when our controller sits on a strong frozen core; the TARGETED-NULL ablation design (§IV-E): apply the intervention to systems both above and below the threshold and require it to be INERT below, which is the causal-evidence pattern any gate we claim should satisfy; paired McNemar plus paired-bootstrap CI on final-iteration outcomes as the statistic; the compute-equivalent comparison table (equal API calls across refinement / self-consistency / single-shot, Table IV) as the mandatory budget-matched comparator; the response-pattern finding that verification rhetoric appears in ~100% of traces and is therefore useless as a signal (Table VI) — a standing warning against surface-form proxies for a controller's internal checks; and ASC's dual stop criteria together with the honest accounting that the confidence-elicitation prompt itself costs 3.8 pp (an instrument that perturbs the system it measures).

**备注**：Fully training-free (prompting + measurement only); models are treated as black boxes throughout, which makes the measurement protocol interface-compatible with our contract even though its LABELS are not.

### 2604.25235 — VLM Judges Can Rank but Cannot Score: Task-Dependent Uncertainty in Multimodal Evaluation

**域**：vision-language / VLM-as-a-Judge with conformal prediction (MLLM-as-a-Judge 5,717 instances across 14 task categories; Polaris captioning 8,726)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P9 decision-calibration / admissibility gates (turning a raw judge score into a licensed or unlicensed decision input) and, partially, label-free headroom estimation — though note this route is label-DEPENDENT, which is itself the informative negative for our label-free slot; P10 (a judge that ranks but cannot score is exploitable whenever a controller selects against an absolute threshold); evaluation component of P6.

**方法面**：The judge emits a Likert score in {1..K}; the feature vector is the K-dimensional set of LOG-PROBABILITIES of the score tokens at the score position (§3.1, Eq.2) — entropy/margin/actor-entropy features were tried and did not help. Split conformal prediction on a calibration set produces C(x) = [y_hat - q_hat, y_hat + q_hat] with distribution-free marginal coverage >= 1-alpha under exchangeability, post-hoc and with no retraining of the judge (§3.2). Three extensions (§3.3): boundary adjustment mapping continuous endpoints onto valid integer labels (Eq.5); Mondrian group-conditional CP partitioning the 14 datasets into easy/medium/hard by pilot-split width, giving group-conditional guarantees; and a new statistic, Ranking-Scoring Gap RSG(d) = |rho_d| - (1 - w_d/(K-1)), where the second term measures interval informativeness. Setup: 3 judges (LLaVA-Critic-7B, Phi-4-reasoning-vision-15B, Gemini 2.5 Flash — the last a closed API), 50/50 calibration/test, 10 seeds, alpha = 0.10. Findings: 8 CP methods fall into three tiers — R2CCP is best calibrated (coverage .900, width 3.05), CHR slightly narrower but under-covers, and CQR variants DEGENERATE to full-range intervals when features are weak predictors (§5.1, Table 2); boundary adjustment adds 8+ coverage points on sparse integer labels versus 2-5 reported for denser scales. Interval width is TASK-driven, from 2.08 (AesBench) to 3.50 (InfographicsVQA), with per-dataset widths correlating across judges at Spearman 0.93 and 0.82 — width is a property of the task, not the model (§5.4); Mondrian narrows easy-group intervals 16.6% while IMPROVING hard-group coverage (Table 5). Ranking-scoring decoupling (§5.5): ChartQA has the highest Pearson (.507) among all 14 datasets yet among the widest intervals (3.08), while WIT has rho = .164 with narrower intervals (2.38) — a judge can order responses correctly while assigning unreliable absolute scores, a failure mode aggregate metrics miss. Judges compress toward the scale middle, overscoring GT=1 by +0.89 to +1.98 and underscoring GT=5 by -0.74 to -1.04 (Table 4). Same judge, same method, clean multi-annotator Polaris yields 4.5x narrower intervals (0.68 vs 3.05) at equal coverage (§5.6, Table 7) — width is dominated by task definition and annotation quality, not by method or model. Counter-intuitively, Gemini is the most overconfident in softmax (83% of maxima > 0.99) yet yields the NARROWEST intervals: interval quality tracks the discriminative signal in the feature vector, not softmax calibration (§5.2). Multi-judge feature fusion DEGRADES width versus the best single judge (Table 8).

**自述与显见局限**：Stated (Limitations): 68.8% of boundary-adjusted intervals span more than 75% of the rating scale on MLLM-Judge, so per-instance discrimination is limited there (attributed to genuine task difficulty and single-annotator noise, evidenced by the Polaris contrast); conditional coverage at GT=1 falls to 88.9%, below the 90% target, because of systematic overscoring of poor answers; the score-token extraction is a backward-scan heuristic verified on 100 samples per judge and may break on other output formats; only alpha = 0.10 is tested; CP requires a human-annotated calibration set; and marginal coverage does not imply conditional coverage.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level steps remaining: every construction here is calibration-set dependent, so a label-free or weakly-labelled calibration route is the open step; conditional coverage at the low-quality end is unresolved and is exactly where a selection controller is most exposed; and interval width is used as a DIAGNOSTIC and an informal guideline ('wide -> prefer pairwise'), never evaluated as an ACTUATOR inside a decision loop — turning width into a live routing signal with measured end-to-end effect is untouched.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：The entire feature vector is score-token log-probabilities, which our black-box contract does not guarantee; with no logprobs this CP construction has no input at all, and the paper's own cross-judge analysis (§5.2) attributes interval quality precisely to the discriminative content of that vector rather than to the judge's accuracy — so a substitute feature (e.g. repeated-sampling score histograms) is not evidenced here to carry the same signal. Exchangeability requires the calibration set to be drawn from the deployment distribution; speech deployments key on acoustic condition (speaker, channel, noise, domain), so a single global quantile would be miscalibrated per condition — their Mondrian variant is the right shape, but its groups are TASK categories fixed once on a pilot split, not conditions estimated online. A human-annotated Likert calibration set is required, and their own Polaris-vs-MLLM contrast shows annotation quality dominates the result by 4.5x, so a cheaply-annotated speech calibration set would land in the uninformative-width regime by construction. Most portable pieces: the ranking-scoring decoupling construct and the operational branch it implies, both of which are interface-agnostic.

**可借鉴**：The operational guideline and its concrete branch — narrow interval means the absolute score may be used for thresholding, wide interval means fall back to pairwise comparison (§6) — as a ready-made admissibility rule for a selection controller; RSG (Eq.7) as the statistic that catches high-correlation/low-informativeness evaluators, a failure aggregate metrics hide; the three-tier taxonomy of CP methods with the explicit warning that conditional-quantile variants collapse to full-range intervals under weak features (§5.1, Table 2) — i.e. report width, not just coverage, or a vacuous gate looks perfect; boundary adjustment for discrete scales (Eq.5) plus the finding that it matters MORE the sparser the label set; Mondrian group-conditional quantiles as the structural shape of a CONDITION-KEYED gate; bias-by-ground-truth-level stratification (Table 4) as a required error-structure report rather than a single MAE; the same-judge/same-method ablation across clean vs noisy ground truth (Fig.2, Table 7) — the single most reusable experiment here, since it cleanly separates method quality from label quality and is the control our own calibration claims will need; and the multi-judge fusion negative result (Table 8) as a caution against ensembling weak evaluators on the assumption that more signal helps.

**备注**：Post-hoc and retraining-free with respect to the judge, but NOT black-box-legal: requires score-token log-probabilities and a human-annotated calibration set. Numbers are vision-language domain anchors.

### 2604.27003 — When Continual Learning Moves to Memory: A Study of Experience Reuse in LLM Agents

**域**：text-agent / embodied-text environments (ALFWorld, BabyAI) with a FROZEN LLM backbone plus external memory　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P3 condition-keyed memory + write-gates / poisoning defenses (primarily by NEGATIVE SPACE — it quantifies what un-gated accumulation costs and supplies the instruments to measure a gate's effect); P5 tool/skill credit and induction (the distilled Insight is the induced artifact, and Raw-vs-Insight is a clean induction ablation); P6 composition; P10 (memory-induced degradation on already-solved cases is a selection-pressure failure shape).

**方法面**：A study, not a system. Two-phase sequential protocol: the agent runs a stream of 200 Task-A instances from EMPTY memory, then continues on 200 Task-B instances while carrying the accumulated pool forward (§2.1, Fig.1); ReAct interaction loop with the ReMe memory module, retrieved experiences inserted into context before the first action step; the backbone is frozen so there is no parametric forgetting. Central claim: external memory does not remove the stability-plasticity problem, it RELOCATES it from weight-space interference to retrieval — carrier = external memory, interference = retrieval pollution, bottleneck = context window (Table 1), with three named mechanisms (retrieval pollution, context competition, memory dilution). The (k,v) framework (§2.2) disentangles VALUE = how experience is represented (Raw action-observation trajectory vs Insight = LLM-distilled strategy) from KEY = how it is organized and accessed, split further into storage granularity (Cond-Agg: one bundled entry per episode keyed by the task instruction, vs Cond-Ind: each insight a separate entry with its own short applicability-description key) and retrieval granularity (Cond-Ind: one query per episode, vs Cond-Step: periodic re-query during execution). Metrics (§2.3): FWT and BWT in the classical CL sense, indexed by training sequence, PLUS an RR/NL subset diagnostic that partitions the held-out test set by the no-memory baseline into a baseline-success subset (Retention Rate) and a baseline-fail subset (New Learning), so Delta RR < 0 means harmful reuse (broke what already worked) and Delta NL < 0 means ineffective reuse (failed the hard cases). All conditions are matched on the number of insights surfaced per retrieval, keeping the information budget comparable (§4). Findings: Raw gives NEGATIVE forward transfer in A->B in both environments (-9.5 ALF, -7.5 Baby) while Insight flips it positive (+6.5, +9.0) (Table 3); negative transfer concentrates on hard cases — ALFWorld A->B Raw has Delta RR = +4.6 but Delta NL = -26.1 (§3.2, Fig.3), a split a net average would hide; within-task advantage does NOT predict cross-task benefit — Raw's large within-task lead on ALFWorld Task B disappears or reverses under cross-task reuse (§3.3, Fig.4); abstraction also reduces forgetting (BabyAI B->A: Insight +3.5 BWT vs Raw -4.0) (§3.4). On organization (§4.1): finer storage granularity helps only when splitting produces entries that are genuinely different AND separately retrievable — Cond-Ind is best on BabyAI A->B (+15.0 FWT, Delta RR +19.4, Delta NL +10.8) because Task A has three distinct sub-task types, but drops to -1.0 in B->A because homogeneous 'find [object]' insights split into near-identical entries the retriever cannot distinguish, a failure they name RETRIEVAL DIVERSITY COLLAPSE (the pool looks large but homogeneous queries keep pulling the same few entries). Step-level retrieval helps only when information needs change within an episode (§4.2), and its large within-task gain (79.5% vs 55.0% scratch on ALFWorld Task B) does not carry across tasks. §4.3: the stability-plasticity trade-off reappears through retrieval — BabyAI A->B Cond-Ind has the strongest FWT and simultaneously the worst BWT (-10.0), the same run being best at the new task and worst at the old one.

**自述与显见局限**：Stated (Limitations): deliberately resource-constrained — two environments, ONE representative task pair each, one sparse retrieval backbone, two runs per condition; no claim of exhaustiveness; broader task coverage, additional retrievers and larger repetition are named as needed for generality. Read-level additions: there is NO write-gate anywhere — every episode's experience is stored unconditionally, so nothing here measures selective retention; task boundaries are clean by construction (the phase switch is externally given); the 'Insight' abstraction is produced by the same LLM and its quality is never independently audited; retrieval budget is fixed rather than optimized; and success is a programmatic environment bit.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level steps remaining on top of this paper: a write-gate of any kind — utility-scored retention, deduplication against the diversity collapse they name, or verify-then-promote — since un-gated accumulation is the baseline they study rather than a design they improve; condition-keyed indexing (their keys are task-semantic natural-language descriptions, not deployment-condition keys); pricing the retrieval budget instead of fixing the number of surfaced insights; and making the design ADAPTIVE — the study establishes which (k,v) configuration wins in which regime but never selects the configuration online from an observable property of the stream, which is the obvious controller-side step.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：Forward/backward transfer are measured against programmatic environment success bits on held-out instances of two clean, externally-declared task families with a sharp A->B boundary. A speech deployment stream is not segmented that way and has no success bit, so FWT/BWT would have to be defined against a proxy reward AND against a segmentation the controller itself estimates — two error sources this design never carries. Retrieval keys are natural-language applicability descriptions matched by a sparse text retriever; the acoustic-condition keys our program needs (speaker, channel, noise, domain) have no counterpart here, and their own diversity-collapse result says the DISCRIMINABILITY of the key space is what decides whether finer granularity helps — so the finding is a warning about, not a recipe for, keys built from acoustic descriptors of unknown separability. Memory content is text insights consumed in-context; retaining audio exemplars would incur a re-observation/re-encoding cost this study never pays, and their information-budget matching (equal insights per retrieval) would need a different unit. Effect signs are also directional in their own data (A->B vs B->A reverse), so nothing here supports a modality-transferred effect claim.

**可借鉴**：The (k,v) decomposition (§2.2) as the axis pair any memory ablation must vary INDEPENDENTLY — representation vs organization, and within organization storage granularity vs retrieval frequency; FWT/BWT with the explicit 'indexed by training sequence' discipline and the requirement to run BOTH directions, since their strongest conclusions reverse by direction (§2.3, Table 3); the RR/NL subset diagnostic partitioned by the no-memory baseline, with Delta RR and Delta NL reported separately — the single most reusable instrument here, because it distinguishes 'broke what already worked' from 'failed to help what didn't' where a net delta would average to near zero (ALFWorld Raw: +4.6 / -26.1); the within-task vs cross-task contrast (Fig.4) as a mandatory control before adopting any memory representation on within-distribution evidence; the information-budget matching rule ('all conditions surface roughly the same number of insights per retrieval', §4) so granularity ablations are not confounded with context volume; retrieval diversity collapse as a named failure to test for whenever finer keys are proposed; and the stability-plasticity-through-retrieval result (§4.3) as the reason FWT alone can never justify a memory design.

**备注**：Frozen backbone, no gradient use anywhere — mechanism is interface-compatible with a black-box core. Domain is text-agent/embodied-text; two environments, two runs per condition, so effect sizes are weak evidence and the instruments are the donation.

**组级综合（agent 提交）**：SCOPE: nine papers, all 2026-04, read in full. Domain mix: four text/LLM (14853, 16453, 18419, 22273), one text-agent/embodied-text (27003), three vision-language judge papers (17768, 18164, 25235), one vision-GUI security paper (18860). No speech or omni paper in this read set, and two papers state audio as untested future work (18164 Limitations; 22273 §V-A on redefining correctness for open-ended generation). Nothing here licenses a cross-modality effectiveness claim; H5 stays withheld.

WHAT THIS SUBSET SETTLES.
(1) The budget/stop slot now has two complementary formal spines, and they are compatible. 14853 gives the ALLOCATION spine: a Lagrange multiplier that is literally a price of compute, a monotonicity theorem that makes binary search an exact budget-targeting device, and a regret decomposition (task regret <= imitation error x worst-case per-instance gap) that tells us how good a router must be for a system-level number to hold. 18419 gives the STOPPING spine: abstention as an explicit action with a scalar fallback reward, J = alpha*r_bot + (1-alpha)*S, abstain-iff-value-below-fallback, a dominance result against both no-abstention and fixed-checkpoint abstention with the residual named as an option value of waiting, and — the single most portable technical fact in this read set — an exact invariance: when the threshold is set as an alpha-quantile to target an abstention RATE, selective accuracy is unchanged under any monotone reparametrization of the score. That licenses a ranking-only, uncalibrated reward for rate-targeted stopping, which is exactly the regime a proxy speech reward lives in. 22273 supplies the third leg for REPAIR: iterate only when ECR/EIR > Acc/(1-Acc), with a steady state that for a high-accuracy base sits below baseline. Read together, allocation, stopping, and repair each get a pre-flight admissibility inequality rather than a heuristic.
(2) The admissibility slot is now well-instrumented at the certificate level. 18164 contributes the dual-lens design (Bias-Deviation for perturbations that should lower a score, Bias-Conformity for perturbations that should not move it, applied selectively so a volatile judge cannot be mistaken for a discerning one) plus the anti-degenerate guard (inter-sample variance, because a constant scorer trivially achieves BC=1.0). 17768 contributes the channel-reliance and misleading-subset instruments: IRS as the accuracy delta from withholding an evidence channel, and the IDS/CDS partition with IB as the gap — an evaluator is admissible only where the surface-attractive candidate is wrong. 25235 contributes the per-instance uncertainty layer and, crucially, a decision branch (narrow interval -> absolute score usable; wide -> fall back to pairwise) plus the RSG statistic that catches evaluators which rank well and score badly. These three are non-overlapping lenses on the same question and should be composed, not chosen between.
(3) A convergent methodological finding that is independent of modality: aggregate evaluator numbers are mixture artifacts. 17768 shows judge accuracy falls from >65% to ~55% purely by rebalancing the misleading-subset ratio from 32% to 50%. 27003 shows a memory design's net transfer of roughly zero can decompose into +4.6 retention and -26.1 on the hard subset. 25235 shows the same judge and method give 4.5x different interval widths depending only on annotation quality. Three independent papers, three domains, one lesson: every headline number our program reports must be accompanied by its subset decomposition and its mixture.

WHAT TRANSFERS.
Decision-theoretic layers and measurement instruments transfer; mechanisms mostly do not. Concretely portable to a frozen black-box speech/omni core: the dual-price/binary-search budget targeting (14853 §3), abstention formalism + quantile calibration + the monotone-invariance licence (18419 §4, §6.4), the EIR/ECR repair gate and its pool-size-asymmetry explanation (22273 Thm.1, §IV-I), BD/BC certificate design and the abstention-aware validity re-run (18164 §3.2, §4.4), IRS / IDS-CDS / length-equalized control (17768 §3), RSG and the width-driven routing branch (25235 §3.3, §6), the (k,v) axis decomposition and the RR/NL subset diagnostic (27003 §2.2-2.3), and staleness-as-inadmissibility with a measured observation-to-decision gap (18860 §3-4). Three experimental-design patterns recur strongly enough to adopt as house rules: budget-matched comparison (14853's realized-cost column, 16453's tokens-not-N sweep, 22273's equal-API-call table, 27003's equal-insights-per-retrieval), the targeted-null causal design (22273 §IV-E: an intervention must be INERT on systems already below the threshold), and publishing structural blind spots as named negative results (18860's ~0% on DOM injection).

WHAT BREAKS.
Two failure modes dominate this subset's transfer boundary and they are shared, not per-paper. First, ORACLE DEPENDENCE. 14853's utility table, 16453's unit-test and PRM potentials, 18419's BCE correctness targets, 22273's per-round exact-match rates, 25235's human Likert calibration set, and 27003's environment success bits are all deterministic or human-labelled correctness signals with exact-match equivalence. A frozen black-box speech/omni core with free-form output has none of these. The sharpest instance is 22273: a 1-2 percentage-point EIR difference flips the sign of a self-correction loop, so the quantity we would have to estimate is plausibly smaller than proxy-judge noise — the gate is well-formed and, on current instruments, not estimable. Second, INTERFACE DEPENDENCE. 16453's winning configuration needs token logprobs and mid-generation prefix re-entry (its logprob-free residue is the variant that plateaus in its own Fig.1); 18419's value probe reads final-layer hidden states and is gradient-trained; 25235's entire feature vector is score-token logprobs. All three are donors for measurement and design, never for mechanism. Two additional modality-specific breaks: 18860's defense presumes a static, cheaply re-samplable observation whose benign variation is near zero (speech has no re-observable past and continuous benign drift), and 18164/17768 presume minimal single-axis perturbations and a deterministically re-inspectable evidence channel — audio perturbations move several attributes at once, so a low stability score could reflect a genuine quality change rather than evaluator instability, collapsing the metric's semantics.

RESIDUAL GAPS AFTER THIS SUBSET.
Two of our slots remain structurally unserved here.
