(a) LABEL-FREE HEADROOM ESTIMATION: 25235 is the closest occupant and is explicitly calibration-set dependent; 14853's oracle is label-supervised; 18419's threshold is quantile-calibrated on labelled held-out data. This subset supplies the shape of the estimate and no label-free route to it. (b) MEMORY WRITE-GATES: 27003 is the best-instrumented memory paper here and contains no write-gate at all — everything is stored — so it quantifies the cost of un-gated accumulation and hands us the instruments (RR/NL, both-direction FWT/BWT, budget-matched retrieval) without any gate to compare against. Also thin in this subset: reward hacking under selection pressure is approached only obliquely (17768 and 18164 show evaluators are exploitable; 22273 shows a loop degrading a strong base) with no paper here applying explicit selection pressure to a proxy reward and measuring the degradation curve.


## 供体组 5

### 2601.21872 — WebArbiter: A Principle-Guided Reasoning Process Reward Model for Web Agents (ICLR 2026)

**域**：vision-GUI (web agent; observations are text-only accessibility trees per §3.1, so it is a GUI-task paper with text observations, not a pixel-VLM paper)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P8 (reward-as-controller: external PRM drives per-step gradient-free selection over a frozen policy), P9 (reward-admissibility: explicit principles = declared conditions under which a signal is allowed to speak; BoN-Acc as the admissibility statistic), P10 (selection pressure: BoN-Acc's all-distractors criterion is the right shape of stress test, though this paper does not sweep it), P6 (system composition: judge/policy separation with a frozen policy)

**方法面**：Casts process reward modeling as constrained text generation rather than scalar regression (§3.3): given instruction I, current observation o_p, action history (a_<p, c_<p) and two candidate (action, reasoning-trace) pairs, the model autoregressively emits a structured justification that first INDUCES task-specific principles (clarity/helpfulness, correctness of reasoning, task progress) from intent+state, applies them, then terminates in a preference verdict. Trained, two stages (§3.3.1-3.3.3): Stage 1 reasoning distillation from o3-synthesized principle-guided traces (SFT); Stage 2 RL with verifiable binary reward R∈{-1,+1} aligning verdict with the ground-truth preferred action. GRADIENT USE: yes, both stages — the mechanism is a trained 3B/7B reward model, not a prompting scheme. Deployment surface is however frozen-policy and gradient-free: the reward model is an external judge consumed by a frozen GPT-4o / GPT-4o-mini policy. Downstream control loop (§5.2.1): per-step Best-of-N with N=5 candidate actions, selection by Knockout Tournament over pairwise verdicts, on WebArena-Lite. Evaluation artifact WEBPRMBENCH (§4): 1,150 step-level preference instances over Mind2Web / WebArena / AssistantBench / WorkArena; positives from successful trajectories, four synthesized rejected alternatives per state sampled from DIVERSE policy models, rule-based filtering, expert verification, targeted false-negative auditing, side-randomization against positional bias, reasoning traces truncated to fixed length to suppress formatting cues.

**自述与显见局限**：Stated by the authors (LIMITATIONS AND FUTURE WORK, l.721-732): text-only accessibility-tree observations miss layout/spatial/visual cues, with concrete failure cases in Appendix J; training and evaluation are English-only web environments; the PRM is only exercised at inference-time trajectory search, with post-training use left open. Read-set observations beyond the authors' list: the RL stage requires expert-annotated positive/negative action pairs (WEBPRMCOLLECTION), i.e. a labeled oracle at construction time; the ablation in §5.1.3 reports 'Cold Start RL collapses out-of-domain' — reward optimization alone is unstable, so the reported robustness is attributed to distillation+principles rather than to RL; downstream search is evaluated at a single N (=5) with a single tournament mechanism, so this paper does not chart the selection-pressure curve in N.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level steps this paper leaves open, scoped to this paper: (i) it demonstrates that MAKING THE CRITERIA EXPLICIT (principles) is what buys cross-environment generalization, but never treats the principle set as an object to be audited, versioned, or gated — there is no admissibility test on a principle before it is allowed to drive a verdict; (ii) the auditable justification trace is produced but never consumed as a control signal (e.g. to abstain when principles conflict or evidence is insufficient) — the verdict is always emitted; (iii) BoN Acc is measured against Q=4 fixed distractors, so the paper does not measure what happens to reward validity as selection pressure grows; (iv) the reward is purely comparative (which of two) with no absolute confidence, so it cannot support stop/abstain/budget decisions.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：Breaks on a frozen black-box speech/omni core in four places. (1) Observation type: the entire method presumes a re-readable, discretely tokenized structured observation (accessibility tree) of the CURRENT state; a speech turn's observation is a waveform with no enumerable element list, no stable element identity across re-observation, and no lossless textual rendering — 'the action most conducive to task completion under the current context' has no analogue when the 'state' is an acoustic condition. (2) Deterministic oracle: WEBPRMBENCH positives come from verified successful trajectories with expert-annotated step labels and a task evaluator that decides completion from the final state; speech has no per-step deterministic oracle, so both the distillation corpus and the Stage-2 verifiable ±1 reward are unconstructible without paid human transcription/judgment. (3) Statable step-correctness principles: 'principle induction' assumes crisp, verbalizable criteria of progress (a click either advances or does not); acoustic-conditioned decisions (re-listen? repair a segment? abstain?) do not decompose into similarly checkable step propositions, and the principles would silently become style rubrics. (4) Free-form outputs vs. exact match: BoN Acc requires the positive to outrank ALL Q distractors under an exact-identity notion of 'the correct action'; speech outputs are free-form and admit many exact-match-inequivalent but equally acceptable renderings, so the ranking indicator 1[π(a+) ≻ π(a-)] is not well-defined without an equivalence relation this paper never needs. Also: the mechanism itself (trained 3B/7B PRM) is outside a training-free contract; only its DESIGNS and MEASUREMENTS transfer. No logprob dependence — the judge is generative, so that one constraint is satisfied.

**可借鉴**：(a) The Pairwise-Acc vs BoN-Acc pair as complementary reward-quality statistics with the explicit argument that BoN Acc (correct must outrank ALL Q distractors simultaneously, Eq. 8, Q=4) is the stricter criterion that better tracks downstream agent performance (§4.2) — directly reusable as our reward-admissibility metric, with 'exact identity' replaced by a declared equivalence relation. (b) The benchmark-construction hygiene list (§4.1) as a checklist for any reward-quality set we build: negatives sampled from MULTIPLE policy models, rule-based invalid-action filtering, expert verification, TARGETED FALSE-NEGATIVE AUDITING, side randomization against positional bias, fixed-length truncation of traces to kill formatting cues. (c) The four-arm training-design ablation lattice (§5.1.3, Tab. 3): Instruct / +Cold-Start-RL / +Cold-Start-RL+Principles / +SFT-without-Principles+RL — a clean design for isolating whether an explicit criteria layer (not just narrative rationalization) is load-bearing; the paper's finding that narrative justification without principles 'rationalizes post hoc based on surface plausibility' is the exact failure mode our judge-as-controller must be tested against. (d) The reasoning-supervision-under-matched-data-budget design (Tab. 4, full-data vs 10K with identical budgets) as a way to attribute gains to objective rather than scale. (e) Knockout Tournament as a concrete gradient-free selection mechanism over pairwise-only reward signals — usable when our reward emits comparisons rather than scalars. (f) BASELINE numbers as anchors for the shape of the claim (not the magnitude): reward-guided trajectory search lifts a frozen GPT-4o-mini policy 23.48→40.52 avg success (+17.04) and GPT-4o 31.90→47.01 (+15.11) on WebArena-Lite (Tab. 5), with the prior WebPRM at +10.86/+8.75 — i.e. a fully external, frozen-policy reward controller is worth ~15-17 points where the judge-as-reward baseline (GPT-4o-mini as its own judge) is worth ~1 point. That ~1-point self-judge result is itself the sobering anchor.

**备注**：Domain-label discipline: surfaced in a GUI lane but its observation modality is text; do not read its cross-environment generalization as evidence about visual grounding. Level discipline: this paper's numbers are effectiveness claims IN WEB NAVIGATION ONLY (H5 withheld) — the borrowables are the eval protocol, the ablation lattice, and the corpus-hygiene checklist, plus the anchor that self-judging bought ~+1 while a dedicated external reward bought ~+15.

### 2602.01485 — Predicting and improving test-time scaling laws via reward tail-guided search

**域**：text / LLM (mathematical reasoning; Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct and Qwen-7B on AMC 2023 / AIME 2024 / AIME 2025 with Skywork-Reward-V2 ORMs)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P1 (supply pricing / value-of-information: pilot samples priced against predicted return), P9 (label-free headroom estimation — this is the sharpest instance in this read set), budget-allocate (cross-cutting: convex multi-prompt allocation), P8 (reward-as-controller: a scalar reward's TAIL, not its mean, drives the allocation decision, gradient-free)

**方法面**：Two coupled contributions, both TRAINING-FREE and requiring only sample-and-score access. (1) Prediction: define V_N(s) = E[max of N i.i.d. reward draws rolled out from state s] as the 'scaling potential' of a state (§2, Eq. 1), assume a Gaussian UPPER TAIL for the reward distribution above the α-quantile (Assumption 1, §3.1, validated by Q-Q plots in appendix with R² ≈ 0.97-0.99), then estimate the tail location/scale (μ,σ) by Method of Moments on TRUNCATED-normal identities (Eq. 4a/4b, inverse Mills ratio λ(z)=φ(z)/(1-Φ(z))), deliberately estimating from the top α/2 quantile rather than the full α tail to avoid boundary irregularity. This extrapolates V_N(s) for large N from only m ≪ N samples, with a non-asymptotic bound (Theorem 1): |V̂_N - V_N| ≲ c2·sqrt(log N · log(1/δ)/m) + (1-α/2)^N. (2) Allocation and search: because V̂_n(x_i) is CONCAVE in n under the tail model, allocating a total budget n_total across K prompts to maximize Σ w_i V_{n_i}(x_i) is a CONVEX program after relaxing integrality (§3.2.1, Eq. 6) — a pilot budget n_pilot ≪ n_total estimates per-prompt scaling laws, then the convex solve gives the allocation. SLG search (Algorithm 2, §4.1): generate K intermediate states, spend m samples per state, estimate V̂_N per state, pick argmax, dump the remaining N - K·m on that single state. Only TWO sequential rounds; fully parallel within a round. Theory: vanishing regret vs. a full-information oracle (Theorem 2, VN(SLG) ≥ V_{⌊N/2m⌋}(A*) - c·log N/√m), and an explicit additive win over BoN of (√2 t/4(t+1))·σ0·sqrt(log N) in a Gaussian instance (Proposition 1). GRADIENT USE: none — this is a pure inference-time selection/allocation algorithm over a frozen policy and a frozen scalar reward.

**自述与显见局限**：Author-stated (§6 Discussion): does not extend to recursive settings like MCTS pruning; not evaluated on agentic workflows/code; explicitly flags that the error structure of IMPERFECT reward models is NOT incorporated into the scaling-law predictions. Read-set observations: the reward model is treated as the optimization oracle and the primary metric is TOTAL REWARD (Σ_x max_y r̂(y,x)) under that very reward model (§5.1 'Baselines and metrics') — so the headline 'SLG beats BoN' is measured in proxy-reward units, and this paper reports no task-accuracy or reward-hacking check; a method that concentrates budget on the highest predicted tail is exactly the shape of thing that could gain proxy reward while losing true quality, and this read set contains no measurement that rules that out. The Gaussian-tail assumption is validated empirically only on the studied model/reward pairs. State = 'first-100-token partial response' (§5.1 Implementation), an arbitrary and text-specific segmentation. Hyperparameters are scheduled (m ≈ (1/5)(ln N)³, K ≈ N/2m) rather than tuned; the K/m ablation at N=300 shows a sharp peak (K=3,m=40: 59.5 vs baseline 36.9), so 'minimal tuning' is a claim about robustness of the sign, not of the magnitude.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Scoped to this paper: (i) the tail estimate is a per-state HEADROOM ESTIMATE computed without any labels — but it is never used as a STOP or ABSTAIN signal, only as an allocate signal; predicted V̂_N compared against a cost or an acceptability floor would give exactly the stop/abstain rule the program needs, and this paper does not build it; (ii) no reward-error term enters the prediction (the authors name this themselves), so the predicted headroom is headroom-in-proxy, not headroom-in-truth — inserting a calibrated proxy-vs-truth gap term is the obvious next step; (iii) selection is winner-take-all after one estimation round (argmax over K), with no hedging or re-estimation, so mis-estimation of one state's tail is unrecoverable; (iv) the concavity result makes multi-prompt allocation convex, which is a strong lever the paper only demonstrates conceptually (§3.2.1) rather than evaluating.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：What breaks moving this to a frozen black-box speech/omni core. (1) The tail assumption is about the REWARD distribution, not the model, so it is modality-agnostic in principle — but the empirical validation (Q-Q, R²≈0.97-0.99) is on text ORM scores over math solutions; speech proxy rewards (ASR-derived confidence, judge scores over transcripts, acoustic-condition-keyed scores) may be discrete, heavily tied/atomic, or bounded-and-clipped, and the Method-of-Moments truncated-normal inversion (Eq. 4a/4b) degenerates when the top α/2 tail contains ties or a point mass — the Gaussian-tail assumption must be RE-VALIDATED per acoustic condition before any of the theory is invoked, and 'per acoustic condition' means the tail parameters (μ(s),σ(s)) become condition-keyed, which Assumption 2's uniform-constants requirement (σ(s)∈[σmin,σmax]) may violate across clean/noisy/accented regimes. (2) No deterministic oracle: the paper's metric is total proxy reward and its benchmarks are verifiable math; in speech there is neither an exact-match verifier nor a way to distinguish 'high predicted tail' from 'high predicted proxy-exploitability'. (3) 'State' as first-100-token prefix has no speech analogue — an omni core's decode does not expose a comparable resumable intermediate state, and re-rolling from a partial audio-conditioned prefix may not be an available API operation on a black-box endpoint (this is an API-capability question, not just a modelling one). (4) Free-form outputs: max-over-draws is well-defined for any scalar reward, so this part transfers; but the sqrt(log N) advantage is stated for continuous non-atomic reward distributions, and heavily-tied speech rewards flatten it. (5) Logprobs: NOT required — this is one of the few methods here that is fully compatible with a no-logprob black-box contract.

**可借鉴**：(a) The whole PREDICT-BEFORE-SPEND primitive: estimate V̂_N(s) from m ≪ N pilot samples and decide the budget from the estimate — this is the cleanest label-free headroom estimator in this read set and the most direct feed to P1/P9. (b) The specific estimator: MoM on the top-α/2 truncated tail via Eq. 4a/4b with α=0.2, plus the deliberate α/2-not-α conservatism for boundary robustness. (c) Theorem 1's error rate O(sqrt(log N/m)) as the sample-size sizing rule for our pilot phase, and the m ≍ (log N)³ / K ≍ N/2m schedule as a concrete default. (d) The convexity-of-V̂ argument turning multi-prompt budget allocation into a convex program (§3.2.1) — reusable verbatim as our budget-allocate mechanism across a batch of utterances/conditions with importance weights w_i. (e) The Q-Q + R² tail-validation protocol (appendix) as the admissibility check we must run before trusting any tail-based headroom claim on a new acoustic condition. (f) Two ablations worth copying: MEAN-SELECTION vs tail-selection (Fig 3b, 91.2 vs 61.5 at N=500) which isolates that tail-awareness, not just 'more statistics', is load-bearing; and REWARD-MODEL SWAP (Skywork-V2-Llama-8B → Skywork-V2-Qwen-8B, Fig 3c) which tests that gains are not an artifact of one reward. (g) BASELINE_ANCHOR-flavored numbers for the compute-multiplier claim: SLG at N=500 matches BoN at N=1000 on AIME24/Qwen-7B (338.1 vs 337.9), and SLG N=500 beats BoN N=700 for Llama-1B (133.9 vs 119.8) — i.e. a ~2× and ~1.4× budget amplification, in proxy-reward units.

**备注**：Theory-heavy paper with a genuinely training-free, logprob-free algorithm — unusually well-matched to our black-box contract on the mechanism side. The metric caveat is the thing to carry forward: everything reported is measured in the same proxy reward the algorithm optimizes, so this paper is a donor for the ESTIMATOR and the ALLOCATION MATH, and must not be cited as evidence about true-quality gains. Pairs adversarially with 2603.05739 and 2602.12276: SLG maximizes concentration on the highest-tail state, which is precisely the pressure those two papers say degrades proxy-vs-true agreement.

### 2602.12276 — CATTS: Confidence-Aware Test-Time Scaling for Web Agents (paper title in file: 'Agentic Test-Time Scaling for WebAgents')

**域**：vision-GUI (web agents on WebArena-Lite and GoBrowse; observations are HTML state, policy is gpt-oss-120b, LLM-as-judge eval with Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B-Instruct)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P10 (reward hacking / harmful override under selection pressure — the dose-response protocol), P9 (decision-calibration and admissibility gates: the uncertainty band in which the selector may act, with the negative band explicitly reported), budget/stop calibration (cross-cutting: gate the expensive step on a calibrated statistic), P1 (supply pricing: tokens as the priced resource, though this paper gates on uncertainty only, not on price), P8 (reward-as-controller in the weak sense: a vote-derived statistic, not a reward model, drives the decision)

**方法面**：Fully TRAINING-FREE, gradient-free, and explicitly logprob-free control policy over a frozen agent. Per step t the agent samples N candidate actions, a lightweight SEMANTIC DEDUPLICATOR LLM clusters textually-different but semantically-identical actions ('N/A' vs 'Not found') into clusters A_t, giving a vote distribution p_t(a)=n_t(a)/N (§3, Eq. 1-2). Two uncertainty statistics are computed from that distribution ALONE (§4.1, Eq. 6-7): entropy H_t = -Σ p log p, and top-1/top-2 probability margin Δ_t. The control rule (CATTS, Eq. 9) gates an expensive ARBITER (an LLM reranker over the candidate set, same model as the agent) on a threshold: execute plain majority vote when U_t ≤ τ, invoke the arbiter only when U_t > τ, with U_t^(ent)=H_t or U_t^(mrg)=1-Δ_t. The paper's empirical backbone is a regime study establishing WHEN the extra selection step is admissible: uniform scaling saturates (N=1→10 lifts WebArena-Lite 38.8→43.2%, N=10→20 adds 0.2% for 2× tokens, Tab. 1); arbiter scaling is non-monotonic (K=10 → 44.6%, K=20 → 42.0%, Tab. 2); and crucially the failure is PREDICTABLE — over 495 task-runs, tasks where the arbiter overrode a high-consensus step (Δ_t>0.7) succeed at 35.0% vs 46.9% for tasks with no such override, an 11.9-point gap at p=0.026 (Fisher's exact test), with a DOSE-RESPONSE ladder 46.9% / 36.6% / 29.6% for 0 / 1 / 2+ overrides (Fig. 3). Stratifying by average trajectory entropy, the arbiter's net advantage is -4.4% in the low band (0.0-0.3) and +4-6% in the higher bands (Fig. 4). GRADIENT USE: none anywhere.

**自述与显见局限**：Not given a dedicated limitations section in this file. Read-set observations, scoped to this paper: τ is tuned by grid sweep per benchmark (τ=0.2 for WebArena-Lite, τ=0.5 for GoBrowse) on the same benchmarks it is reported on — the authors partially defend this by reporting the sweep-averaged number (45.6%, still +2.4 over majority vote) but there is no held-out threshold-transfer experiment in this paper; the vote-derived statistics require a semantic deduplicator LLM whose clustering quality is itself an unmeasured dependency (a mis-clustering directly corrupts both H_t and Δ_t and this paper reports no sensitivity analysis for it); the correlational claim 'failed trajectories have higher entropy' (Fig. 2) is not disentangled from task difficulty; results are averaged over 3 seeds with no confidence intervals on the headline table; the arbiter is the same model as the agent, so 'arbitration' has no independent information source — its value is re-reading, not new evidence; and the study is single-policy (gpt-oss-120b) so the entropy thresholds are not shown to be model-portable.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Scoped to this paper: (i) the gate is a fixed scalar threshold on a single statistic — it does not price the arbiter call (token cost is reported but not entered into the decision rule), so a value-of-information formulation where the gate compares expected gain against the arbiter's cost is the obvious next step; (ii) the gate chooses between two selection rules but never chooses to ABSTAIN, to acquire NEW observation, or to STOP — the decision-right menu is {vote, arbitrate} only; (iii) the harmful-override finding identifies the pathology but the mitigation is avoidance (don't call the arbiter) rather than repair (constrain the arbiter to justify an override against consensus); (iv) no adaptation of N itself — N is fixed at 10 and only the arbiter is gated; (v) the uncertainty statistic is purely agreement-based, so it cannot distinguish 'confidently wrong' consensus (the exact failure mode 2603.21172 documents for entropy in the selective-prediction setting) — this read set contains both halves of that argument and neither paper combines them.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：What breaks on a frozen black-box speech/omni core. (1) The load-bearing precondition is a DISCRETE, COMPARABLE action space in which 'two samples agree' is decidable — web actions cluster because click(elem_id) and type(text) have canonical forms. Speech/omni outputs are free-form continuous-length text (or audio) with no exact-match equivalence: p_t(a)=n_t(a)/N is undefined until we supply an equivalence relation, and the semantic-deduplicator LLM this paper uses as a cheap fix becomes a first-class, error-prone component whose failures are indistinguishable from genuine disagreement. Entropy over a badly-clustered free-form sample set is a measure of surface-form variance, not decision uncertainty — this is the single biggest transfer hazard here. (2) Waveform observations: the paper's arbiter 'uses the observation context to break ties'; our arbiter would have to re-read audio, which on a black-box endpoint may mean re-paying for a full re-encode rather than re-reading a cached HTML string, changing the cost model that makes gating profitable. (3) No deterministic oracle: success is scored by LLM-as-judge here and by a task evaluator on WebArena-Lite; the 495-run override analysis needs per-task binary success labels to build the 46.9/36.6/29.6 ladder — in speech we would need a stand-in success label, and if that label is itself a judge, the harmful-override statistic becomes judge-vs-judge. (4) Acoustic-condition keying: this paper's thresholds are per-benchmark; in speech, the vote distribution's entropy is confounded by acoustic condition (noisy audio raises sample diversity for reasons unrelated to decision difficulty), so a single global τ is not admissible — thresholds must be condition-keyed, which this paper never has to face. (5) Logprobs: EXPLICITLY NOT REQUIRED, and the paper makes this a selling point against DeepConf ('DeepConf requires access to token-level log probabilities, which limits applicability to API-only models', §3.4) — this is the strongest black-box-compatibility signal in the read set. Multi-step: the method is genuinely multi-step and gradient-free.

**可借鉴**：(a) The HARMFUL-OVERRIDE PROTOCOL, essentially verbatim, as our P10 instrument: define a high-consensus regime (here Δ_t > 0.7), count per-episode overrides of consensus by the selector, stratify episode success by override count, report a dose-response ladder plus Fisher's exact test. This is a label-free-ish, deployment-shaped measurement of selection-pressure damage and it is the single most transferable artifact in this paper. (b) The NET-ADVANTAGE-BY-UNCERTAINTY-BAND table (Fig. 4: bands 0.0-0.3 / 0.3-0.5 / 0.5-0.7 / 0.7+ with signed advantage -4.4 / +5.6 / +4.1 / +4.4) as the standard way to state an admissibility region for a controller: 'this signal is allowed to act only in this band', with the negative band reported rather than hidden. (c) The two statistics themselves (entropy and top-1/top-2 margin over a clustered sample set) as black-box-compatible uncertainty proxies requiring no logprobs. (d) The gate-and-report-the-whole-sweep discipline: report both best-τ and sweep-averaged results so the gain is not a threshold-selection artifact (47.9% best-τ vs 45.6% sweep-average vs 43.2% majority-vote). (e) The accuracy-vs-token Pareto framing (Fig. 5) with token count as the priced resource — margin-gated CATTS reaches 47.9% at 405K tokens vs majority vote's 43.2% at 920K, a 56% token reduction WITH a 4.7-point gain; that is the right shape of claim for a budget-allocate result. (f) The saturation baseline itself as a BASELINE_ANCHOR: uniform per-step scaling saturates (N=10→20 buys +0.2% for 2× tokens on WebArena-Lite; on GoBrowse gains are non-monotonic), i.e. 'spend more uniformly' is a weak opponent and our program must beat the gated version, not the uniform one. (g) The negative result on RSA (Recursive Self-Aggregation, up to 80 LLM calls/step) achieving 'comparable but not better' than single-round arbitration — a warning that deeper aggregation is not automatically a stronger baseline.

**备注**：Strongest black-box-contract match in this subset: training-free, no logprobs, frozen policy, multi-step, and it argues AGAINST a logprob-dependent competitor on exactly our grounds. Its findings are web-agent effectiveness results (H5 withheld); what we borrow is the override protocol, the banded admissibility table, and the Pareto reporting format. Note the tension with 2601.21872: WebArbiter shows a strong external reward-arbiter is worth +15 points, CATTS shows an arbiter drawn from the SAME model as the policy is worth ~+0.4 unconditionally and is actively harmful on high-consensus steps — together they say the value of an arbiter tracks whether it brings independent information, which is precisely our read-out vs. new-info discriminator.

### 2602.16699 — Calibrate-Then-Act: Cost-Aware Exploration in LLM Agents

**域**：text / LLM agents (three settings: synthetic Pandora's Box, knowledge QA with optional retrieval on PopQA, and a FILEREADING coding task with selective unit testing; base model Qwen3-8B)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P1 (supply pricing / value-of-information — the most direct occupant in this subset: exploration actions priced by a discount factor, decision made against an explicit cost), P9 (decision-calibration: verbalized confidence recalibrated by isotonic regression, and the finding that CALIBRATION QUALITY is the enabling condition for the whole controller), stop/abstain/budget calibration (cross-cutting: the explore-vs-commit threshold rule), P8 (reward-as-controller in the weak sense: the discounted-reward objective shapes a gradient-free prompted policy in CTA-PROMPTED)

**方法面**：Formalizes cost-aware exploration as a POMDP W=(S,A,O,O,T,R,D_θ) with a parameterized DISCOUNT function D_θ carrying the cost, latent problem-critical variables Z, and an action space split into EXPLORATION actions and a COMMIT action; reward is R = 1[task completed] · D_θ(a_1:T) (§2). Table 1 gives a unified instantiation table across the three tasks (latent z*, prior p, action set A, observations O, cost θ, reward R). The method (§4) is a one-line intervention: explicitly inject an ESTIMATED PRIOR p̂(Z|x) into the agent's context, decoupling uncertainty estimation from action selection. Two variants: CTA-PROMPTED (training-free, frozen model, prompt-only) and CTA-RL (GRPO fine-tuning on the discounted reward with the same prompt) — GRADIENT USE: only in CTA-RL; CTA-PROMPTED is fully training-free and is where the largest qualitative effect appears. Prior estimation is the interesting part and differs per task: for QA (§4.1) the prior is the model's own probability of answering correctly WITHOUT retrieval, obtained by VERBALIZED CONFIDENCE (explicitly chosen because 'it can apply to both blackbox and glassbox model usage') and then recalibrated by ISOTONIC REGRESSION fit on a validation set — ECE drops 0.618 → 0.029 on PopQA. For FILEREADING (§4.2) the prior over file-format attributes is a BERT-tiny (4.4M param) filename→format predictor achieving 67% average accuracy, deliberately capped because the ground truth is itself sampled from a soft prior whose mode is ~67%. Oracle policies are derived in closed form (Pandora: commit iff posterior > γ; QA: retrieve iff p_ret·γ ≥ p_da) so behavior can be scored against the optimum.

**自述与显见局限**：Author-stated (Appendix A, l.1023-1038): the settings are controlled and 'simpler than open-ended agent deployments with richer action spaces, longer horizons, and noisier observations'; CTA depends on USEFUL prior estimates and 'performance can degrade when priors are highly miscalibrated or CONFIDENTLY WRONG' (sensitivity analysis in Appendix C shows smooth degradation, with noisy predictors still giving benefit); CTA gives no optimality guarantee — the model must still correctly interpret the prior, and multi-step value-of-information, posterior updates, and dependencies among multiple latent variables may exceed the base model's reasoning. Read-set observations: the isotonic recalibrator is FIT ON A VALIDATION SET WITH CORRECTNESS LABELS — this is a supervised, label-consuming component sitting inside an otherwise training-free method, and the paper does not report how many labels are needed or how the calibrator degrades under distribution shift; the FILEREADING prior predictor is trained on the task's own training split, so both RL and CTA-RL 'have access to the same data' but CTA-PROMPTED 'uses more information than PROMPTED by virtue of the trained model' — the authors flag this comparability caveat themselves; absolute effect sizes are small on the realistic tasks (contract-eligible/Without-Training half, Table 4: QA reward 0.283 → 0.293, FILEREADING 0.229 → 0.240; the RL half is measurement-only: RL 0.259 → CTA-RL 0.268, a 3.5% relative gain) while the headline 94% vs 23% optimal-match is on the synthetic Pandora task.【复核更正 wf_40068490】

**改进空间（方向级）**：Scoped to this paper: (i) the priors are STATIC per instance — there is no posterior update as observations arrive, so the value-of-information reasoning is effectively one-step even in multi-turn settings (the authors name multi-step VOI as out of reach); (ii) the calibrator is fit offline and never audited at deployment — no mechanism detects that the prior has drifted or become confidently wrong, which is exactly the failure mode they identify as most damaging; (iii) the prior is injected as TEXT and the model is trusted to do the expected-value arithmetic — there is no external arbiter that computes the threshold rule and hands the agent a decision, so the whole method's reliability is bounded by the base model's numeracy (Pandora shows non-thinking mode collapses to 20% optimal match with the SAME prior injected: the prior alone is insufficient, thinking mode is required); (iv) costs are exogenous and known exactly (γ, d_u, d_c given in the prompt) — no estimation of cost under uncertainty; (v) 'abstain' is not in the action menu, only explore-vs-commit.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：What breaks on a frozen black-box speech/omni core. (1) The BLACK-BOX-COMPATIBLE HALF TRANSFERS: verbalized confidence + isotonic recalibration needs no logits, no hidden states, no logprobs — this is the piece that survives our contract intact. (2) What does not: the ORACLE POLICY that makes every headline metric computable. Optimal-match rate, the retrieve-iff p_ret·γ ≥ p_da rule, and the Pandora optimal policy all require knowing the ground-truth answer to score p_da and p_ret; in speech there is no deterministic oracle to define 'the model would have been correct without re-listening', so 'oracle match rate' — the paper's most attractive metric — is not directly constructible. A judge-based stand-in makes the calibration target itself noisy, and the isotonic fit inherits that noise. (3) Free-form outputs without exact-match equivalence: correctness labels for the isotonic regression come from short-answer QA scoring against reference strings; speech outputs need an equivalence relation before 'correct' is even binary, so the calibration currency is more expensive and more contestable. (4) Acoustic-condition keying: the latent variable Z here is a file schema or a fact — discrete, task-intrinsic, and stable. In speech the analogous latent is the ACOUSTIC CONDITION (SNR, accent, overlap), which is continuous, shifts within a session, and is partially observable from the waveform itself — so p̂(Z|x) becomes a per-condition estimator that must be re-fit or keyed per condition rather than fit once on a validation set. (5) Waveform observation supply: the paper's exploration actions (retrieve, UNIT_TEST) are discrete, priced, and re-issuable with a well-defined information yield; 'listen again' on a black-box speech core may return the identical encoding (zero new information) rather than a new observation — the cost is real but the information gain may be nil, which is a failure mode the paper's discount-factor framing does not represent. (6) CTA-RL's GRPO half is outside our training-free constraint entirely; only CTA-PROMPTED is mechanism-eligible, and it is the weaker variant on the realistic tasks.

**可借鉴**：(a) The UNIFIED FORMALIZATION TABLE (Tab. 1: latent z*, prior p, action space A, observations O, cost θ, reward R, one row per task) as the template for specifying our own decision-rights instantiations — it makes the cost model and the latent variable explicit per task, which is exactly the discipline our P1/budget slot needs. (b) VERBALIZED CONFIDENCE + ISOTONIC REGRESSION as a black-box-legal calibration pipeline, with the reported effect size as the anchor: ECE 0.618 → 0.029 on PopQA. The 0.618 baseline is itself a useful anchor for how bad raw verbalized confidence is. (c) The CLOSED-FORM ORACLE POLICY + ORACLE-MATCH-RATE metric as an evaluation design: where a threshold rule is derivable (commit iff posterior > γ; retrieve iff p_ret·γ ≥ p_da), score the agent's DECISIONS against the optimum rather than only scoring outcomes — this separates 'made the right call' from 'got lucky'. (d) The COST-SWEEP BEHAVIORAL DIAGNOSTIC (Fig. 4/5): sweep the relative cost parameter ρ = log d_c / log d_u across {0.5,1,2,4} and plot the DISTRIBUTION OF ACTION TRACES per cost regime, with the fraction of 'guess-and-go' annotated. The finding that both PROMPTED and end-to-end RL COLLAPSE TO A STATIC POLICY (0% code-first traces at every ρ) while CTA remains adaptive is a sharp, reusable test for whether a controller is actually cost-sensitive or just tuned. This is a strong instrument for our budget-allocate claims. (e) The PRIOR-QUALITY SENSITIVITY SWEEP (Appendix C): degrade the estimator and show smooth rather than cliff-edge degradation — the right robustness experiment for any calibrated-prior component we build. (f) BASELINE anchors for honest expectations: on realistic tasks the gains are small (contract-eligible half, Table 4 Without Training: QA 0.283→0.293, FILEREADING 0.229→0.240; the RL half — RL 0.259→CTA-RL 0.268, +3.5% rel. — is measurement-only), with the dramatic numbers (94% vs 23% optimal match) confined to the synthetic setting; also PROMPTED-NONTHINK retrieves 97.7% of the time (cost-insensitive) — 'always acquire' is the default failure mode a controller must beat.【复核更正 wf_40068490】

**备注**：Two-speed paper: CTA-PROMPTED is training-free and contract-eligible; CTA-RL uses GRPO and is a donor for its MEASUREMENTS only. The single most valuable insight for our program is structural rather than numerical — the authors show that end-to-end RL does NOT learn to recalibrate ('if RLed for a long time on a QA dataset, a model is more likely to memorize specific facts than to systematically recalibrate its beliefs', §4.1) and collapses to a static cost-insensitive policy, whereas an external, explicitly-injected calibrated prior produces adaptive behavior. That is an argument that a CONTROL PLANE carrying calibration is doing something a trained policy does not do, which is directly load-bearing for our positioning — but it is a text/LLM finding and must not be reported as a cross-modality effectiveness conclusion.

### 2602.20323 — PhysMem: Scaling Test-Time Memory for Embodied Physical Reasoning

**域**：vision-VLM / embodied robotics (frozen VLM planner — Gemini-3-Flash, Gemini-ER-1.5, GPT-5.1, Qwen3-VL-235B — over an xArm6 with fin-ray grippers and RealSense cameras, plus the Reflect-VLM MuJoCo brick-insertion benchmark)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P3 (condition-keyed memory + write-gates — the primary and best-fit slot: symbolic-state keying, surprise-triggered writes, verify-then-promote, refutation, folding, decay), P5 (tool/skill credit + induction: action-level attribution, and typed AVOID/PREFER/SEQUENCE induction from clustered traces), P9 (label-free deployment monitoring: resonance as a drift/headroom proxy), P6 (system composition: three-tier memory + frozen planner + fixed executor, with the executor deliberately fixed to make attribution valid)

**方法面**：TRAINING-FREE test-time memory over a frozen VLM planner; GRADIENT USE: none (explicitly 'without updating model parameters'; the design separates high-level VLM planning from low-level motion control precisely so that measured gains are attributable to reasoning, not to a learned controller). The core is a 'scientific memory loop' with four phases (§III-C, Algorithm 1). (1) Experience collection with RESONANCE CHECKING: each experience e=(o,ω,r,c,s) records observation, chosen option, binary outcome, context, and a SYMBOLIC STATE s (discrete features: action type, object properties). A resonance score ρ(e,P_active) = |{p ∈ P_active : consistent(e,p)}| / |P_active| (Eq. 3) measures how well the outcome matched the principles that were actually applied; ρ<1 marks a SURPRISE and triggers consolidation, ρ=1 merely reinforces. This is surprise-driven write-gating — the memory does not write on every episode. (2) Hypothesis generation: experiences are clustered by symbolic-state similarity; clusters with |C_k| ≥ n_min are handed to a reflection model f_φ together with the EXISTING principle and hypothesis sets (to suppress duplicates), producing TYPED hypotheses — AVOID ('don't do X when Y', from failures), PREFER ('do X when Y', from successes), SEQUENCE ('do X before Y'). (3) ACTION-LEVEL ATTRIBUTION: confidence is updated using only experiences where the SPECIFIC action the hypothesis is about was attempted (Eq. 5), explicitly to isolate that action's effect from episode-level confounds. (4) VERIFY-THEN-PROMOTE: a hypothesis is promoted to a principle only at conf(h) ≥ τ_p ≈ 0.8 WITH |E_support| ≥ 3; on promotion, source experiences are FOLDED into the principle and deleted (Eq. 6, compressing episodic memory); hypotheses with conf ≤ τ_r and ≥2 contradicting experiences are REFUTED and removed. Three memory tiers (§III-D): episodic (raw, capacity-bounded N_max), working (hypotheses under test with confidence), long-term (verified principles with importance decaying at γ=0.995 to forget stale knowledge). Retrieval (§III-E) is symbolic filtering (action type + object properties) then semantic ranking, top-k=5, injected into the VLM prompt as typed constraints WITH CONFIDENCE SCORES SHOWN (Fig. 3).

**自述与显见局限**：Author-stated (§VI): observation space is visual + outcome feedback only, and richer signals (tactile, force, AUDIO — they cite audio sensing revealing material properties inaccessible to vision) could accelerate principle discovery; text-based principles 'excel at discrete rules but struggle with continuous dynamics like trajectories or force profiles' and language cannot fully capture continuous physical interaction; the work covers high-level planning only, with integration into end-to-end VLA execution open. Read-set observations: verification is by PHYSICAL INTERACTION with a real object, i.e. the environment returns ground truth on demand — the entire write-gate rests on an oracle that is free and repeatable here; the ablation shows w/o Forgetting scores 91%/78%/36% vs full 89%/76%/39% at 3.4× the tokens, i.e. forgetting is a COST optimization that costs a little accuracy on easy/medium, which the paper reports honestly but which means the forgetting mechanism is not accuracy-motivated; memory benefit scales with backbone capability (+23% for Gemini-3-Flash vs +5% for Gemini-ER-1.5 on medium), so the authors concede memory 'amplifies existing capabilities rather than compensating for fundamental limitations'; real-world tasks run 10-20 episodes with 10 trials per OOD condition — small n, and the transfer table (Tab. III) reports successes as x/10.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Scoped to this paper: (i) verification is unconditional — every hypothesis is tested by acting, with no pricing of the verification act itself, so there is no value-of-information decision about WHETHER a hypothesis is worth testing; (ii) the promotion thresholds (τ_p=0.8, |E_support|≥3, τ_r with ≥2 contradictions, γ=0.995) are fixed constants with a sensitivity analysis in appendix but no principled derivation from a target error rate — a calibrated promotion gate targeting a stated false-principle rate is the obvious step; (iii) the symbolic state s is hand-designed per task, which is what makes clustering and retrieval work; (iv) principles are additive and never CONFLICT-RESOLVED — the paper notes 'persistent failures occur when learned principles conflict or do not apply' but provides no arbitration mechanism; (v) no adversarial/poisoning treatment: the loop assumes the reflection model's hypotheses are honest and the environment's feedback is not gamed, so there is no defense against a hypothesis that is confirmed for the wrong reason; (vi) OOD detection is post-hoc (they observe transfer fails for new ball types) rather than a runtime signal that would suppress stale principles before they fire — though ρ is arguably exactly that signal, used for writing but not for gating READS.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：The decisive break: VERIFICATION-BY-INTERACTION. PhysMem's entire write-gate is 'test each hypothesis against new observations instead of applying retrieved experience directly', and it can do this because pushing a ball or stacking a stone returns a binary outcome r ∈ {0,1} from the physical world for free, on demand, repeatably. A frozen black-box speech/omni core has NO deterministic oracle: there is no action we can take that returns ground truth about whether a transcription or an answer was correct, so a hypothesis like 'in this acoustic condition prefer re-listening before committing' cannot be promoted by a τ_p=0.8 / |E_support|≥3 rule without an external label source — the confidence update (Eq. 5) would be driven by a judge, making promotion a judge-agreement statistic rather than a verification. Further breaks: (2) SYMBOLIC STATE — clustering, retrieval filtering, and resonance's consistent(e,p) all require discrete features (action type, object properties); the speech analogue is the ACOUSTIC CONDITION, which is continuous, drifts within a session, and is only partially inferable from a waveform, so 'cluster by symbolic state' becomes 'cluster by an estimated condition key' with estimation error propagating into every downstream gate. (3) OBSERVATION SUPPLY — the paper's o is a re-acquirable multi-camera image of a stable scene; re-observing a speech turn does not yield a new view, so the 'collect stronger evidence in subsequent rounds' loop has no cheap analogue. (4) FREE-FORM OUTPUTS — outcomes here are binary task success (bricks placed / tower stood); speech outputs need a declared equivalence relation before r ∈ {0,1} exists. (5) CONTINUOUS-DYNAMICS LIMIT — the authors' own caveat that text principles struggle with continuous dynamics applies with extra force to prosody, timing, and acoustic phenomena. (6) No logprobs, no gradients, no hidden states are used anywhere — on THOSE axes it is fully compatible with our contract. Note the authors themselves gesture at audio as an untapped modality (§VI-a), which is a positioning fact, not an effectiveness claim.

**可借鉴**：(a) The VERIFY-THEN-PROMOTE WRITE-GATE as a three-state memory lifecycle (experience → hypothesis-under-test → verified principle) with explicit promotion (conf ≥ τ_p AND |E_support| ≥ 3) and REFUTATION (conf ≤ τ_r AND ≥2 contradicting) — this is the cleanest write-gate design in this read set and it is a gate on EVIDENCE, not on similarity. (b) The RESONANCE SCORE ρ (Eq. 3) as a label-free, deployment-time monitor of whether stored knowledge still predicts outcomes: it doubles as a surprise-triggered write signal and as a headroom/drift indicator, and the paper shows a usable trajectory (ρ ≈ 0.2 → 0.9 across 10 episodes, crossing 0.7 at episode 5-6) plus the honest observation that ρ 'asks the planner to actually predict the outcome rather than just produce an acceptable result' — i.e. it separates genuine understanding from safe-but-suboptimal strategies that inflate success rate. (c) ACTION-LEVEL ATTRIBUTION (Eq. 5) — credit a hypothesis only from experiences where its specific action was attempted, never from episode-level success. Directly reusable for P5 tool/skill credit. (d) TYPED memory entries (AVOID / PREFER / SEQUENCE) with confidence shown in the prompt — and the striking scaling observation that on HARD tasks 68% of learned principles are AVOIDANCE constraints (vs 41% on medium), i.e. the system improves mainly by ruling moves OUT. That is a design hint for what a speech control plane's memory should mostly contain. (e) MEMORY FOLDING + importance decay (γ=0.995) as an explicit compression/forgetting mechanism with a measured token cost (w/o Forgetting = 3.4× tokens). (f) The FIVE-PARADIGM BASELINE SUITE, all run frozen-base with identical budget and top-K=5: No Memory / Direct Retrieval / MemER-style retrieval / MemoryVLA-style token memory / Reflexion-style — with the crucial methodological note that they built TRAINING-FREE PROXIES of the two methods that normally require parameter updates, in order to isolate the retrieval mechanism inside the frozen-base setting. That is exactly the comparison discipline our program needs. (g) BASELINE ANCHORS (Tab. II, 100 episodes/cell, 3 seeds, easy/medium/hard): No Memory 83/53/28; Direct Retrieval 48/23/8 (raw episodic replay is WORSE THAN NO MEMORY at every difficulty — the strongest negative result in this read set for similarity-only retrieval); MemER-style 85/53/19 (lands exactly at no-memory on medium); MemoryVLA-style 69/35/13 at 2.5× tokens (more context is worse); Reflexion-style 86/61/31; PhysMem 89/76/39. (h) The COMPONENT ABLATION at 1.0× token normalization: w/o Resonance 81/58/21, w/o Verification 85/64/27, w/o Working Memory 84/69/28 — verification and resonance are separately load-bearing and their importance SCALES WITH DIFFICULTY. (i) The 2×2 TRANSFER ABLATION (prior knowledge × test-time adaptation, Tab. III) which cleanly exhibits both a transfer success (Balanced Stacking: prior alone 8/10) and a transfer FAILURE (Ball Navigation with new ball types: prior alone ≈ zero-shot, adaptation essential) — the honest reporting of when memory does NOT transfer is the design we should copy for condition-keyed memory.

**备注**：Domain label matters here: this is embodied robotics with a physical oracle, and its headline numbers are physical-manipulation effectiveness results (H5 withheld). The single most important cross-domain finding to carry — as a HYPOTHESIS to test, not a conclusion — is that DIRECT RETRIEVAL UNDERPERFORMS NO MEMORY (48/23/8 vs 83/53/28), i.e. unverified similarity-based memory is actively harmful, and the harm grows with difficulty. If that shape holds in speech, our P3 slot must be built around a write-gate from day one rather than around a retriever. The corresponding hazard is that PhysMem's gate is powered by an oracle we do not have, so designing the label-free substitute for verify-then-promote is precisely the step remaining on top.

### 2603.05739 — Revisiting the (Sub)Optimality of Best-of-N for Inference-Time Alignment

**域**：text / LLM (pure theory — no experiments in this file; the setting is abstract inference-time alignment over a reference LM policy and a learned reward model)　**供体级别**：CONSTRUCT_DONOR　**喂给**：P10 (reward hacking under selection pressure — the primary slot: an explicit hacking term, a proof that it is unavoidable for argmax-BoN, and a monotone alternative), P8 (reward-as-controller: formalizes what a scalar proxy reward can and cannot buy under gradient-free selection), P9 (admissibility: ε_pw as the reward-quality currency and M as an explicit admissibility/conservatism dial), budget-allocate (cross-cutting: N decoupled from M)

**方法面**：A theoretical re-analysis of Best-of-N under a CHANGED OBJECTIVE, plus a new algorithm. Setup (§2.1) is the 'sample-and-evaluate' oracle model (Definition 1): the learner may sample y ~ π_ref independently and query a learned reward r̂(y) on any sample; efficiency is measured by N, the number of samples. GRADIENT USE: none — everything is inference-time selection over a frozen reference model. Three re-definitions carry the paper. (1) OBJECTIVE: replace expected true reward with WIN-RATE, R^{r*}(π) = P(r*(y) > r*(y')) + ½P(r*(y)=r*(y')) for y~π, y'~π_ref (Eq. 1), argued to be the ground-truth notion because reward models are TRAINED by pairwise comparison and evaluated by win-rate (AlpacaEval-style), and because a numerical r* arguably does not exist beyond the ordinal information it induces. (2) REWARD-MODEL QUALITY: replace mean-squared error ε²_RM with PAIRWISE WIN-RATE ERROR ε_pw(r̂) = E_{y,y'~π_ref}|φ_r̂(y,y') - φ_{r*}(y,y')| (Eq. 2), which is SCALE-INVARIANT (multiplying r̂ by a constant changes nothing about selection, and correctly changes nothing about ε_pw) and matches how reward models are actually trained; the paper argues ε_pw ≪ ε²_RM under Bradley-Terry with finite samples, and shows the two are in general incomparable (Appendix A). (3) REFERENCE-MODEL QUALITY: replace χ²-divergence with E_M-DIVERGENCE, E_M(π*‖π_ref) = E_{y~π_ref}[(dπ*/dπ_ref(y) - M)_+] (Eq. 3), motivated by its known equivalence to the sample complexity of approximate rejection sampling, and related to coverage. Results: Theorem 3 upper-bounds BoN's win-rate regret by N·ε_pw·log(1/ε_pw) + E_{N/log(1/ε_pw)}(π*‖π_ref) — the FIRST term is exactly the reward-hacking term (as N grows, BoN is more likely to pick outputs where r̂ and r* disagree), the second is the coverage/search term. Theorem 4 gives a matching lower bound over ALL sample-and-evaluate algorithms (inf_M {M·ε + E_M}), and Proposition 1 a matching computational lower bound — so BoN is statistically AND computationally optimal for win-rate up to a log factor, contradicting the prior verdict (Huang et al.) that BoN is suboptimal under expected reward. Then the new algorithm (§4): E_M-REGULARIZED BoN solves max_π E_π[r̂] - R_max·E_M(π‖π_ref) (Eq. 6), and Lemma 1 shows the optimal policy has a remarkably simple closed form — π_M(·) = π_ref(· | r̂(·) ≥ λ) where λ is the (1-1/M)-quantile of r̂ under π_ref. Implementation: draw N samples, return one UNIFORMLY AT RANDOM from the top-⌈N/M⌉ by r̂, with randomized tie-breaking; TV distance to the ideal π_M is ≤ 1/N. Theorem 5 bounds its regret by E_M(π*‖π_ref) + M·ε_pw + 1/N — the key structural change is that N (the compute knob) is DECOUPLED from M (the regularization knob), so performance is MONOTONE in N. Proposition 2 shows the prior χ²-regularized BoN can be arbitrarily worse (factor c for any c>1) under the win-rate objective.

**自述与显见局限**：Author-stated (§7): no unifying paradigm covering both expected-reward and win-rate objectives, so it is unresolved when tuned BoN is optimal vs. when sophistication is needed; the sample-and-evaluate framework excludes ADAPTIVE sampling and other richer interaction; and — most importantly for us — the proposed variant 'may still be too conservative in practice due to its reliance on information-theoretic guarantees', with 'leveraging the rich structure that learned representations provide' named as the way past that. Read-set observations, scoped to this paper: THERE ARE NO EXPERIMENTS in this file — every claim is a theorem under stated assumptions (bounded rewards 0 ≤ r*, r̂ ≤ R_max; fixed prompt with expectations over prompts deferred; π* ≪ π_ref; non-atomic π_ref for the lower bound). M must still be tuned, and the paper says plainly it expects 'BoN to remain the dominant alignment algorithm when monotonicity is not required'. Neither ε_pw nor E_M is shown to be estimable from data anywhere in this file — they are analysis quantities, so the guarantee is not directly instantiable as a deployment-time certificate.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Scoped to this paper: (i) ε_pw and E_M are unestimated — a deployment-time estimator for the pairwise win-rate error of our reward, with the resulting M chosen to hit a stated hacking budget, is the step that converts this theory into a control-plane component; (ii) the objective is single-shot selection from i.i.d. draws, so nothing here covers MULTI-STEP control, repair, stop, or abstain — the decision right is 'select', period; (iii) no adaptivity: the authors flag that adaptive sampling is outside the framework, which is exactly where 2602.01485's tail-guided allocation lives (the two papers are complementary and, on selection pressure, in tension); (iv) the top-quantile selector discards the reward's ordinal information WITHIN the accepted set (uniform over the top N/M), which is maximally conservative — a graded rule interpolating between argmax and uniform-over-quantile is unexplored here; (v) win-rate is defined against π_ref (or a comparator q, §6) via an oracular preference source, and the paper does not treat the case where THAT judge is itself corruptible.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：What breaks on a frozen black-box speech/omni core. (1) The framework's requirements are unusually mild and mostly SURVIVE: sample-and-evaluate needs only i.i.d. sampling from a frozen policy plus scalar scoring of a sample — no logprobs, no gradients, no hidden states, no attention. The top-⌈N/M⌉-then-uniform selector is implementable verbatim against a black-box endpoint. That makes this the most contract-compatible MECHANISM in this read set. (2) What breaks is the SEMANTICS. Win-rate presupposes an 'oracular alignment source (either human labellers or a strong LM)' that renders a pairwise preference treated as ground truth; for speech there is no validated pairwise judge, and substituting an LM judge over transcripts silently redefines r* as 'what the judge prefers about the text', discarding acoustic content — so the paper's central move (win-rate IS the ground truth because that's how rewards are trained and evaluated) is an assumption we would be IMPORTING, not inheriting. (3) TIES: φ_r(y,y') half-counts ties, and the analysis assumes non-atomic π_ref for the lower bound; speech reward proxies (WER-derived scores, discrete judge grades) produce heavy tie mass, which inflates ε_pw mechanically and degrades the quantile construction (λ as the (1-1/M)-quantile is ill-defined at a point mass — the paper's randomized tie-breaking handles this operationally but the guarantee's sharpness degrades). (4) FREE-FORM OUTPUTS WITHOUT EXACT-MATCH EQUIVALENCE: the theory never needs an equivalence relation because it works entirely with the ordinal comparison φ, so this is actually a point of RESILIENCE — but it means the whole apparatus is only as meaningful as the comparator judge. (5) ACOUSTIC-CONDITION KEYING: ε_pw and E_M are defined under a single fixed prompt with a single π_ref; in speech the reward's pairwise error and the reference policy's coverage both vary by acoustic condition, so a single global M is not admissible — M must be condition-keyed, and the paper's uniform-constant analysis gives no guidance for that. (6) NO ORACLE: r* is a mathematical device here, never queried; that is fine for theory but means the paper offers no way to VERIFY empirically that hacking has been eliminated in our setting.

**可借鉴**：(a) The E_M-REGULARIZED BoN MECHANISM as a drop-in, gradient-free, logprob-free anti-hacking selector: draw N, take the top-⌈N/M⌉ by proxy reward, return one UNIFORMLY AT RANDOM with randomized tie-breaking (Lemma 1 + §4). It requires no auxiliary error model, no online estimation, no rejection sampling, and no extra compute beyond BoN itself — this is the cheapest reward-hacking mitigation in this read set, and its cheapness is the point (contrast 2604.04648's pessimistic BoN, which the scout flags as requiring a fitted auxiliary error model). (b) The DECOUPLING PRINCIPLE as a design rule for our budget slot: separate the compute knob N from the selection-pressure knob M so that spending more compute cannot increase hacking exposure. Monotonicity-in-N is a property we should demand of any selector we ship, and this paper gives both the reason and the construction. (c) ε_pw (Eq. 2) as the RIGHT MEASURE OF REWARD QUALITY for a selection-based controller — scale-invariant, ordinal, and matching how any pairwise/judge-based reward is actually produced. Adopting ε_pw instead of a correlation or MSE against a gold score is a concrete methodological choice our evaluation slot can make. (d) The DECOMPOSITION IN THEOREM 3 as a reporting template: total regret = a HACKING term growing in N (N·ε_pw·log(1/ε_pw)) + a COVERAGE term shrinking in N (E_{N/log(1/ε_pw)}). Reporting our empirical selection results in this two-term shape — 'how much of the curve's turnover is proxy error vs. how much is exhausted coverage' — is exactly the honest accounting P10 needs. (e) The METRIC-CHOICE-CHANGES-THE-VERDICT lesson, which is the paper's real contribution: the SAME algorithm (BoN) is 'statistically suboptimal' under expected reward and 'statistically and computationally optimal' under win-rate. Any claim we make about a selector's optimality must state its objective, and any inherited claim from prior work must be checked against the objective it was proven under. (f) E_M-divergence / coverage as the formal vocabulary for 'can the frozen core even produce the thing we want' — a cleaner statement of the headroom question than χ²-divergence, with the rejection-sampling interpretation (M samples needed to approximate π* within ε) giving M an operational meaning.

**备注**：Verdict is CONSTRUCT_DONOR rather than PROTOCOL_DONOR because there are no experiments and no estimable statistic in this file — we adopt its FORMALISM (ε_pw, E_M, the two-term regret decomposition) and its MECHANISM (top-quantile-then-uniform), not a measurement protocol. It is also the sharpest available corrective to a hazard our own program faces: 2602.01485 in this same read set proposes concentrating budget on the state with the highest predicted reward TAIL, which is exactly the direction Theorem 3's first term says amplifies proxy-vs-true disagreement. Reading the two together, the composition question 'can tail-guided allocation be made monotone via E_M-style quantile regularization' is a well-posed step that neither paper takes.

### 2603.16664 — Kestrel: Grounding Self-Refinement for LVLM Hallucination Mitigation

**域**：vision-VLM (LVLM hallucination mitigation on POPE across MS-COCO / A-OKVQA / GQA and MME-Hallucination; backbones Qwen3-VL 8B and InternVL3.5 8B; external tool is SAM3 promptable concept segmentation)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P9 (decision-calibration and admissibility gates — primary: the evidence-gated update is a repair-admissibility gate, and the over-correction measurement is the calibration evidence), repair decision right (cross-cutting: when is a controller permitted to overwrite the core's output), P10 (over-correction as the selection/repair-pressure damage term, measured rather than netted out), P3 (external memory in the weak sense: the stateful evidence chain accumulating across rounds), P1 (observation supply: typed evidence acquisition routed by claim type — though unpriced here), stop/budget (the K=3 cap plus stability early-stop with a reported per-round funnel)

**方法面**：TRAINING-FREE, frozen-backbone, four-step iterative pipeline; GRADIENT USE: none. (1) Initialization (§3.1): query the LVLM for an initial answer Â(0), then rewrite the question-answer decision into a small set of VISUALLY CHECKABLE CLAIMS, each anchored to one or two concrete visual entities, and TYPE each claim (existence / color / count / position) — the type routes which grounding is invoked. (2) Agent grounding (§3.2): SAM3 takes the claim's entities as concept prompts and returns matched instances, from which three visual evidence forms are collected — segmentation overlays, instance bounding boxes, and crop-and-zoom views. These are then CONVERTED INTO STRUCTURED TEXTUAL EVIDENCE per claim type by deterministic rules where possible (existence = instance count > 0; count = number of predicted masks; position = derived from union bounding box and inter-centroid relation; color = an LVLM observation conditioned on the masked crop-zoom), and EACH TEXTUAL EVIDENCE ITEM CARRIES A CITATION IDENTIFIER. (3) Claim-level verification (§3.3): an LVLM-as-judge is instructed to decide ONLY from the provided evidence and to CITE it, emitting per claim a verdict from a THREE-VALUED set {supported, contradicted, insufficient}, a confidence score, and a citing rationale. Claim verdicts consolidate to a top-level verdict: contradicted if ANY claim is confidently refuted with cited evidence; supported ONLY if ALL claims are confidently supported; insufficient otherwise. (4) EVIDENCE-GATED SELF-REFINEMENT (§3.4): an answer flip is permitted only when evidence STRENGTH, CONFIDENCE, and EVIDENCE COVERAGE JOINTLY satisfy predefined criteria; otherwise the current answer is preserved and the loop collects stronger evidence next round. Refinement is STATEFUL — the revision conditions on prior rounds' claims, evidence, and decisions, and proposes a new claim set prioritizing claims that remain uncertain or are implicated by contradictions. Stopping: max K=3 iterations, early stop when the answer is stable with two consecutive supported verdicts. Concrete thresholds: SAM3 confidence 0.5 with a 0.35 recheck threshold for existence claims; refinement confidence thresholds in [0.82, 0.90] varying by claim type.

**自述与显见局限**：No dedicated limitations section in this file. Read-set observations, scoped to this paper: latency is 'substantially higher than a single-pass LVLM inference' (§4.3) and the authors motivate K=3 by a cost/benefit argument rather than by an accuracy ceiling — best POPE accuracy is already reached after the SECOND iteration, so the third iteration is near-dead weight on a small subset; the confidence thresholds [0.82,0.90] are per-claim-type constants with no stated derivation and no sensitivity analysis in this file; the pipeline is evaluated on CLOSED-FORM, CLAIM-DECOMPOSABLE benchmarks (POPE is yes/no object-existence style; MME-Hallucination decomposes into existence/count/position/color) — the claim-decomposition step is exactly what those benchmarks make easy, and this paper does not test it on open-ended generation; the LVLM judge and the answering LVLM are the same backbone, so verification is not independent except through the SAM3 evidence; absolute gains are modest on the easy splits and concentrated on popular/adversarial splits; the human study is n=60 with 5 methods compared.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Scoped to this paper: (i) the gate is a conjunctive threshold rule over strength/confidence/coverage with hand-set constants — a calibrated gate targeting a stated over-correction rate (which they already measure!) is the natural step; (ii) evidence acquisition is unpriced: SAM3 is invoked for every claim of every unresolved answer, with no value-of-information decision about which claim is worth grounding; (iii) the three-valued verdict includes 'insufficient' but the SYSTEM never abstains — insufficient only means 'keep the current answer and iterate', so the abstain decision right is present in the signal but absent from the action menu; (iv) claim decomposition is prompted and unvalidated — a wrong decomposition silently makes all downstream verification vacuous, and this paper measures no decomposition quality; (v) the stopping rule is a fixed K plus a stability heuristic rather than a budget-vs-expected-gain calculation, even though the per-round case counts they report (9000 → 4978 → 477) are exactly the data needed to build one.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：What breaks on a frozen black-box speech/omni core. (1) The EVIDENCE SOURCE. Kestrel's whole gate rests on evidence produced by a tool whose output is CHECKABLE AGAINST A PERSISTENT, RE-INTERROGABLE OBSERVATION: SAM3 can be re-prompted on the same image, crop-and-zoom yields genuinely NEW information (higher effective resolution on a sub-region), and existence/count evidence is derived DETERMINISTICALLY from mask counts, not from a language model's opinion. Speech has no equivalent: the analogous 'evidence' (ASR spans, speaker-diarization segments, VAD boundaries, re-decoded confidences) is produced by models that share the same failure modes as the core, and 'zoom in on this region' has no acoustic counterpart that returns strictly more information — re-running a decoder on the same audio segment is a RE-READ, not new evidence. This is the information-boundary hazard in its clearest form, and it is what would silently convert Kestrel's gate from an evidence check into a self-consistency check. (2) CLAIM DECOMPOSABILITY: existence/color/count/position are crisp, visually checkable, and exhaustively typed for these benchmarks; speech outputs (free-form answers, transcriptions, dialogue turns) do not decompose into similarly checkable atomic propositions with an evidence type per proposition, and there is no exact-match equivalence to say whether a claim was verified. (3) NO DETERMINISTIC ORACLE for the final evaluation: POPE/MME give binary correctness, which is what makes the four-way prediction-transition statistic (below) computable; in speech that statistic needs a declared equivalence relation or a judge. (4) ACOUSTIC-CONDITION KEYING: SAM3's confidence threshold (0.5, recheck 0.35) is a fixed constant because image statistics are comparable across the benchmark; any speech evidence tool's confidence is strongly condition-dependent, so a global threshold is not admissible. (5) COMPATIBLE AXES: no gradients, no hidden states, no logprobs, frozen backbone, multi-step, external tool — on the black-box contract itself, Kestrel is a clean match, and the LVLM-judge-with-citation-requirement is implementable against an API-only core.

**可借鉴**：(a) The EVIDENCE-GATED UPDATE as the canonical repair-admissibility gate: a repair is permitted ONLY when evidence strength, confidence, and COVERAGE jointly clear thresholds; otherwise preserve and gather more. The three-way conjunction (not just confidence) is the design detail worth copying — coverage in particular is the guard against a confidently-cited but partial justification. (b) The THREE-VALUED VERDICT {supported, contradicted, insufficient} with asymmetric consolidation — contradicted if ANY claim is confidently refuted, supported only if ALL are — is directly reusable as our admissibility logic, and the explicit 'insufficient' state is the hook our abstain decision right needs (which this paper leaves unused). (c) The CITATION-ID DISCIPLINE: every evidence item carries an identifier, the judge is instructed to decide only from provided evidence and must cite; the result is an auditable evidence chain. This is the mechanism that makes the gate inspectable rather than a black-box confidence number. (d) THE FOUR-WAY PREDICTION-TRANSITION STATISTIC (Fig. 4): classify every instance before/after refinement into {correctly preserved, error corrected, OVER-CORRECTED, incorrectly preserved}. This is the single most valuable borrowable here — it is the correct way to report any repair mechanism, because it exposes the damage (over-correction) alongside the benefit rather than netting them into a delta. Our P9/P10 slots should adopt this table as a standard output. (e) The PER-ROUND FUNNEL as budget evidence (§4.3): iteration 1 runs on all 9000 cases, only 4978 reach round 2, only 477 reach round 3 — reporting the case count per round alongside per-round latency is how to show a stopping rule is actually saving compute, and the observation that peak accuracy arrives at round 2 justifies the cap empirically. (f) The NINE-ROW COMPONENT ABLATION LATTICE (Tab. 4, s1-s9 over six binary components: grounding agent / structured textual evidence / claim-level verification / evidence-gated update / self-refinement / history-aware): baseline 87.37, naive self-refinement alone 87.44 (+0.07), grounding agent alone 87.50 (+0.13), full 89.45. The headline lesson — NEITHER ITERATIVE REVISION NOR TOOL ACCESS IS SUFFICIENT ALONE, and removing the evidence-gated update from the full system costs 0.15 (s6 89.30 vs s9 89.45) — is a lattice design we can reuse wholesale. (g) The EVIDENCE-TYPE ABLATIONS (Tab. 5 visual: segment/box/crop-zoom; Tab. 6 textual: exist/count/color/position) showing every evidence form is non-redundant, with removal of position (748.33) and count (751.67) hurting most vs. full 760.00 — the design for isolating which observation channel is actually paying for itself. (h) BASELINE ANCHORS: on POPE the training-free baselines (VCD, OPERA, RITUAL, Woodpecker, DeGF) cluster within ~1-2 points of each other and Kestrel's margin over the best of them is +1.38/+1.47 average, while its margin over the raw backbone is +3.31/+3.03 — i.e. the training-free field is crowded and tightly packed, and beating the BACKBONE is much easier than beating the field. Also: the Qwen3-VL agent WITH a zoom-in tool sometimes UNDERPERFORMS the plain baseline (A-OKVQA adversarial 78.03 vs 81.87), a clean instance of tool access hurting without a gate.

**备注**：Its numbers are LVLM-hallucination effectiveness results (H5 withheld) — we borrow the gate architecture, the three-valued verdict, the citation discipline, the four-way transition statistic, and the ablation lattice, never the conclusion that grounding-plus-refinement works in another modality. The critical caution to carry into design: the reason Kestrel's gate is a genuine admissibility gate rather than a self-consistency ritual is that SAM3 supplies information the LVLM did not already have. Reproducing the architecture in speech without an equivalent independent-evidence source would reproduce the form and lose the substance — precisely the read-out vs. new-info distinction our program has to enforce.

### 2603.21172 — Entropy Alone is Insufficient for Safe Selective Prediction in LLMs

**域**：text / LLM (short-form QA: TriviaQA, BioASQ, MedicalQA; four model families at 3B-8B: Ministral-8B-Instruct-2410, Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct, Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507, Gemma-3-4B)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P9 (decision-calibration and admissibility gates — primary: TCE and E-AURC are the metrics, and 'entropy alone is inadmissible as a steering signal' is the finding), abstain/stop calibration (cross-cutting: the risk-coverage-at-target-α framing IS the abstain decision right formalized), P10 (indirectly: a risk score with a dead zone is exactly what a controller under selection pressure will exploit — low-entropy hallucinations are 'free' wins for any selector that treats low entropy as quality)

**方法面**：A measurement paper about ABSTENTION POLICY, not a new mechanism. Selective-prediction setup (§2.1): given input x and answer ŷ with binary hallucination indicator L(x), the system answers iff a scalar risk score r(x) ≤ τ; given a TARGET RISK α, τ is tuned on a held-out CALIBRATION SET as the largest τ whose observed hallucination rate among answered examples is ≤ α. Risk scores compared (§2.2): three entropy-family signals — Sequence NLL (mean negative log-likelihood, no extra inference), Semantic Entropy (cluster K sampled completions by mutual entailment, then measure semantic dispersion), and Semantic Entropy Probe (a linear probe on PROMPT REPRESENTATIONS predicting binarized SE, amortizing SE's sampling cost) — versus a Probability-of-Correctness (PC) probe, a logistic regression on prompt representations trained on EXTERNAL CORRECTNESS LABELS, with risk u_pc = 1 - p_pc. The COMBINATION is a two-feature logistic regression over (u_ent, u_pc) fit on a held-out training split. GRADIENT USE: probes are trained (logistic regression / linear probe on frozen activations) — light, but supervised and requiring internals. The paper's real contribution is the EVALUATION MOVE (§2.3): replace global detection metrics (AUROC/AUPRC) with DEPLOYMENT-FACING ones — E-AURC (excess area under the risk-coverage curve relative to an oracle ranking that abstains on all hallucinations before any correct example, unitless in [0,1]) and TARGET CALIBRATION ERROR, TCE = E_{α~A}|R_test(α) - α|, i.e. how far the REALIZED risk lands from the risk you asked for, swept over an operational range A=[0.05,0.30] chosen with the lower bound reflecting benchmark difficulty and the upper bound approximating the base hallucination rate 'above which abstention yields no benefit'. Findings: entropy efficacy is strongly MODEL-DEPENDENT (on TriviaQA, SE AUROC 0.884 vs PC 0.813 for Llama, but 0.844 vs 0.888 for Qwen; Spearman ρ between the two risk scores only 0.34-0.54), and the named failure mode is the CONFIDENTLY WRONG REGIME — a substantial mass of answers collapse to SE ≈ 0, mixing correct answers and hallucinations into a vertical line (Fig. 2), while the PC probe spreads them across a range of logits. The deployment consequence (§3.4, Fig. 3, Ministral-8B on BioASQ) is a RISK FLOOR: entropy-based risk-coverage curves fail to enter the high-trust regime (α ≤ 0.15) at non-trivial coverage, so a threshold tuned to a strict α yields a realized hallucination rate well above it — the calibration curve diverges sharply from the diagonal at strict targets, while PC-combined methods track it.

**自述与显见局限**：Author-stated (Limitations section): restricted to SHORT-FORM QA where answers are judgeable against reference strings, and 'it is unclear whether the confidently wrong failure mode, or the complementarity between entropy and correctness probe signals, generalizes to long-form generation, multi-step reasoning, or open-ended tasks where hallucinations can be harder to define'; only four model families at 3B-8B, with the Qwen-specific degradation pattern possibly varying across architectures and scales. Read-set observations: the PC probe requires (a) access to internal PROMPT REPRESENTATIONS and (b) EXTERNAL CORRECTNESS LABELS to train, and the combined score requires a further held-out labeled split — so the paper's remedy is doubly label-hungry and doubly white-box; the authors' claim that gains come 'without additional inference overhead beyond a single forward pass' is true of the probe but elides that the probe's supervision is not free; the clearest negative case is reported honestly (SE on MedicalQA, where neither the PC probe alone nor the combination reliably improves TCE) with the authors' own diagnosis that 'when base model accuracy is low, the correctness probe itself becomes harder to calibrate'.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Scoped to this paper: (i) the abstention policy is a single global threshold on a scalar — no conditioning on input regime, and given that the paper's central finding is MODEL-dependence, the obvious generalization is CONDITION-dependence (per-regime thresholds), which it does not attempt; (ii) both remedies (PC probe, SEP) are white-box and label-supervised, so the paper leaves entirely open what a black-box, label-free correctness signal complementary to agreement-based uncertainty would be — this is precisely the gap our program sits in; (iii) TCE is measured but never used as a control signal — nothing detects at deployment that the realized risk has drifted from the target and re-tunes τ; (iv) the operational range A=[0.05,0.30] is chosen by inspection of base hallucination rates rather than derived from a cost model; (v) the abstain decision is binary — no escalation, no repair, no budget-allocate alternative to abstaining.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：What breaks on a frozen black-box speech/omni core. (1) BOTH FAMILIES OF SIGNAL ARE CONTRACT-VIOLATING AS IMPLEMENTED. Sequence NLL requires token-level logprobs, which our black-box contract does not guarantee. The PC probe and the Semantic Entropy Probe both read PROMPT REPRESENTATIONS — hidden states — which the contract excludes outright. Of the four risk scores studied, only Semantic Entropy is in principle reconstructible black-box (it needs K sampled completions plus an entailment clusterer, no internals), and SE is exactly the signal the paper shows to be insufficient. So the paper's DIAGNOSIS transfers and its REMEDY does not: we inherit the problem without the fix. (2) NO DETERMINISTIC ORACLE: the hallucination indicator L(x) is computed against reference strings for short-form QA; speech outputs are free-form with no exact-match equivalence, so both the τ-tuning procedure and every metric (E-AURC, TCE) require a declared equivalence relation or a judge, and if the judge is the stand-in then the calibration target inherits the judge's error. (3) ACOUSTIC-CONDITION KEYING: the paper's headline is that the failure mode is MODEL-dependent, so no single uncertainty method is universally safe; in speech the analogous and stronger statement is that the risk score's meaning shifts with acoustic condition — a threshold τ calibrated on clean audio does not control risk on noisy or accented audio, so calibration must be per-condition and the calibration set must be stratified. The paper's own evidence that a method can be excellent on one model and degenerate on another is the strongest available argument that our thresholds cannot be global. (4) WAVEFORM OBSERVATIONS: entropy over sampled completions in speech conflates decision uncertainty with acoustic ambiguity — high sample dispersion may reflect a genuinely ambiguous waveform rather than model uncertainty about a fact, and this paper's construct does not distinguish them.
