(5) The authors' own scope caveat (short-form only; unclear for long-form or multi-step) compounds every point above.

**可借鉴**：(a) TARGET CALIBRATION ERROR, TCE = E_{α~A}|R_test(α) - α|, as our stop/abstain calibration metric, together with the authors' explicit argument for preferring it over ECE: 'our goal is accurate risk control at specific operating points, rather than global probability calibration over [0,1]' (§2.3). This is the correct metric for a system that must honor a stated risk budget, and it is the single most transferable artifact here. (b) E-AURC — excess area under the risk-coverage curve relative to an ORACLE ranking that abstains on all errors before any correct example — as the unitless, oracle-normalized selective-prediction score. The oracle-normalization is what makes it comparable across conditions of differing base difficulty, which matters acutely for speech. (c) The RISK-COVERAGE + CALIBRATION-DIAGONAL PLOT PAIR (Fig. 3) with a shaded HIGH-TRUST REGIME (α ≤ 0.15) as the standard deliverable for any abstention policy we ship: left panel shows whether the policy can even reach the regime at non-trivial coverage, right panel shows whether it lands where it promised. (d) The RISK-FLOOR diagnostic itself: a signal can have strong AUROC and still be unable to enter a strict-risk regime at usable coverage — 'strong AUROC does not imply reliable selective prediction at strict safety thresholds'. This is a direct argument that our evaluation slot must not report ranking metrics alone. (e) The COMPLEMENTARITY PROTOCOL: report the Spearman correlation between two candidate risk signals alongside their individual AUROCs (Tab. 1: ρ_s ∈ [0.34, 0.54]) to justify combining them, then combine by a simple two-feature logistic regression rather than an ad-hoc rule. The moderate-correlation-motivates-combination argument is reusable for any pair of black-box signals we construct. (f) The CONFIDENTLY WRONG construct and its scatter diagnostic (Fig. 2: plot signal A against signal B and look for a degenerate axis where one signal collapses to a point while the other spreads) — a cheap, general test for whether a candidate uncertainty signal has a dead zone. (g) The operational-range selection rationale (A=[0.05,0.30], upper bound ≈ base hallucination rate 'above which abstention yields no benefit') as a principled way to bound where calibration is even meaningful. (h) BASELINE ANCHORS: SE AUROC 0.752-0.884 across four models on TriviaQA, with the best method SWITCHING between models — the anchor is the instability, not any single number.

**备注**：Sharpest single warning in this read set for our program: the most natural black-box uncertainty signals available to us (agreement/entropy over sampled outputs — the very statistics CATTS gates on in 2602.12276) have a documented CONFIDENTLY-WRONG dead zone in the selective-prediction setting, and this paper's remedy for that dead zone is unavailable to us because it requires hidden states and correctness labels. Reading 2603.21172 against 2602.12276 in this subset: CATTS demonstrates vote-entropy gating works well enough to gate an arbiter in web agents, while this paper demonstrates entropy fails to control risk at strict targets in QA — these are different tasks and different decisions (allocate compute vs. abstain), so there is no contradiction, but the composition 'use an entropy-family statistic to make an ABSTAIN decision' is the specific move that this read set flags as unsafe without a complementary, non-entropy signal. Finding a black-box, label-frugal substitute for the PC probe is a well-posed open step for our program.

### 2603.23234 — MemCollab: Cross-Model Memory Collaboration via Contrastive Trajectory Distillation

**域**：text / LLM agents (mathematical reasoning: MATH500, GSM8K; code generation: MBPP, HumanEval; backbones Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct, Llama-3-8B-Instruct)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P3 (condition-keyed memory + write-gates + poisoning defenses — primary: provenance-labeled shared bank, hard-then-soft keyed retrieval, documented negative transfer, and the JSD method for validating the key), P5 (skill/constraint induction from execution traces: contrastive distillation of typed invariants and violations), P6 (system composition: one shared memory serving heterogeneous cores), P9 (weakly: the retrieval-budget non-monotonicity is an admissibility question — how much retrieved guidance is too much)

**方法面**：An external memory framework targeting a specific failure: memory written by one model DEGRADES another because entries entangle task-relevant structure with model-specific bias. GRADIENT USE: none — memory construction and retrieval are prompt-level; no backbone is updated. Two stages. (1) CONTRAST-DERIVED CONSTRUCTION (§2.2, Algorithm 1): for each training task x, run N heterogeneous agents to get trajectories {τ_i}; select the PREFERRED trajectory τ+ by a rule that is robust to strongest-model failure — if the strongest model i* fails and some other agent succeeds, sample uniformly among the correct ones, else take τ_{i*} (Eq. 2); the rest form the unpreferred set T-. Then contrast τ+ against EACH τ- independently, summarizing differences at the STRUCTURAL reasoning level (high-level algorithm, planning decisions, interpretation of tool feedback) rather than step-by-step, and distilling two typed items per pair: VIOLATION PATTERNS v_k (systematic failures in τ-: premature computation, incorrect symbolic mapping, misinterpretation of tool feedback) and REASONING INVARIANTS i_k (preserved in τ+, absent or violated in τ-). Each pair becomes a normative constraint m_k = (enforce i_k; avoid v_k) (Eq. 5). Crucially, entries are TAGGED WITH MODEL-IDENTITY LABELS (ℓ+, ℓ-) recording which agent produced the preferred and which the unpreferred trajectory — explicitly NOT creating separate banks, but enabling personalized retrieval from one shared bank. The bank stores ONLY abstract normative constraints, never raw trajectories, demonstrations, or solutions. (2) TASK-AWARE + MODEL-AWARE RETRIEVAL (§2.3): each entry m = (η, c, u, ℓ+, ℓ-) carries task category and subcategory inherited from its source task; at test time an LLM classifier assigns (c_q, u_q) to the query, retrieval FIRST filters to matching task labels (Eq. 9), THEN retains only entries whose contrastive construction involved the target agent in either role (Eq. 10) — if the target was ℓ-, the entry encodes a failure it should avoid; if ℓ+, an invariant it can reinforce — and finally ranks by semantic similarity and takes top-p=3 (Eq. 11). The theoretical gloss (§2.4): model trajectory as τ = f(s,b) with s task structure and b agent bias; contrasting two trajectories on the SAME task cancels b and retains s, so distilled memory m = φ(s).

**自述与显见局限**：No dedicated limitations section in this file. Read-set observations, scoped to this paper: memory construction REQUIRES a training set D where each task has 'a ground-truth solution or executable verifier' (§2.1) and an indicator I(·) evaluating trajectory correctness — the write-gate is a deterministic oracle, and 1000 labeled instances per dataset are consumed to build the bank; it also requires MULTIPLE heterogeneous models solving the identical task, so the mechanism has an irreducible N≥2-comparable-models precondition; the number of retrieved entries is NON-MONOTONIC (Fig. 3 — performance rises then falls past a task-dependent threshold), with p=3 chosen globally and the authors offering a search-space-contraction argument (ρ = 1 - k/b^d) that is heuristic rather than derived; the three-model experiment (Tab. 3) shows adding Llama3-8B does NOT help Qwen2.5-7B (67.0 → 66.8) — the authors explain this as overlapping failure patterns and are careful to say the result 'supports robustness ... rather than suggesting that performance should increase simply by adding more agents'; baselines are inconsistent (Dynamic Cheatsheet is BELOW vanilla on MBPP 36.7 vs 47.9 and HumanEval 40.7 vs 42.7; Buffer of Thoughts is below vanilla on MATH500 45.8 vs 52.2), so the field is high-variance; the claimed inference-time efficiency gain is asserted in the abstract/conclusion but the efficiency numbers are not in the main tables in this file.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Scoped to this paper: (i) memory is built OFFLINE from a labeled training set and is then static — there is no online write path, no drift detection, and no mechanism to retire an entry whose invariant stops holding (contrast PhysMem's refutation and decay in this same read set); (ii) the keying variable is TASK CATEGORY plus MODEL IDENTITY, both discrete and known; there is no notion of keying on an inferred deployment CONDITION, which is what our P3 slot needs; (iii) no poisoning or adversarial treatment — the bank trusts the summarizer model (the strongest agent) to distill honestly, and a wrong invariant enters the bank permanently and is retrieved forever; (iv) the non-monotonic retrieval curve is diagnosed but not controlled — a per-query adaptive p, or a write-side deduplication that would flatten the curve, is unexplored; (v) the b-cancellation argument (τ = f(s,b)) is informal and never measured — nothing verifies that a distilled entry is actually bias-free rather than carrying the SUMMARIZER's bias, which is a real risk since the summarizer is the strongest agent.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：What breaks on a frozen black-box speech/omni core. (1) THE CONTRAST PRECONDITION. The mechanism needs MULTIPLE COMPARABLE MODELS solving the IDENTICAL task so that differences can be attributed to model bias. A deployment with ONE frozen speech/omni core has no second agent to contrast against — the paper's own 'self-contrast memory' variant (contrasting multiple sampled trajectories from the same agent) is the only available substitute, and this paper measures it: it helps (7B avg 67.5 vs vanilla 57.1) but is consistently below MemCollab (71.6), so the single-core setting forfeits part of the mechanism's value by construction. This is the primary structural break. (2) DETERMINISTIC ORACLE: the preference selection rule (Eq. 2) is driven by I(τ) ∈ {0,1} from ground truth or an executable verifier; speech has neither, so τ+ would have to be selected by a judge, which reintroduces exactly the model-specific bias the contrast is meant to cancel — and worse, biases it toward whatever the judge prefers. (3) FREE-FORM OUTPUTS WITHOUT EXACT-MATCH EQUIVALENCE: 'checking the final answer or execution result' is trivially defined for MATH/GSM8K/MBPP/HumanEval and undefined for a speech response. (4) TASK-CATEGORY KEYING: retrieval keys on (category, subcategory) from a clean taxonomy (Algebra / Number Theory / Geometry / ...), and §3.5 validates the taxonomy by showing error-type distributions cluster by category via pairwise JSD. Speech has no comparable clean taxonomy; the operative key is ACOUSTIC CONDITION, which is continuous, estimated with error, and drifts within a session — so both the classifier f(q) and the hard filter c_m = c_q become error-prone, and a hard equality filter on a noisy key silently drops relevant memory. (5) TEXT-SUMMARIZABLE TRAJECTORIES: the distillation summarizes 'structural reasoning level' differences from text traces including code and execution feedback; a speech interaction's trajectory includes acoustic content that does not survive textual summarization, so the invariants extracted would be about the transcript, not about the audio. (6) COMPATIBLE AXES: no gradients, no logprobs, no hidden states, frozen backbones, prompt-level injection — the mechanism respects the black-box contract cleanly on those axes.

**可借鉴**：(a) The NEGATIVE-TRANSFER MEASUREMENT as an experimental design, which is the most valuable thing here: hold the memory MECHANISM fixed and vary only the memory SOURCE (w/ Memory(7B) vs w/ Memory(32B) vs w/ Self-Contrast vs MemCollab), so that the source's effect is isolated. The result is a clean documented harm — memory distilled from the 32B agent DEGRADES the 7B agent on MATH500 (50.6 vs 52.2 vanilla) and HumanEval (34.1 vs 42.7), and degrades Llama-3-8B far worse (MATH500 18.8 vs 27.4; GSM8K 56.6 vs 73.0; average 36.3 vs 41.7). That is the anchor: cross-source memory is not merely unhelpful, it can cost 17 points. Our P3 slot must include this exact ablation. (b) TYPED NORMATIVE ENTRIES of the form (enforce invariant; avoid violation) — an entry that carries both the positive constraint and the failure it guards against, rather than a raw exemplar. Converges with PhysMem's AVOID/PREFER/SEQUENCE typing in this read set; two independent papers arriving at typed normative memory is a design signal. (c) PROVENANCE LABELS ON MEMORY ENTRIES (ℓ+, ℓ-) in a SINGLE SHARED BANK, with retrieval filtering on provenance rather than partitioning the bank. This is a directly reusable pattern for condition-keyed memory: tag each entry with the condition under which it was learned and which role that condition played, then filter at read time — it preserves cross-condition abstraction while reducing interference, and it is strictly more flexible than per-condition banks. (d) The TWO-STAGE HARD-THEN-SOFT RETRIEVAL (hard filter on task label → hard filter on provenance → soft semantic ranking → top-p), with the ablation (Fig. 4) showing this beats pure embedding similarity and pure prompt-based selection on all four benchmarks — i.e. semantic similarity alone is the weakest retrieval rule. (e) The NON-MONOTONIC RETRIEVAL-BUDGET CURVE (Fig. 3, sweep p ∈ [1,10], peak at 3) as a required diagnostic: more retrieved memory is not better, and the turnover point is task-dependent. Any memory component we build must report this curve rather than fix a k. (f) The ERROR-PATTERN-BY-CATEGORY JSD ANALYSIS (§3.5): represent each task category as an empirical distribution over ERROR TYPES and compute pairwise Jensen-Shannon divergence to validate that the retrieval key actually separates failure mechanisms (algebra-family categories form a low-divergence cluster; Number Theory and Counting & Probability separate). This is a rigorous, reusable way to JUSTIFY a keying variable rather than assume it — directly applicable to validating acoustic condition as our memory key. (g) The HETEROGENEITY-INTERFERENCE TEST (Tab. 3): add a third source to the shared bank and check whether existing agents degrade — and the honest reading that the newly added agent's gain (42.4 → 43.2) and Qwen-7B's non-gain (67.0 → 66.8) together indicate overlap, not additivity. (h) BASELINE ANCHORS: MemCollab lifts Qwen2.5-7B 57.1 → 71.6 average and Llama-3-8B 41.7 → 53.9, while the memory-method baselines are erratic (Dynamic Cheatsheet below vanilla on both code benchmarks; BoT below vanilla on MATH500) — the anchor is that external-memory methods in this field routinely UNDERPERFORM no-memory on some tasks, so 'beats vanilla on every benchmark' is itself a nontrivial bar.

**备注**：The verdict is PROTOCOL_DONOR for its ablation designs and its provenance-labeling pattern, not for its mechanism, which has an irreducible multi-model precondition our single-frozen-core deployment does not satisfy — the paper's own self-contrast variant is the degraded fallback and it measures the gap (67.5 vs 71.6 average for the 7B agent). Its numbers are text-reasoning and code-generation effectiveness results (H5 withheld). Read against PhysMem in this same subset, the two converge on typed normative memory and diverge on the write-gate: PhysMem gates by VERIFICATION-BY-INTERACTION at deployment, MemCollab gates by CONTRAST against a labeled offline oracle — neither gate is available to us as-is, and constructing a label-free, single-core write-gate is the composition step this subset makes visible.

**组级综合（agent 提交）**：SCOPE. Nine papers, read from fulltext. Domain mix: 3 text/LLM-theory-or-agent (2602.01485, 2603.05739, 2602.16699), 1 text/LLM selective prediction (2603.21172), 1 text/LLM memory (2603.23234), 2 vision-GUI/web agent (2601.21872, 2602.12276), 1 vision-VLM (2603.16664), 1 vision-VLM/embodied robotics (2602.20323). All claims below are scoped to THIS READ SET; no cross-modality effectiveness conclusions are drawn (H5 withheld).

WHAT THE DONORS SETTLE.

(1) The GATE, not the signal, is where the value is. Four independent papers in three domains converge on the same architecture: compute a cheap signal, then use it to decide WHETHER an expensive or risky operation is admissible — CATTS gates an arbiter on vote entropy/margin, Kestrel gates an answer flip on evidence strength+confidence+coverage, PhysMem gates a memory write on verified confidence (tau_p=0.8, |E_support|>=3) with explicit refutation, CTA gates explore-vs-commit on a recalibrated prior. Two of them measure the DAMAGE of ungating: CATTS shows arbitration on high-consensus steps costs 11.9 points of task success (46.9% vs 35.0%, p=0.026, Fisher exact) with a dose-response ladder 46.9/36.6/29.6 for 0/1/2+ overrides; PhysMem shows unverified similarity retrieval is WORSE THAN NO MEMORY at every difficulty (48/23/8 vs 83/53/28). This read set therefore settles that our P9 slot is not an add-on: an ungated controller is demonstrably net-negative in two separate domains.

(2) Reward hacking under selection pressure has a formal shape and a cheap mitigation. 2603.05739's Theorem 3 decomposes BoN win-rate regret into a HACKING term growing in N (N*eps_pw*log(1/eps_pw)) plus a COVERAGE term shrinking in N, with a matching lower bound proving argmax-BoN is optimal FOR WIN-RATE (reversing the prior expected-reward verdict — the metric choice flips the conclusion). Its E_M-regularized variant is the cheapest mitigation available: draw N, take the top-ceil(N/M) by proxy reward, return one UNIFORMLY AT RANDOM. This decouples the compute knob N from the pressure knob M, giving monotonicity in N, and it needs no auxiliary model, no logprobs, no gradients. That mechanism is directly implementable against a black-box endpoint.

(3) There are two genuinely label-free, black-box-legal headroom estimators here, and they are complementary. 2602.01485 estimates V_N(s) — the expected best-of-N reward — from m << N pilot samples by Method-of-Moments on the top-alpha/2 Gaussian tail, with error O(sqrt(log N/m)) (Theorem 1) and a CONVEXITY result that turns multi-prompt budget allocation into a convex program. PhysMem's resonance score rho = |consistent principles|/|active principles| is a label-free deployment monitor of whether stored knowledge still predicts outcomes (rho climbs 0.2 -> 0.9 over 10 episodes). One predicts headroom before spending; the other detects that prior knowledge has gone stale. Neither needs gold labels at decision time.

(4) The metrics for stop/abstain are settled and better than what the field defaults to. 2603.21172 supplies TCE = E_alpha|R_test(alpha) - alpha| and oracle-normalized E-AURC, with the explicit argument that AUROC is the wrong instrument ("strong AUROC does not imply reliable selective prediction at strict safety thresholds") and a demonstrated RISK FLOOR where entropy methods cannot enter alpha<=0.15 at non-trivial coverage. Kestrel supplies the repair analogue: the four-way prediction-transition table {correctly preserved, error corrected, OVER-CORRECTED, incorrectly preserved}, which reports damage alongside benefit instead of netting them into a delta. Together these fix the reporting format for our P9 deliverables.

(5) Typed normative memory beats exemplar memory, and provenance is the keying primitive. PhysMem (AVOID/PREFER/SEQUENCE) and MemCollab ((enforce invariant; avoid violation) with model-identity labels ell+/ell- in a SINGLE shared bank filtered at read time) arrive at the same design from robotics and text respectively. MemCollab additionally supplies the method for VALIDATING a keying variable — represent each key value as a distribution over error types and compute pairwise JSD to show the key separates failure mechanisms — which is directly reusable for justifying acoustic condition as our memory key rather than assuming it.

WHAT TRANSFERS.

Mechanism-eligible under our contract (training-free, no gradients, no hidden states, no logprobs): SLG's tail estimator + convex allocation (2602.01485); E_M-regularized top-quantile selection (2603.05739); CATTS's vote-derived statistics and threshold gate (2602.12276, which explicitly argues AGAINST the logprob-dependent DeepConf on our exact grounds); Kestrel's evidence-gated update, three-valued verdict {supported/contradicted/insufficient}, and citation discipline (2603.16664); PhysMem's verify-then-promote lifecycle, action-level attribution, folding and decay (2602.20323); MemCollab's provenance-labeled shared bank with hard-then-soft retrieval (2603.23234); CTA's verbalized-confidence + isotonic recalibration, chosen by its authors precisely because it works blackbox (2602.16699).

Protocols and statistics: BoN-Acc with all-distractors criterion and the corpus-hygiene checklist incl. targeted false-negative auditing (2601.21872 §4); the harmful-override dose-response protocol and the net-advantage-by-uncertainty-band table with the NEGATIVE band reported (2602.12276); TCE + E-AURC + the risk-coverage/calibration-diagonal plot pair (2603.21172); the four-way prediction-transition statistic and the 9-row component ablation lattice (2603.16664); the five-paradigm frozen-base memory baseline suite with TRAINING-FREE PROXIES built for methods that normally need parameter updates (2602.20323 — the right way to compare against trained systems inside our constraint); the source-varied negative-transfer ablation and the non-monotonic retrieval-budget curve (2603.23234); the cost-sweep behavioral diagnostic showing whether a controller is actually cost-sensitive or merely tuned (2602.16699 Fig. 4/5); mean-selection-vs-tail and reward-model-swap ablations (2602.01485).

Anchors worth carrying: external reward controller lifts a frozen web policy +15-17 points where SELF-JUDGING lifts it ~+1 (2601.21872 Tab. 5) — the value of a controller tracks whether it brings independent information; uniform test-time scaling saturates (N=10->20 buys +0.2% for 2x tokens, 2602.12276 Tab. 1); gated selection reaches 47.9% at 405K tokens vs 43.2% at 920K (a 56% token cut WITH a 4.7-point gain); tail-guided allocation is a ~2x compute multiplier in proxy-reward units; cross-source memory can cost 17 points (2603.23234, Llama-3-8B GSM8K 56.6 vs 73.0 vanilla).

WHAT BREAKS — and the breaks are structured, not scattered.

The dominant failure axis across all nine is THE MISSING DETERMINISTIC ORACLE. Seven of the nine depend on one somewhere load-bearing: WebArbiter's ±1 verifiable RL reward and expert step labels; SLG's verifiable math benchmarks; CTA's oracle-match-rate and the correctness labels feeding isotonic regression; PhysMem's verification-by-physical-interaction (the environment returns r in {0,1} free, on demand, repeatably); MemCollab's I(tau) from ground truth or an executable verifier over 1000 labeled tasks; 2603.21172's reference-string hallucination indicator; CATTS's per-task success labels for the 495-run override analysis. Substituting a judge in each case does not merely add noise — it changes the object: PhysMem's verify-then-promote becomes judge-agreement-then-promote; MemCollab's bias-cancelling contrast reintroduces the judge's bias; 2603.05739's win-rate silently becomes "what the judge prefers about the transcript," discarding acoustic content. Designing a label-free, single-core write-gate and a label-frugal admissibility gate is the composition step this whole subset makes visible and none of the nine takes.

Second axis: OBSERVATION SEMANTICS. Kestrel's gate is a genuine admissibility gate only because SAM3 supplies information the LVLM did not have — crop-and-zoom returns strictly more resolution. Speech has no such operation: re-decoding the same audio is a RE-READ, not new evidence. Reproducing Kestrel's architecture without an independent evidence source would preserve the form and lose the substance — exactly the read-out vs. new-info discriminator our program must enforce. Similarly CTA's "acquire more information" actions have well-defined yield; "listen again" may return the identical encoding at nonzero cost.

Third axis: DISCRETE-ACTION / EXACT-MATCH PRESUPPOSITIONS. CATTS's entropy and margin require a decidable "two samples agree"; WebArbiter's BoN-Acc requires a canonical correct action; MemCollab and 2603.21172 require reference-string correctness. Speech outputs are free-form with no exact-match equivalence, so every one of these statistics needs a DECLARED equivalence relation as a first-class, error-prone component. CATTS's semantic-deduplicator LLM is the honest preview: in web agents it is a cheap fix with unmeasured sensitivity; in speech it becomes load-bearing, and entropy over badly-clustered free-form samples measures surface-form variance, not decision uncertainty.

Fourth axis: CONDITION KEYING. Every threshold in this read set is global or per-benchmark: CATTS tau=0.2/0.5, Kestrel SAM3 conf 0.5 and refinement [0.82,0.90], PhysMem tau_p=0.8/gamma=0.995, SLG alpha=0.2 with Assumption 2's uniform sigma bounds, 2603.05739's single M, MemCollab's hard category equality, 2603.21172's single tau per model. Acoustic condition is continuous, estimated with error, and drifts within a session — so a hard equality filter on a noisy key silently drops relevant memory, and a global tau calibrated on clean audio does not control risk on noisy audio. 2603.21172's central finding that the best uncertainty signal SWITCHES BETWEEN MODELS (SE 0.884 vs PC 0.813 for Llama; 0.844 vs 0.888 for Qwen) is the strongest available argument that our thresholds cannot be global.

Fifth axis, narrower but sharp: WHITE-BOX REMEDIES. 2603.21172 is the one paper whose DIAGNOSIS transfers while its REMEDY does not. Of its four risk scores, Sequence NLL needs logprobs and both probes (PC and SEP) need prompt representations — hidden states. Only Semantic Entropy is black-box reconstructible, and SE is exactly the signal shown to have a confidently-wrong dead zone. We inherit the problem without the fix.

CONTRADICTIONS AND TENSIONS INSIDE THIS SUBSET (all well-posed, none resolved here).

(a) 2602.01485 vs 2603.05739: SLG concentrates the remaining budget on the single state with the highest predicted reward TAIL — precisely the direction Theorem 3's hacking term says amplifies proxy-vs-true disagreement, and SLG measures success in the same proxy reward it optimizes with no accuracy or hacking check. "Can tail-guided allocation be made monotone via E_M-style quantile regularization" is a concrete open composition.

(b) 2602.12276 vs 2603.21172: CATTS gates on entropy successfully (allocate decision, web agents); 2603.21172 shows entropy fails to control risk at strict targets (abstain decision, QA). Different tasks, different decisions, no contradiction — but the composition "use an entropy-family statistic to make an ABSTAIN decision" is specifically flagged unsafe by this read set without a complementary non-entropy signal we do not yet have a black-box construction for.

(c) 2601.21872 vs 2602.12276: an INDEPENDENT trained external reward buys +15-17; an arbiter drawn from the SAME model as the policy buys ~+0.4 unconditionally and is actively harmful at high consensus. Together: arbiter value tracks information independence, not arbiter capability.

(d) 2602.16699's structural claim, which is the most program-relevant finding in the subset: end-to-end RL does NOT learn to recalibrate and collapses to a static cost-insensitive policy (0% code-first traces at every cost regime), whereas an externally injected calibrated prior produces adaptive behavior. That is evidence a CONTROL PLANE carrying calibration does something a trained policy does not — but it is a text/LLM result on controlled tasks with small realistic effect sizes (QA reward 0.283->0.293), and must be carried as positioning rationale plus a hypothesis to test, never as a cross-modality effectiveness conclusion.

NET. This subset densely occupies P8/P9/P10 with reusable gates, statistics, and one formal decomposition; supplies P1 its best two instruments (SLG's predict-before-spend and CTA's priced exploration); and gives P3 two convergent typed-memory designs plus a validated keying methodology. The step remaining ON TOP, common to nearly all nine, is the same one: every gate here is powered by an oracle, an exact-match equivalence, a discrete action space, or a white-box internal — our contribution surface is the label-free, condition-keyed, free-form-output substitute for those gates, plus the read-out-vs-new-info discipline that keeps an evidence gate from degenerating into a self-consistency ritual.


## 供体组 6

### 2606.28733 — Agentic Abstention: Do Agents Know When to Stop Instead of Act?

**域**：text (LLM tool-use agents; WebShop / Terminal-Bench 2.0 / retrieval QA)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：stop/abstain/budget calibration (cross-cutting, primary); P9 (admissibility - when is accumulated evidence sufficient to stop); P3 (playbook = external memory, here without write-gates, so it marks the slot); P5 (stopping rules as the induced skill artifact)

**方法面**：Formalizes agentic abstention as a POMDP with action space A={ANSWER, ABSTAIN, ACT} and a history-dependent policy pi(a_t|h_t); abstention is explicitly a SEQUENTIAL decision, and infeasibility may only be revealed after environment interaction (Sec.2). Reward R is part of the formalism but deliberately NOT optimized. Consolidated benchmark of ~28,000 instructions adapted from 16 AbstentionBench datasets plus two constructed abstention types on WebShop and Terminal-Bench: Request-based (instruction made ambiguous, warranted step w_i=1) and Environment-based (environment modified so the original instruction can no longer be satisfied, e.g. all red shirts removed, w_i=2) (Sec.3). Evaluates 13 LLM-as-agent systems x 2 scaffolds (Terminus 2, Codex CLI). Metrics (Sec.4.2, App.F): first-abstention step tau_i; oracle warranted step w_i; success iff w_i <= tau_i < inf, so PREMATURE abstention is scored as failure; AbsRec@K = fraction of abstain-warranted episodes abstaining within K steps after warranted; Timely Recall = AbsRec@w; Overall Recall = AbsRec@10 (K_max=10); SPL (success weighted by normalized inverse path length) to penalize delay; over-abstention rate on paired solvable instances. CONVOLVE (Sec.5) is training-free context engineering: reflection agent produces episode-level feedback y=phi(tau) from FULL rollouts, curator applies c^(k+1)=U(c^k,tau,y) building a playbook of reusable stopping rules appended to the system prompt; reflector 1024 tokens, curator 512, playbook budget 80,000 tokens, <=2 reflection/update rounds per example, 20 training trajectories, 1 epoch, weights frozen, WebShop tool schema unchanged.

**自述与显见局限**：App.A states four limits: only three environment families (web/terminal/RAG-QA), so richer interfaces, private tools, long-lived user state are uncovered; benchmark built by adapting existing datasets and the environment-based infeasibility is limited to missing targets/prerequisites, excluding permission boundaries, stale external resources, conflicting tool outputs, dependence on prior user context; findings are a snapshot over the models/scaffolds tested; possible pretraining exposure to benchmark-like examples. Additionally, in this paper the oracle warranted step w_i is annotation-by-construction, over-abstention is only reported on solvable terminal instances, and the CONVOLVE gain over a plain in-context-learning baseline on the 70B is small (57.4 vs 55.1 timely recall).

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level headroom on top of this paper: (a) the timeliness metric is oracle-anchored on w_i, so a deployment-time, label-free surrogate for 'abstention has become warranted' is untouched; (b) the playbook is one global text artifact with no condition-keying, no write-gate, no per-lesson credit assignment and no contamination defense; (c) the mechanism is under-isolated against plain ICL on the large model, so isolating what the reflection/curation loop adds beyond exemplar exposure remains open; (d) the abstain-precision side (over-abstention as a function of the same knob that raises timely recall) is reported thinly, so no operating curve trading timely recall against over-abstention is established here; (e) no budget-allocation right is exercised (K_max is fixed at 10 for all instances).

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：Breaks moving to a frozen black-box speech/omni core: (1) w_i, the earliest warranted step, exists only because the environment was edited by the authors - with waveform observations there is no deterministic oracle marking when infeasibility became evident, so Timely Recall as defined is not computable; (2) the Environment-based construction requires a resettable, editable symbolic environment (delete all red shirts), which has no waveform analogue - the speech analogue (unintelligible segment, absent speaker, out-of-vocabulary request) is a graded acoustic condition, not a binary environment edit; (3) ABSTAIN is a distinguished terminal action in a discrete action space, whereas a speech core emits free-form output in which 'abstained' must itself be detected/classified, adding an estimation error the metric does not model; (4) success/failure labels come from WebShop and Terminal-Bench programmatic checks - free-form speech output has no exact-match equivalence; (5) favorable: the method needs no weights, gradients, hidden states or logprobs, only a controllable system-prompt channel, so the CONVOLVE loop itself is contract-compatible.

**可借鉴**：Sec.4.2 + App.F: the (w_i, tau_i) timeliness formalism with premature-abstention-counted-as-failure (Eq.3) - this is the correct anti-gaming convention for any stop-early controller; AbsRec@K as a full curve rather than a point (Eq.5), giving a stopping-calibration diagnostic; SPL as a delay-penalized abstention score; over-abstention on a paired solvable-contrast set as the mandatory second axis. Sec.2: the three-action POMDP (ANSWER/ABSTAIN/ACT) with clarification-requests folded into ABSTAIN as a defensible decision-rights taxonomy. Sec.3: the Request-based vs Environment-based split (infeasible-from-the-instruction vs infeasible-only-after-interaction) as a benchmark-construction recipe we can re-instantiate. Sec.5 + Table 1: cross-model playbook transfer as an ablation design (author-model x consumer-model grid: 8B->8B, 8B->70B, 70B->8B, 70B->70B) which cleanly separates 'the artifact is useful' from 'the authoring model is strong'; also the ICL arm as the mandatory control for any context-engineering claim.

**备注**：Headline numbers for anchoring: Llama-3.3-70B base 26.7 AbsRec@1 / 83.2 AbsRec@10 / 55.3 SPL; +CONVOLVE(own lessons) 57.4 / 100.0 / 78.9; +CONVOLVE(8B-authored lessons) 55.3 / 99.0 / 76.4; +ICL 55.1 / 97.0 / 77.2. Llama-3.3-8B base 6.9 / 92.1 / 39.4 -> 12.9 / 94.1 / 39.7 with the 70B playbook. Scale mainly lifts OVERALL recall not TIMELY recall; reasoning/thinking raises timely recall but changes the over-abstention side. No gradient use anywhere in CONVOLVE.

### 2606.28864 — On Test-Time Scaling for Vision-Language Models

**域**：vision (LVLM; 13 instruction-tuned models, 6 benchmarks)　**供体级别**：BASELINE_ANCHOR　**喂给**：P1 (observation supply pricing / value-of-information - the regime where more compute or more elaboration actively hurts); P8 (the method grid our reward-as-controller must beat); budget calibration (cross-cutting)

**方法面**：First broad zero-shot test-time-scaling study for LVLMs. Sec.3 defines nine frozen-weight methods, ALL implemented as a think-prompt inserted as {question};{think-prompt};{post-prompt}: (1) CoT, (2) Structured CoT, (3) Plan-and-Solve, (4) Self-Consistency (b independent CoTs, majority answer), (5) Self-Aggregation (b chains concatenated, model aggregates, b+1 passes), (6) Self-Refinement (k sequential revision rounds), (7) Describe-Answer (caption then answer, 2 passes), (8) Compositional CoT (scene graph then answer), (9) Prompt Repetition (question repeated, 1 pass, zero thinking tokens). A deterministic answer-extraction repair pass is built in: on extraction failure, re-prompt with {question};{r};{answer-only};{post-prompt}. Models: Qwen2.5-VL 7/32/72B, Qwen3-VL 2/4/8/32B, InternVL-3.5 2/4/8/38B, Molmo2 4/8B; max 1024 tokens; ~$6,000 total compute. Sec.5 decomposes per-generated-token attention into image / prompt / previously-generated components (average and max-over-layers-and-heads) to show image attention decays through the chain. Sec.6 uses an external LLM judge for two constructs: Rationale Sufficiency (judge answers from the rationale TEXT ALONE, no image - i.e. can the chain carry the decision without the modality) and Rationale Dynamics (each sentence labelled supports-positive/negative/uncertain, yielding earliest decision point, contradiction shifts, and normalized position of final commitment), validated against human evaluation. Scoped absence in this paper: the nine-method suite contains NO external reward model or verifier-guided Best-of-N - all selection is majority vote or the model's own aggregation.

**自述与显见局限**：Zero-shot only, 1024-token cap; conclusions are LVLM-specific; the compute axis is wall-clock seconds on Pareto plots rather than cost-normalized statistics; the perception-degradation mechanism is diagnosed by attention traces and a judge proxy rather than by causal intervention; the discrepancy with prior negative results (SmolVLM2, LLaVA-OneVision) is attributed to poor instruction compliance, an explanation supported by qualitative examples in the supplement; the judge is a single external LLM (human agreement reported but the construct rests on one proxy).

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level headroom: (a) no reward-as-controller arm exists in the suite, so an external scalar signal selecting among the nine methods per instance is entirely open; (b) their own finding that extra compute HELPS reasoning benchmarks and HURTS perception-only benchmarks implies a per-instance budget/verbosity routing policy that they never build - the 300-token vs 1024-token ablation shows the two regimes exist but no controller exploits it; (c) no abstain or stop right anywhere; (d) the Rationale Dynamics statistics (final commitment at ~0.75 of the chain) are descriptive, never turned into an early-stopping rule.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：Breaks moving to a frozen black-box speech/omni core: (1) per owner directive, the effectiveness conclusions (small capable models benefit most, up to ~30 points) are LVLM-scoped and must NOT be read as cross-modality effectiveness for speech - H5 withheld; (2) Sec.5's attention decomposition requires attention maps over image tokens - flatly excluded by a black-box contract with no attention access; (3) Self-Consistency and Self-Aggregation presume an answer identity relation (MCQ letter, single word/phrase) so that 'most frequent answer' is well-defined - free-form speech output has no exact-match equivalence and majority vote would have to be replaced by a semantic-clustering estimate whose own error is unmodeled here; (4) Prompt Repetition exploits autoregressive text attention over repeated prompt tokens and has no defined waveform analogue (repeating audio is a different intervention); (5) the second-pass answer extraction assumes the chain r is available as re-injectable text and that the core is re-promptable - contract-compatible, but it presupposes text-form intermediate output; (6) the token-budget ablation assumes a controllable max output length.

**可借鉴**：Sec.3: the nine-method grid as the positioning surface any training-free controller must be scored against, with per-method forward-pass accounting (1, 2, b, b+1, k+1 passes) so cost is explicit; the deterministic answer-extraction repair pass as a cheap format-repair decision right. Sec.4 Table 2: the token-budget-cap ablation (cap at 300 vs 1024, with reliable extraction preserved so truncation does not confound) as the diagnostic separating 'the chain drives the answer' (reasoning tasks degrade when truncated) from 'the model loses focus' (perception tasks improve when truncated) - directly reusable as a per-task-family budget prior. Sec.4: Pareto compute-vs-accuracy frontier comparing a small model + TTS against an 8x larger model at fixed budget, as the cost-effectiveness framing. Sec.6: Rationale Sufficiency (answer from the chain alone with the modality removed) is portable as a 'can the transcript alone carry the decision' probe for speech, and Rationale Dynamics (earliest decision point, uncertainty position, position of final commitment) as design input for where an early-stop gate could sit. Also the instruction-compliance confound as the explanation to check before attributing a TTS null to the modality.

**备注**：Key anchors: Qwen3-VL-2B on WeMath 35% -> 64% with Self-Consistency (~+30 points); Qwen3-VL-4B + CoT beats Qwen3-VL-32B baseline on HallusionBench, WeMath and LogicVista (+13% on LogicVista); CoT judged the best runtime/performance tradeoff overall. TTS DEGRADES perception-only benchmarks (RealWorldQA, A-OKVQA, non-reasoning MMStar categories), less pronounced but still present for GPT-5.2. No gradient use (all methods frozen-weight).

### 2606.31612 — What Memory Do GUI Agents Really Need? From Passive Records to Active Task-Driving States (ATMem / STR-GRPO / DataScope)

**域**：vision-GUI (mobile GUI agents; AndroidWorld, MobileWorld, own DataScope benchmark)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P3 (condition-keyed memory + write-gates; this paper supplies the marginal-utility measurement but no gate and no keying); P1 (memory contribution as value-of-information); P6 (system composition - memory as a priced component)

**方法面**：ATMem (Sec.3.1): memory state m_t = (phi_t, E_t) where phi_t holds workflow-level progress and task constraints and E_t = {e_i} is a dynamic set of item entries, each with a stable item identifier, structured task-critical fields, and an execution status s_i in {remaining, finished, skipped}. Memory is injected into the system prompt before each model call; the output parser extracts the UPDATED memory block, the reasoning trace, and the executable action from the same response - memory maintenance is in-band with acting. Sec.3.2 verified online rollout synthesis produces SFT data via task-template instantiation, teacher-agent rollouts and environment validation. Sec.3.3 STR-GRPO is GRADIENT-BASED (GRPO objective Eq.5 with clipped importance ratio; ATMem-block tokens masked from the loss on memory-OFF rollouts): its DESIGN is the donor - paired rollouts with a memory-conditional intervention z_i in {0,1} that enables or masks ONLY the explicit ATMem channel while instruction, observation, action history and reasoning traces stay available in both conditions, with balanced assignment N_g/2 per condition inside each task group; trajectory reward R_i = V(s_T) - alpha*z_i*rho_i where V in {0,1} is a terminal-state verifier and rho_i is the fraction of memory-active steps, with the cost gated to memory-ON rollouts only; group-normalized advantage over the balanced ON/OFF mixture gives a shared baseline so the advantage directly expresses ATMem's marginal utility. Sec.3.4 DataScope: online mobile benchmark of multi-entry tasks with same-schema distractors, requiring action on every entry satisfying the instruction and rejection of violating entries; metrics App-Level Progress (per-app operation completion) and Scope-Aware F1 (target coverage under distractor filtering) reported separately from binary success rate.

**自述与显见局限**：No dedicated limitations section in this paper. Scoped to this read: a binary terminal-state verifier V(s_T) is assumed available for every task; the ON/OFF intervention requires cheap, repeatable, resettable rollouts with controlled initialization; the counterfactual is at the CHANNEL level (all memory on vs all off), never per-write, so no individual entry receives credit; Mem. measures invocation frequency, not per-entry usefulness; memory is single-writer and agent-authored with no adversarial or poisoning consideration; residual failure modes are stuck loops (88 cases), premature termination before all data operations are verified (limitation in completion checking, Sec.4.5) and incomplete operation tracking; results are on 8B-class open policies in mobile GUI only.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level headroom: (a) per-WRITE credit rather than channel-level ON/OFF, so a controller can gate individual entries; (b) the selectivity is learned into weights - an EXTERNAL write-gate enforcing the same marginal-utility criterion at write time is exactly the training-free slot this paper leaves open; (c) entries are keyed by item identity, not by environment/operating condition, so condition-keyed retrieval is untouched; (d) the ON-minus-OFF marginal utility is consumed only as an RL advantage and never surfaced as a deployment-time decision statistic; (e) no defense against contaminated or adversarial writes; (f) memory cost is a step-fraction proxy, not a priced budget.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：Breaks moving to a frozen black-box speech/omni core: (1) STR-GRPO is a gradient update on an 8B policy with importance ratios over token logprobs - the MECHANISM is excluded by the black-box contract (no weights, no gradients, no guaranteed logprobs); only its measurement design transfers; (2) the paired ON/OFF intervention needs a resettable environment with repeatable task initialization and a deterministic binary terminal verifier V(s_T) - a speech deployment supplies neither, since outputs are free-form and there is no state oracle to grade; (3) execution status {remaining, finished, skipped} presumes discrete item-level operations with observably committed completion; a speech turn has no analogous committed/uncommitted item state, so the schema must be re-derived rather than imported; (4) balanced ON/OFF grouping assumes the two conditions differ only in the memory channel - under acoustic variation the same input is not reproducible across rollouts, so condition drift would confound the contrast unless the audio is fixed and only the memory channel varies; (5) favorable: memory injection via system prompt and memory extraction from the response are contract-compatible, but the parser assumes reliable structured-output compliance, which is a real risk on a frozen speech core.

**可借鉴**：Sec.3.3: the paired memory-ON / memory-OFF balanced-group intervention is directly runnable TRAINING-FREE as a value-of-information measurement (K/2 rollouts with the memory channel, K/2 without, same task group, compare verifier outcome) - this is the cleanest available protocol for pricing external memory; the memory-active cost term rho gated to ON rollouts only, as a memory-price statistic; the group-shared baseline so that the contrast is a marginal utility rather than a difficulty artifact. Sec.4.3: Mem. (fraction of tasks invoking memory at least once) reported ALONGSIDE success rate, as the selectivity metric that exposes 'succeeded because of memory' vs 'succeeded despite it' - the ablation ladder base -> SFT -> standard GRPO -> STR-GRPO isolates exactly the failure of outcome-only reward to assign memory credit. Sec.4.5: the ON-minus-OFF reward-gap curve over training as an audit that a memory system is not merely being avoided, plus normalized memory-length trajectory as an over-writing check. Sec.3.4: the same-schema distractor benchmark design and the two-sided metric pair (App-Level Progress for under-scope, Scope-Aware F1 for over-scope) so that partial credit is separable from binary success.

**备注**：Anchors: ATMem-UI 8B reaches 76.6% AndroidWorld success, +3.3 pts over UI-TARS-2-230B and +5.9 over same-size MAI-UI. Ablation on Qwen3-VL-8B (AndroidWorld / MobileWorld): base 47.6 / 9.4 SR; +SFT +23.1 / +7.8 with Mem. 48.2% / 49.6%; +standard GRPO +4.1 / +3.5 SR with Mem. only -2.5 / -4.9; +STR-GRPO a further +1.8 / +2.6 SR with Mem. -13.8 / -14.6 (vs SFT: -16.3 / -19.5 Mem., +5.9 / +6.1 SR). GRADIENT USE FLAGGED: STR-GRPO is online RL; donor status is for its measurement design only.

### 2607.03436 — How Much of the Routing Gap Is Real? Decomposing the Router-to-Oracle Gap into Reproducible Specialist Advantage and Single-Draw Label Noise

**域**：text (LLM routing / model selection; theory + measurement protocol)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P9 (label-free headroom estimation and honest accounting of what a controller can possibly recover - strongest donor in this read set); P8 (what reward-guided selection can and cannot recover); P10 (an inflated oracle is itself an artifact of selection pressure on noisy labels)

**方法面**：Recasts the per-instance routing oracle. Under temperature T>0 a recorded correctness label b_im is one Bernoulli draw of a latent reproducible success probability p_im (A1). Defines single-draw oracle O_i^single = max_m b_im, expected oracle O_i^exp = E[O^single], and reproducible/committable oracle O_i^repro = max_m p_im. Theorem 1 (assumption-free algebra): G = G_rec + G_noise with G_rec = (1/N)sum(O_i^repro - q_i^r) and G_noise = (1/N)sum(O_i^exp - O_i^repro) >= 0 by Jensen on the convex max, needing no cross-model independence. Theorem 2 (recoverability asymmetry, the main result): EVERY single-commit router - deterministic, randomized, or any mixture - is capped at O^repro, while the same per-query budget spent as best-of-K on the committed model provably dominates the independent-pool single-draw oracle. Corollary 1: noise share S(K)=Delta_i/O_i^exp increases toward 1-p for homogeneously hard queries. Corollary 2: a Theta(1-pbar) lower bound, predicting noise concentrates on thin-support queries. Corollary 3: recovery scope is VERIFIER-GATED - the verifier-free aggregation ceiling O^agg (majority vote, Lemma 4) reclaims Delta_know, while the residual Delta_guess needs a deploy-time ground-truth verifier. Theorem 4: finite-sample consistency at O(1/sqrt(k)) with winner's-curse bias 0 <= E[max_m phat] - max_m p <= sqrt(ln K / 2k). Method (Sec.IV, Algorithm 1): introduces NO new router - it only resamples (k>=10, k>=20 in practice) and recomputes plug-in estimators, under pre-registered assumption gates (known-p simulation TV <= 0.01 < 0.06; per-draw overdispersion 1.0; >=94% of cells inside the independence band); a failed gate means the magnitude is not licensed to report. Fixed per-draw seeds realize seed-aligned K-tuples (A7).

**自述与显见局限**：Stated in Sec.VII and throughout: estimates use k samples at a SINGLE temperature on an open-model pool over three benchmarks - larger k, multiple temperatures, further domains and live frontier pools are future work; the ~20-point LLMRouterBench figure is explicitly a prospective, conservative localization, NOT an audit, since O^repro is non-identifiable at k=1; lineage structure (a Qwen-distilled model counts as Qwen) remains a material confounder that the one-per-lineage pool controls for but does not eliminate; the noise SHARE is baseline-dependent by construction (it is computed against the strongest single-commit baseline, which maximizes it) while only the absolute floor G_noise is router-invariant; the pool-cardinality monotonicity of Cor.1 is a homogeneous-pool statement and is non-monotone on GPQA.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level headroom: (a) it is a LABEL-USING headroom accounting - p_im is estimated from k graded draws, so a genuinely label-free headroom estimator is not built here and the authors themselves list 'cheap, calibrated ex-ante quality estimators' as next step (i); (b) the decomposition covers single-commit selection over a model pool, not multi-step stateful control with repair/stop/budget rights, so extending the asymmetry to a decision-rights setting is open; (c) no cost or latency pricing of the k resampling draws against the recovered headroom; (d) the verifier-gated residual Delta_guess is bounded but no construction of a deployable verifier is attempted; (e) cross-model estimator-error correlation and its effect on routing optimality is listed as their own open item (ii).

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：Breaks moving to a frozen black-box speech/omni core: (1) p_im requires a BINARY correctness label per (query, model, draw) - free-form speech output has no deterministic exact-match oracle, so every p-hat becomes an estimate through a proxy judge whose own error variance is entirely outside this framework's error model, and that judge error would be absorbed into G_noise, silently inflating the 'irrecoverable' floor; (2) the apparatus needs repeated i.i.d. draws at fixed input with controlled temperature and fixed per-draw seeds (A7) - a black-box speech API may expose neither seeds nor a stable temperature; (3) acoustic-condition keying multiplies the sampling budget: the k>=20 requirement is per stratum, so a per-condition decomposition (speaker, SNR, accent) costs k x strata draws; (4) the unit is 'which model to commit to' for a single query, which maps only loosely onto our decision rights (select / repair / stop / abstain / budget) which are multi-step and stateful; (5) favorable: the whole protocol needs no weights, no gradients, no hidden states and no logprobs - it is contract-compatible in every respect except the correctness oracle.

**可借鉴**：Sec.III Theorem 1: the exact decomposition G = G_rec + G_noise is the discipline any headroom claim our controller makes must pass - 'of the oracle-selection gap you report, how much can ANY single-commit selector reach, and how much is single-draw noise?'. Theorem 2 and Fig.3: the selection-axis cap at O^repro vs the sampling-axis recovery, i.e. resampling recovers what better selection provably cannot - this directly frames when our budget-allocate right dominates our select right. Corollary 3: the verifier-free aggregation ceiling O^agg vs the verifier-gated residual Delta_guess is exactly the boundary of what a selection layer can claim WITHOUT an oracle. Theorem 4(b): the winner's-curse bias bound sqrt(ln K / 2k) quantifies how much any max-over-noisy-estimates overstates - reusable for our best-of-N reporting. Sec.IV: the multi-sample oracle protocol (report BOTH O^repro and O^exp with the noise share S(K), never a single-draw union) as a reporting standard; the k-sweep robustness showing the estimate is 0 at k=1 and settles by k~20-30; the thin-support stratification (partition queries by how many candidates are correct on a single draw) as the diagnostic for where selection headroom is illusory; the pre-registered ASSUMPTION GATES that block magnitude reporting on failure; the lineage jackknife and pool-composition sweep; and the share-is-baseline-dependent / floor-is-router-invariant distinction, verified by swapping in a learned TF-IDF k-NN router that changes the share (36%->31%) but leaves the floor identical (0.036).

**备注**：Anchors: primary target LLMRouterBench (33 models, 391,645 instances) builds its oracle as a per-query union of single T=0.2 draws, so its ~20-point gap is by construction a union of stochastic draws. Three controlled open-pool regenerations (11 models): GSM8K gap 3.3 pts / noise share 12% [6,19]; MATH-500 gap 10.1 pts / 36% [31,42]; GPQA-Diamond 13% [10,17] but the largest ABSOLUTE floor at 5.6 pts. Thin-support stratum (<=3 of 11 correct) carries 43% on MATH-500 vs 14% dense; MATH-500 places 28% of queries there vs 3% for GSM8K and 52% for GPQA. Effective participation-ratio pool size only 2.1 of 11 on MATH-500. Falsifiable check passes: best-of-K on the committed model reaches 0.946 on MATH-500 vs matched-budget independent-pool oracle 0.874. CRITICAL negative: verifier-free majority vote reaches only 0.824 on MATH-500, BELOW the reproducible ceiling 0.837 - self-consistency does not beat committing to the best model there, so the sampling-recoverable mass is dominated by the verifier-gated guessing residual. No gradient use.

### 2607.03751 — Look Before You Leap: Distilling Tree Search into Action Evaluation for Frozen VLA Models (SVA)

**域**：vision-action / embodied VLA (EmbodiedBench EB-Habitat & EB-Navigation, SimplerEnv, LIBERO, RoboTwin 2.0)　**供体级别**：CONSTRUCT_DONOR　**喂给**：P8 (reward-as-controller: an external scalar evaluator selecting among a frozen core's candidates is our exact architecture, here with a gradient-trained evaluator); P1 (value-of-information via diminishing pass@k returns); budget-allocate (N as the knob)

**方法面**：Diagnosis (Sec.3): a pass@k study with N=50 independent rollouts per task and the unbiased estimator Pass@k = E[1 - C(N-c,k)/C(N,k)] shows frozen VLAs already contain competent behaviors - average success 33% at pass@1 rising to 92% at pass@32 - so the deployment bottleneck is reframed as action EVALUATION, not action generation. Three observations: the evaluation bottleneck concentrates at INTERMEDIATE difficulty (Turn Switch 0.31 -> ~1.0, Rotate QRCode 0.35 -> ~1.0), easy tasks saturate by pass@4 (Pick up Book 0.84 -> 0.99), and beyond-capability tasks stay low (Stack Cubes 0.02 -> 0.50 even at k=32); marginal returns decay 0.13, 0.13, 0.12, 0.10, 0.08 across k doublings. SVA has three stages. Search (Sec.4.1): MCTS in a resettable simulator with PUCT selection (Q + c_puct * P * sqrt(n(s))/(1+n(s,a))), expansion by sampling N candidates from the frozen policy with priors P = pi_theta(a|s_L), evaluation by cloning the simulator state and rolling forward D steps for a discounted return G(s_L), and backup as a running mean. Value (Sec.4.2): GRADIENT-BASED distillation of the search returns into a lightweight Q-model Q_phi on a small VLM backbone (Qwen3.5-0.8B) with LoRA adapters and an ensemble of MLP value heads reading the hidden state at an appended <VALUE> token; Smooth-L1 loss; only heads and LoRA are trained; ensemble mean is the Q prediction and ensemble standard deviation is the uncertainty. Act (Sec.4.3): NO simulator at deployment - the frozen policy proposes N candidates, the empirical prior p(a|s) is the sampling frequency among the N samples, and the selection rule is argmax_i [ mu_phi(s,a_i) - lambda_1 * sigma_phi(s,a_i) + lambda_2 * log p(a_i|s) ], i.e. an uncertainty-regularized, prior-anchored best-of-N.

**自述与显见局限**：Sec.6 and App.A state three: the pipeline is deliberately staged with decoupled tree search and value learning, so search is blind to the evaluator being learned and the policy never benefits from improved values beyond test-time re-ranking; the Search stage relies on a RESETTABLE SIMULATOR with a task-success signal, limiting applicability where high-fidelity simulation or reward functions are unavailable; evaluation is entirely in simulation and the Q-model's calibration on physical robots is untested. Scoped to this read: no over-optimization regime is probed (scaling is reported only up to N=32 where gains are still monotone), and the regularization coefficients lambda_1 / lambda_2 are not ablated in the sections read.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level headroom: (a) the ensemble-disagreement term sigma_phi is used ONLY as a score penalty, never as an abstain trigger, a stop rule, or a budget allocator - turning the same uncertainty into a decision right is untouched; (b) no reward-hacking / Goodhart analysis under increasing selection pressure, so where the Q-model starts being exploited by the proposal distribution is unmeasured in this paper; (c) the prior term lambda_2 log p acts as a Goodhart brake but its weight is unexamined; (d) no per-condition keying of the evaluator; (e) the search-value loop is open-loop, which they name themselves; (f) the pass@k headroom diagnostic is run once offline and never used online to allocate N per instance despite their own three-regime finding that only intermediate-difficulty tasks pay.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：Breaks moving to a frozen black-box speech/omni core: (1) the Q-model is distilled from SIMULATOR MCTS rollouts with empirical returns - a privileged offline oracle with state cloning and rollback that no speech deployment has, and their own limitation section flags this; (2) actions are discrete skills or continuous control primitives in a resettable state; a speech core emits free-form language with no rollback and no 'execute until completion or invalid feedback' semantics; (3) the empirical prior p(a|s) is estimated by EXACT REPETITION FREQUENCY among N samples, which requires an equality relation over candidates - free-form speech/text outputs have no exact-match equivalence, so the prior term needs a semantic-clustering surrogate whose error is unmodeled here; (4) pass@k itself needs a deterministic per-rollout success oracle; (5) the uncertainty sigma_phi comes from a TRAINED ensemble of value heads reading hidden states of an auxiliary model - available only because they train it, so a black-box analogue must derive uncertainty from sampling dispersion instead; (6) the latency argument depends on VLA architecture (heavy VLM backbone runs once per observation, only the small action expert is invoked N times) - a monolithic speech/omni core gives no such N-times-cheap proposal path, so the cost model does not carry over.

**可借鉴**：Sec.3: the pass@k diagnostic as the HEADROOM-EXISTENCE test that licenses building a selection layer at all, with its three-regime reading (saturated / intermediate / beyond-capability) telling us precisely where an external controller can pay; the marginal-return table (0.13 -> 0.08) as budget-allocation input. Sec.4.3 Eq.5: the decision rule argmax[mu - lambda_1*sigma + lambda_2*log p] as a template for an uncertainty-regularized, prior-anchored selection score, where the prior term is an explicit brake against selecting low-prior outliers the scorer happens to like. Sec.5.2: the minimum ablation triple for any selection controller - w/o evaluator (replace with majority vote), w/o search-derived targets (train on plain policy-rollout returns), w/o multi-candidate (collapse to single-shot base) - which cleanly attributes the gain; here removing the Q-model is the largest drop (56.11 -> 43.33 on EB-Navigation) while collapsing to single-shot gives 39.17. Sec.5.3: the latency accounting that separates PROPOSAL cost from SCORING cost and reports both against N, plus the 'scaling evaluation beats scaling model size' framing (9B+SVA outperforms 27B by 7 points at 27% lower latency).

**备注**：Anchors: +15.4 avg on EB-Habitat and +13.2 on EB-Navigation across five backbones; largest gains on Long Horizon (+23.34) and Common Sense (+28.33) with Qwen3.5-27B; SimplerEnv pi_0+SVA 50.7% avg, beating RoboMonkey without curated preference annotations; RoboTwin pi_0.5 36.0 -> 43.5 (+7.5, and +5.2 over RoboMonkey); test-time scaling N=1..32 gives 42.8 -> 57.2 with evaluation latency <=1.33s against generation up to 11.2s. GRADIENT USE FLAGGED: the Q-model is LoRA+value-head trained on MCTS returns; donor status is for the diagnostic, the decision rule form, and the ablation design, never the mechanism.

### 2607.04334 — Do GUI Agents Believe Their Eyes? Diagnosing State-Belief Reliance on Pixels versus Structure

**域**：vision-GUI (multimodal GUI agents; web, mobile, desktop; Multimodal-Mind2Web, MiniWoB++, AndroidWorld, CLAY/RICO)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P9 (decision-calibration / admissibility gates - the gate is cross-channel agreement, and the paper supplies the cost-of-refusal accounting a gate must be scored on); P1 (observation supply - which channel is load-bearing); P10 (a gate a selector can be steered around; scaling 7B->30B repaired one failure and worsened another)

**方法面**：Formalizes VISUAL STATE RELIANCE - attribution of an agent's state belief to pixels P, serialized structure S, or priors - and measures it with PAIRED SINGLE-CHANNEL INTERVENTIONS that hold content fixed and switch exactly one channel. The headline structure-swap pairs a real screenshot (P = a*) with an edited structure (S = b != a*), so following structure means answering b. Output schema is forced-choice JSON (answer in a closed vocabulary, a confidence, and a self-reported evidence channel), scored by DETERMINISTIC match with NO model judge. Central metric (Eq.1): pfg = (1/|C|) * sum_{i in C} 1[y_swap_i = b_i], restricted to the perception-correct subset C = {i : y_pix_i = a*_i} - it therefore separates a PERCEPTION failure from a FUSION failure. A strict variant pfg_strict closes the residual loophole (a model might pass the pixels-only check using page context without resolving the target region) by conditioning on a tight crop of the target region with no structure; the two agree within 0.05 on every open model. Benchmark: 735 probes = 120 web-text, 50 mobile-widget, 60 desktop-graphic, 30 mobile visible controls (perception baseline), 250 mobile-stale candidates, 225 web-natural ZERO-EDIT divergences mined from 38 live production websites (aria-label vs visible label, title attribute vs visible text, and stale snapshots where a ticker/clock/counter updates between DOM capture and screenshot - the exact capture race agent frameworks face). Severity calibration: synthetic edits are compared against the 225 natural divergences under five string-severity measures (normalized edit distance, token Jaccard, length difference), falling inside the natural 5th-95th percentile band at coverage 0.93-1.00, with synthetic medians at or slightly above natural - so the intervention is proven to sit in the deployment severity regime. Gold-label audit by two independent annotators seeing only the screenshot, raw agreement 0.880 and Cohen's kappa 0.86. Statistics: bootstrap and cluster-robust 95% CIs, exact McNemar with Holm correction for model-pair differences.

**自述与显见局限**：Stated: closed-model corroboration spans one vendor and the capability contrast rests on open checkpoints 7B-30B, so the capability-reliance relation is charted rather than established as a law; the compounding result uses click-style MiniWoB++ episodes of at most six steps; the harshest natural kind has only 42 consensus-audited stale web probes from 11 sites, so its hijack estimates carry moderate intervals; the expanded families use model annotators (the decisive mobile-stale subset and the human baseline rest on human annotation); and the consistency gate trusts geometry, so the coordinate-swap threat is explicitly out of its scope. The white-box ablation and gradient attribution require weight access.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level headroom: (a) the consistency gate is fixed-cost per action (1.8-2.0 queries/action, 3.3-5.5 on dense AndroidWorld screens) with no risk-triggered policy deciding WHEN to spend it - a budget-allocated gate is untouched; (b) pfg is a diagnostic requiring a gold pixel read, not a deployment-time signal, so a label-free proxy for 'this belief is mis-sourced' is open; (c) gate thresholds are not condition-keyed; (d) the abstain arm is a blanket certificate refusal (0.59-0.77 of conflicted actions blocked) with no graded abstention or repair-then-abstain ladder; (e) no measurement of the gate under an adaptive adversary that targets the gate itself.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：Breaks moving to a frozen black-box speech/omni core: (1) the entire design requires TWO channels describing the same state that can be edited independently - speech has no free, near-deterministic second channel; an ASR transcript is itself a model output, not ground-truth structure, so 'edit exactly one channel' has no clean analogue and any transcript perturbation confounds the intervention with transcriber error; (2) gold labels come from human PIXEL reads with kappa 0.86; a speech gold requires listening annotation whose own agreement must be re-established and whose severity distribution (SNR, overlap, accent) is continuous rather than a string-distance band; (3) the source-aware consistency gate re-grounds by SCANNING on-screen elements for the one whose pixels show the goal - this presumes an enumerable, addressable element set that audio does not provide; (4) the white-box ablation tracing the effect to a single copied structural value, and the gradient attribution showing visual evidence is processed yet overridden, both require weights and gradients - excluded by the black-box contract; (5) deterministic forced-choice scoring depends on a closed answer vocabulary, whereas free-form speech output has no exact-match equivalence; (6) the structural provenance (DOM, aria attributes) that makes the whole intervention cheap is absent in a waveform.

**可借鉴**：Eq.1 and the strict variant: the PERCEPTION-CORRECT CONDITIONING trick - measure the fusion/deference failure only on instances where single-channel perception is demonstrably correct, then guard the construct with a stricter isolated-slice re-verification. This is the sharpest construct-validity device in this read set. Sec. benchmark construction: SEVERITY CALIBRATION of synthetic interventions against a mined natural divergence distribution, with coverage of the natural 5th-95th band reported per measure - the answer to 'is your perturbation representative of deployment?'. Deterministic forced-choice scoring with no model judge, plus a self-reported evidence channel to check whether the model can even name its source. Table 5: the FOUR-ARM MITIGATION COMPARISON on IDENTICAL probes, models and conditions, with an explicit cost column in queries-per-action and separate hijack and task-error columns - so a defense cannot win by refusing everything; the certificate arm (hijack 0.22-0.38 but refuses 0.59-0.77 of conflicted actions, task error unchanged) vs the consistency gate (hijack 0.28-0.43 AND task error cut 24-44 points, aligned behavior within 0.04 of baseline) is exactly the template for scoring our admissibility gates. Controls: the reverse/blur counter-family (180 probes with structure truthful and the target blurred: blur alone drops accuracy to 0.54/0.59, correct structure recovers 0.95/1.00) proving mis-calibrated fusion rather than a gold-definition artifact; the human-annotator floor (structure-following only 0.03 vs 0.30-0.79 for models); seed stability (sfr varies at most 0.04 over five T=0.7 seeds); and the action-format sweep showing structure-following rises monotonically with how directly the action names the structured channel (coordinate 0.11 -> element id 0.64 -> index 0.90 -> text label 0.98) while the coordinate agent still acts on structure-only ghost nodes on 0.84-1.00 of probes - i.e. neither pure channel is safe.

**备注**：Anchors: on unedited stale live snapshots models follow the outdated structure on up to 0.88 of probes; one mis-sourced belief at the first step compounds to task failure with self-recovery at most 0.03 (vs 0.41-0.63 recovery under first-step-only perturbation); MiniWoB++ reward flips from ~+0.85 when aligned; AndroidWorld tap lands on a wrong page on 25 of 36 audited probes. Capability does not settle it: 7B->30B repaired ghost-node visibility grounding yet WORSENED text-value deference. No gradient use in the training-free consistency gate; gradient attribution is diagnostic only.

### 2607.06503 — Doomed from the Start: Early Abort of LLM Agent Episodes via a Recall-Controlled Probe Cascade

**域**：text (LLM agents; TextCraft, WebShop, ALFWorld boundary test)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：stop/abstain/budget calibration (cross-cutting, primary donor); P9 (decision-calibration with a distribution-free guarantee and an honest account of what the data can certify)

**方法面**：An abort cascade places a gate at each of the first R_g=6 interaction rounds; at gate r a per-round scorer f_r aborts the still-running episode if f_r(x) > tau_r, losing any potential success but saving the remaining cost C - c_r. Signal: a feature vector taken from the agent LLM's INTERNAL ACTIVATIONS - the residual-stream hidden state at the final token of the agent's generated action in round r, at a single layer chosen once per matrix cell by a preliminary per-layer probe-AUC sweep and then frozen before any recall-target evaluation. Probe f_r is a logistic regression on standardized features with L2 and C=1 predicting eventual failure, produced by task-grouped stratified group k-fold CROSS-FITTING so no episode is scored by a probe that saw its task. Calibration: tau_r is the smallest calibration score whose exact binomial CLOPPER-PEARSON lower confidence bound p(tau) = Beta^{-1}(alpha; k(tau), n_r - k(tau) + 1) on the gate's survival rate among SUCCESSFUL episodes is >= the per-round recall budget t_r, at alpha = 0.05 - deliberately stronger than a marginal conformal quantile, and a gate whose n_r is insufficient ABSTAINS (aborts nothing). Central structural insight: per-round guarantees do NOT compose - two gates each passing 98% of successes give 98% if their false-abort sets coincide but only 96% if disjoint - so the budget vector t=(t_1..t_6) is treated as a hyperparameter and searched over a grid {0.85,0.90,0.95,0.98,0.99,1.0}^6 = 46,656 candidates on a validation split, maximizing savings subject to rho_hat_val(t) >= rho* + delta with an a-priori margin delta = 0.02 sized from the binomial standard error of validation recall. Splits are grouped by task throughout: 20% calibration, 20% validation for budget search, 60% test. Optional independent post-selection certificate: freeze the selected cascade and compute a Clopper-Pearson lower bound at alpha_m=0.05 on global recall using an INDEPENDENT certification sample - exact and distribution-free regardless of how large the search was. Failure mode is conservative by construction: if no candidate is margin-feasible or the certificate fails, the policy abstains and aborts nothing. Structural baselines: single-gate (entire budget at one searched round, recovering prior single-decision-point monitoring) and uniform.

**自述与显见局限**：Stated in Discussion: distribution-free calibration holds under EXCHANGEABILITY, and grouped splits avoid task leakage but remain in-distribution - category-held-out tasks are a natural next test; under distribution shift the probe, the thresholds AND the global control must all be refreshed on labeled data, and the pilot-fixed layer may need reselecting; the coarse grid fails conservatively (a suboptimal feasible point preserves recall at the cost of savings) and exhaustive cost grows as |T|^R_g; when successes are scarce, grouped splits can select different discrete budgets; teacher-forced replay supports only offline evaluation, so wall-clock latency and dollar cost are UNMEASURED (the efficiency metric is generated tokens), and online use needs an instrumented serving stack that persists activations and currently requires chunked prefill disabled; what the aborted compute buys (reallocation to retries) is explicitly not measured.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level headroom: (a) the load-bearing signal is hidden-state, and the paper itself reports behavior-only monitoring is CONSISTENTLY WEAKER and adding behavioral features to hidden-state probes gives no further gain - so the black-box-observable version of this cascade is an open construction, not a solved one; (b) recall is controlled but abort PRECISION and realized savings carry no guarantee; (c) calibration requires labeled binary episode outcomes; (d) no per-condition (stratified) recall control - a single global recall floor is enforced across all task types; (e) the certificate's attainable target is capped by available successes independent of scorer quality, so an efficiency-of-certification improvement is a separate open lever; (f) the freed compute is never actually reallocated, so the end-to-end value of aborting is unquantified here.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：Breaks moving to a frozen black-box speech/omni core: (1) the signal is residual-stream activations at a chosen layer - FLATLY EXCLUDED by the black-box contract (no hidden states, no attention, no weights); and the paper's own comparison says the observable-behavior substitute is near chance at round 1 and only becomes informative around rounds 3-4, by which time over a third of episodes have finished and much of the recoverable compute is gone - so importing this cascade to a black-box core means importing the calibration machinery while LOSING the signal that made it pay; (2) calibration needs labeled binary episode outcomes y in {0,1}, which free-form speech tasks do not deterministically supply; (3) exchangeability across acoustic conditions is doubtful - a cascade calibrated on clean speech carries no validity guarantee on a different SNR / speaker / accent stratum, so recall control would have to be per-condition, multiplying the ~114-299 certification successes needed PER STRATUM; (4) the design presumes multi-round episodes with a stable round boundary at which a hidden state can be read, whereas many speech tasks are single-turn; (5) the efficiency metric is generated tokens, which for a speech/omni core with audio input does not price the dominant acquisition cost.

**可借鉴**：The entire risk-control skeleton is transferable INDEPENDENTLY of the feature source and is the strongest stop/abstain calibration donor in this read set: per-round Clopper-Pearson-calibrated gates on a user-specified per-round recall budget; the explicit NON-COMPOSITION argument for sequential gates (98% x 98% is not the global rate; it depends on whether false-abort sets overlap) - this is precisely the correctness bug a multi-step stop/abstain design must avoid; joint budget search over the budget vector under a GLOBAL episode-level recall constraint rather than per-gate constraints; the margin delta sized from the binomial standard error of validation recall as a guard against selection bias from searching 46,656 candidates; the freeze-then-certify pattern (an independent post-selection Clopper-Pearson bound is exact regardless of search size) which cleanly separates flexible empirical search from statistically clean certification; abstain-on-infeasible as the conservative default; the sample-complexity curve (~114 successful certification episodes support ~0.974, 0.98 needs ~149, 0.99 needs ~299, INDEPENDENT of scorer quality) as a pre-deployment feasibility check on what recall promises the available data can support; task-grouped cross-fitting and grouping by task across every split; single-gate and uniform as the two structural baselines; savings measured as the fraction of generated tokens saved counting only each aborted episode's REMAINING computation.

**备注**：Anchors: 2x3 matrix (TextCraft, WebShop x Qwen-2.5-7B, Llama-3.2-3B, Qwen3-1.7B), 800 episodes per cell. Cascade beats the best single-gate baseline in ALL 24 configurations, saving 1.5-8.8x more compute at a 90% recall target; strongest settings reduce generated tokens 60.2% (TextCraft) and 54.9% (WebShop) at 90% recall, retaining 45.0% and 41.5% at 95%; achieved recall lands within one standard deviation of target in all 24 configurations over 20 seeds. Hidden-state probes at ROUND 1 already match or exceed the surface scorer's eventual PEAK (attained 2-3 rounds later) and peak themselves at round 2; adding behavioral features to hidden-state probes adds nothing. ALFWorld boundary test: probe AUC 0.815, 10.7% savings vs 3.3% for a single gate at the 0.95 target, mean recall 0.943. No gradient use on the agent policy (probes are logistic regressions; the policy is frozen), but hidden-state access is required.

### 2607.08147 — Prismata: Confining Cross-Site Prompt Injection in Web Agents
