**域**：vision-GUI / web-agent security (BrowserGym + WebArena, WASP, adaptive attacks)　**供体级别**：CONSTRUCT_DONOR　**喂给**：P3 (memory write-gates and poisoning defenses - the injection-free-label construct is the strongest formal write-gate primitive in this read set); P9 (provenance as an admissibility criterion: a signal is admissible only if it clears a structural test); P5 (tool/skill capability restriction as a decision right)

**方法面**：Enforces CONTEXTUAL LEAST PRIVILEGE as a transparent layer between browser and agent, operating on the CAPTURED observation rather than the live DOM (so it adds no page-drift channel), with no developer annotations required. Three stages (Sec.2.2). (1) Action gate: for each interactive element, trace its CRITICAL PATH - the full HTML ancestor chain from the DOM root - and have an LLM judge task-scope using ONLY the user's task plus that path; no other page content is visible to the labeler, so an injection lying anywhere outside the critical path cannot influence the labeling decision. The key empirical observation licensing this is that critical paths are typically composed almost entirely of benign developer-authored structural elements (container divs, layout wrappers, navigation) and only rarely include untrusted content. (2) Biba parsing: classify content origin as developer / user / hosted-party / external, labeling each element from itself and its ancestry with children and siblings MASKED (no-read-down); once resolved a label is LOCKED and trust labels cannot exceed the parent's (no-write-up); when structural cues (headings, accessibility attributes, developer class names) signal untrusted content the label cascades to the whole subtree without further queries. (3) Enforcement: a separate policy model sees ONLY task details with no DOM content and decides which content origins the task requires, allowing whole origin classes to be pruned; mechanical confinement then deterministically removes out-of-policy nodes from the observation and downgrades over-capability interactive elements by removing or disabling their target identifiers, mapping labels onto the accessibility-tree observation. Effective capability cap(e,task) = min(cap_gate, cap_biba). Every returned action is checked against the same capability map BEFORE Playwright executes it. Security argument (Sec.2.3-2.4): Definition 2.1 (Injection-Free Label) - a label is injection-free if no untrusted content on path(e) is an input to the labeling decision; Table 1 gives a three-case analysis where Case 1 (injection off the critical path) and Case 2 (on path but a structural cue precedes it) are injection-free, and only Case 3 (on path, no earlier cue) is not - and it suffices for EITHER mechanism to be injection-free since capability is the min of the two. Labels can only decrease in privilege and mislabelings are bounded.

**自述与显见局限**：Explicitly stated: Prismata protects only where the attacker's goal EXCEEDS the task's required privileges - in-scope attacks (fake reviews influencing product selection when the task legitimately requires reading reviews) fall within least privilege and are left to model-level reasoning; the system operates in the TEXTUAL input modality only, and extension to non-textual inputs by mapping the DOM-based permission structure onto screenshots is declared out of scope; Case 3 leaves residual risk; mislabeling potential is acknowledged since the labelers are LLMs; attacker-supplied JavaScript confinement to sandboxed iframes and arbitrary execution on the trusted origin (classical XSS) are assumed out of scope. Scoped to this read: the two metric families (attack effectiveness and labeling precision/recall/F1) are reported separately with no measured relationship between labeling quality and end-to-end ASR/TSR.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level headroom: (a) trust is derived PER OBSERVATION with no memory across sessions and no accumulation of provenance evidence over time - a persistent, updatable provenance store with its own write-gate is untouched; (b) labels are categorical, with no degree of trust or evidence weight, so a graded admissibility score is open; (c) the policy model's origin-class decision is per-task and static, not adaptive to evidence observed during execution; (d) no calibration linking labeling precision/recall to end-to-end attack or utility outcomes; (e) the ~3.3-point benign utility tax is reported but never optimized against a risk budget.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：Breaks moving to a frozen black-box speech/omni core: (1) the whole confinement rests on the DOM supplying a FREE, near-deterministic structural provenance parse - critical path, ancestry, origin classes, developer-authored class names; a speech/audio stream has no structural ancestry, so 'who authored this segment' is at best a diarization or speaker-verification ESTIMATE with its own error rate, and the injection-free property (label computed from an input the adversary cannot reach) has no obvious audio analogue that is both restricted and decisive; (2) mechanical confinement requires an enumerable element set with stable identifiers to redact or downgrade - audio observation is continuous and cannot be partially masked with the same guarantee, so 'remove the untrusted node from the observation' does not carry over; (3) the paper is explicitly text-modality-only and defers even screenshots, let alone waveforms; (4) TSR/ASR are graded by WebArena's combination of programmatic checks, LLM-as-a-judge and string matching, presuming a deterministic-ish task oracle that free-form speech lacks; (5) favorable: the mechanism is deterministic code interposed on every action, independent of any LLM reasoning, and needs no weights, gradients, hidden states or logprobs from the agent core - the ARCHITECTURE is contract-compatible even where the provenance substrate is not.

**可借鉴**：The central design principle is the write-gate primitive we need: the DECISIVE labeling step must be computed from an input the adversary cannot reach (a restricted view / critical path), rather than trusting a model to resist injection - Definition 2.1 gives the formal statement and Table 1 the case analysis showing when the property holds. Monotone-degradation labeling (labels can only DECREASE in privilege, locked once resolved) and min-of-two-mechanisms composition as a bounded-error confinement pattern that survives an imperfect labeler. Sec.4.3.2: the allowed-element precision / recall / F1 family against expert annotation, with the explicit reading that LOW PRECISION means over-permissiveness and weakens security while LOW RECALL means over-restrictiveness and degrades utility - all three reported because the tolerable trade-off is application-specific. The paired reporting of ASR alongside benign TSR so a defense cannot win by refusing everything (the same discipline as the refusal-cost arm in 2607.04334 - two independent papers in this read set converge on it). Sec.4.4: defense_harness() as a VARIANCE-CONTROLLED replay evaluation - replay a clean reference trajectory step by step and ask whether the defense made each action impossible, giving a lower-bound defense-utility metric that localizes which stage over-restricts while holding base-agent nondeterminism fixed so the only remaining variance is the defense model. The adaptive-attacker arm (multi-round attacks aimed at the trust-derivation process itself, explicitly WITHOUT assuming adversarial robustness of the labeling models).

**备注**：Anchors: across the main attack settings ASR falls 85.5% -> 0.7% average while task completion UNDER ATTACK rises 4.5% -> 23.0%; benign task success 29.9% -> 26.6% (a ~3.3-point utility tax). Shortcut attack: TSR 11.2 -> 24.4, ASR 63.9 -> 2.1, DSR 88.4. WASP goal-0/goal-1 driven to 0/21 in both end-to-end and intercept settings. Adaptive WA20: Shortcut ASR 100.0 -> 0.0, Completion 65.0 -> 0.0. Labeling validated against ~20 hours of security-expert DOM annotation. No gradient use anywhere.

### 2607.09438 — Nika at ImageCLEF 2026 Task on Multimodal Reasoning: More Tokens, Fewer Trees - Test-Time Scaling for Small VLMs on Multilingual Visual MCQ

**域**：vision (small open VLMs; EXAMS-V multilingual visual multiple-choice, 11 languages)　**供体级别**：BASELINE_ANCHOR　**喂给**：P8 (reward-as-controller: this is the strongest negative result in this read set that a trained multimodal PRM and a training-free generative critic both fail to beat majority vote); P9 (admissibility: when should a scorer be allowed to override consensus); P10 (a selector that regresses high-confidence items is selection pressure doing net harm); P1 (budget-axis pricing: token budget vs chain count)

**方法面**：Head-to-head comparison under a single-GPU envelope (A100-40GB, matching the shared task's A40 constraint), policies Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct and Qwen3.5-4B, bf16, vLLM with prefix caching, evaluated on the full EXAMS-V validation split (n=4,651) with a 200-question (language, subject)-stratified dev subset (seed 42, min ten per language) for expensive ablations. Two pipelines. (a) Describe-then-reason with PRM-guided beam annealing: sample N image-grounded descriptions at T=0.7, then with the image REMOVED generate reasoning either flat (M chains) or via PRM-BAS-style search - initial beam width B_0=4 expanded with B=2 continuations per surviving beam, each step scored by Qwen-VL-PRM-7B (which retains image access), beam width HALVED whenever the score range among top beams exceeds tau=0.05, termination on the answer cue or depth d=6; ~13 calls/question. (b) Flat self-consistency: N in {8,16} chains at T=0.7 with max_new_tokens=2048, a regex answer-letter extractor mapping Cyrillic labels to A-E, majority across parseable chains with log-probability tie-breaking. Three post-hoc selectors on the SAME chain pool: majority vote (no extra inference); a training-free generative critic scoring each chain on three rubric axes; and a discriminative cross-model PRM (Qwen-VL-PRM-7B, one-shot) deliberately chosen to control for sycophantic self-preference. All selectors use a skip rule preserving high-confidence majorities (>=5/8 agreement passes through; only the weak <=4/8 tier and the all-unparseable tier are rescored). Guided parse repair: when EVERY chain for a question fails to emit a letter, concatenate the chain's reasoning with the cue and decode a single token under vLLM's guided_choice constraint over {A,B,C,D,E} - eliminating parse failures by construction. Pairwise comparisons use McNemar's paired test with Bonferroni correction.

**自述与显见局限**：Scoped to this paper: MCQ letter answers only, one benchmark family, open policies <=7B under a single-GPU envelope; the authors themselves note the CoT-to-SC(1k) gain CONFLATES truncation absorption with diversity benefit because 16-18% of chains were unparseable at the 1k budget; selector evaluation is strictly post-hoc on a fixed candidate pool so a selector can never influence generation; the dev-200 subset's +2 pp N-scaling signal COLLAPSED at full validation, which they report as an anti-small-subset warning against their own earlier reading; this is a workshop working note, not a controlled study of PRM design.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level headroom: (a) the null is a SELECTION null on a pool where majority vote is already strong and a skip rule already protects high-agreement questions - the untouched question is whether a controller that spends UNEQUAL budget on the weak-agreement tail (<=4/8, 23% of questions, below-chance accuracy that more sampling does not fix) can beat both flat SC and the selectors; (b) no abstain option is offered on that below-chance tail, which is the natural admissibility move; (c) no calibration of the selector's own confidence, so there is no rule for when to let a selector override the majority; (d) no cost-normalized comparison of selection against simply raising the token budget, even though their own numbers show the budget axis dominates; (e) the PRM's saturation (P(+) 0.962 on descriptions, 0.849 on reasoning) is diagnosed but never repaired by recalibration or by margin-based abstention.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：Breaks moving to a frozen black-box speech/omni core: (1) MCQ letter answers give an exact-match oracle and make majority vote trivially well-defined - free-form speech output has neither, so BOTH the anchor (majority vote) and the accuracy metric would have to be replaced by a semantic-equivalence estimate that introduces its own error, and the corrections-vs-regressions ledger would inherit that error; (2) log-probability tie-breaking uses logprobs, which our black-box contract does not guarantee; (3) guided_choice constrained decoding requires a constrained-decoding API on the core - unavailable black-box, so the guided parse repair (their single best cost/benefit lever) does not transfer as implemented and would need a re-prompt-and-extract substitute; (4) the token-budget axis assumes a controllable max_new_tokens; (5) the PRM scores discrete textual reasoning STEPS, and a speech response has no per-step decomposition at the same granularity; (6) the parseability diagnosis itself is format-specific - the speech analogue (a response that reasons correctly but never commits) exists but has no regex-checkable commit token.

**可借鉴**：The diagnosis discipline is the primary donor: before crediting any search or selection method, check PARSEABILITY, then TOKEN TRUNCATION, then the POLICY, in that order - what looks like a reasoning failure is often an extraction failure, and TTS studies that do not control for parseability risk attributing engineering artifacts to the scaling method. Sec.5.4: guided repair as a cheap format-repair decision right that recovered more than doubling N at a fraction of the compute. Sec.5.2 and App.A: the AGREEMENT-TIER STRATIFICATION (>=7/8, <=4/8, all-unparseable) as the correct slicing to localize where a selector helps and where it REGRESSES - PRM-BAS regresses -2.16 pp on the high-confidence tier (48 corrections vs 119 regressions), which dominates its overall deficit, while being clearly positive only on the 108 all-unparseable questions. The CORRECTIONS-VS-REGRESSIONS LEDGER instead of a net delta (233 vs 250; 51 vs 55; 37 vs 59). The sharpest scorer-discrimination test in this read set: the score gap between the top-ranked and runner-up chain is 0.049 on corrections and 0.048 on degradations, i.e. the scorer carries no discriminative signal even where its net effect looks neutral. The critic-degeneracy check (40% of chains receive the modal score 0.75; correct/incorrect mean gap 0.713 vs 0.687). The cross-model PRM as a sycophancy control. The beam-diversity collapse statistic (71.9% of questions have all surviving beams agreeing; Shannon entropy 0.259 of 2.322 bits, an 89% reduction) as the diagnostic for search that spends compute without producing candidate diversity. The skip rule as a budget-allocation primitive. The dev-200 -> val-full collapse as the anti-small-subset warning.

**备注**：Anchors, full validation n=4,651: Q3.5 zero-shot 57.11 -> CoT 69.66 (16.2% parse-fail) -> SC-8 at 1k 77.81 (18.4% parse-fail) -> SC-8 at 2k 81.49 (2.7%) -> SC-16 at 2k 81.64 (1.9%). Token budget 1k->2k = +3.7 pp (171 questions); chain count 8->16 = +0.15 pp (7 questions, sampling noise). Policy swap Q2.5->Q3.5 = +11.4 pp at matched settings, widening to 15.1 pp with the budget increase, and all eleven languages improve by >=3 pp. PRM-BAS 80.74 vs SC majority 81.13 on the same 4,319 questions = -0.39 pp at 8.7x the cost. Selectors: Q2.5 pool (65.60 anchor) critic +0.00, PRM +0.45 n.s. (McNemar chi2=1.80, p~0.18); Q3.5 pool (81.49 anchor) PRM -0.09, critic -0.47. Temperature inverted-U at 0.7, spread compressing to 0.65 pp at scale. Best configuration 84.1% on the held-out ImageCLEF 2026 test split, first on the Visual MCQ leaderboard. The PRM is a trained model but is used only as a frozen scorer - no gradient use in this paper's own pipeline.

### 2607.17136 — Teach it to stop, not just to click

**域**：vision-GUI (computer-use agent; Holo3-35B-A3B primary, Fara-7B replication; five oracle-graded environments: Fiverr, Shopify, Indeed, LinkedIn and one more)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：stop/abstain calibration (cross-cutting; here STOP is the object of study, not a side effect); P8 (verifier as controller across four decision roles); P5 (tool/skill credit - which correction channel repairs which failure); P6 (system composition); evidence discipline across every P-slot

**方法面**：Testbed is verifier-guided repair of a 35B CUA. The apparatus (Sec.4) is a COMPLETION VERIFIER - a second, more capable VLM judging from the SAME observation the policy sees whether the task is complete and what the corrective next action is - deployed in four roles on equal footing, which is the paper's decomposition of decision rights over one signal: verifier-as-hint (injected into the observation each step; 'renting the fix'), verifier-as-teacher (behavior-cloning target), verifier-as-reward (step scores: acting after completion = over-execution = 0, progress = 1), and verifier-as-GATE at inference (a proposed done() or any irreversible action is accepted only if the verifier confirms completion). Five correction channels are compared on equal footing: naive in-context prompting, verifier-guided in-context hints, imitation fine-tuning, reward-based updates, and inference-time override - the first two 'rent' the fix (re-supplied every step), the latter two 'buy' it into weights. The weight update SA-OPSD (segment-aggregated on-policy self-distillation) is GRADIENT-BASED: L = GRPO(A_i, log pi_theta, log pi_old) + sigma(-A_i/tau) * BC(a_teacher), with a clipped importance ratio against captured log pi_old and a Schulman-k3 KL anchor to the LoRA-disabled base, conditioned on screenshots. The paper's SPINE (Sec.6) is a reliability methodology: a variance-components decomposition on a crossed grid of D=3 INDEPENDENT data draws (fresh disjoint base-rollout batches) x k=8 training seeds, each checkpoint re-evaluated, fit by Henderson crossed-ANOVA with parametric-bootstrap CIs; plus a formal bimodality test (Hartigan dip, plus a two-component mixture BIC comparison) and a seed-budget power analysis, released as the cua_reliability library.

**自述与显见局限**：Sec.8 states six: two policies, not many - the variance decomposition and end-to-end results are on ONE agent (Holo3-35B-A3B), with only the two-tier gradient replicated on Fara-7B in a small-n SFT-only check, so the reliability MAGNITUDES (the paper's central numbers) are characterized on one policy; small n - held-out results are decision-state emission rates over 9-34 decision frames from 6-9 trajectories per cell at k=5 seeds, establishing graded existence rather than calibrated deployment magnitudes; end-to-end n is 5 rollouts per arm on three cells, establishing the transfer boundary but not calibrated magnitudes; verifier generality rests on a 16-frame probe with n=2 completion-positives, directional only; an earlier 'spatial grounding cannot be bought' boundary is EXPLICITLY RETRACTED; and verifier-authored labels and frame selection share one source (frame selection, teacher, and metric are all the verifier's judgment), which they confront directly and mitigate by resting broader claims on oracle-graded transfer and multi-verifier agreement rather than the frame-level proxy.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level headroom: (a) the verifier-as-gate has NO calibrated threshold - no recall or precision target, in sharp contrast to the Clopper-Pearson machinery of 2607.06503, so a calibrated version of exactly this gate is an open slot; (b) the variance protocol is built for a TRAINING pipeline (data draw x seed), and the analogous decomposition for a training-free controller (condition draw x prompt draw x sampling seed) is not instantiated here; (c) no cost accounting for verifier calls, so the gate's price is unmeasured; (d) the two-tier repairability gradient is characterized on a single grounding cell, and they report probing three further tasks for a clean second far-target grounding decision and finding none, so purpose-built tasks are needed; (e) a calibrated verifier benchmark (hundreds of labeled completion frames) is named as their own next test.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：Breaks moving to a frozen black-box speech/omni core: (1) SA-OPSD is a gradient update on a 35B VLM policy requiring captured log pi_old (token logprobs) and a KL anchor to a LoRA-disabled base - excluded twice over by the black-box contract (no weights, no guaranteed logprobs); only the MEASUREMENTS and DESIGNS transfer; (2) the environments are ORACLE-GRADED with mutation-log grading, and the authors explicitly report that early pilots scoring success from screenshots were 'too ambiguous to use' - so the very measurement fidelity that licenses their numbers is unavailable for free-form speech, and their own experience says the weaker proxy is not merely noisier but unusable; (3) the verifier judges 'from the same observation the policy sees', which for a speech core means the verifier must consume the waveform - and their capability gradient (Qwen2.5-VL-7B scores 0/2 on the decisive done-detection frames vs 1/2 at 72B) warns that small open verifiers fail exactly the decision that matters; (4) 'decision frames' presume a discrete step boundary with an inspectable state and a well-defined corrective next action, whereas a speech turn has no frame-level corrective-action target; (5) sigma_data here is TRAINING-DATA composition, which a training-free system does not have - the analogue must be re-derived (condition draw, probe draw, prompt draw), not imported; (6) favorable: verifier-as-hint and verifier-as-gate are pure 'renting' channels that need no weights, so those two of the four roles are contract-compatible as-is.

**可借鉴**：The reliability protocol is the primary donor and is the strongest evidence-discipline instrument in this read set. Sec.6: the variance-components decomposition on a CROSSED data-draw x seed grid with each checkpoint re-evaluated (Henderson crossed-ANOVA, bootstrap CIs) - for us the cells become condition-draw x prompt/sampling seed; the finding that evaluation variance can be a MEASURED ZERO while upstream draw variance dominates tells us exactly where replication budget should go. The methodological corollary they verified in passing: measuring sigma on SUBSAMPLES OF ONE BATCH rather than independent draws halves it (0.18 vs 0.36 on the hard cell) and erases the catastrophic-failure mode entirely (0/24 vs 3/10 runs below 0.10) - a variance estimate that resamples within one draw understates and can hide the very instability being measured. The Hartigan dip test plus two-component-mixture BIC as the formal bimodality check, and IQM with a stratified bootstrap (rliable style) as the correct summary when the distribution is bimodal. The seed-budget power curve: set k from the cell's measured sigma and the effect claimed, with an explicit split between EXISTENCE claims (k<=5) and SEPARATION claims (k~46 for Delta=0.18 at sigma=0.3). The wrong-sign stress test as a referee-facing statistic (a published-size single-run improvement would have the wrong sign 33-44% of the time in this variance regime; even a +0.2 gain flips 25-34%). The 'sole remaining blocker' framing as the honest transfer boundary for any component-level repair claim. The verifier-generality probe design (agreement / done-detection / false-positive columns, with the conservative direction identified: all four verifiers make zero false-positives, none prematurely calls an incomplete task done - the safe direction for a gate). The four-roles-of-one-verifier decomposition (hint / teacher / reward / gate) and the rent-vs-buy channel taxonomy, where 'renting' is precisely our training-free lane. The constrained-vs-open-ended repairability gradient as a prior on which repairs a controller can expect to land.

**备注**：Anchors. Variance table (three cells, D=3 x k=8): Fiverr token cell (mean 0.79) sigma_data 0.10 / sigma_resid 0.18 / sigma_train 0.07 / sigma_frame 0.12 / sigma_eval ~0; Shopify near-coordinate (0.72) 0.10 / 0.13 / 0.065 / 0.13 / ~0; Indeed far-coordinate (0.29) 0.26 / 0.22 / 0.12 / 0.10 / ~0. Two invariants: evaluation variance is a measured zero everywhere (re-evaluating a fixed checkpoint gave 0.176 twice) and the training-seed effect is <=10% everywhere; the data draw's share grows with difficulty to 48% on the hardest cell. Hardest cell is bimodal: Hartigan dip p=0.07 at k=10 (underpowered at k=5, p=0.38), Delta-BIC=13 for a two-component mixture, modes at ~0.09 (40% of runs) and ~0.80 (60%), IQM=0.56 with 95% CI [0.19, 0.81]. Two-tier repairability: fixed-token done-detection installs at 0.97+/-0.06, field-targeting 0.71+/-0.26, grounding 0.53+/-0.35, generative field-fill 0.14+/-0.04, each with a bootstrap CI excluding its base. End-to-end transfer: LinkedIn 8/20 vs base 0/15, Fisher exact p=0.006 - repair reaches task success exactly when the corrective action is the sole remaining blocker. Two self-falsifications reported (a sample-efficiency curve and a 'grounding cannot be bought' boundary, the latter formally retracted). GRADIENT USE FLAGGED: SA-OPSD is an on-policy RL/distillation update; donor status is for the reliability protocol and the verifier-role decomposition only.

**组级综合（agent 提交）**：SCOPE. Ten papers, all 2026-06/07: three text/agent (2606.28733, 2607.03436, 2607.06503), two vision/VLM test-time-scaling (2606.28864, 2607.09438), four vision-GUI/web-agent (2606.31612, 2607.04334, 2607.08147, 2607.17136), one embodied VLA (2607.03751). Zero web queries issued; all facts from the provided fulltexts. Every claim below is scoped to THIS READ SET. No novelty verdicts are offered and no cross-modality effectiveness conclusion is drawn (H5 withheld per owner directive).【复核更正 wf_40068490】

WHAT THE DONORS SETTLE.

(1) P9 gets a formal spine it did not have. 2607.03436 settles, by proof rather than measurement, that a per-instance oracle computed from single stochastic draws is an upward-biased estimator of any reproducible ceiling, that the router-to-oracle gap decomposes exactly as G = G_rec + G_noise with G_noise >= 0 under Jensen alone, and — the load-bearing asymmetry — that G_noise is closed by NO single-commit selector, deterministic or randomized, yet IS recovered by resampling at the oracle's own budget. Empirically the noise share runs 12% (saturated), 36% (unsaturated), 43% (thin-support stratum), 13% on a non-math benchmark whose absolute floor is nonetheless largest. Two corollaries bind our program directly: the share is baseline-dependent while the FLOOR is router-invariant (verified by swapping in a learned router: share 36%->31%, floor identical at 0.036); and recovery scope is verifier-gated — verifier-free majority vote reached only 0.824 on MATH-500, BELOW the reproducible ceiling 0.837, so self-consistency did not beat committing to the best model there. Read together with 2607.09438's independent selector null, this read set converges on: unverified aggregation is not a free lunch, and any headroom number we publish must state which part a selector can reach.

(2) Stop/abstain calibration is settled as a statistical object, twice, from opposite directions. 2607.06503 supplies the machinery: per-round Clopper-Pearson gates, the non-composition argument (98% x 98% is not the global rate — it depends on whether the false-abort sets overlap), joint budget search under a GLOBAL episode-level recall floor, a freeze-then-certify post-selection guarantee exact regardless of search size, abstain-on-infeasible as the conservative default, and a scorer-quality-independent sample-complexity curve (~114 successes support ~0.974 recall; 0.98 needs ~149; 0.99 needs ~299). 2606.28733 supplies the timeliness semantics the machinery needs: (w_i, tau_i) with premature abstention counted as FAILURE, AbsRec@K as a curve, SPL as a delay-penalized score, and over-abstention on a paired solvable set as the mandatory second axis. 2607.17136 supplies the third leg — how many replicates a stop claim needs — and independently confirms the constrained/open-ended repairability gradient (fixed-token stop installs at 0.97, generative fill at 0.14).

(3) Evidence discipline is settled at a level our program has not been holding. 2607.17136 measures that evaluation variance can be exactly zero while the upstream draw dominates (48% on the hardest cell), that the distribution can be bimodal (dip p=0.07, only visible at the tenth seed), that existence claims cost k<=5 while separation claims cost k~46, and that a published-size single-run improvement would carry the wrong sign 33-44% of the time in that regime. Its sharpest transferable warning: estimating sigma from subsamples of ONE batch halves it (0.18 vs 0.36) and erases the catastrophic-failure mode entirely (0/24 vs 3/10 below 0.10). 2607.03436 adds the pre-registered assumption gates that block magnitude reporting on failure. 2607.09438 adds the anti-small-subset warning (a +2 pp signal on dev-200 collapsed at val-full) and the corrections-vs-regressions ledger.

WHAT TRANSFERS.

Contract-compatible as-is (no weights, gradients, hidden states, attention or logprobs required): the G_rec/G_noise decomposition and multi-sample oracle reporting standard (2607.03436); the whole risk-control skeleton of the abort cascade minus its feature source (2607.06503); the (w_i,tau_i)/AbsRec@K/SPL/over-abstention metric family and the CONVOLVE playbook loop (2606.28733); the paired memory-ON/OFF balanced-group intervention run as a training-free value-of-information measurement plus the Mem. selectivity metric and the ON-minus-OFF gap audit (2606.31612); the perception-correct conditioning trick with its strict-variant construct guard, severity calibration of synthetic interventions against a mined natural distribution, and the four-arm mitigation comparison on IDENTICAL probes with an explicit cost column (2607.04334); the injection-free-label / monotone-degradation / min-of-two-mechanisms write-gate pattern and the allowed-element precision/recall/F1 family (2607.08147); the variance-components protocol, dip/IQM reporting, seed-budget power curve and verifier-generality probe design (2607.17136); the pass@k headroom-existence test with its three-regime reading and the minimum ablation triple for a selection controller (2607.03751); the agreement-tier stratification, corrections/regressions ledger, and the score-gap-on-corrections-vs-degradations statistic (2607.09438); the token-budget-cap ablation and Rationale Sufficiency probe (2606.28864).

Two independent convergences worth flagging. First, THREE papers (2607.04334 Table 5, 2607.08147 Sec.1.2/4.3, 2606.31612 Sec.4.3) independently arrive at the same scoring discipline: a controller that buys safety or success with refusals, restrictions or memory invocations must be scored on BOTH axes simultaneously — hijack AND task error, ASR AND benign TSR, success rate AND Mem. — because each admits a degenerate winner otherwise. That discipline should be a hard requirement on every gate we build. Second, 2607.09438 and 2607.03436 independently deliver the same warning about verifier-free selection, one empirically (PRM and generative critic both fail to beat majority vote; the PRM's top-vs-runner-up score gap is 0.049 on corrections and 0.048 on degradations — no discriminative signal) and one theoretically (majority vote falls below the reproducible ceiling; the recoverable mass is dominated by the verifier-gated guessing residual).

WHAT BREAKS.

(a) The deterministic oracle is the single most load-bearing missing ingredient across the read set. 2607.03436's p_im, 2607.06503's binary episode label, 2606.31612's V(s_T) in {0,1}, 2607.03751's pass@k, 2606.28733's w_i, 2607.17136's oracle-graded environments and 2607.09438's MCQ letter all presume it. 2607.17136 is the most instructive: they report that scoring success from screenshots was 'too ambiguous to use' and switched to oracle/mutation-log grading — i.e. the weaker proxy was not merely noisier but unusable. For free-form speech output, every one of these statistics must be re-derived through a proxy judge whose own error is unmodeled in all ten papers, and in 2607.03436's framework that judge error would be silently absorbed into G_noise, inflating the 'irrecoverable' floor.

(b) Internal-state signals are excluded and the read set tells us the cost. 2607.06503's entire advantage comes from residual-stream activations, and it reports that behavior-only monitoring is near chance at round 1 and only informative by rounds 3-4 — by which time over a third of episodes have finished. Importing that cascade to a black-box core means importing the calibration machinery while losing the signal that made it pay. 2607.04334's white-box ablation and gradient attribution, 2606.28864's attention decomposition, and 2607.03751's trained value-head uncertainty are likewise unavailable.

(c) Exact-match equivalence is assumed wherever candidates are compared. Majority vote (2606.28864, 2607.09438), the empirical prior p(a|s) estimated by exact repetition frequency (2607.03751 Eq.4), and the answer-identity relation underlying the reproducible oracle (2607.03436) all need it. Speech's semantic-clustering substitute introduces an error term none of these papers model.

(d) Resettable, editable, enumerable environments are assumed by four papers. 2606.28733's environment-based infeasibility (delete all red shirts), 2606.31612's balanced ON/OFF rollouts with identical initialization, 2607.03751's simulator state cloning, and 2607.04334/2607.08147's DOM manipulation all require it. Audio supplies no free structural provenance parse, no enumerable addressable element set to redact, and no rollback — 2607.08147 is explicit that it is text-modality-only and defers even screenshots.

(e) Acoustic-condition keying multiplies every sampling budget and threatens the exchangeability every calibration result rests on. 2607.06503's guarantee holds under exchangeability and its own discussion says distribution shift requires refreshing probe, thresholds AND global control on labeled data. A cascade or oracle estimate calibrated on clean speech carries no validity on a different SNR/speaker/accent stratum, so both the ~114-299 certification successes and 2607.03436's k>=20 draws become per-stratum costs.

RESIDUAL GAPS THIS SUBSET DOES NOT CLOSE. No paper here builds a genuinely label-free headroom estimator (2607.03436 is label-USING and names calibrated ex-ante quality estimation as its own next step). No paper here gates memory writes at write time by an external, training-free criterion — 2606.31612 learns selectivity into weights, 2607.08147 gates observation not memory, so P3's write-gate slot is described from two sides but occupied by neither. No paper here probes a reward-hacking / over-optimization regime under increasing selection pressure: 2607.03751 reports only monotone gains to N=32, and the closest thing in this read set to a Goodhart observation is 2607.09438's finding that a selector REGRESSES the high-confidence tier by -2.16 pp (48 corrections vs 119 regressions), so P10 is under-served by this subset. And no paper here calibrates a verifier gate to a stated risk target — 2607.17136 uses the verifier as a gate with no threshold, while 2607.06503 has the calibration machinery but a black-box-unavailable signal; joining those two is the clearest composition opportunity this read set exposes.


## 供体组 7

### 2606.12935 — MARS: Margin-Adversarial Risk-controlled Stopping for Parallel LLM Test-time Scaling

**域**：text (LLM competition-math reasoning; parallel decoding)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：stop/abstain/budget calibration (cross-cutting, primary); P9 decision-calibration/admissibility gates (commitment certification); P8 reward-as-controller (gradient-free multi-step allocation)

**方法面**：Test-time early-stopping layer over parallel self-consistency voting; frozen generator, no weight updates. (§1.1) Probing: a running trace is interrupted with a short stop-thinking suffix to extract its current answer a_j(t), then the probe suffix is discarded and the original trace continues unmodified. Votes V_a(t)=Σ w_j·1{a_j(t)=a} with per-trace weights w_j (uniform → self-consistency; confidence-derived → DeepConf). Leader L(t), margin M_k(t)=V_L−V_k over each challenger k. (Def 2.1) Adversarial switch cost c_j^k = 2w_j if trace j currently votes leader, −w_j if it votes k, w_j otherwise — the worst-case damage from a switch, independent of destination. Expected adversarial damage Γ_k(t)=Σ_{j∈A_t} q_j c_j^k. (§2.2, Eq 3) Stop when M_k(t) ≥ Γ_k(t)+ε(N,δ) for EVERY challenger in K(t), where K(t) = all non-leader answers with positive vote PLUS a synthetic unseen challenger ⊥ with zero current votes (guards against answers that have not yet appeared). (Thm 2.4 / Cor 2.5) Hoeffding on Σ X_j c_j^k plus a union bound over challengers gives P(L(τ)≠L(T))≤δ with ε(N,δ)=w_max·sqrt(2·N_active·log(N/δ)). (§2.3) Calibrated relaxation: contraction γ∈[1/2,1] applied only to the 'which challenger benefits' portion (leader-voter loss w_j is irreducible), calibrated per question as γ_out=min(1, γ_warmup + z/sqrt(n_elig)), z=1.0. (§2.4) Two learned quantities per question, both fit on 16 warmup traces run to completion: a 5-feature per-question logistic switch model q̂_j(t) over {checkpoint position, probe confidence, answer-flip count, streak length, confidence trend}, and γ. Algorithm 1 is the full loop. Decision right exercised: STOP/BUDGET only — MARS never changes what is generated, only when generation halts.

**自述与显见局限**：The guarantee is relative to the FULL-BUDGET VOTE, not to ground truth (§1.1 Stopping: 'a guarantee relative to the full-budget vote, not directly to the unknown ground truth'). Assumption 2.3 requires conditional independence of switch indicators given F_t — unverified. Appendix F: the margin decomposition assumes constant per-trace weights w_j, exact only for uniform weighting, approximate for DeepConf's position-dependent weights. Cor 2.5 covers ONLY the certified variant (γ=1 and TRUE q_j); the deployed calibrated variant with γ<1 or estimated q̂ is explicitly labelled an empirical relaxation with no theorem (§2.3). Evaluation is three competition-math benchmarks × three reasoning models only; other domains 'remain to be validated'. Probing presupposes thinking-mode models that emit an answer when interrupted. Warmup cost is 16 complete traces per question (included in the savings denominator, §3.1) — a real per-item tax.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level steps left on top of this paper: (a) the certification target is answer-identity stability, not quality — extending it to a risk statement about the true objective is open; (b) ε comes from Hoeffding + union bound and is admittedly conservative (Remark 2.6: certified MARS stops at the same time or later than Bayes-optimal, never earlier) — an anytime-valid / sequential-testing bound would tighten it; (c) the destination distribution is handled by one scalar contraction γ rather than modelled, and the paper flags this as an approximation that warmup data only partially recovers (§3.3 ablation: γ calibration adds 4–7pp SC / 2–5pp DeepConf over γ=1); (d) conditional independence of switches is assumed, not tested; (e) nothing here calibrates γ or q̂ per operating CONDITION, only per question.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：REQUIRED — what breaks on a frozen black-box speech/omni core: (1) Votes, margins and the challenger set K(t) all presuppose a discrete answer identity with exact-match equivalence over candidates. Speech/omni outputs are free-form (text or waveform) with no deterministic equality relation; V_a(t) is undefined unless candidate 'sameness' is itself estimated, and that estimator's error enters M_k(t) completely unmodelled — the Hoeffding bound would then certify the wrong quantity. (2) Probing requires mid-generation access to PARTIAL traces at checkpoints; a black-box API returning only a completed response gives no checkpoint surface at all, so the observable that makes the whole method possible is absent. (3) 'Probe confidence' and 'confidence trend' are two of the five switch-model features, and confidence/logprobs are not guaranteed under our black-box contract. (4) Warmup is 16 complete traces per question — under waveform observations each draw costs audio encoding, so the savings arithmetic (savings computed with warmup in the denominator) shifts materially. (5) γ and q̂ are calibrated per QUESTION; there is no acoustic-condition key, and switch behaviour under an SNR/accent shift is a different distribution from the one warmup measured. (6) No deterministic oracle is needed by MARS itself (it certifies against the full-budget vote) — this is actually the one piece that survives, and is the reason the construct is worth borrowing even though the instantiation is not.

**可借鉴**：(a) The margin-versus-adversarial-damage stopping FORM: certify a commitment by requiring lead > worst-case future erosion, rather than by detecting stability — plus the paper's diagnosis of why consensus/stability rules fail exactly on hard items (§1, Fig. 2: on HMMT/DeepSeek-8B consensus stopping drops accuracy 70%→35%). (b) The synthetic unseen challenger ⊥ with zero current votes and cost 2γw_j for leader-voters / γw_j otherwise (§2.4) — directly reusable for open-ended speech outputs where the candidate set is not closed. (c) The two-part decomposition: LEARN which candidates will change (from their own observable history), treat WHERE they land ADVERSARIALLY and calibrate a single contraction from warmup — a reusable pattern for any signal we cannot model. (d) Upper-confidence calibration Eq 8: γ_out = min(1, γ_warmup + z/sqrt(n_elig)). (e) Explicit certified-vs-calibrated variant separation with the theorem scoped only to γ=1 + true q (§2.3) — an honesty discipline we should copy verbatim. (f) The oracle-q diagnostic ablation: replace the learned switch model with the retrospective indicator q*_j = 1{a_j(t)≠a_j(T)} to upper-bound what better estimation could buy (§3.1, §3.3: learned model within 1–4pp of oracle-q). (g) The accuracy–savings dilemma reporting (Table 2): report savings AND accuracy at matched settings and show the baseline's whole tradeoff curve (Parallel-Probe at u=3 saves 20–51% but loses 6–45pp; at u=14 preserves accuracy but saves ≤4%). (h) Counting warmup cost inside the savings denominator (§3.1).

**备注**：Frozen core, no gradients on the generator; the only fitted objects are a per-question 5-feature logistic regression and a scalar γ, both from 16 warmup traces — this is within a training-free control plane. Headline numbers (Table 1) are 25–47% SC token savings and 14–29% on top of DeepConf across 18 settings with accuracy preserved within 0.6pp; these are TEXT/MATH numbers and are not a cross-modality effectiveness claim for speech.

### 2606.13156 — Iterative Visual Thinking and the Self-Correction Mirage in VLM Grounding

**域**：vision-GUI / VLM (referring-expression grounding; measurement-critique paper)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P9 decision-calibration/admissibility gates + label-free headroom estimation (primary, strongest fit in this read set); stop/abstain calibration (cross-cutting); P8 reward-as-controller (negative evidence on self-verification as controller signal)

**方法面**：Two objects in one paper. (i) A competently trained iterative self-correction system, IVT (§3): predict box b^(0) → render it as a semi-transparent red overlay on the original image → inject the rendered image plus a corrective instruction into the same autoregressive turn → refine, T=2 (three predictions total). Trained in two phases: SFT warm-up on the STUDENT's own predictions interpolated toward GT with teacher-written step-conditioned corrective reasoning (§3.2 — they report that seeding from randomly perturbed GT instead caused 'step-0 sandbagging' under GRPO), then GRPO with reward R(τ)=IoU(b^(T),b_GT)+0.1·format-fraction (Eq 3), KL β=0.04, entropy λ=0.01, Qwen3-VL-4B + LoRA r64. GRADIENT USE: yes, SFT+GRPO. (ii) The audit, which is the actual donor (§4–§6). The standard report keeps, per sample, the trajectory step whose box best matches GT — an oracle requiring the answer it predicts. Re-scored under label-free policies {single-shot(step 0), last, last-valid, stability τ=0.90, movement ε=0.05, self-verify gated/argmax}. §6 formalises the Honest-Trajectory Evaluation Protocol: Oracle m_or = E_x[max_t m(b^(t),b_GT)]; Shippable m_π = E_x[m(b^(π(x)),b_GT)] for a label-free policy π reported as the best over a FIXED, PRE-REGISTERED policy set that MUST include single-shot; Oracle gap Δ = m_or − max_π m_π (how much reported headroom no label-free policy can reach; a faithful method has Δ≈0); Honest self-correction gain g = max_{π≢0} m_π − m_single-shot (iteration justified only if g>0). Verification-bottleneck probes (§5): self-verification P(yes) read from logits on a rendered box; and a counterfactual overlay that holds the step-0 text fixed and swaps ONLY the box drawn on the fed-back image (own / ground-truth / GT-mirrored).

**自述与显见局限**：One base model family (Qwen3-VL-4B), one task (REC grounding), T=2, 505-sample test set, 2400 training samples. The 'no observable signal recovers the oracle' claim is scoped by the authors to the signals they test — expression length (r≈−0.14), box geometry, self-verification P(yes), and the counterfactual overlay; they state this is 'a statement about a binding constraint, not an impossibility proof' (§7 What would change the verdict) and name training a separate box verifier as the untried remedy. The protocol's Δ and g both require ground truth to compute m_or, so it is an offline evaluation discipline, not a deployment-time estimator. Self-verification is read from logits — a white-box-ish signal even here.

**改进空间（方向级）**：(a) The paper diagnoses the verification gap but builds nothing to close it — an external calibrated critic is named and not attempted. (b) Δ (oracle gap) is computed with labels; a LABEL-FREE estimator of attainable headroom is exactly the missing piece and is untouched. (c) The pre-registered policy set is fixed and small; there is no search over richer stopping rules, no ensemble/agreement rule, no abstain option. (d) The counterfactual-overlay finding ('the loop reacts to the presence of a rendered box, not its content') is a diagnostic, not a repair — no mechanism forces evidence-reading. (e) Difficulty stratification (Table 3) shows the oracle gain lives entirely in the 20% hard stratum moving mean IoU 0.104→0.198 (failure→failure); nothing exploits the corollary that a controller should be routing by difficulty rather than iterating uniformly.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：REQUIRED — what breaks on a frozen black-box speech/omni core: (1) The entire protocol is anchored on a CRISP DETERMINISTIC PER-STEP ORACLE (IoU against a GT box) that makes m_or exactly computable. Speech has no per-step deterministic oracle: WER exists only where a reference transcript exists, and for open-ended speech dialogue / paralinguistic outputs there is none — so m_or becomes an estimate with its own error, and Δ = m_or − max_π m_π degenerates into a difference of two noisy quantities whose sign is not decidable at the +2.4pp scale this paper operates at. (2) The self-verification signal is P(yes) READ FROM LOGITS (§5) — unavailable under our no-guaranteed-logprobs contract; a verbalised-confidence substitute is a different signal with (per 2606.25760) vendor-dependent behaviour, so the r≈0.22 correlation cannot be assumed to carry. (3) The counterfactual overlay requires planting a known-correct answer INTO THE OBSERVATION CHANNEL as a rendering. The speech analogue (injecting a correct transcript into the prompt) changes the information set rather than the rendering, so it is not a modality-matched intervention and would not test the same thing. (4) Their refinement steps are cheap re-renders of a FIXED image — no new information is acquired, which is why iterating can only lose. In speech, a 're-observation' is a re-listen that likewise adds no information unless segmentation or acoustic conditioning changes; the structural lesson transfers, the cost model does not. (5) No acoustic-condition keying: one image, one trajectory, one policy — nothing in the protocol stratifies by an environment key, which a speech version must add.

**可借鉴**：(a) THE HONEST-TRAJECTORY PROTOCOL (§6) essentially verbatim: report the triple (m_single-shot, max_π m_π, m_or), foreground g and Δ, pre-register the label-free policy set, and always include the do-nothing baseline. This is the single most directly adoptable artifact in my read set for P9. (b) The demonstration that the ORACLE ARTIFACT ALSO INFLATES THE NEGATIVE headline: the widely quoted 48.7% / −31pp collapse for naive iteration is itself a best-step/last-valid figure; the deployable last-step number is 17.0%, i.e. 79.6→17.0 = −63pp (§4.2). Removing the oracle made the negative worse, not better — a reporting check we should run on our own baselines. (c) The 'GRPO is defensive, not additive' decomposition (§4.3): training lifted g from −11.1pp to −3.8pp and halved per-step degradation without ever beating single-shot; i.e. separate 'makes iteration less harmful' from 'makes iteration helpful'. (d) The verification-quality diagnostic set: correlation of self-confidence with correctness (r=0.229/0.223), calibration check (mean P(yes)≈0.40 even on correct boxes), argmax-picks-oracle-step rate (84.6% for GRPO) reported ALONGSIDE the fact that it still loses — because the oracle advantage is tiny and the 15% wrong picks cost more than the right picks gain. That last decomposition (selection accuracy × payoff asymmetry) is a reusable admissibility argument. (e) The counterfactual-evidence probe as an ablation DESIGN: hold everything fixed and swap only the evidence content (correct / wrong / own) to test whether the loop reads evidence at all. (f) Difficulty-stratified oracle-gain table (Table 3) with per-stratum later-helps / later-hurts rates.

**备注**：Explicitly a VISION-domain result. Nothing here licenses a cross-modality effectiveness conclusion for speech; the borrowable is the measurement discipline, not the finding. Note the paper's own v1→v2 history (footnote 1): an earlier version reported only best-step accuracies and concluded the iterative method beats the base — same method, same trajectories, only the evaluation changed. That is the cleanest available demonstration that the evaluation protocol, not the mechanism, decided the verdict.

### 2606.15608 — On the Adversarial Robustness of Multimodal LLM Judges (RobustMLLMJudge framework + MGSIA attack)

**域**：VLM (multimodal LLM-as-judge; image quality and image safety evaluation)　**供体级别**：CONSTRUCT_DONOR　**喂给**：P10 reward hacking under selection pressure (primary); P9 reward-admissibility gates (which judge protocols may be trusted, and under what input provenance)

**方法面**：(§III-A) A taxonomy of MLLM-judge protocols into three families sharing quality and safety instantiations: (i) Token Probability Scoring — s = P_F(a_tar | v, q) from a single holistic binary VQA (VQAScore for quality; Ensemble/Single for safety); (ii) Fine-grained Decomposition — decompose the reference text t or safety policy π into sub-questions, score each, aggregate s = Agg({s_i}) (TIFA, DSG for quality; CLUE for safety); (iii) MLLM-as-a-Judge Prompting — s ← F(v, p_judge), free-form rating optionally with rationale (VIEScore, T2I-CompBench++, 'Fooling'; Brief and LLaVAGuard-style for safety). All scores rescaled to 100. (§III-B) Threat model: attacker generates and fully controls the candidate image v only; the reference text t, safety policy π, evaluation protocol and prompt templates are IMMUTABLE and publicly known (they serve as the ground-truth benchmark); objective is score inflation T* = argmax_T J(T(v),·;F); primary instantiation is additive δ with ||δ||_∞ ≤ ε (ε=8/255). Three model-access settings: white-box, black-box (input/output only), and a gray-box surrogate-then-transfer setting. (§III-C) An escalating attack ladder: image degradation (Gaussian blur/noise) → handcrafted black-box perturbations (Padding, Bounding Box, Target Text Overlay) → learnable targeted attacks (AttackVLM-ii/-it, Chain of Attack, and Target Text Embedding δ* = argmax_{||δ||∞≤ε} log P_F(t_tar | v+δ)). (§IV) MGSIA = Affirmative Semantic Induction (maximise probability of the affirmative token 'Yes' to fine-grained binary semantic queries produced by a DSG-style decomposer, prepended with a holistic VQAScore-style query) + High-score Manifold Alignment (regularise the adversarial representation toward high-score centres estimated from OFFLINE PROXY protocols and disjoint proxy samples' hidden states at a critical layer), δ* = argmin L_ASI + λ·L_HMA (Eq 10). GRADIENT USE: yes — both objectives are gradient-optimised, and HMA reads hidden states.

**自述与显见局限**：MGSIA is not a black-box attack: ASI optimises token likelihood and HMA reads hidden states at a critical layer, so it is white-/gray-box on the judge backbone. NO DEFENSE is proposed or evaluated anywhere in what I read — the paper is diagnosis-only and ends on 'highlighting the need for robust MLLM judges'. Judge backbones are two 7B open-weight models (LLaVA-1.5-7b, Qwen2.5-VL-7b); no frontier/API judge is tested. Fixed perturbation budget ε=8/255 with no budget sweep in the main tables. No human study establishing that the inflated images are in fact low-quality/unsafe (the benign score is the only anchor). The 'shared, protocol-agnostic geometric flaw' / 'evaluative manifold' claim (§V-C, Fig. 5) rests on a latent-space visualisation plus transfer numbers, not on a proof or a controlled causal test. Attacks are one-sided (inflation only; deflation/sandbagging untested).

**改进空间（方向级）**：(a) The attack suite is not turned into an ADMISSIBILITY CERTIFICATE — nothing says 'this judge score is inadmissible because the input sits in a shifted high-score region'. (b) No cross-protocol agreement gate is tested, even though the paper's own data shows attacks are protocol-heterogeneous (e.g. CLUE resists on LLaVA at +1.0 while Single moves +21.0) — an agreement-across-protocol-families gate is the obvious untried defense their own tables would score. (c) The diagnosis of WHY baselines fail (§V-B) is sharp — semantic-targeted attacks optimise a GENERATIVE objective that conflicts with the judges' DISCRIMINATIVE nature, and image-targeted attacks need an ideal target image that is 'paradoxically unavailable, as ground-truth (gold standard) images are inherently unavailable in evaluation scenarios' — but this insight is used to build a stronger attack, not a detector. (d) No pricing: attack cost vs inflation achieved is not reported.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：REQUIRED — what breaks on a frozen black-box speech/omni core: (1) The attack surface is CONTINUOUS PIXEL-SPACE additive perturbation on an artifact the adversary fully controls. In our program there is no adversary; the pressure comes from our own selection among candidates the frozen core produced, so no gradient-optimised δ exists and the mechanism is entirely non-transferable — only the taxonomy and the measurement survive. (2) The Token Probability Scoring family is defined by s = P_F(a_tar|v,q) — requires logprobs, excluded by our black-box contract. (3) The Fine-grained Decomposition family presumes the reference decomposes into sub-questions each with a crisp checkable answer; speech sub-questions (was the emotion right? was the turn attributed to the right speaker? was the overlap resolved?) have no deterministic per-question oracle. (4) The threat model's load-bearing assumption is an IMMUTABLE PUBLIC REFERENCE t (or policy π) that anchors every protocol. In speech deployment there is frequently no reference at test time at all, which removes the anchor rather than merely making it noisy. (5) Acoustic-condition keying is wholly absent: one image → one score. The speech analogue of 'Gaussian blur as a benign-artifact control' is a channel/SNR perturbation that is NOT semantically neutral — it changes what is actually recoverable from the signal, so the degradation rung of their ladder does not port as a control condition. (6) Their Target Text Overlay shortcut (judge reads OCR'd text as evidence of alignment, Fig. 4 attention maps) has an audio analogue only if the model has a comparable 'read the injected token instead of assessing the content' shortcut — that is a hypothesis for us to test, not a finding to inherit.

**可借鉴**：(a) THE THREE-FAMILY JUDGE TAXONOMY (§III-A) with its explicit score-extraction formalism — token-probability / fine-grained-decomposition / free-form-prompting — is a clean way to enumerate which of our reward channels are even available under a black-box contract (family (i) is immediately excluded for us; this is a useful structural constraint, not an effectiveness claim). (b) The threat-model discipline (§III-B): state exactly what the pressure can and cannot modify, and hold the reference/policy/protocol immutable as the benchmark. (c) The ESCALATING LADDER as an ablation design: benign baseline → semantically-neutral degradation → cheap handcrafted shortcut → optimised attack, with per-protocol deltas reported against the benign score for EVERY protocol in the matrix (Tables I, II) — this is directly reusable as our P10 reward-hacking stress-test structure. (d) The attention-map diagnosis that separates 'the judge was genuinely deceived' from 'the judge took a shortcut' (§V-B: Target Text Overlay shifts focus to the overlaid text; Padding/Bounding Box disperse attention to edges and are read as image defects, so scores often go DOWN). The idea that a perturbation can be read as a DEFECT rather than as evidence — producing negative deltas — is a control we should build in. (e) The observation that gold-standard targets are unavailable at evaluation time and attackers must fall back on surrogates (§V-B) — that constraint is structurally identical to ours.

**备注**：VISION domain, judges are 7B open-weight. Gradient use flagged: MGSIA optimises token likelihood and reads hidden states — mechanism non-borrowable under our contract; it is a donor for its MEASUREMENT STRUCTURE and its taxonomy only. Verdict is CONSTRUCT_DONOR rather than PROTOCOL_DONOR because the statistic (score inflation vs benign) is generic while the taxonomy/threat-model formalism is what actually does work for us; the attack-ladder ablation design is a secondary protocol borrowable.

### 2606.17929 — PreAct: Computer-Using Agents that Get Faster on Repeated Tasks

**域**：vision-GUI (computer-use agents: AndroidWorld mobile, OSWorld desktop, WebArena web)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P3 condition-keyed memory + write-gates/poisoning defenses (primary — this is the write-gate donor); P5 tool/skill credit + induction; P1 supply pricing (replay vs re-derive as a priced decision); stop/budget calibration

**方法面**：A verified compile–extend–replace harness around any CUA; the harness code is fixed and only the program corpus grows. (§3.1) The stored artifact is a state-machine program P=(S,T,M,V): states carry a VERIFICATION PREDICATE (accessibility-tree pattern with a timeout, e.g. expect_element xpath=class=android.widget.EditText&&hint=First name, timeout_ms=2000), transitions carry actions, concrete values typed during the trace are lifted to named parameters, metadata carries a DEDUP SIGNATURE identifying the task family, plus data-extraction predicates for QA tasks. Listing 1 is a verbatim compiled program. (§3.2, Algorithm 1) Loop: Selector queries the corpus → Replayer walks the graph, CHECKING EACH STATE PREDICATE AGAINST THE LIVE SCREEN BEFORE FIRING THE TRANSITION (observe-first-then-act; zero LLM calls on a successful warm path); warm path is trusted only if r.success ∧ r.cov > τ (τ=0.5 default) → on any predicate failure or action error, hand control to the full CUA which continues FROM THE LIVE SCREEN → compile the new trace into P′ → gate. (§3.3) VERIFY-BEFORE-STORE GATE: reset the environment to the task's clean initial state, replay P′ there, and consult the benchmark's own evaluator; store only if BOTH r′.success (replay reached terminal state without error) AND score′ ≥ 1.0. Storage is UPSERT keyed on the dedup signature — replace, not append, so the corpus refines rather than accumulates variants. Both halves of the gate earn their place: evaluator-pass alone admits programs whose actions silently no-op while the environment still holds passing state from an earlier step; replay-success alone admits the important failure, cov=100%/score=0. Fully training-free: no gradients anywhere; the only LLM roles are the compiler, the selector, and the fallback agent.

**自述与显见局限**：§5.7 enumerates six. L1: the state-machine-vs-flat-script architectural AB is suggestive not significant (10.6±1.14 vs 11.67±0.58, p=0.125). L2: benchmark subsets are tiny (6-task OSWorld test_tiny, 15-task AndroidWorld official-15, 12-task WebArena shopping_admin), so absolute pp magnitudes are denominator-dependent (43/17/15pp for 1.75–2.6 absolute tasks). L3: verify-replay costs median +217% wall overhead on OSWorld, +162% on Android per compile cycle. L4: the LLM compiler is lossy on a non-trivial fraction — audit finds ~28% of Android programs miss navigate_back and 100% of audited WebArena programs use inspect_screenshot on dynamic state; the gate filters this at storage but its cost scales with compile ATTEMPTS, not stored programs. L5: selector is 73% exact-id verbatim, 75.6% functional under paraphrase, 24% no-pick, 0% wrong-task-family (conservative by design). L6 (the one that matters most to us): the gate verifies by RE-EXECUTING, so it presupposes a resettable or idempotent environment and an available task evaluator; outside a benchmark it would need a side-effect-free check (read-only end-state check, sandboxed dry-run, or transactional rollback). Additionally §4.3 documents an over-strict-gate failure: on WebArena the gate-on agent still regresses (−4.0) because answer-extraction tasks read values that have since changed, so the gate correctly rejects nearly everything and EMPTIES the corpus — patched by an explore-afresh fallback rather than by a graded admission threshold. Corpus is small (58 Android programs); behaviour at 10^3–10^4 uncharacterised (§5.8).

**改进空间（方向级）**：(a) No side-effect-free verification path — L6 is the open problem and it is exactly our problem. (b) Credit is all-or-nothing at whole-program granularity; there is no partial credit or per-state/per-transition attribution, so a program that is 90% right is discarded entirely. (c) The empty-corpus regime is handled by a fallback rather than by a graded/probabilistic admission threshold — a calibrated write-gate with an explicit admission risk level is untried. (d) No condition keying beyond the dedup signature (task family) and application context. (e) No retirement/demotion of programs that degrade over time — UPSERT replaces on collision but nothing evicts on observed decay.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：REQUIRED — what breaks on a frozen black-box speech/omni core: (1) The gate's second half is the BENCHMARK'S OWN DETERMINISTIC EVALUATOR — a deployment-time oracle we do not have. Strip it and the double gate collapses to 'the replay ran', which is precisely the half the paper proves insufficient (the five reproducible cov=100%/score=0 programs, Table 3). So the gate's demonstrated 1.75–2.6-task value is an ORACLE-BACKED number, not a label-free one. (2) The gate verifies by LITERAL RE-EXECUTION from a scripted reset. Speech has no resettable environment: an utterance cannot be re-uttered under identical acoustic conditions, the acoustic scene has already moved on by the time we would verify, and there is no 'clean initial state' to reset to. This is L6 in its most severe form. (3) State predicates are near-deterministic STRUCTURAL matchers over an accessibility tree (xpath, resource_id, hint text) — a free true/false parse. Waveform observations admit no structural parse yielding a free predicate; the closest analogue (string-matching a partial ASR transcript) is itself a noisy estimate whose error rate is keyed to acoustic condition, so 'predicate false ⇒ fall back' becomes a decision under uncertainty rather than a check. (4) The entire economic case is skipping the model on REPEATED IDENTICAL tasks; speech turns are rarely bit-identical repeats, so the reuse unit cannot be an executable action trace and must instead be a condition-keyed policy fragment — a different artifact with different admission criteria. (5) No exact-match equivalence is available for free-form outputs, which is what the evaluator supplies here.

**可借鉴**：(a) THE WRITE-GATE DESIGN AS A DOUBLE PREDICATE with an explicit argument for why each half is necessary (§3.3), and the failure taxonomy it targets: cov=100%/score=0, 'runs but doesn't work' — five logged reproducible instances (Table 3). This is the cleanest memory-write-gate specification in my read set. (b) THE 2×2 GATE ABLATION DESIGN: cold/warm × gate-on/gate-off, reporting the cold→warm DELTA in each cell and the diff-of-deltas as 'the gate's gain' (Table 2: ΔON +1.2/+0.2/−4.0 vs ΔOFF −1.4/−2.4/−5.75; gain 2.6/2.6/1.75). (c) THE STATISTICAL HONESTY DISCIPLINE (§4.3, §5.3): per-platform sign tests reported (p≈0.031 Android and OSWorld, p=0.125 WebArena) and the pooled p≈1.2e−4 explicitly read as a DIRECTIONAL LOWER BOUND because within-platform repetitions are correlated rather than independent, with the claim rested on the per-platform tests and the consistent direction (13 of 14 paired runs, 14th a tie). Copy this pattern. (d) THE STRUCTURAL-MECHANISM ARGUMENT (§5.3): the gate's value lands in the same 1.75–2.6 band across three platforms, three LLMs and three evaluator semantics, and they enumerate and rule out the model-specific / task-specific / evaluator-quirk alternatives, concluding the magnitude reflects the LOSSY-COMPILE RATE of the trace→program step. That is a template for arguing a mechanism is structural rather than incidental. (e) REPORTING WHAT DID NOT MATTER (§4.6, §5.8): prompt wording, runtime guardrails, and LLM-vs-embedding selector all made little difference (retrieval 100% vs 75.6%) — a null-result discipline that guards against crediting the wrong component. (f) UPSERT-by-dedup-signature (refine in place) vs append-only as a memory-corpus contract. (g) The replay-coverage threshold τ as a separate trust knob from the gate.

**备注**：Training-free throughout — no gradients. VISION-GUI domain. The 8.5–13× warm-replay speedup and the 1.75–2.6-task gate gain are GUI numbers and carry no cross-modality effectiveness implication; the transferable object is the gate contract and the ablation/statistics design.

### 2606.18448 — VISUALSKILL: Multimodal Skills for Computer-Use Agents

**域**：vision-GUI (computer-use agents; CUA-World and OSExpert-Eval, Claude Code CLI agent on Claude Opus 4.6)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P3 condition-keyed memory (artifact structure, retrieval key, on-demand loading); P5 tool/skill credit + induction (primary); P1 supply pricing / value-of-information (index-then-load-on-demand is a priced acquisition policy)

**方法面**：(§2.1) A skill is one artifact PER TARGET APPLICATION (not per task), organised as a two-layer hierarchy: a central skill.md index listing every topic with a one-sentence 'when to use' description, over per-topic guides g_t = (p_t, F_t) pairing a text body with a set of UI figures. (§2.2) At inference the system prompt contains ONLY the index; the agent calls a single MCP tool load_topic(t) that returns the topic's text with each figure delivered inline immediately after the sentence that names it, callable repeatedly as the task moves between UI surfaces. (§2.3) Two-stage construction. Stage 1 mines the vendor's authored manual: parse its table of contents and REUSE IT AS THE TOPIC SET T, pull every figure on the covering pages as F_t. Stage 2 drives the LIVE application in two sub-passes — (a) free explorer: an Opus-class planner partitions the idle window into K=8 regions and spawns a Sonnet-class worker per region in an isolated Docker container to capture cropped screenshots of every interactive element and emit a structured note; (b) trajectory-targeted explorer: a reviewer reads held-out training-task trajectories (instruction, action sequence, verifier feedback, skill consultations) and nominates UI REGIONS the Stage-1 skill under-explained — scoped to regions rather than tasks 'so the patch transfers to any test task that touches the same UI surface'. An assembler consolidates, an LLM mapper assigns each region to a topic, and an inline step appends. THE LOAD-BEARING METHODOLOGICAL DEVICE: a matched text-only control S_txt shares the same index, the same topic set, and the same procedural content, differing ONLY in modality, guaranteed by construction because every per-topic generation step is a SINGLE LLM call that reads the topic source and emits p_mm and p_txt side by side — 'any gap in agent performance between S_mm and S_txt is attributable to modality alone, not to differences in the underlying content.' Training-free at inference; no gradients anywhere.

**自述与显见局限**：Construction depends on preconditions that are strong: an authored vendor manual with a usable table of contents (Stage 1), and a drivable live application in isolated resettable containers plus held-out training-task trajectories WITH VERIFIER FEEDBACK (Stage 2). One agent stack only (Claude Code CLI + Claude Opus 4.6) — no cross-backbone check in what I read. Per-domain task counts are small (GIMP n=6, Tableau n=20, OSExpert LibreOffice n=24). Benchmarks' own verifiers used unchanged, so the scores inherit those verifiers' properties (CUA-World is a checklist VLM verifier with partial credit; OSExpert-Eval is deterministic state-based 0/1). Named negative (§4.1 'When figures don't help'): the multimodal gap collapses on tasks consisting of short, explicitly specified action sequences. There is no write-gate anywhere in the pipeline — captured notes and figures enter the skill unconditionally.

**改进空间（方向级）**：(a) NO ADMISSION GATE on skill content: nothing decides whether a captured note or figure is correct before it enters the artifact — the direct contrast with 2606.17929's verify-before-store gate, and an obvious composition. (b) No retirement/demotion of stale or harmful entries. (c) No per-entry credit attribution: the +8.3pp modality slice is measured at artifact granularity, not per topic or per figure, so we cannot tell which figures earned it. (d) The 'when to use' one-liner is the ENTIRE retrieval key and its quality is never measured or ablated. (e) No condition keying beyond application identity. (f) Stage 2(b) uses failed rollouts to nominate under-explained regions but does not close the loop — no re-measurement of whether the patch fixed those rollouts specifically.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：REQUIRED — what breaks on a frozen black-box speech/omni core: (1) THE MATCHED-CONTROL TRICK DOES NOT PORT AS-IS. It works because a UI figure has a faithful verbal paraphrase emitted in the same LLM call, so content is shared by construction. A retained audio exemplar has no matched text control preserving the load-bearing information (prosody, accent, overlap, channel), so an 'audio-retained vs verbalised-away' ablation cannot be made content-matched by construction the way theirs is — the modality-attribution claim would be confounded by information loss in the control arm. (2) The paper's stated second mechanism (§4.1(ii)) is that a reference screenshot can be DIRECTLY MATCHED against the live screen for post-action state verification. A retained audio clip cannot be matched against a live utterance without an embedding whose similarity threshold is itself acoustic-condition-keyed — turning a free comparison into an estimated one. (3) Stage 1 requires vendor-authored documentation; there is no vendor manual for an acoustic domain, and Stage 1 alone supplied +10.5pp of the total lift, i.e. the largest single contribution has no speech analogue. (4) Stage 2 requires driving a live application in isolated resettable Docker containers — no resettable acoustic scene exists. (5) The MCP-tool-vs-Read result is about the TOKEN/CALL ECONOMICS of image delivery; delivering audio exemplars into context costs far more per item, may be unsupported by a black-box API, and the '~10× more figures per task' effect has no reason to reproduce. (6) No deterministic oracle is needed by the artifact itself, but Stage 2(b)'s region nomination consumes VERIFIER FEEDBACK on training rollouts — which is an oracle we lack.

**可借鉴**：(a) THE MATCHED-MODALITY CONTROL CONSTRUCTION (§2.1) as an ablation DESIGN principle: generate both arms from the same source in a SINGLE call so content is shared by construction, then attribute the gap to the manipulated factor alone. Even where we cannot instantiate it for audio, the principle 'make the control content-identical by construction, not by post-hoc matching' is directly adoptable, and its failure mode for audio is itself a finding worth stating. (b) THE THREE-LEVEL DECOMPOSITION OF THE HEADLINE (Table 1): no-skill 0.303 → matched text-only Stage 2 0.373 → VISUALSKILL Stage 2 0.456, so the +15.3pp total is decomposed into a knowledge slice (+7.0) and a modality slice (+8.3). Always decompose 'my artifact helps' into 'having content at all' vs 'the specific representation'. (c) THE DELIVERY-MECHANISM ABLATION (Table 2) with behavioural instrumentation, not just accuracy: load rate (92.6% vs 100%), figures consumed per task (0.8 vs 7.9, ~10×), and MEDIAN LAST-CONSULT STEP (1.5 vs 10.4) — i.e. measure whether the agent actually keeps using the memory across the trajectory, not just whether it opened it. That triple is directly reusable for our observation-supply and memory-read instrumentation. (d) THE PHASE-CONTRIBUTION TABLE (§4.3, Table 3) showing Stage 2(a) and 2(b) each add ~2–3pp and are SUPER-ADDITIVE when combined (0.436→0.462 / 0.436→0.453 individually, 0.504 together) with an explicit account of WHY they are complementary — static idle-window surfaces vs dynamic surfaces reachable only during real interaction. (e) Scoping induced knowledge to a REGION (a reusable surface) rather than to a task, so the patch transfers to any task touching that surface — a condition-keying design choice. (f) Reporting the regime where the effect vanishes (§4.1).

**备注**：Training-free at inference, no gradients. VISION-GUI domain; the +15.3pp / +8.3pp numbers are GUI numbers with no cross-modality implication. Verdict PROTOCOL_DONOR is for the ablation designs (matched control, delivery-mechanism instrumentation, phase decomposition), which are the transferable objects; the artifact format itself is largely modality-bound.

### 2606.21724 — Denoising Iterative Self-Correction: Structured Verification Loops for Reliable LLM Reasoning (DISC)

**域**：text (LLM reasoning: BIG-Bench Mistake, HotpotQA, GPQA Diamond; Claude Sonnet 4.5, gpt-5.2, GPT-4o, gpt-4.1-nano)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P9 decision-calibration/admissibility gates (primary — the judgment gate IS an admissibility gate on whether a signal may trigger a repair); repair and stop decision rights; P10 reward hacking (the I:D statistic is the guard against a repair loop that trades D for I)

**方法面**：(§3.2, Algorithm 1) A test-time verify–judge–correct loop over frozen models with four ROLES that need not share a backbone: generator M_g, verifier M_v, judge M_j, corrector M_c. Per iteration t: (1) M_v emits 3–10 targeted verification questions Q_t probing specific claims/steps in y_t; (2) M_v answers each question INDEPENDENTLY of y_t, producing evidence E_t; (3) M_j(x, y_t, Q_t, E_t) → (d_t ∈ {MISTAKE, NO_MISTAKE}, rationale r_t), where the judge is instructed to require a SPECIFIC CONTRADICTION between E_t and y_t before flagging, prioritising precision; (4) if NO_MISTAKE, return y_t immediately (early stop — the common case, 79% on GPQA cross-model at K=3); else M_c rewrites conditioned on r_t, treating the rationale as ADVISORY, verifying the flagged issue affects the final answer before rewriting, and leaving unflagged content unchanged. K=3 default. Prompts are rule-based, no few-shot or CoT scaffolding; built-in reasoning modes disabled so DISC itself is the mechanism under test. Fully training-free, no gradients. (§4.3) The load-bearing statistic is a PAIRED PRECISION/RECALL DECOMPOSITION of correction quality against the initial answer: I (improvements, wrong→right), D (degradations, right→wrong), Net = I−D, I:D = I/D as correction PRECISION (below 1 means net-negative correction), and repair rate = I/N_wrong_init as correction RECALL (equal to Yang et al.'s Critique Score under single-sample evaluation). Reported as PAIRED quantities throughout, because a method can buy high I:D by attempting few corrections or high repair by attempting many. 95% bootstrap CIs (1000 resamples) on accuracy/F1/repair; count-based I, D, I:D, Net as point estimates.

**自述与显见局限**：Stated in §Limitations: the judgment gate is PROMPT-SENSITIVE and functions as a tunable operating point with no principled control — they name two untried extensions, explicit prompt sweeps targeting a desired precision/recall and thresholding on the judge's P(MISTAKE) token probability. All three benchmarks have OBJECTIVELY VERIFIABLE answers; applicability to open-ended tasks (summarisation, creative writing) is unaddressed and the gate 'assumes that correct and incorrect are well-defined; this assumption weakens for tasks with subjective or multi-faceted evaluation criteria'. Operating-regime sensitivity: at low baselines the precision-priority gate can block more would-be-improvements than would-be-degradations — demonstrated, not hypothetical (§5.3: the gate gains +134 EM on HotpotQA Sonnet but LOSES 88 EM on GPT-4o). Strong-generator + different-strong-verifier configurations are untested. Compute is higher than baselines (Table 3: 9.45–15.87 calls/example vs CoVe 6.88–10.09, Self-Refine 1.03–2.77).

**改进空间（方向级）**：(a) No calibrated operating point — the paper names P(MISTAKE) thresholding as future work and does not build it, so the gate is a binary prompt artifact with no risk level. (b) NO ADMISSIBILITY TEST ON THE EVIDENCE ITSELF: the judge is the only filter, and E_t is never scored for reliability — yet §5.2 Finding 3 shows the verifier produced contradicting evidence in 89 of 90 cases while the judge acted on none of them, so evidence quality and evidence USE are separable and only the latter is instrumented. (c) No value-of-information pricing: the verifier emits 3–10 questions with no per-question cost/benefit. (d) NO ABSTAIN: the gate chooses repair-or-keep and never 'refuse to answer', so an item the system cannot fix is returned wrong rather than declined. (e) The capability floor is characterised qualitatively (Blindness vs Inability to act) but not turned into a runtime detector that would tell a deployed system its judge is inert.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：REQUIRED — what breaks on a frozen black-box speech/omni core: (1) I, D, I:D and repair rate ALL require per-example correctness to be decidable before and after the edit — exact match for BBM/GPQA, EM/F1 for HotpotQA. Speech has no exact-match equivalence for free-form outputs, so I and D become estimates from a proxy (WER delta, judge-score delta) and the I:D RATIO INHERITS THAT ESTIMATOR'S BIAS MULTIPLICATIVELY.
