Specifically, a proxy correlated with verbosity or fluency will systematically undercount D, and I:D is exactly the statistic that then looks best — this is the composition of their metric with our P10 hazard. (2) The gate's decision criterion is 'a specific contradiction between E_t and y_t', which presumes evidence and answer live in the SAME SYMBOLIC SPACE. A speech repair gate must adjudicate evidence that is partly acoustic and partly transcribed; 'contradiction' between a waveform observation and a text hypothesis is not a well-formed predicate without an intermediate transcription step that is itself the contested object. (3) The economics rest on a 79% early-exit rate keeping call counts tolerable; speech per-call cost is dominated by audio re-encoding, so the K sweep (K=3 captures 97% of max net gain at 70% of average iteration count, 1.86 vs 2.63) would re-price entirely. (4) The capability-floor result is about a TEXT judge failing to act on TEXTUAL contradiction; the speech analogue — a judge that must hear a contradiction between audio and a transcript — is untested here and is precisely the cross-modal case H5 withholds. (5) Their cross-model role allocation assumes a menu of independently selectable models for each role; a single frozen omni core may have to fill several roles, reintroducing exactly the self-confirmation bias the paper identifies as the primary obstacle.

**可借鉴**：(a) THE PAIRED I:D + REPAIR-RATE REPORTING (§4.3) — correction precision and correction recall as inseparable quantities, with the explicit argument that net accuracy alone obscures the tradeoff and that the two are independent in principle. This is directly adoptable as our repair-decision-right metric. (b) THE FORCED-MISTAKE GATE ABLATION (§5.3, Table 5): hold verifier, corrector and ALL PROMPTS fixed, make the judge fire MISTAKE unconditionally, and measure the delta — isolating the gate from everything else. Result: I:D drops 2–4× in every cell, degradation roughly doubles (Sonnet) to triples (GPT-4o), while the verifier-corrector pass still recovers substantially — establishing that PRECISION AND RECALL ARE ARCHITECTURALLY SEPARABLE. (c) THE PROMPT-SWAP CONFOUND CONTROL (§5.3): plug DISC's prompts into the baseline's gateless architecture to rule out 'it was just better prompts' — 67.2% vs native CoVe 69.7% vs DISC 79.3%. (d) THE FIVE-CONFIGURATION ROLE-STRENGTH GRID (Table 4) crossing weak/strong across generator, verifier, judge and corrector: all-weak +0 (gate inert, 100% early exit), strong-judge-only +3, strong-verifier-only +0, all-strong +8, strong verify+judge with weak gen/corr +34 — establishing SUPER-ADDITIVE verifier-judge synergy (3 + 0 vs 34) and that judge quality beats iteration count (cross-model K=1 at +22 beats same-model strongest at K=3 with +8). (e) THE RATIONALE-CLASSIFICATION FAILURE TAXONOMY (§5.2 Finding 3): of 89 cases where the verifier surfaced a contradiction and the judge said NO_MISTAKE, 48 are BLINDNESS (judge does not acknowledge the contradiction) and 41 are INABILITY TO ACT (judge states the contradiction in language and still concludes NO_MISTAKE). A gate-diagnosis instrument we should replicate. (f) The self-confirmation-bias quantification: same model surfaces 51 errors in another model's outputs vs 18 in its own; judge precision/recall 71.3/85.6 on another's outputs vs 62.0/63.3 on its own. (g) Refusing to use the benchmark's backtracking baseline because it feeds ground-truth mistake locations, i.e. it is an oracle not a self-correction method (§4.2).

**备注**：Training-free, no gradients — the closest architectural match in my read set to our control plane (external roles over frozen cores, decision rights = repair/stop). TEXT domain; the 149:1 I:D and 81.6% accuracy figures are text-reasoning numbers with no cross-modality implication.

### 2606.24515 — Reinforcement Learning for Computer-Use Agents with Autonomous Evaluation

**域**：vision-GUI (computer-use agents; macOSWorld, Windows Agent Arena, OSWorld; UI-TARS policy, Qwen2-VL-7B evaluator)　**供体级别**：CONSTRUCT_DONOR　**喂给**：P9 decision-calibration + label-free headroom estimation (judge-as-noisy-channel formalism, separability admissibility test, calibration-split design); P8 reward-as-controller (construct only — mechanism is gradient-based)

**方法面**：(§3) CUA as an MDP with terminal-only reward. A Qwen2-VL-7B VLM evaluator observes ONLY the pair (final GUI screenshot x_{i,T}, instruction d_i) and emits a binary judgment r̃_i ∈ {0,1}, zero-shot, FULLY DECOUPLED from the acting agent — it sees neither the action history nor the agent's internal reasoning. (§3.3) Noise model over that channel: e+ := Pr(r̃=0 | r*=1) false-negative rate, e− := Pr(r̃=1 | r*=0) false-positive rate, separability constant D := 1 − e+ − e− assumed > 0 ('ensuring the evaluator provides informative feedback beyond random guessing'). Since E[r̃ | r*] = e− + D·r* (Eq 6), the corrected estimator r̂ := (r̃ − e−)/D satisfies E[r̂ | τ] = r* exactly (Eqs 7–8); the deployed plug-in form is r̂♯ := (r̃ − ê−)/D̂ (Eq 9), asymptotically unbiased as calibration data grows and D̂ stays bounded away from zero. Then GRADIENT USE: terminal discounted return R̂_i = γ^T r̂_i is propagated backward through the trajectory and consumed by PPO (Eqs 10–11). (§3.1) The design that is the actual donor: a THREE-WAY DISJOINT SPLIT — for each of 42 applications × 3 OSes, 60 tasks split into three disjoint 20-task blocks; two blocks (2/3) for RL rollouts, one block (1/3) RESERVED FOR EVALUATOR CALIBRATION where ground-truth success labels are used solely to estimate (e+, e−) PER OS, explicitly 'preventing information leakage'. Per-OS normalised confusion matrices reported (Table 2).

**自述与显见局限**：Stated in §5 and load-bearing for us: the formulation assumes (e+, e−) are approximately FIXED for a given model and evaluation protocol and reliably estimable from a held-out split, but 'evaluator behavior may be non-stationary: error rates can vary with task type, UI complexity, language phrasing, visual themes, and operating-system-specific interface conventions'. Worse, 'as the agent improves during training, the distribution of visited GUI states may shift, potentially changing the evaluator's error profile and introducing residual bias when using plug-in estimates' — i.e. the correction is self-undermining under exactly the improvement it is meant to drive. They name conditional per-task-family/per-application error-rate estimation as the natural extension and do not do it. The signal is binary, sparse and effectively terminal: 'the proposed correction improves reward accuracy but does not increase reward density', so exploration remains hard on long-horizon tasks. Ground-truth labels are required on the calibration split. Mechanism is PPO — gradient-based.

**改进空间（方向级）**：(a) Conditional / keyed error rates (their own named gap). (b) No variance or risk control on r̂: D̂ near zero inflates variance without bound and the paper only requires it be 'bounded away from zero' — no CI, no minimum-D admissibility test. (c) The correction is a POPULATION-LEVEL debias; there is no per-item admissibility decision, so an individual corrected judgment is no more trustworthy than the raw bit. (d) No abstain: the evaluator must emit 0 or 1. (e) One evaluator, one channel — no multi-signal agreement, no cross-checking against a second protocol.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：REQUIRED — what breaks on a frozen black-box speech/omni core: (1) Estimating (e+, e−) REQUIRES GROUND-TRUTH SUCCESS LABELS on a calibration split. For speech that means human-labelled turn outcomes, and — per the paper's own non-stationarity warning — labelled SEPARATELY PER ACOUSTIC CONDITION rather than per OS, since SNR / accent / overlap / channel are exactly the covariates that would move e+ and e−. Their per-OS keying is the structural analogue of the acoustic-condition keying we need, and it is the part they flag as unvalidated. (2) The evaluator's whole observable is a FINAL SCREENSHOT — a persistent terminal state that encodes the outcome and can be inspected after the fact. A speech turn leaves NO persistent state to inspect; the evidence is the transient signal itself, so the 'final observation that carries the success evidence' does not exist. (3) MECHANISM IS NON-BORROWABLE: r̂ is consumed by a PPO gradient step. To use it training-free we would feed r̂ into selection or stopping instead — and here is the non-obvious limit: r̂ = (r̃ − e−)/D is a MONOTONE AFFINE RESCALING of a binary variable, so it changes NOTHING about the ranking of candidates within a group that share the same r̃. It is useful for comparing success rates ACROSS conditions with different (e+, e−) and for label-free-ish population estimates, but it cannot arbitrate a per-candidate select/repair decision. Anyone borrowing this into a selection rule without noticing that is importing a no-op. (4) No deterministic oracle exists at speech deployment time, and D > 0 must be re-verified per acoustic condition, not once globally — an evaluator that is informative at high SNR can fall to D ≈ 0 under overlap, at which point r̂ diverges.

**可借鉴**：(a) THE NOISE-CORRECTED REWARD ESTIMATOR as a formal construct: with e+ := Pr(r̃=0|r*=1), e− := Pr(r̃=1|r*=0), D := 1−e+−e−, then E[r̃|r*] = e− + D·r* and r̂ = (r̃−e−)/D is unbiased (Eqs 3–8). Cheap, exact, and the right lens for reasoning about a fallible judge as a channel rather than as an oracle. (b) THE SEPARABILITY CONDITION D > 0 as an explicit ADMISSIBILITY TEST on a reward channel: a judge whose false-positive and false-negative rates sum to ≥ 1 carries no information and must not be allowed to steer. That is a directly reusable gate for P9. (c) THE THREE-WAY DISJOINT SPLIT DESIGN (§3.1): reserve a dedicated calibration block whose ground-truth labels are used ONLY to characterise the judge and never exposed to the controller — a clean answer to the information-boundary problem of accidentally feeding test-item gold into the control path. (d) Reporting the judge as a PER-REGIME NORMALISED CONFUSION MATRIX (Table 2) rather than as a single accuracy number, which makes the false-positive/false-negative ASYMMETRY visible — and the paper's argument for why the asymmetry matters directionally (false positives reinforce bad behaviour; false negatives merely thin the signal). (e) The raw-vs-corrected comparison as an ablation arm (Table 3), including the finding that raw evaluator rewards can improve in-domain while REDUCING generalisation.

**备注**：GRADIENT USE FLAGGED: PPO fine-tuning of the policy. This is a donor for its MEASUREMENT and DESIGN (the estimator, the separability condition, the disjoint calibration split, the per-regime confusion matrices), never for its mechanism. VISION-GUI domain; the 0.084→0.203 / 0.331→0.442 / 0.283→0.432 success-rate figures are GUI numbers and carry no cross-modality implication. Verdict is CONSTRUCT_DONOR rather than PROTOCOL_DONOR because the estimator's affine form is inert for within-condition candidate ranking — see transfer_boundary (3).

### 2606.24525 — VisCritic: Visual State Comparison as Process Reward for GUI Agents

**域**：vision-GUI (GUI agents; Mind2Web, AndroidWorld, OSWorld, WebArena, AITW; InternViT-6B + Qwen2.5-7B critic)　**供体级别**：CONSTRUCT_DONOR　**喂给**：P8 reward-as-controller (per-step scalar consumed by accept/retry/backtrack decision rights — construct only, mechanism is trained); P9 admissibility (threshold γ as a gate); P3 memory (weak-label construction discipline)

**方法面**：(§3.1) A visual process reward model defined as C : (s_t, a_t, s_{t+1}, l) → (ŷ_suc ∈ [0,1], ŷ_prog ∈ [−1,1], ŷ_err ∈ {success, no-op, wrong-target, page-error, timeout}) — i.e. it scores an action by DIFFING the pre-action and post-action screenshots rather than by textual reasoning about the state. (§3.2) Visual Difference Encoder: shared-weight Siamese ViT gives F_t, F_{t+1}; patch-level ΔF = F_{t+1} − F_t; change-magnitude map M_i = ||ΔF_i||_2; change-region attention α_i = softmax(M_i/β) with learnable β; attended difference v_Δ = Σ α_i ΔF_i (Eqs 1–5). Explicitly NOT pixel differencing, 'which is sensitive to rendering noise, animation artifacts, and minor layout shifts'. (§3.3) Action-Aware Critic Head: cross-attention with ΔF as queries and [h_a; h_l] (encoded action and instruction) as keys/values, plus spatial self-attention, change-magnitude-weighted pooling, three MLP heads. (§3.4) Integration: an OPTIONAL pre-execution Best-of-N over predicted post-action states (requires an external world model), and the PRIMARY mode — post-execution verification against the REAL s_{t+1}, requiring no state prediction; when ŷ_suc < γ (=0.5) the agent enters a FIXED retry/recovery hook held constant across all compared settings, so task-SR gains are attributed to the verification SIGNAL under a fixed integration protocol rather than to recovery-policy design. (§3.5) GRADIENT USE: yes — LoRA r16 on InternViT-6B plus critic head, two phases (InfoNCE contrastive pre-training with an auxiliary KL localisation loss on the action-region mask, then multi-task BCE/MSE/CE fine-tuning), ~202K samples, 8×A100.

**自述与显见局限**：Trained critic, so outside a training-free contract. Labels are WEAK PROXIES: every step of a successful trajectory is labelled y_suc = 1 (optimistic), and the divergence point in failed trajectories is estimated heuristically by comparing against the closest successful trajectory. Error-type labels are derived deterministically from which perturbation generated the sample and agree with manual annotation only 81.4% (500 cases). ~202K training samples required. One interactive case (Qwen2.5-VL on OSWorld) where a text critic marginally wins. The recovery policy is explicitly outside quantitative scope. AITW is demoted by the authors to 'an offline logged-screenshot consistency diagnostic' that 'should not be interpreted as live inference-time gains for arbitrary candidate actions'. Effect sizes are 1.7–6.8 pts absolute over 3 runs with sd from 0.3 pts. Overhead +6.2B params, 66ms/step. Fixed threshold γ=0.5 with no calibration.

**改进空间（方向级）**：(a) The critic is a point estimate at a single fixed threshold — no calibration, no confidence interval, no abstain, no per-condition threshold. (b) No credit assignment back to whatever proposed the action (memory entry, skill, tool) — the signal stops at the step. (c) The pre-execution Best-of-N path depends on a learned world model whose fidelity is never audited, which is why the authors themselves recommend post-execution as default. (d) NO REWARD-HACKING ANALYSIS: nothing checks whether an agent optimising ŷ_suc learns to produce large-but-meaningless visual changes, even though the score is literally 'how much did the screen change in the direction the action implies' — a Goodhart surface the paper leaves untested. (e) Weak positive labels mean the ceiling on critic quality is set by trajectory-level success, never audited at step level.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：REQUIRED — what breaks on a frozen black-box speech/omni core: (1) THE ENTIRE SIGNAL IS A DIFF BETWEEN TWO COMPARABLE OBSERVATIONS OF A PERSISTENT STATE. A GUI screen is a stable, re-observable state that an action perturbs; the diff is therefore attributable to the action. A speech turn has NO persistent state to diff — the nearest analogue (embedding the audio before and after) compares two different acoustic events, not one state before and after an intervention, so ΔF would be dominated by content change rather than by action effect. This is the single hardest break in my read set. (2) The no-op detector keys on near-identity SSIM > 0.98 between screenshots. Audio has no near-identity relation meaning 'nothing happened'; ambient variation alone destroys it, so the highest-value category in their ablation (no-op detection 84.2% vs text critic 62.7%) has no speech analogue. (3) The negative-sample generators all rely on RECOMBINING observations across trajectories (swap s_{t+1} from a different trajectory) while keeping the semantics interpretable. Recombining audio segments across sessions changes speaker and channel, so the resulting negative is an ACOUSTIC-CONDITION CONFOUND rather than a clean 'unexpected outcome' — the weak-label pipeline would manufacture a shortcut the critic learns instead of the intended signal. (4) Trained with white-box access to its own ViT features; under our contract an external critic is permitted but the transferable object is the DESIGN, not the mechanism. (5) γ = 0.5 fixed with no calibration would not survive an acoustic-condition shift, and nothing in the paper measures threshold stability across regimes.

**可借鉴**：(a) THE PAIRED-OBSERVATION CONTRAST FORMALISM: score an action by comparing the observation before and after in a LEARNED SEMANTIC space rather than a raw-signal space, with an explicit control showing why raw-signal differencing fails (the Pixel-Critic baseline: 63.8% vs 85.6% F1 on semantic changes, because pixel diffs are large but uninformative under navigation/loading). (b) THE WEAK-NEGATIVE GENERATION TAXONOMY (§3.5) — four named, orthogonal perturbation families that manufacture supervision without human labels: ACTION MISMATCH (swap the action, hold the observation pair), STATE MISMATCH (swap the post-observation, hold the action), NO-OP (pair an observation with a near-identical successor), and POST-DIVERGENCE SAMPLING from failed trajectories. Even where the audio instantiation is confounded (see transfer_boundary), the taxonomy of WHICH FACTOR TO SWAP is a reusable design for building a label-free critic-evaluation set. (c) THE DOUBLE MATCHED-CONTROL ABLATION (§4.4, Table 3): a Text-Critic with the SAME backbone and IDENTICAL critic head differing only in modality (66.2% vs 84.6% F1), AND a Pixel-Critic with the same head but raw-signal diffs (63.8% vs 85.6%) — two controls that jointly isolate 'the paired comparison' from 'the model scale' from 'the learned representation'. (d) THE FIXED-INTEGRATION-HOOK DISCIPLINE (§3.4): hold the downstream recovery policy CONSTANT across all compared settings so that task-level gains are attributable to the verification signal and not to recovery design — and say so explicitly. (e) Demoting an offline benchmark to a named 'consistency diagnostic' with an explicit warning against reading it as a live gain. (f) Reporting the cheap-backbone operating point (SigLIP-400M, 35ms, 80.3% F1 vs 6.2B/66ms/85.2%) so the accuracy-per-cost curve is visible. (g) Category-stratified critic quality (visual-only changes vs semantic changes vs no-op) rather than a single F1.

**备注**：GRADIENT USE FLAGGED: LoRA fine-tuning of a 6B vision encoder plus a critic head over ~202K weakly supervised samples. Donor for MEASUREMENT and DESIGN only. VISION-GUI domain; the +2.9 to +5.4 pt interactive-average lifts and 85.2% verification F1 are GUI numbers with no cross-modality implication.

### 2606.25760 — Uncertainty Quantification for Computer-Use Agents: A Benchmark across Vision-Language Models and GUI Grounding Datasets (ARGUS)

**域**：vision-GUI / VLM (single-step executable GUI click grounding; 4 open-weight agents + 3 closed-source frontier vendors)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P9 decision-calibration/admissibility gates + label-free headroom estimation (primary — the strongest donor in this read set for the black-box observability constraint); stop/abstain/budget calibration (cross-cutting, via conformal regions and rejection); P1 supply pricing (by omission — it names the gap)

**方法面**：(§3) A cross-REGIME benchmark for POST-HOC uncertainty quantification, where a regime is defined as the triple (dataset, model class, OBSERVABLE MODEL INTERFACE). Open-weight panel: 27 methods across seven families — logit, sampling/consistency, hybrid, attention, density/probe, verbalised, VLM-native — × 4 agents (Qwen2.5-VL-7B, Qwen2.5-VL-72B-AWQ, UI-TARS-1.5-7B, POINTS-GUI-G-8B) × 4 datasets (ScreenSpot-v2, ScreenSpot-Pro, OSWorld-G, UI-Vision-EG) = 16 cells where logits, hidden states, attention traces, stochastic samples and perturbation responses are all available. Closed-source extension: the API-COMPATIBLE 8-method intersection (CCP, SelfCons, SE, LexSim, Verbalised-1S, Verbalised-2S, HEDGE, IMGHEDGE) × 3 vendors (GPT-5.4, Claude Sonnet 4.6, Gemini 3.1 Pro) × 4 datasets = 12 cells where logits, hidden states and attention are inaccessible. Method inclusion is a stated triple criterion (reported in ≥2 peer-reviewed evaluations or directly motivated by GUI-grounding UQ; admits faithful adaptation to coordinate-valued outputs; computable in ≤30 min per cell), with exclusions documented. Every method is normalised to an ACTION-LEVEL RISK SCORE (larger = higher predicted error probability). All learned probes, density estimators, calibration maps, conformal thresholds and panel-selection decisions use ONLY the calibration split; headline metrics on held-out test; 80/20 default with rankings stable across cal/test ratios {10/90…50/50} (mean cross-ratio ρ = 0.98 open-weight, 0.92 closed-source). Metric card: AUROC_incorrect, PRR^norm_0.5, AURC, ECE^iso, Brier^iso, miss-only AUSE on ε_i = log(1 + d_norm,i) with target-scale-normalised error d_norm,i = ||c_i − c*_i||_2 / sqrt(w_i·h_i), ranking-transfer Spearman ρ, and split-conformal click-disk coverage/radius with r̂_α = Quantile_{1−α}({R_i : i ∈ D_calib}) in three variants (Disk-Fixed, Disk-Normalized weighted by a plug-in UQ score, Disk-CQR). All metrics with 95% CIs from 500-resample stratified bootstrap; significance from paired bootstrap differences; per-cell top-1 vs top-2 paired Wilcoxon with BH-FDR q < 0.05. No new estimator is proposed — this is purely a measurement instrument.

**自述与显见局限**：Stated in §8: isolates SINGLE-STEP executable clicks, not multi-step trajectories; n_samples = 5, which is explicitly below the budget needed for reliable spatial-dispersion scores (SafeGround-style scores are therefore relegated out of the headline matrix); proposes no new UQ estimator. The calibration split uses ground-truth labels. The closed-source panel is 8 methods over 12 cells — small, and the paper says so when reporting a CI that includes zero. Their own conformal results show coverage failures under mismatch (Gemini×ScreenSpot-Pro Disk-Fixed coverage 0.926 / 0.884 / 0.795 against targets 0.95 / 0.90 / 0.80), which they read as calibration-test mismatch across API strata.

**改进空间（方向级）**：(a) No trajectory-level / multi-step UQ — the single-step restriction is the stated frontier. (b) NO COST MODEL: every method is treated as free, so there is no value-of-information layer pricing sampling-based scores (n draws) against a single verbalised call — a direct P1 gap this benchmark would be well-placed to close and does not. (c) Nothing CONDITIONS the calibration on an environment key, even though their own undercoverage finding is diagnosed as calibration-test mismatch across strata — stratified/conditional conformal is the obvious next step and is absent. (d) NO CLOSED LOOP: UQ is measured, never fed to a controller that acts on it; rejection, reranking and deferral are named as intended interventions but none is executed and scored end-to-end. (e) No abstention policy or budget allocation built on top of the scores.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：REQUIRED — what breaks on a frozen black-box speech/omni core: (1) The correctness oracle is a TARGET BOX with a known centre and known width/height. AUROC_incorrect, d_norm and conformal disk coverage are all defined against that deterministic ground-truth coordinate. Speech has no coordinate-valued output and no per-item deterministic oracle, so 'the region contains the target' has no analogue; a conformal set over speech outputs must be built over a SEMANTIC-EQUIVALENCE relation that is itself estimated, and split-conformal's marginal validity does not survive an estimated inclusion predicate without further assumptions. (2) The geometry is 2D spatial and severity is normalised by TARGET-BOX SCALE (d_norm); speech has no established equivalent of target scale — plausible analogues (segment duration, SNR) are environment covariates, not target properties, which changes what 'severity' means. (3) The panel assumes repeated sampling is cheap (n = 5); each speech resample costs audio re-encoding, so the value-of-information ranking of sampling-based versus verbalised scores would reorder before any accuracy comparison is made. (4) The calibration split requires ground-truth labels, and — per their own Gemini undercoverage result — must be stratified by the regime that actually shifts. For us that stratification variable is ACOUSTIC CONDITION, which has no counterpart in their design. (5) Their headline transfer results are WITHIN-VISION. The finding that open-weight→API-only ranking transfer is statistically indistinguishable from zero must NOT be read as a claim about speech; what it licenses is a METHODOLOGICAL REQUIREMENT on us — any confidence signal we adopt must be re-ranked and coverage-checked on the target regime rather than inherited from a proxy. (6) Two of the seven families (density/probe, attention) and most of a third (logit) are structurally unavailable to us by contract, which is exactly what their Panel B isolates.

**可借鉴**：(a) THE REGIME CONSTRUCT ITSELF — regime = (dataset, model class, OBSERVABLE INTERFACE) — with transfer measured as Spearman ρ between per-cell method RANKINGS rather than between score values, on the explicit ground that cells differ in accuracy, score scale and error geometry (§4). This is the right shape for our acoustic-condition-keyed generalisation question. (b) THE OBSERVABILITY-TIER SPLIT AND ITS RESULT STRUCTURE: an open-weight matrix versus an API-ONLY matrix restricted to the method intersection that survives loss of logits/hidden-states/attention. Their Panel B (8 methods × 12 vendor×dataset cells) is a ready-made menu of the ONLY UQ families a black-box contract permits — sampling/consistency, hybrid, verbalised, and model-native — and the finding that API-only observability PROMOTES response-level and verbalised signals (Verbalised-1S/2S top-1 on 6 of 12 closed cells; CCP on the other 6) is a structural constraint we should inherit as a search space, not as an effectiveness claim. (c) THE CROSS-TIER TRANSFER STATISTIC reported with its CI and read honestly: mean ρ = +0.08 over 12 pairs, range −0.76 to +0.88, bootstrap 95% CI [−0.219, +0.373] includes zero, therefore 'we cannot detect meaningful cross-tier transfer' and 'recommendations should be reranked on the target calibration split rather than extrapolated'. The phrasing discipline — a null result stated as non-detection at the available sample size, not as absence — is directly copyable. (d) THE THREE-OBJECTIVE SEPARATION: discrimination (AUROC), severity ranking (miss-only AUSE), and calibration (ECE^iso) are DIFFERENT objectives that select different methods — same top method on only 2 of 16 open-weight cells — plus the warning that PRR is accuracy-dependent and approaches a structural ceiling on high-accuracy cells, so it is interpretable only within comparable-accuracy regimes. (e) THE SET-VALUED DEPLOYMENT CHECK: 'score-level UQ is insufficient for deployment. A score can rank errors well by AUROC yet produce regions that are too large, under-covered, or depend on the output interface.' Disk-Normalized shrinks radii 40–60% when the plug-in score is calibrated, but coverage degrades under calibration-test or interface mismatch. Always pair a ranking metric with a coverage metric. (f) THE SCALE-ONLY CONFOUND CONTROL (§6): decomposing a vanilla→specialist transition into scale-only (Q7→Q72), fixed-backbone fine-tune (Q7→UI), and tune-plus-backbone (Q7→PT), showing that scale-only produces LARGER sampling (−0.277) and hybrid (−0.257) losses than fine-tuning (−0.039, −0.010) — i.e. losses attributed to the intervention were partly scale. (g) THE REGIME-AWARE SELECTION RECIPE (Table 5) framed explicitly as a PRIOR that must be revalidated on the target calibration split. (h) The explicit method-inclusion and method-exclusion criteria as a corpus-construction discipline. (i) Cal/test-ratio robustness as a protocol sanity check.

**备注**：No gradients on any core; purely post-hoc measurement over frozen models — the closest match in this read set to our observability contract, and the only paper here that explicitly instruments what survives when logits, hidden states and attention are removed. VISION-GUI domain: every number (ρ = 0.705, ρ = +0.08, AUROC 0.966, 40–60% radius reduction) is a GUI-grounding number and carries no cross-modality effectiveness implication for speech. Secondary BASELINE_ANCHOR role: their API-only 8-method panel is the family of confidence signals our controller's own signal would have to be compared against, once re-instantiated and re-ranked in a speech regime.

**组级综合（agent 提交）**：SCOPE. Nine papers, read in fulltext. Domain mix: 2 text/LLM (2606.12935 MARS, 2606.21724 DISC), 7 vision (2606.13156 grounding self-correction audit; 2606.15608 MLLM-judge robustness; 2606.17929 PreAct; 2606.18448 VISUALSKILL; 2606.24515 autonomous-evaluation RL; 2606.24525 VisCritic; 2606.25760 ARGUS). All absence claims below are scoped to THIS READ SET. No effectiveness conclusion is carried across modalities; every quoted number is a text or vision number.

WHAT THE DONORS SETTLE.

(1) P9 is the best-served gap in this subset, and it is settled by three papers that together form a complete measurement stack. 2606.13156 supplies the OFFLINE honesty layer: the honest-trajectory protocol (report the triple m_single-shot / max_π m_π / m_oracle; foreground the oracle gap Δ and the honest gain g; pre-register the label-free policy set and always include the do-nothing baseline). Its demonstration that the SAME method, SAME trajectories and SAME trained checkpoints flip from '+2.4pp win' to 'negative honest gain' purely by changing the evaluation is the single most transferable result here — and note it also inflated the paper's NEGATIVE headline (the quoted −31pp collapse is itself an oracle number; the deployable figure is −63pp). 2606.25760 supplies the DEPLOYMENT layer: regime = (dataset, model class, observable interface); measure transfer as Spearman ρ between method RANKINGS, not score values; pair every ranking metric with a coverage metric because 'score-level UQ is insufficient for deployment'; and treat discrimination, severity and calibration as three objectives that select different methods (same top method on only 2 of 16 open-weight cells). 2606.24515 supplies the CHANNEL layer: model a fallible judge as a noisy binary channel with (e+, e−) and separability D = 1 − e+ − e− > 0, and reserve a disjoint calibration block whose gold labels characterise the judge and never reach the controller. Composed, these give us: an offline protocol that refuses to credit unattainable headroom, a deployment protocol that refuses to inherit a confidence signal across regimes, and an admissibility test that refuses to let an uninformative judge steer.

(2) P3 write-gates are settled — sharply — by 2606.17929, and the adjacent papers show what a missing gate costs. PreAct's contract is a DOUBLE predicate (the re-run must complete AND an independent evaluator must pass), and the paper earns each half by exhibiting the failure that only the other catches: the cov=100%/score=0 program that replays every action perfectly and leaves the task undone (five reproducible logged instances). The gate is worth 1.75–2.6 tasks across three platforms, three models and three evaluator semantics, and the paper argues from that convergence that the magnitude is STRUCTURAL — it tracks the lossy-compile rate of the trace→program step, not any platform property. The instructive contrast is 2606.18448, which builds a rich induced-skill artifact with NO admission gate at all, and the instructive counter-example is PreAct's own WebArena regime, where an over-strict gate correctly rejects nearly everything and EMPTIES the corpus. Together they bracket the design space: no gate ⇒ decay; binary all-or-nothing gate ⇒ starvation. Neither paper builds the graded/calibrated admission threshold in between — that is open in this read set.

(3) Repair/stop decision rights are settled by 2606.21724 with the cleanest statistic in the subset: the PAIRED I:D (correction precision) and repair rate (correction recall), reported together because net accuracy hides the tradeoff. Its forced-MISTAKE ablation (hold verifier, corrector and every prompt fixed; make the judge fire unconditionally) shows precision and recall are ARCHITECTURALLY SEPARABLE — I:D drops 2–4× in every cell while the verifier-corrector pass still recovers substantially. Its 5-configuration role-strength grid establishes super-additive verifier-judge synergy (strong judge alone +3, strong verifier alone +0, both +34) and that judge quality beats iteration count. And its capability-floor decomposition (of 89 cases where the verifier surfaced a contradiction and the judge said NO_MISTAKE, 48 are Blindness and 41 are Inability to act) is a gate-diagnosis instrument, not just an anecdote.

(4) Stop/budget calibration is settled by 2606.12935 at the level of FORM, not instantiation: certify a commitment by requiring lead > worst-case future erosion rather than by detecting stability, and prove why the stability heuristic fails exactly where it matters (consensus stopping halves accuracy on hard items). Two sub-devices are the real prizes: the synthetic UNSEEN challenger ⊥ with zero current votes, which reserves probability mass for answers that have not yet appeared — directly relevant to open-ended speech outputs where the candidate set is not closed; and the decomposition 'learn WHICH candidates will change from their own observable history, treat WHERE they land ADVERSARIALLY and calibrate one contraction from warmup', which is a general recipe for a signal we cannot model.

(5) P8 is served only as CONSTRUCT in this subset. 2606.24525 gives the paired-observation-contrast formalism (score an action by the semantic difference between the observation before and after, with a raw-signal control proving why naive differencing fails) and a four-family weak-negative taxonomy (swap the action / swap the post-observation / near-identity no-op / post-divergence sampling). 2606.24515 gives the noise-corrected reward. Both are trained mechanisms and are donors for their designs only.

WHAT TRANSFERS.
Six things transfer essentially intact because they are protocol, not mechanism: (a) the honest-trajectory triple and the oracle-gap/honest-gain pair; (b) the paired-precision/recall correction statistic with a forced-gate ablation; (c) the write-gate double predicate with a named failure taxonomy and a 2×2 on/off ablation; (d) the regime construct with rank-transfer measurement and the observability-tier split; (e) the judge-as-channel formalism with the D > 0 separability admissibility test and a dedicated gold-labelled calibration block; (f) the margin-vs-adversarial-erosion stopping form with an unseen-challenger reserve.
Four reporting disciplines transfer and should be adopted wholesale: PreAct's directional-lower-bound framing (rest the claim on per-platform tests and consistent direction, treat the pooled p as a lower bound because within-platform reps are correlated); ARGUS's non-detection phrasing (a CI including zero means 'cannot detect at this sample size', not 'no effect'); PreAct's and 2606.13156's explicit reporting of what did NOT matter (prompt wording, guardrails, selector choice; expression length, box geometry); and ARGUS's and 2606.24525's confound controls (scale-only vs fine-tune decomposition; matched-backbone text control plus raw-signal control).

WHAT BREAKS — and the breaks are correlated, which is the finding.
Every donor in this subset rests on at least one of four affordances that a frozen black-box speech/omni core does not provide, and most rest on several:
(i) A DETERMINISTIC PER-ITEM ORACLE. 2606.13156's IoU-vs-GT-box, PreAct's benchmark evaluator, DISC's exact match, MARS's answer identity, ARGUS's target box, VisCritic's trajectory success, 2606.24515's gold calibration labels. Strip it and the borrowed statistic does not merely get noisier — it changes meaning. The oracle gap Δ becomes a difference of two estimates; PreAct's double gate collapses to the half it proves insufficient; DISC's I:D inherits the proxy's bias MULTIPLICATIVELY, and a proxy correlated with fluency will undercount degradations, which is precisely the P10 hazard composing with the P9 metric.
(ii) A PERSISTENT, RE-OBSERVABLE, RESETTABLE STATE. PreAct verifies by literally re-executing from a scripted reset (its own L6); VisCritic diffs a screen before and after; 2606.24515's evaluator reads a FINAL SCREENSHOT that still encodes the outcome; VISUALSKILL matches a reference screenshot against the live screen. A speech turn leaves no persistent state, cannot be re-uttered under identical conditions, and the acoustic scene has already moved on. This is the deepest structural break in the set, and it is not repairable by substituting a noisier estimator — the object being estimated does not exist.
(iii) INTERNAL OBSERVABILITY. 2606.13156's P(yes) from logits, MARS's probe confidence and mid-generation partial-trace access, 2606.15608's token-probability judge family, ARGUS's density/probe/attention/logit families, VisCritic's own ViT features. ARGUS is the one paper that instruments this loss directly, and its Panel B is therefore doubly valuable: it both demonstrates the constraint and enumerates the surviving method families.
(iv) CONDITION KEYING, which is absent from all nine. MARS calibrates γ per QUESTION; 2606.24515 estimates (e+, e−) per OS and explicitly warns that error rates 'may be non-stationary' and vary with task type and interface convention while offering conditional estimation as untried future work; ARGUS diagnoses conformal undercoverage as calibration-test mismatch across API strata and does not stratify; VISUALSKILL keys on application identity only. This is the clearest shared gap: several papers arrive at the doorstep of condition-keyed calibration and none walks through it, which is where an acoustic-condition-keyed control plane has room that this read set does not occupy.

TWO NON-OBVIOUS CAUTIONS worth flagging to the program.
First, 2606.24515's corrected reward r̂ = (r̃ − e−)/D is a MONOTONE AFFINE RESCALING of a binary variable. It is genuinely useful for comparing success across conditions with different error rates and for population estimates, but it changes NOTHING about candidate ranking within a group sharing the same raw judgment. Importing it into a selection rule without noticing that imports a no-op. Its real donation is the separability test D > 0 as an admissibility gate.
Second, 2606.13156's argmax-P(yes) policy picks the oracle step 84.6% of the time and STILL loses to not iterating, because the oracle advantage is tiny and the 15% of wrong picks cost more than the right picks gain. That decomposition — selection accuracy × payoff asymmetry — is the correct general form of an admissibility argument, and it warns that a high-accuracy selector is not sufficient justification for exercising a decision right. Any headroom estimate we build must carry both terms.

CROSS-CUTTING GAP THIS SUBSET LEAVES OPEN. Nobody here prices anything. ARGUS treats all 27 UQ methods as free; DISC's verifier emits 3–10 questions with no per-question cost/benefit; VISUALSKILL's on-demand loading is the closest thing to an acquisition policy and is never priced; MARS is the only paper that budgets at all, and only in tokens against a fixed vote. P1 (supply pricing / value-of-information) is therefore fed by this read set mainly by ABSENCE. Likewise P10 is touched only by 2606.15608 (adversarial, external, gradient-based) and by DISC's I:D-as-guard; nothing in this subset studies Goodhart under the system's OWN selection pressure — notably 2606.24525 trains a critic on 'how much the screen changed in the direction the action implies' and never checks whether optimising it induces large-but-meaningless changes. P6 (system composition) is served only implicitly, by the observation that the strongest results in this set — PreAct's gate, DISC's judgment gate, VISUALSKILL's staged construction — are all ARCHITECTURAL rather than capability-driven, a point DISC states outright: 'the gains trace to architectural choices in the verification pipeline rather than to the raw capability of any single component.'


## 供体组 8

### 2303.11366 — Reflexion: Language Agents with Verbal Reinforcement Learning

**域**：text (LLM agents: text-game decision-making, QA, code)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P8 (reward-as-controller, gradient-free multi-step: the canonical scalar->verbal->context loop), P9 (admissibility: FP/FN contingency reporting and the FP-is-terminal rule for stop gates), P3 (external memory, as the pre-write-gate baseline our design must beat), stop/abstain/budget (trial cap + Evaluator-pass stop rule as the null controller)

**方法面**：Three frozen LLM roles — Actor (ReAct/CoT generator), Evaluator M_e (scores trajectory), Self-Reflection M_sr (turns a sparse scalar/binary reward into a natural-language 'lesson') — plus a bounded episodic memory mem of the last Omega=1-3 reflections appended to the Actor prompt (§3, Alg. 1). No weights touched; explicitly framed as 'policy = LLM params + memory encoding' (§1 contributions). Evaluator is instantiated three ways depending on task: exact-match grading (HotPotQA), hand-written heuristics (AlfWorld: same action+response repeated >3 cycles, or >30 actions => reflect), and self-generated unit-test suites (code: CoT-generate tests, AST-filter for syntactic validity, sample n<=6). Loop runs until Evaluator passes or max trials. Zero gradient use.

**自述与显见局限**：Authors' §5: it is policy optimization in natural language and can sit in non-optimal local minima; memory is a fixed-capacity sliding window (no vector/DB structure); test-driven self-evaluation breaks on non-deterministic, impure, hardware-dependent, or concurrent functions. Their own §4.3 analysis quantifies the oracle-quality problem: self-generated tests give false positives at 16.3% on MBPP-Python vs 1.4% on HumanEval-Python, and this alone flips Reflexion from +11 to -3 vs the GPT-4 baseline (Tables 1-2). Ablation Table 3 (HumanEval-Rust, 50 hardest): base 0.60, self-reflection omitted 0.60, test-generation omitted 0.52 (i.e. worse than doing nothing), full 0.68 — so the verbal channel is inert without a grounded pass/fail gate, and the gate alone is inert without the verbal channel. Evaluated on 2023-era GPT-3/GPT-4; no calibration, no abstain, no budget accounting beyond 'max trials'.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level steps remaining on top: (a) the memory is append-and-truncate with no write-gate, no utility score, no condition key — nothing decides whether a reflection deserves to persist; (b) the stop rule is 'Evaluator says pass OR trial budget exhausted', with no calibrated admissibility test on the Evaluator itself, so a false-positive evaluator terminates silently and a false-negative one burns budget; (c) no per-item budget allocation — every unsolved item gets the same trial allowance regardless of estimated headroom; (d) the Evaluator's error rates (their TP/FN/FP/TN table) are measured post hoc but never fed back into the controller.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：Every Evaluator instantiation here needs something our deployment lacks: exact-match grading assumes a discrete answer identity relation (speech outputs are free-form text over waveform observations, where 'same answer' is itself an estimate); self-generated unit tests assume an executable deterministic oracle (no analogue for an ASR/audio-QA/speech-instruction response); the AlfWorld heuristic assumes a symbolic action/observation log with exact repeat detection, whereas repeated speech turns differ acoustically even when semantically identical. The verbal-reflection channel itself assumes the failure is legible in the text trace; in a frozen speech/omni core the causal error is often in the audio front-end (mis-segmentation, speaker confusion, noise) and never surfaces in text, so 'reflect on your trajectory' has nothing to reflect on. Their FP asymmetry result also assumes a fixed, task-independent oracle; for us oracle quality is itself acoustic-condition-keyed, so an FP rate measured on clean audio does not bound the FP rate on noisy audio. Finally the 22%/20%/11% gains are 2023 GPT-3/GPT-4 text numbers and carry no cross-modality effectiveness claim.

**可借鉴**：(1) The FP/FN/TN/TP contingency table over (self-generated verifier verdict x true correctness) as the standard reporting object for any self-constructed oracle (Table 2) — plus their asymmetry argument that FN is recoverable and FP is terminal, which is directly a design rule for our stop/abstain gate. (2) The three-arm compromise ablation (Table 3: base / signal-only / verbal-only / both) as the minimal factorial for separating 'grounded gate' from 'verbal repair instruction' — we can run exactly this factorial with an acoustic-condition gate vs a textual critique. (3) The AlfWorld hand-written reflection trigger (action-repetition >3, step-count >30) as a cheap, deployment-available loop-detector that needs no oracle. (4) The episodic-memory ablation design (§4.2: baseline / +most-recent-trajectory / +reflection) which isolates 'memory' from 'reflection' and reports an 8-point absolute separation. (5) Memory cap Omega=1-3 as a documented context-budget constraint.

**备注**：2023 paper — per the program's recency doctrine this is a lineage/context anchor rather than a state-of-the-art comparator, but its ablation grammar is the cleanest in this read set. MemRL (2601.03192) explicitly ablates against 'single-task reflection, conceptually equivalent to Reflexion' and reports +9.0 (OS) / +5.1 (ALFWorld) for cross-task retrieval but a tie on low-similarity HLE (0.610 single-task vs 0.606 cross-task) — useful calibration on when this baseline is actually weak.

### 2502.00271 — Scaling Flaws of Verifier-Guided Search in Mathematical Reasoning

**域**：text (LLM math reasoning; step-level beam search with OVM/PRM verifiers)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P10 (reward hacking / selection-pressure degradation: the canonical 'advantage shrinks then reverses with N' measurement), P9 (admissibility gates — the paper names the unbuilt detector; oracle-selection headroom instrument), P1 (budget-matched comparison as the pricing frame), P8 (select decision right under an imperfect signal)

**方法面**：A measurement study, not a method paper. Step-level beam search over K candidates with b parallel paths, scored by a trained Outcome-supervised Value Model or Process Reward Model, compared against repeated sampling at matched 'sample size' (b for search, attempts for sampling), metric = coverage (fraction of problems where at least one sampled path is correct), 3 repeats with std reported (§4.1). Settings: GSM8K, MATH500, plus two constructed OOD splits (train MATH L1/2/3/5 -> eval L4; train L1-L4 -> eval L5). The load-bearing instrument is a failure-attribution protocol (§5.2): every unsolved problem is labelled generation-failure (no valid partial path ever produced) vs selection-failure (valid paths existed but were not selected), where 'valid partial path' is established by rolling out each candidate 4x (GSM8K) / 16x (MATH, OOD) and checking whether any rollout reaches the correct answer. Second instrument: an oracle-selection baseline at the first selection stage, upper-bounding selection performance independent of verifier quality. Third: valid-path sparsity (fraction of valid paths among candidates) bucketed into four bins with failed-selection-stage counts per bin (§5.3). Verifiers are trained (gradient use in building OVM/PRM); the analysis protocol itself is training-free.

**自述与显见局限**：Stated §7: no study of verifier size or verifier training-set size — larger verifiers might reduce the effect. Focus is coverage, not precision (coverage upper-bounds precision and equals it only under oracle final selection, e.g. theorem proving / code). Only step-level beam search is studied (MCTS, lookahead excluded as orthogonal). Mitigations are explicitly 'preliminary': stochastic softmax-temperature selection and one-time Monte Carlo rollout are evaluated only as selection-stage accuracy gain, not end-to-end. Everything depends on a deterministic answer-checking oracle both for labelling valid paths and for coverage.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level: (a) they identify but do not build the detector — §7 future work is explicitly 'detecting verifier failures and adapting verifier usage accordingly, uncertainty measures could be useful', i.e. an admissibility gate, unoccupied here; (b) temperature is a fixed global hyperparameter and its optimum inverts between in-distribution (low tau better on GSM8K) and OOD (high tau better), so per-item or per-condition adaptation of reliance-on-verifier is left open; (c) sparsity is diagnostic only — never estimated online, never used to allocate budget; (d) no cost accounting for the Monte Carlo rollout mitigation despite it being the strongest one.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：The whole attribution protocol rests on a deterministic correctness oracle applied to rolled-out completions — 'valid path' means 'some rollout reaches the correct answer'. A frozen black-box speech/omni core yields free-form outputs over waveform observations with no exact-match equivalence, so partial candidates cannot be labelled valid/invalid and the generation-vs-selection split is not computable as written; re-deriving it against a noisy proxy judge reintroduces the very error the protocol isolates. Coverage/Pass@K likewise presume the oracle. Step-level partial paths presume an intervenable decoding process with step boundaries — a black-box core gives whole responses, no logprobs, no mid-generation pruning, so 'beam search' degenerates to whole-candidate reranking. Their OOD axis is problem difficulty level; ours is acoustic condition (noise, accent, speaker, channel), which is not a property of query text and has no analogous curriculum label. Their verifiers are trained on in-domain process labels (gradient use); no equivalent process supervision exists for speech.

**可借鉴**：(1) The matched-sample-size comparison of guided selection vs plain repeated sampling as the mandatory control — guided wins at n=1 across MATH levels (+2.5 to +23.0) and loses at n=32 (-3.1 to -43.8), with OOD columns worse still (-25.8 to -46.1) — Table 1, OVM and PRM rows, so any 'our controller helps' claim must be a curve over budget, not a point.【复核更正 wf_40068490】 (2) The generation-vs-selection failure attribution with rollout-established validity labels: selection accounts for 77.1-88.6% of OVM-guided failures across all four settings (Table 2). (3) Oracle-selection upper bound at a fixed stage as a headroom instrument. (4) Valid-path-sparsity stratification of failures (monotone: sparser => higher failure share, Fig. 4) as a difficulty-conditioning axis. (5) Softmax-over-scores stochastic selection with temperature sweep {0.1, 1, 10} as a cheap hedging baseline (gains up to +11.2 on OOD; tau-optimum flips with distribution shift). (6) Explicit OOD construction by held-out difficulty level.

### 2504.21776 — WebThinker: Empowering Large Reasoning Models with Deep Research Capability

**域**：text (LLM web-research agent; QwQ-32B / DeepSeek-R1 backbones)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P6 (system composition: orchestrator/executor split, digest-returning acquisition tool, tool set as decision rights), P5 (tool/skill credit: lexicographic correctness>>calls>>length; per-sub-capability ablation), P1 (supply pricing — weakly occupied: tool cost is a tie-break, not a price), P9 (multi-axis judged rubric for free-form outputs)

**方法面**：A two-mode agentic system over a large reasoning model. Problem-Solving Mode gives the LRM one composite tool, a Deep Web Explorer that is itself LRM-driven and recursively issues search queries and link/button navigation until it returns a condensed answer O_exp to the main chain (§3.3, Eq. 2-3) — hierarchical delegation of information acquisition, so the main context sees digests not raw pages. Report Generation Mode adds three tools {draft, check, edit} executed by a separate assistant LLM against a document memory M of every explored page, retrieving top-k documents per writing instruction (§3.4, Eq. 4); the main LRM only orchestrates and decides when to explore vs write. Training: iterative online DPO (2 iterations, gradient use — off-limits as mechanism) on self-sampled trajectories with a strictly prioritized preference rule: 1) correctness/report-quality dominates; 2) tie-break on fewer total tool calls; 3) tie-break on shorter output when length ratio exceeds gamma (§3.5). Base (training-free) and RL variants are both reported.

**自述与显见局限**：Stated §5: cannot process multimodal information (images, video) at all; supports only a limited fixed tool set, so tool scalability/generalization is open; no GUI-based interaction. Beyond that: answers are judged by Qwen2.5-72B-Instruct and reports scored 1-10 by DeepSeek-R1-671B and GPT-4o on four axes — the evaluation oracle is itself an LLM with no reported agreement study or FP-rate analysis. The RL benefit is task-dependent and small on the open-ended arm (report avg 8.1 RL vs 8.0 Base; Comprehensiveness drops 8.4 -> 8.3), so the trained mechanism buys little where the judge is soft. Information-scope evidence is a qualitative t-SNE picture. Cold-start SFT needed for R1-7B/14B stability means the framework is not backbone-agnostic without gradients.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level: (a) tool invocation is unpriced — the preference rule penalizes tool calls only as a tie-break after correctness, so no value-of-information estimate decides whether a search is worth its cost before issuing it; (b) document memory M is accumulate-everything with top-k similarity retrieval, no write-gate, no utility score, no poisoning defense despite ingesting arbitrary web pages; (c) no abstain and no calibrated stop — the loop ends when the LRM emits EOS; (d) check-and-edit is invoked at the model's discretion with no admissibility test on whether the checker is trustworthy for that report; (e) their own limitation 2 (tool scalability) is exactly the tool/skill-induction slot.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：The acquisition tool is a web search engine returning text documents; a speech/omni deployment's analogous 'supply' is re-listening, re-segmentation, or a different acoustic view of the same waveform — there is no external corpus that answers 'what was actually said', so the value-of-information calculus differs in kind (bounded endogenous supply vs unbounded exogenous supply). Document memory is keyed by text similarity over crawled pages; our memory must be keyed by acoustic condition, not recoverable from query text. The DPO mechanism is gradient-based and needs trajectory logprobs — inadmissible against a black-box core with no guaranteed logprobs; only the preference criteria transfer, not the optimizer. The judged rubric transfers in form, but their judges score English research prose; a text judge cannot see transcription errors that changed meaning, so Factuality as scored here would silently absorb ASR error. Their own §5 states the system cannot handle non-text modalities, so nothing here is evidence about audio. GAIA 48.5 / WebWalkerQA 46.5 / HLE 15.8 / report avg 8.1 are text-web numbers, not cross-modality effectiveness claims.

**可借鉴**：(1) The orchestrator/executor split: main model holds decision rights (when to acquire, when to write, when to revise) while a separate cheaper assistant model performs text manipulation against retrieved evidence — directly reusable for a control plane over a frozen core. (2) The hierarchical acquisition tool that returns a digest rather than raw evidence, keeping controller context bounded — an observation-supply pattern. (3) The lexicographic preference ordering (correctness >> tool-call count >> length) as a budget-aware credit rule needing no reward model. (4) The leave-one-component-out ablation grid run on BOTH a closed-form arm and an open-ended judged arm (Table 3), including ablation of one sub-capability inside a tool: removing the explorer costs 42.1 -> 38.3 avg while removing only link-clicking costs 42.1 -> 42.6 (non-monotone — a useful cautionary datum); on reports, removing autonomous drafting is the largest drop (8.0 -> 6.6) and removing check-and-edit hits coherence hardest (7.9 -> 6.9). (5) The four-axis judged rubric (Comprehensiveness / Thoroughness / Factuality / Coherence) with two independent judge models as a template for scoring free-form outputs where exact match does not exist. (6) Training-free Base vs RL reported side by side — the honest way to show how much of a system's effect is architectural.

### 2506.11930 — Feedback Friction: LLMs Struggle to Fully Incorporate External Feedback

**域**：text (LLM iterative self-improvement; Llama-3.3 / Llama-4-Scout / Llama-4-Maverick, Claude 3.7 +/- extended thinking)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P9 (label-free headroom estimation and decision calibration: target-accuracy ceiling, semantic entropy as a repair-admissibility statistic, self-report disqualified), P8 (repair decision right — the negative result bounding how much a gradient-free multi-step controller can extract from a frozen core), stop/abstain/budget calibration (entropy-conditioned allocation is the named open step), P10 indirectly (Feedback-Quality vs Feedback-Resistance separates signal corruption from actuation failure)

**方法面**：A controlled ceiling experiment, not a system. Solver M_solver answers; if wrong under metric f against ground truth, a feedback generator M_feedback that HAS the ground-truth answer/solution and the full history of prior attempts produces guidance identifying the error without revealing the answer; the solver retries with the whole (answer, feedback) history in context, up to 10 iterations, iterating only on still-incorrect items so accuracy is monotone non-decreasing by construction (§2.1). Three feedback strengths: F1 binary ('the answer is wrong!'), F2 solver self-reflective with ground truth, F3 strong-model reflective (GPT-4.1 mini) (§2.2). Nine tasks: AIME 2024, MATH-500, TriviaQA, PopQA, MMLU, MMLU-Pro, GPQA, plus decimal and hexadecimal 5-digit multiplication. A 'target accuracy' line marks what the solver would reach if it incorporated all feedback. Two mitigations: progressive temperature schedule (0.0, 0.15, 0.3, ...) and temperature + rejection sampling (generate 25, delete any final answer already tried and therefore known-wrong, pick randomly among survivors, else random). Diagnosis instruments: an o4-mini automatic trajectory classifier into Feedback-Resistance / Feedback-Quality / Other validated at 96% agreement with two human annotators on 50 sampled errors per task (vs 78% for GPT-4.1 mini as annotator); semantic entropy over n=50 samples at T=0.7 clustered by final answer, bucketed at width 0.2, plotted against absolute improvement rate (Final-Initial)/(1-Initial); binomial standard errors throughout. Fully training-free; no gradients.

**自述与显见局限**：Stated §6: no mechanistic explanation of why resistance occurs; mitigations yield only modest gains and never reach target; SFT/RL fixes not run due to cost of 70B+ models. Structurally: the feedback generator holds the gold answer, so the setup is deliberately un-deployable — it measures a ceiling, not a controller. Only objective ground-truth tasks by design. The 'Other' bucket absorbs format/metric mismatches, so some measured resistance is evaluation artifact. Rejection sampling exploits knowing previously tried answers are wrong, which is only knowable because the loop had an oracle.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level: (a) the semantic-entropy correlate is diagnostic only — entropy is never used online to decide whether to repair, stop, or abstain, which is exactly a calibrated repair gate and is unoccupied here; (b) budget is a flat 10 iterations per item regardless of measured headroom, so entropy-conditioned allocation is open; (c) mitigations are global schedules, not per-item policies; (d) feedback modality is fixed free-text critique — no structured or evidence-attached feedback explored; (e) the error taxonomy is post-hoc annotation, not an online signal.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：The protocol's core move — give the feedback generator the ground-truth answer and solution — has no deployment analogue for speech, and even offline it needs per-item gold, which for free-form speech responses means human adjudication rather than a string. The correctness function f is exact-match/rule-based on nine objective text tasks; for speech 'still incorrect' is itself an estimate, breaking the monotone-non-decreasing guarantee (a proxy-judged loop can flip an item from right to wrong unnoticed). Semantic entropy as computed clusters by final answer string; for free-form speech responses the clustering relation must be replaced by a semantic-equivalence estimator whose own error inflates or deflates entropy — and critically, entropy over resampled text does not capture uncertainty originating in the audio front-end, so a model can be confidently wrong about what it heard with low output entropy. n=50 samples per item at T=0.7 is a heavy budget against an API-priced omni core. Rejection-sampling-against-previous-answers needs the oracle plus answer identity for de-duplication. All curves are text tasks on 2025 text models — no cross-modality effectiveness claim carries.

**可借鉴**：(1) The near-oracle feedback ceiling protocol: instrument the feedback channel with privileged information to separate 'our signal is bad' from 'the core cannot act on any signal' — a legitimate offline diagnostic for bounding how much of a speech repair failure is reward quality vs core plasticity. (2) The monotone-by-construction accuracy protocol (only re-attempt currently-wrong items) plus an explicit 'target accuracy' ceiling line on every curve. (3) The three-level feedback ladder F1/F2/F3 as a dose-response design on signal richness. (4) The LLM-annotator error taxonomy (Feedback-Resistance / Feedback-Quality / Other) with human-agreement validation and annotator selection (o4-mini 96% vs GPT-4.1-mini 78%) — a reusable recipe for validating any judge we deploy. (5) Semantic entropy as a label-free confidence statistic (n=50 samples, cluster by final answer; they report this matched bidirectional-entailment clustering) with entropy-bucketed improvement-rate plots: improvement rate rises from near-zero at low entropy to 0.4-0.8 at high entropy. (6) Rejection sampling against already-attempted answers as diversity forcing. (7) The self-report-vs-behavior probe: >95% of solvers say they understand the feedback and >96% say they will update, yet do not — disqualifying self-reported confidence/compliance as a control signal. (8) Binomial standard errors on every bucket.

**备注**：Strongest instrument-donor in this read set for P9: supplies both a ceiling construction and a label-free confidence statistic that correlates with actionability, and explicitly forbids using stated model confidence.

### 2506.18203 — Shrinking the Generation-Verification Gap with Weak Verifiers (Weaver)

**域**：text (LLM repeated sampling + verifier ensembling; math/science/knowledge QA)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P9 (label-free verifier calibration and admissibility — the central donor for this slot; also supplies the generation-verification gap as our headroom statistic), P1 (exaFLOP-per-query controller-overhead accounting), P8 (select decision right with multiple weak signals), P10 (weighted ensembling as a Goodhart mitigation vs single-proxy argmax; correlated-verifier collusion left open)

**方法面**：Given K=100 repeated samples per query from a frozen generator (Llama 3.3 70B Instruct) and m=33 weak verifiers (7B-72B reward models from RewardBench plus prompted LM judges)【复核更正 wf_40068490】, Weaver estimates each verifier's accuracy WITHOUT labels via weak supervision and combines them into a posterior Pr(y=1 | s_1..s_m) used to rank candidates (§4.2). Mechanics: binarize heterogeneous verifier outputs (continuous RM scores vs discrete judge verdicts) using dataset statistics; discard low-quality/adversarial verifiers; assume conditional independence of verifiers given latent correctness Y; recover accuracy parameters Pr(S_i=1|Y=1) by solving, via gradient descent on a matrix objective (Eq. 5), the pairwise-agreement system Pr(S_i,S_j) = Pr(S_i|Y=1)Pr(S_j|Y=1)Pr(Y=1) + ... plus the marginal consistency constraint — labels never needed, only observable verifier agreement. A tiny labelled dev set (1% of test, ~5-10 query-answer pairs) supplies only the global class prior Pr(Y=1). Optional refinement: cluster queries by empirical difficulty (observed correct/incorrect generation ratio) and fit a separate Weaver model per bucket (App. B.4). Separately a 400M ModernBERT cross-encoder is distilled on Weaver's pseudolabels (§6) — that step is gradient-based; the aggregation itself is training-free at deployment.

**自述与显见局限**：Stated §7: verifier effectiveness varies by domain and specialized verifiers are needed; on very hard datasets (AIMO 2024) Weaver struggles because correct responses are too rare; multimodal verification (images, audio, video) is explicitly listed as unaddressed and as introducing new challenges. Structurally: conditional independence across 33 verifiers is a strong, unverified assumption; the method still needs 5-10 gold-labelled items per task for the prior; the gap to Pass@100 remains 4.2% on average; K=100 generations per query is expensive; all evaluation uses exact-match-checkable benchmarks so the latent Y is well-defined.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level: (a) difficulty bucketing uses the empirical correct/incorrect ratio, which presupposes oracle-checkable outcomes — a condition-keyed partition with a label-free key is open; (b) verifier discarding is a fixed pre-filter, not an online admissibility gate that retires a verifier when its estimated accuracy drops on a subpopulation; (c) no abstain — Weaver always selects an argmax even when the estimated posterior is uniformly low; (d) no budget policy — K is fixed rather than allocated by estimated headroom; (e) correlated/adversarial verifiers are handled only by discarding, so the collusion failure mode under selection pressure is unmodelled.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：The latent Y is binary correctness from an exact-match/rule oracle; for free-form speech outputs there is no such Y, so the weak-supervision model has no well-defined latent — it would need redefining against a graded or adjudicated adequacy notion, which breaks the binary graphical model and the quadratic identity in Eq. 2. Majority voting and Pass@K, both used as anchors, require answer identity across candidates. Conditional independence is far more suspect for us: plausible speech verifiers (a re-transcription, a text judge over that transcription, an audio-quality heuristic) fail together on the same acoustic conditions, so errors are correlated exactly where it matters and the estimator would confidently mis-weight under noise. Their verifier pool is 33 open-weight text RMs/judges with published benchmarks; no comparable speech/omni verifier pool exists, and any we assemble may share the same frozen core, making them non-independent by construction. Difficulty bucketing by empirical correct-ratio needs the oracle again. The authors themselves list audio verification as an open problem, so nothing here is evidence about speech.

**可借鉴**：(1) The generation-verification gap = Pass@K - Success Rate as the headline diagnostic separating 'the core can produce a good answer' from 'our controller can find it'. (2) The full weak-supervision estimator for verifier accuracy without labels (Eq. 1-5) — pairwise agreement + marginal consistency solved as matrix least-squares — the most directly reusable label-free calibration machinery in this read set. (3) The 1%-dev-set trick: use a handful of gold items ONLY for the scalar class prior; they also report that fitting a supervised weighted ensemble on the same 0.01n labels drops accuracy 20.1%, which is the argument for the WS route. (4) The weighted-vs-naive-ensemble control (naive averaging underperforms weighted by up to 11.2 points; individual verifier success rates span a 37.5-point range) — never report an unweighted ensemble as the ensemble baseline. (5) Score binarization + verifier discarding as required preprocessing when mixing heterogeneous signals. (6) Difficulty-bucketed refitting as the template for our condition-keyed refitting. (7) Distillation cost accounting: 35.35 exaFLOPs for the full ensemble per query's 100 samples vs 1.01 for the 400M cross-encoder retaining 98.2% of gains — a template for reporting controller overhead as a fraction of generation cost (0.57% here). (8) The baseline ladder: First Sample, Majority Voting, best single RM, naive top-10 ensemble, Self-Verification, Multi-Agent Verification, oracle Pass@K.

### 2506.19248 — Inference-Time Reward Hacking in Large Language Models (Best-of-Poisson + HedgeTune)

**域**：text (theory + experiments on BoN/SBoN over LLM candidate pools; math/science QA and human preference)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P10 (reward hacking under selection pressure — the formal core of this slot: hacking-threshold definition, unimodality, hedging), P9 (admissibility: Cov(r_true,u)=0 stationarity as a calibration statistic; lambda=0 fallback as abstain), stop/abstain/budget calibration (choosing n / mu / lambda as the budget knob with a principled optimum rather than 'more is better')

**方法面**：Formal treatment of Goodhart under inference-time selection over a frozen reference policy pi_ref. Proxy rewards are quantile-transformed via their own empirical CDF so they are uniform on [0,1] (Assumption 1, rank-preserving; discrete relaxation in App. B.2). Definition 1 defines the hacking threshold theta-dagger: the parameter beyond which expected TRUE reward decreases while proxy reward and KL both keep increasing. Theorem 1 proves that for any family {pi_theta} strictly TP2 in (theta,x) with a score function increasing in x — covering Best-of-n, Best-of-Poisson, and exponential-family tilts (Corollary 1) — expected true reward is either monotone or has exactly ONE interior extremum; four regimes follow (monotone improvement, hacking, 'grokking', immediate decline). Best-of-Poisson draws a Poisson(mu)-governed pool, giving a single-parameter family provably near the optimal reward-KL frontier (Theorems 2, 5). HedgeTune (§4, Alg. 4) solves a one-dimensional root-finding problem for the optimal parameter — for BoN the integral condition Eq. 5; for SBoN the compact Cov_{u~f_lambda}(r_t(u), u) = 0; for BoP Eq. 7 — via bisection/Newton on the residual R(theta)=E[r_t(u)psi(u,theta)]. Explicitly: no access to the LLM distribution is needed, only proxy and true reward scores on a calibration set. No gradients on the policy.

**自述与显见局限**：Stated §4: 'the main limitation is that we require black-box access to the true reward to perform a one-time calibration' — they propose verifiable domains, LLM-as-judge, or a stronger/costlier RM as substitutes. Assumption 1 and the TP2/MLR regularity conditions are technical restrictions; the continuous approximation is defended as negligible for dense sample sets. The verifiable-setup experiments reuse the pre-existing PPE dataset and select exactly the benchmark-RM pairs already known to exhibit hacking, so prevalence is not estimated. The 'in the wild' arm trains proxy RMs (Pythia 44m) against a designated gold RM (AlpacaRM) with injected 25% label noise on a 1.4B Pythia reference policy — small-scale, and the 'true' reward is itself a model. Calibration is one-shot and global (one theta per dataset/RM pair), not per-item or per-condition.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level: (a) a single global theta with no per-item, per-condition, or online-adaptive threshold and no drift detection when the proxy-true relationship changes; (b) the true-reward oracle requirement is the binding constraint and no attempt is made to hedge without it (e.g. via an ensemble-disagreement surrogate); (c) hedging is expressed only through pool size / temperature — repair, abstain, and stopping are not in the parameter family; (d) single-step selection over i.i.d. candidates, not multi-step control where each step's selection changes the next step's candidate distribution; (e) no uncertainty quantification on theta-dagger itself.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：HedgeTune requires a calibration set with TRUE reward values; the paper's own fallbacks (verifiable domains, LLM-as-judge, stronger RM) are precisely what a frozen black-box speech/omni core lacks — for free-form spoken-language responses there is no verifiable oracle, and a text judge over a transcription is not a 'stronger' true reward but a differently-biased proxy that can share failure modes with the controller's own proxy, silently violating the true/proxy separation the theory assumes. The quantile transform needs a dense pool of comparable proxy scores per prompt; with API-priced omni generation, pools of n=32 per item may be unaffordable. Theorem 1's TP2/MLR conditions are stated for selection over i.i.d. candidates from a fixed reference policy — our controller also repairs, re-prompts, and re-observes, changing the candidate distribution between rounds and putting us outside the proven family, so unimodality must be re-established or treated as a hypothesis. Crucially the single global theta-dagger is calibrated per (dataset, reward model); under acoustic-condition shift the proxy-true relationship changes with SNR/accent/channel, so one threshold is likely mis-set on the conditions that matter most — condition-keyed thresholds are required and are not addressed here. All experiments are text (GPQA/MMLU/MATH, AlpacaFarm).

**可借鉴**：(1) Definition 1 (hacking threshold) as the precise construct for P10 — 'true reward turns down while proxy and divergence keep rising' — with the operational consequence that any budget-scaling curve must be reported against a true-reward axis, not the proxy the controller optimizes. (2) Theorem 1's unimodality, which licenses one-dimensional root-finding rather than grid search and predicts a single peak when selection pressure is monotone. (3) The empirical-CDF quantile transform of proxy scores to [0,1] as standard preprocessing making heterogeneous reward scales comparable while preserving ranking. (4) The SBoN stationarity condition Cov(r_true(u), u)=0 — an interpretable, estimable calibration statistic (stop increasing pressure when true reward stops co-varying with the proxy percentile). (5) Soft Best-of-n with tuned inverse temperature as the hedged alternative to hard argmax, plus the observation that when the proxy is always at odds with truth the optimum is lambda=0, i.e. fall back to the reference policy — an abstain rule expressed as a selection parameter. (6) Scoring one fixed candidate pool with a suite of RMs of varying size (1.8B/8B) and plotting accuracy vs n, with hacking visible even under a strong RM on GPQA. (7) The two-arm design: verifiable-reward arm plus a controlled proxy-vs-gold arm with injected label noise (0% and 25%) over 4 seeds.

### 2510.08191 — Training-Free Group Relative Policy Optimization (Training-Free GRPO)

**域**：text (LLM agents: math reasoning with code interpreter, and web search; DeepSeek-V3.1-Terminus / V3.2-Exp / Qwen3-32B / Qwen2.5-72B / QwQ-32B)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P8 (reward-as-controller, gradient-free multi-step: the closest occupied position to our mechanism — semantic advantage as the update signal over a frozen core), P3 (memory write-gates: Add/Delete/Modify/Keep action space, though without utility scoring), P5 (skill/experience induction from rollout contrasts; tool-call reduction as an induced efficiency), P1 (explicit token/dollar pricing of the control plane)

**方法面**：Keeps GRPO's group-relative structure but moves the update from parameter space to context space. Frozen policy conditions on an external experience library E: pi_theta(o | q, E). Per query, sample G rollouts (G=5 for math/DAPO-100, G=3 for web/AFM-100; T=0.7), score each with reward R;【复核更正 wf_40068490】 a semantic advantage is produced ONLY for groups containing both clear winners and losers (mirroring GRPO's Â_i=0 when std(r)=0). The same LLM first summarizes each rollout (s_i = M(p_summary, q, o_i)), then articulates why some succeeded and others failed and extracts a concise natural-language experience A_text = M(p_extract, q, s_i, E). The batch's A_text set drives an explicit four-operation write-gate on E: Add / Delete / Modify / Keep. Three epochs, one batch per epoch (3 steps), 100 training items (DAPO-100 for math, AFM-100 for web). Evaluation is out-of-domain: AIME24/25 at Mean@32 with T=0.3; WebWalkerQA pass@1. The frozen base model is argued to act as the implicit KL anchor. Zero gradients anywhere.

**自述与显见局限**：No dedicated limitations section. Read out of its own tables: on QwQ-32B on the WebWalkerQA subset pass@1 goes 27.5 -> 25.5, i.e. DOWN, while pass@3 goes 43.1 -> 45.1 — the method can hurt top-1 selection on a weaker core. Directly generated experiences (no group, no reward) fail to help and can hurt (66.7 -> 64.7 pass@1), so the effect is attributed to reward-driven comparison, not to having tips in context. The default configuration uses ground-truth answers to compute rewards during learning; the no-ground-truth variant (implicit majority voting / self-discrimination) reaches 80.7/68.9 AIME24/25 vs 82.7/73.3 with gold — a real but not free degradation, and majority voting itself presupposes answer identity. Learning is only 3 steps on 100 items with no seed variance reported; group size 5. Gains on the strong backbone are modest in absolute terms (+2.7 AIME24, +5.4 AIME25).

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level: (a) the write-gate is a prompted Add/Delete/Modify/Keep decision with no utility score, no retention statistic, no poisoning defense, and no evidence about what happens when a wrong experience is written — MemRL's Q-utility is strictly more instrumented on this axis; (b) experiences form a flat global library, not condition-keyed or retrieved per item, so context cost grows and irrelevant experiences are always injected; (c) no abstain and no per-item budget — every training item gets G=5 rollouts even though groups with no winner/loser spread produce nothing and could be predicted; (d) the reward is assumed available and correct, with no admissibility check; (e) the negative QwQ-32B result is reported but not diagnosed.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：The reward R forming the group ranking is a verifiable answer check (AIME exact answer; WebWalkerQA pass@1) — a frozen black-box speech/omni core has no such oracle at learning or test time. Their no-oracle fallback is implicit majority voting / self-discrimination across rollouts, which needs candidate answer identity to vote; for free-form speech responses 'the same answer' is itself an estimate, so the fallback degrades further than their 80.7/68.9 numbers suggest. The experience library is injected as a text token prior in the same prompt — this requires a core that reliably conditions on long in-context instructions, and with an omni core the failure may live in the audio encoder where no token prior reaches.
