The library is keyed by task domain (math vs web), not acoustic condition, so it cannot express 'in low-SNR far-field audio, do X'. Group rollouts at G=5 (math) / G=3 (web) with 60K input tokens each are priced for a text API; audio inputs are token-expensive and re-listening does not produce i.i.d. diversity the way text resampling does.【复核更正 wf_40068490】 The QwQ-32B pass@1 regression warns that this transfers unevenly even within text.

**可借鉴**：(1) The 'semantic advantage' as a gradient-free substitute for a numerical advantage, including the gating rule that it is computed only for groups containing BOTH winners and losers — the natural-language analogue of std(r)=0 and a free way to avoid burning the controller on uninformative groups. (2) The four-operation write-gate vocabulary (Add / Delete / Modify / Keep) as an explicit, auditable memory-update action space — the minimal decision-rights set for external memory. (3) The two-stage summarize-then-contrast prompt structure, keeping the comparison step's context bounded. (4) Three ablations to replicate verbatim: group size 1 vs 5 (isolates whether comparison, not reflection, carries the effect), directly-generated experiences matched in COUNT to the learned ones (controls for context-length/placebo), and the no-ground-truth variant (bounds how much survives losing the oracle). (5) Mean@32 reporting rather than a single greedy run, with train/eval temperature separated (0.7 learn / 0.3 eval). (6) Explicit token-and-dollar cost accounting ($8-18 total learning; 38M input / 6.6M output tokens; ~60K input + 8K output tokens per inference request with cache-hit pricing) — a template for pricing a training-free controller. (7) The cross-domain transfer table showing a domain-fine-tuned model collapsing off-domain (ReTool 18.3 on WebWalker) while a context-space library is swappable. (8) The learning-dynamics plot showing average tool calls DECREASING across steps — an efficiency effect reported alongside accuracy.

**备注**：Method-occupation reading: occupies 'gradient-free group-relative update over a frozen text LLM' fairly squarely. Steps remaining on top are the write-gate's lack of a utility statistic, absence of condition keying, absence of any admissibility check on the reward, and no budget allocation — i.e. our P3/P9/P1 slots rather than P8's core mechanism.

### 2512.02304 — When Does Verification Pay Off? A Closer Look at LLMs as Solution Verifiers

**域**：text (37 LLMs x 9 benchmarks; solver-verifier pairing study, Llama3 / Qwen2.5 / Qwen3 / DeepSeek-R1, base and post-trained, 0.5B-72B)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P9 (decision-calibration and admissibility: verifier gain as the go/no-go statistic for a selection stage; accuracy disqualified as a proxy), P10 (self-similarity bias raising FPR is a selection-pressure failure mode distinct from proxy overoptimization), P6 (whether the judge may share lineage with the core is a composition constraint), P1 (whether to buy verification at all for a given task/condition)

**方法面**：A systematic measurement study of LLM-as-verifier for rejection sampling. Solver S produces a CoT solution; verifier V (also CoT, then a boxed correct/incorrect) gives a binary judgment; correctness c(x,y) is established by exact match or task rules. Central instrument: verifier gain = Precision(S,V;D) - SolverAcc(S;D) (Eq. 1), justified because under infinite rejection sampling the accepted solution's expected correctness converges to the verifier's precision — so gain is the asymptotic ceiling of rejection-sampling improvement, computable from ONE verification round with no resampling experiment. Three regimes are partitioned and averaged separately: self-verification, intra-family, cross-family (base and post-trained treated as distinct families). Validation: 12x12 solver-verifier pairs run through actual rejection sampling (up to 3 and up to 10 attempts, keep last attempt if none accepted); predicted vs empirical gain gives r=0.796 at 3 attempts and r=0.892 at 10, consistent with the metric's asymptotic nature. Solver-verifier similarity is mean cosine similarity of solution embeddings (all-mpnet-base-v2), cross-checked with log-likelihood. Spontaneous self-verification is measured by keyword scanning of solver traces ('let me check', 'wait', 'reconsider'). Generation at T=0.7, top-p 0.9, 8192 max tokens, vLLM; outputs without a boxed answer are discarded. No gradients; all models off-the-shelf.

**自述与显见局限**：Stated: the origin of the self-similarity bias is not investigated; the study is single-turn solver-verifier only, with multi-turn settings where feedback re-enters the conversation named as future work. Structurally: verifier gain is asymptotic and over-predicts at small budgets (slope 0.221 at 3 attempts vs 0.405 at 10, i.e. realized gain is a fraction of the metric); all 9 tasks need ground-truth labels by construction; the spontaneous-self-verification measure is keyword matching, a coarse proxy; discarding non-boxed outputs is a silent filter; AIME is flagged by the authors as a contamination-suspect outlier.

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level: (a) verifier gain is a comparative selection metric, not a controller — nothing decides online whether to verify this item, how many attempts to allocate, or when to abstain; (b) computing precision needs labels, so a label-free estimator of verifier gain is open (Weaver's weak supervision is the natural pairing and neither paper does the join); (c) the similarity finding motivates 'pick a dissimilar verifier' but offers no procedure for constructing dissimilarity when only one core is available; (d) single-turn only, so credit assignment across a repair chain is untouched; (e) the task-verifiability finding is descriptive, with no predictor of verifiability from task properties.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：Precision, and therefore verifier gain, is computed against ground-truth correctness via exact match or task rules — undefined for free-form speech responses, so the metric must be re-derived against a graded or adjudicated adequacy notion, at which point 'precision' inherits the adjudicator's error. The rejection-sampling validation assumes cheap resampling (up to 10 attempts x 144 pairs), priced very differently for an omni core on audio inputs. The 'prefer a dissimilar verifier' rule is the sharpest transfer hazard: our natural verifier candidates are the same frozen core re-prompted or a sibling omni model, i.e. maximally similar by construction — and similarity in the audio front-end (both mis-hearing the same noisy utterance) is not captured by their text-embedding similarity measure at all, so the diagnostic understates the correlation for us. Their insight 'verification pays when verifying is easier than solving' inverts uncomfortably for speech: judging whether a response reflects what was actually said requires re-solving the perception problem, placing our tasks structurally closer to their low-gain knowledge-recall cluster than to their high-gain math/logic cluster — a hypothesis about our setting, not a finding of this paper. The spontaneous-self-verification keyword scan is English-text-trace specific. All 37 models and 9 benchmarks are text.

**可借鉴**：(1) Verifier gain (precision minus solver accuracy) as a cheap single-round predictor of whether adding a selection stage pays off at all — the correct admissibility question to ask before deploying any selector, with its empirical validation curve supplied. (2) The explicit finding that verifier ACCURACY is not a reliable predictor of test-time improvement while gain is — disqualifying accuracy-only verifier reports. (3) The three-regime partition (self / intra-family / cross-family) as a required decomposition whenever the judge shares lineage with the core — our default situation if we judge a frozen omni core with itself or a sibling. (4) The similarity-vs-FPR analysis: FPR rises and gain falls as solver-verifier solution-embedding similarity rises (cross-family FPR r=0.613 slope 3.521; gain r=-0.257 slope -0.528), giving a measurable design rule and a measurable quantity. (5) The spontaneous-self-verification diagnostic: Qwen3 96% and DeepSeek 73% of solver traces already self-check vs Llama3/Qwen2.5 at 1-2%, explaining away apparent 'no self-improvement' as 'the verification already happened inside the solve' — an essential confound check before claiming a repair stage adds nothing. (6) The task-verifiability contrast: mathematical/logical/structured tasks (AIME, GSM8K, 3SAT, Sudoku) give high gain, knowledge-recall tasks (GPQA, MMLU-STEM, CSQA) near zero, with the mechanistic reading that verification pays only when verifying is easier than solving. (7) Their four-item deployment checklist as a review rubric.

### 2601.03192 — MemRL: Self-Evolving Agents via Runtime Reinforcement Learning on Episodic Memory

**域**：text (LLM agents over frozen API backbones: BigCodeBench, LifelongAgentBench OS/DB, ALFWorld, HLE; GPT-4o / GPT-4o-mini / GPT-5-mini / Gemini-3-pro)　**供体级别**：PROTOCOL_DONOR　**喂给**：P3 (condition-keyed memory + write-gates: the central donor for this slot — triplet schema, utility-gated retrieval, forgetting rate, high-Q-failure retention; condition keying itself remains the open step), P8 (reward-as-controller: outcome feedback drives the retrieval policy with zero gradients), P9 (Q-calibration reliability diagram as a headroom/trust instrument), P5 (procedural experience reuse across tasks with the task-similarity precondition made explicit)

**方法面**：Non-parametric RL performed entirely in memory space over a frozen LLM, formalized as a Memory-Based MDP where the retrieval policy mu(m|s,M) — not the LLM — is the object optimized (§3). Memory is a triplet bank M={(z_i intent, e_i experience, Q_i utility)} (Eq. 5). Two-Phase Retrieval: Phase A recalls a candidate pool by cosine similarity over intent embeddings with a hard sparsity threshold delta and Top-k1 (Eq. 6); Phase B re-ranks with a composite score (1-lambda)*z-normalized similarity + lambda*z-normalized Q and takes Top-k2 (Eq. 7); if the candidate pool is empty the agent falls back to the bare frozen LLM (an explicit no-memory escape). Utility is updated from environmental reward by a Monte-Carlo-style EMA Q <- Q + alpha(r - Q) (Eq. 4, a one-step simplification of the TD rule Eq. 3), applied only to memories actually injected into context. Each trajectory is LLM-summarized and written back as a new triplet with Q_init. §4.4 proves the estimate is unbiased with lim sup Var(Q_t) <= alpha/(2-alpha) * Var(r|s,m), and frames the joint retrieval/utility loop as generalized EM (retrieval ranking = E-step policy improvement, utility update = M-step) converging to a stationary point. Metrics: Success Rate and Cumulative Success Rate over 10 epochs, plus a Transferring setting where the memory bank is frozen and applied to held-out tasks. No weight updates anywhere.

**自述与显见局限**：Stated §6: step-wise updates introduce high-variance noise on long-horizon trajectories (they suggest multi-step updates / periodic consolidation); credit-assignment ambiguity when several retrieved experiences contributed, with Shapley or value decomposition named as the fix; and performance drifts toward mere reflection when task similarity is low — demonstrated on HLE where intra-dataset similarity is 0.186 and cross-task retrieval ties single-task reflection (0.606 vs 0.610), so the advantage is conditional on a dense, related task stream. Memory security is deferred to an appendix discussion. Backbones are chosen per benchmark 'to avoid no-signal or ceiling problems', a selection choice affecting comparability. Absolute margins over the strongest baseline MemP are modest (+3.8 CSR runtime, +2.8 SR transfer).

**改进空间（方向级）**：Direction-level: (a) the reward r is a programmatic success bit with no admissibility check and no treatment of a noisy or gameable reward — under a proxy reward the unbiasedness result no longer speaks to the quantity we care about; (b) memory keys are intent embeddings only, with no environment/condition key, so 'this worked under these conditions' cannot be expressed; (c) no explicit write-gate on ingestion (every summarized trajectory is written; filtering happens later via Q and the similarity threshold) and no poisoning defense despite the paper flagging memory security; (d) credit assignment across multiple retrieved items is named as unsolved; (e) lambda, k1, k2, delta are fixed global hyperparameters tuned by ablation, not adapted per item or condition; (f) no abstain and no budget allocation.

**转移边界（语音下什么失效）**：The environmental reward is a programmatic success bit (code execution, OS/DB task completion, ALFWorld goal satisfaction) — a frozen black-box speech/omni core has no deterministic oracle, so r must be a noisy proxy; the unbiasedness proof then converges Q to E[proxy | s,m], not true utility, meaning a Q-calibration curve could look excellent while tracking a hacked proxy (precisely the failure 2506.19248 formalizes, and MemRL does not model it). Intent keys are query-text embeddings; our retrieval must key on acoustic condition, absent from query text and estimable only by a component that can itself be wrong — an error channel with no analogue here. The stationarity assumption behind the convergence result (fixed task distribution, frozen inference policy) is stressed by acoustic drift across deployments. Their own HLE result — cross-task retrieval loses its advantage at intra-dataset similarity 0.186 — warns that heterogeneous conditions degenerate the method to per-item reflection. Cosine similarity over sentence embeddings presumes a text representation of the episode; a spoken episode's relevant similarity may be acoustic. All benchmarks are text/symbolic-environment agents on 2025-26 text APIs.

**可借鉴**：(1) The Intent-Experience-Utility triplet with a learned scalar utility as the memory record schema — exactly the structure our P3 slot needs and a strict upgrade over Reflexion's flat append or Training-Free GRPO's unscored library. (2) The two-phase retrieval decomposition (similarity recall as a trust region, then value-aware re-rank), with the explicit rationale that the similarity gate prevents retrieving high-Q but semantically irrelevant memories and regularizes cold start. (3) The Monte-Carlo EMA update with its variance bound alpha/(2-alpha)*Var(r|s,m) — a usable rule for setting alpha against reward noise, which matters more for us since our reward will be noisier. (4) The Q-calibration curve: Q-estimate bins vs downstream success rate, Pearson r=0.861, rising 21.5% (bin 0.2-0.3) to 88.1% (bin 0.9-1.0) — the reliability-diagram instrument our P9 slot needs for any learned utility, and cheap. (5) The memory-composition analysis showing ~12% 'failure' memories retained in the highest-Q bins, i.e. high-utility near-misses — evidence that a utility gate is not equivalent to a success filter, and a caution against success-replay write-gates. (6) Forgetting Rate FR = N_lost/N_fail (tasks regressing from success to failure, over current failures): MemRL 0.041 vs MemP 0.051, spiking to 0.073 when z-score normalization and the similarity gate are removed — a reusable poisoning/degradation detector. (7) The Cumulative-vs-current Success Rate gap as a catastrophic-forgetting diagnostic. (8) Three clean ablations: lambda sweep {0,0.25,0.5,0.75,1} with a concave optimum at 0.5 (pure similarity plateaus, pure Q is volatile and context-detached); retrieval bandwidth (k1,k2) in {3/1, 5/3, 10/5} showing an inverted-U (sparse = insufficient guidance, dense = context noise); cross-task vs single-task scope with the task-similarity covariate explaining when it matters. (9) The frozen-memory transfer protocol as a generalization test.

**备注**：Method-occupation reading vs Training-Free GRPO: MemRL occupies 'utility-scored, retrieved memory' while TF-GRPO occupies 'contrastive experience distillation into a flat injected library'. Neither keys on deployment conditions, neither defends against a corrupted write, and neither prices retrieval — those are the steps remaining for us.

**组级综合（agent 提交）**：SCOPE. Nine papers, all TEXT domain (LLM agents, reasoning, verification, memory). Nothing here is evidence about speech or omni; every number is a text-benchmark number and H5 stays withheld. All nine are method/measurement donors only. Gradient use is confined to two: WebThinker's iterative online DPO (2504.21776 §3.5) and Weaver's optional 400M cross-encoder distillation (2506.18203 §6); 2502.00271's verifiers are trained but its analysis protocol is not. The remaining six operate over frozen models with no gradients, which is why their PROTOCOLS, not their mechanisms, are what we take.

WHAT THIS SUBSET SETTLES. (1) The selection-pressure story is now well-formed end to end. 2506.19248 gives the formal object — a hacking threshold where true reward turns down while proxy reward and divergence keep rising — plus a proof that under monotone selection pressure the true-reward curve has at most one interior peak, making the operating point findable by one-dimensional root-finding rather than grid search. 2502.00271 supplies the empirical shape in a different guise: verifier-guided search beats repeated sampling at n=1 by +8.5 to +23.0 coverage and loses by -3.1 to -46.1 at n=32, worse on harder and OOD problems, with 77-89% of failures attributed to selection rather than generation. Together they settle that 'more selection is better' is false and that any budget-scaling claim must be a curve against a true-quality axis, not the proxy being optimized.
(2) The admissibility question — should a selection or repair stage be deployed at all — now has three complementary statistics. 2512.02304 gives verifier gain (precision minus solver accuracy), validated against actual rejection sampling at r=0.892 over 10 attempts and computable from a single verification round; it also disqualifies verifier accuracy as a proxy for usefulness. 2506.18203 gives the generation-verification gap (Pass@K minus success rate) as the headroom object and, more importantly, a label-free way to estimate verifier accuracies from pairwise agreement alone, needing gold labels only for a scalar class prior on ~1% of items. 2506.11930 gives the ceiling from the other end: even with a feedback generator holding the gold answer, solvers plateau below target, with 62.8-100% of residual errors classified as feedback resistance rather than feedback quality.
(3) External memory as a controller now has a schema and a stability metric. 2601.03192 supplies Intent-Experience-Utility triplets, a two-phase retrieval (similarity as trust region, then utility re-rank) with a concave optimum at lambda=0.5 and an inverted-U in retrieval bandwidth, a Q-calibration curve at r=0.861, and a Forgetting Rate that spikes from 0.041 to 0.073 when normalization and the similarity gate are removed. 2510.08191 supplies the write-gate action vocabulary (Add/Delete/Modify/Keep) and the group-comparison rule that only groups with both winners and losers generate a signal. 2303.11366 is the unscored-append baseline both improve on.

WHAT TRANSFERS. Concretely reusable, in rough order of value: the label-free weak-supervision estimator for verifier accuracy (2506.18203 Eq. 1-5) joined to verifier gain as the go/no-go test (2512.02304 Eq. 1); semantic entropy as an actionability statistic with entropy-bucketed improvement-rate plots, plus the hard result that self-reported model confidence is useless (>95% claim understanding, then fail to update) (2506.11930 §4.3); the empirical-CDF quantile transform of heterogeneous proxy scores and soft-selection with a tuned temperature, including lambda=0 as an abstain expressed in the selection parameter (2506.19248); the generation-vs-selection failure attribution with an oracle-selection upper bound (2502.00271 §5.2); the Q-calibration reliability diagram and Forgetting Rate as memory-health instruments (2601.03192 §5.4); the FP/FN contingency table for any self-constructed oracle plus the FP-is-terminal / FN-is-recoverable asymmetry (2303.11366 Tables 2-3); the orchestrator/executor split where a controller holds decision rights and a cheaper model executes manipulations against retrieved evidence, with a lexicographic correctness >> tool-calls >> length credit rule (2504.21776); and token/dollar cost accounting for the control plane as a fraction of generation cost (2510.08191 ~$18 learning; 0.57% overhead in 2506.18203's distilled variant). Also transferable as review discipline: leave-one-component-out ablations run on both a closed-form and an open-ended judged arm; matched-budget comparison against plain repeated sampling as the mandatory control; a validated LLM-annotator with a reported human-agreement rate before any judge is trusted.

WHAT BREAKS. One boundary dominates this entire read set: every load-bearing instrument here is anchored to a deterministic correctness oracle over discrete answers. Coverage, Pass@K, precision, verifier gain, the latent binary Y in weak supervision, valid-path labelling by rollout, majority voting, semantic-entropy clustering by final answer, rejection sampling against previously-tried answers, MemRL's success bit, Training-Free GRPO's group ranking, and HedgeTune's true-reward calibration set all presume either exact-match equivalence or a programmatic pass/fail. A frozen black-box speech/omni core gives free-form outputs over waveform observations with no such relation, so each must be re-derived against a proxy adjudicator — and that substitution is not neutral: it reintroduces the proxy whose failure the instrument was built to detect. Second boundary: independence and dissimilarity assumptions collapse for us. Weaver assumes verifiers are conditionally independent given correctness; 2512.02304's central design rule is to pick a verifier that thinks differently from the solver. In our setting the plausible verifiers are the same frozen core re-prompted, a sibling omni model, or a text judge over a transcription — all of which fail together on the same acoustic conditions, and text-embedding similarity does not even measure that shared front-end failure. Third: condition keying is absent everywhere. Every method here keys on task text, task domain, or difficulty level; none keys on acoustic condition, which is not recoverable from the query and must itself be estimated. 2506.19248's single global hacking threshold is calibrated per (dataset, reward model) and would be mis-set exactly on the conditions that matter; MemRL's own HLE result (advantage vanishes at intra-dataset similarity 0.186) warns that heterogeneous conditions degrade utility-keyed retrieval to plain reflection. Fourth: 2502.00271's step-level pruning and 2506.19248's TP2 family both presume intervenable decoding or i.i.d. candidates from a fixed reference policy; a black-box core with no logprobs gives us whole-response reranking only, and our repair/re-observe loop changes the candidate distribution between rounds, putting us outside the proven family. Fifth, a task-structure hazard rather than a mechanism one: 2512.02304 finds verification pays when verifying is easier than solving (math/logic high gain, factual recall near zero). Judging whether a spoken response matches what was actually said requires re-solving the perception problem, which places our tasks structurally nearer their low-gain cluster — a hypothesis about our setting, to be tested, not a finding of that paper.

RESIDUAL SLOTS THIS SUBSET DOES NOT FILL. P1 supply pricing / value-of-information is barely occupied: WebThinker prices tool calls only as a lexicographic tie-break, nobody in this subset estimates the value of an additional observation before acquiring it, and in our setting supply is endogenous (re-listen, re-segment) rather than an unbounded external corpus. Budget allocation is flat everywhere — 10 iterations per item in 2506.11930, fixed K=100 in 2506.18203, fixed G=5 in 2510.08191 — so headroom-conditioned allocation is unoccupied across all nine. Abstain appears only implicitly (2506.19248's lambda=0 fallback, MemRL's empty-pool escape) and is never calibrated. Poisoning defense for external memory is named but not built (2601.03192 defers memory security; 2510.08191 has no defense). Credit assignment across multiple contributing memories or tools is explicitly open (2601.03192 §6). And no paper in this set joins label-free verifier calibration to an online admissibility gate — 2502.00271 names that detector as future work, 2506.18203 measures accuracies but never retires a verifier online, and 2512.02304 needs labels to compute its gain; that join is the clearest unoccupied step directly on top of this read set.
